ṅṛht1.1


jayati sa dainyagadākulamakhilamidaṃ paśyato jagadyasya |
hṛdayasthaiva galitvā jātā rasarūpiṇī karuṇā || ṅṛht_1.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

śrīmanmahāgaṇādhipataye namaḥ |
bhavabhayarakṣaṇadakṣaṃ natvā mugdhāvabodhinīṃ tanute |
rasahṛdayasuprayuktāṃ ṭīkāmṛjubhāvagāmāptaḥ || 1.1:1 ||
guṇavāridhikuralakule hariharamiśraḥ pratītamahimākhyaḥ |
tatputro bhuvi mahito maheśa iti nāmavikhyātaḥ || 1.1:2 ||
tadanvaye bhāratībhāvasaṃyutastadātmajaḥ prastutavāgbhirīśvaraḥ |
caturbhujo bhāvitabhāvamānasaḥ svalokajātasya kulānubhāvataḥ || 1.1:3 ||
jyeṣṭho'bhūdbhuvi pārijātakataruḥ khaṇḍelavālānvaye tatputraḥ kila nāthaballavasudaḥ prāṇairyaśo'rthānvitaḥ |
tatputreṇa ca sāvareṇa patinā bandhasya dharmārthinā gīrvāṇāśu rugoñcajena satataṃ tenātra yatnaḥ kṛtaḥ || 1.1:4 ||
iha śāstrārambhe ācāryaśrīmadgovindapādāḥ śiṣṭasamayaparipālanārthe śāstrasya deśayato gurupādasya bhagavato vastunirdeśarūpaṃ maṅgalamācaranti jayatītyādi || 1.1:5 ||
maṅgalalakṣaṇaṃ yathā āśīrnamaskriyā vastunirdeśo maṅgala iti || 1.1:6 ||
vastunirdeśaḥ pañcadhā yathāsti bhāti priyaṃ rūpaṃ nāma cetyaṃśapañcakam || 1.1:7 ||
ādyaṃ trayaṃ brahmarūpaṃ śaktirūpaṃ tato dvayamiti || 1.1:8 ||
sa haro jayati sarvotkarṣeṇa vartate || 1.1:9 ||
akhilaṃ jagatpaśyata iti vastunirdeśābjayaddraṣṭā || 1.1:10 ||
tathā ca śrutiḥ apāṇipādo javano grahītā || 1.1:11 ||
paśyatyacakṣuḥ sa śṛṇotyakarṇaḥ |
sa vetti vedyaṃ na ca tasya vettā tamāhuragryaṃ puruṣaṃ purāṇamiti || 1.1:12 ||
yasya hṛdayasyaiva rasarūpiṇī karuṇā ghṛṇā jātā prādurbhūtā || 1.1:13 ||
kiṃbhūtvā galitvādravitvā galitasya sthānāccyutiriti yuktam || 1.1:14 ||
eṣā hṛdayasthaiva || 1.1:15 ||
evāvyayamanyasthānaniṣedhavāci || 1.1:16 ||
rasarūpiṇīti rasaḥ pāradastatsvarūpaṃ yasyāḥ sā || 1.1:17 ||
tathā ca vākyam |
raso jalaṃ raso harṣo rasaḥ śṛṅgārapūrvakaḥ |
svādvādiṣu ca niryāse pārade'pi raso viṣe |
ityanekārthaḥ || 1.1:18 ||
sāmānyatastadrūpaṃ dravatvaṃ viśeṣato rasarūpaṃ sarvopakāritvam || 1.1:19 ||
tathā ca vākyaṃ rasaratnākare |
ājanmapāpakṛtanirdahanaikavahnirdāridryaduḥkhagajavāraṇasiṃharūpaḥ iti || 1.1:20 ||
kiṃbhūtasya harasya dainyagadākulaṃ jagatsaṃsāraṃ paśyataḥ dainyayaṃ ca gadāśca tairākulaṃ vyāptaṃ dainyaṃ dīnabhāvo dāridryaṃ gadā vyādhaya iti || 1.1:21 ||
akhilamiti sarvavyāpipadam || 1.1:22 ||
yathā mañjaryā |
yasya rogasya yo yogastenaiva saha dāpayet |
rasendro haro rogānnarakuñjaravājinām || 1.1:23 ||
iti || 1.1:24 ||
anyanmataṃ ca |
rasabhasma vinā tatra kathyate saṃhitākramaḥ |
anuktamapi vijñeyaṃ tatra tatrāṅgaśāntaye || 1.1:25 ||
iti || 1.1:26 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.2


pītāmbaro'tha balijinnāgakṣayabahalarāgagaruḍacaraḥ |
jayati sa haririva harajo vidalitabhavadainyaduḥkhabharaḥ || ṅṛht_1.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pītetyādi || 1.2:1 ||
anena padyena kavirharajasya hareśca samatvaṃ sūcayati || 1.2:2 ||
sa purāṇakavivarṇito harajo jayati sarvotkarṣeṇa vartate harādīśvarājjāto harajaḥ || 1.2:3 ||
sa utprekṣyate haririva viṣṇuriva || 1.2:4 ||
kiṃbhūto hariḥ pītāmbaraḥ pīte ambare vastre yasya saḥ dukūlayugmatvāt || 1.2:5 ||
punaḥ kimbhūto balijitbaliṃ balināmānaṃ daityaviśeṣaṃ jayatīti tathoktaḥ || 1.2:6 ||
punaḥ kiṃbhūtaḥ nāgakṣayetyādi nāgānāṃ śeṣādīnāṃ kṣayāya nāśāya bahalarāgo bahuprīto yo'sau garuḍaḥ khageśvaraḥ tatra carati gacchati tathoktaḥ || 1.2:7 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ vidalitetyādiḥ viśeṣaṇena dalito dūrīkṛto bhavasya saṃsārasya duḥkhabharo yena saḥ dainyaṃ dāridryaṃ duḥkhaṃ vyādhirūpaṃ tayorbharo bāhulyamiti || 1.2:8 ||
adhunā harajaṃ viśeṣayati kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ pītāmbaraḥ pītāmbaraḥ pūrvārthaḥ || 1.2:9 ||
kathaṃ yuktaḥ tamarthe spaṣṭayatyanena sūtarājenāpi catvāri vāsāṃsi dhṛtāni viprādivarṇabhedāt || 1.2:10 ||
rasaratnākare yathā |
śvetāruṇaharidrābhakṛṣṇā viprādipāradāḥ iti || 1.2:11 ||
brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāḥ śvetaraktapītakṛṣṇavastradhāriṇo jñātavyāḥ na tveṣāṃ svarūpamiti || 1.2:12 ||
rasamañjaryā yathā |
antaḥ sunīlo bahirujjvalo yo madhyāhnasūryapratimaprakāśaḥ iti || 1.2:13 ||
antaḥ svarūpaṃ bahirvāsāṃsīti kiṃvadantī || 1.2:14 ||
atheti samuccaye prasādaḥ || 1.2:15 ||
atheti maṅgalānantarārambhapraśnakālasvādhikārapratijñāsamuccayeṣviti || 1.2:16 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ balijitbalīn jayatīti baliścarma jarākṛtam || 1.2:17 ||
ityanekārthaḥ || 1.2:18 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ nāgakṣayetyādi nā puṃsvarūpaḥ punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ gamyate'neneti gaḥ pakṣayorgaḥ gakṣaye pakṣanāśe sati bahalarāgo bahurāgavān yaḥ sa rasaḥ tena garuḍa iva cāryate iti || 1.2:19 ||
kiṃ pāradaḥ pakṣanāśe sati ākāśagamanaṃ dadātīti tātparyārthaḥ || 1.2:20 ||
rasaratnākare yathā |
hato hanti jarāmṛtyuṃ mūrchito vyādhighātakaḥ |
datte ca khe gatiṃ baddhaḥ ko'nyaḥ sūtātkriyākaraḥ || 1.2:21 ||
iti || 1.2:22 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ vidalitetyādiḥ pūrvārthaḥ karuṇāparatvena dainyaduḥkhahāritvaṃ sūcayati || 1.2:23 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.3

mūrchitvā harati rujaṃ bandhanamanubhūya muktido bhavati |
amarīkaroti sumṛtaḥ ko'nyaḥ karuṇākaraḥ sūtāt || ṅṛht_1.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

yaḥ pūrvaviśiṣṭo harajastasmādanyaḥ karuṇāparo dayāvān kaḥ na ko'pi yato rujaṃ śarīravyathāṃ harati || 1.3:1 ||
kiṃbhūtvā mūrchitvā mūrchito bhūtvā || 1.3:2 ||
mūrchitalakṣaṇaṃ rasaratnākare |
kajjalābho yadā sūto vihāya ghanacāpalam |
dṛśyate'sau tadā jñeyo mūrchitaḥ sūtarāḍbudhaiḥ || 1.3:3 ||
iti || 1.3:4 ||
punarbandhanamanubhūya dhṛtvā muktido bhavati muktiṃ dadātīti || 1.3:5 ||
muktiścaturdhā varṇitā sālokyasārūpyasāmīpyasāyujyabhedāt || 1.3:6 ||
bandhanaṃ ca mukhyatayā dvividhamavāntaravyāpāreṇa ca caturvidham || 1.3:7 ||
adhunā dvividhabandhanoddeśaḥ nirbījabaddho bījabaddhaśca || 1.3:8 ||
nirbījabaddho yathā rasamañjaryām |
rasastu pādāṃśasuvarṇajīrṇaḥ piṣṭīkṛto gandhakayogataśca |
tulyāṃśagandhaiḥ puṭitaḥ krameṇa nirbījanāmākhilarogahartā || 1.3:9 ||
iti || 1.3:10 ||
punarbījabaddho yathā |
bījīkṛtairabhrasattvahematārārkakāntaiḥ saha sādhito yaḥ |
yutastataḥ ṣaḍguṇagandhacūrṇaiḥ sa bījabaddhe'pyadhikaprabhāvaḥ || 1.3:11 ||
iti || 1.3:12 ||
avāntaracaturvidhabandhoddeśo yathā |
poṭaḥ khoṭo jalaukā ca bhasmatvaṃ ca caturvidham |
bandhaścaturvidhaḥ sūte vijñeyo bhiṣaguttamaiḥ || 1.3:13 ||
iti || 1.3:14 ||
tallakṣaṇaṃ saṃketakalikāyām |
poṭaḥ parpaṭībandhaḥ piṣṭīstambhastu khoṭakaḥ |
dhmāto druto bhavetkhoṭastvāhataścūrṇatāṃ vrajet || 1.3:15 ||
punardhmāto drutaḥ khoṭa iti khoṭasya lakṣaṇam |
jalaukā pāṭabandhaśca bhasma bhasmanibhaṃ bhavet || 1.3:16 ||
iti || 1.3:17 ||
punaḥ sumṛtaḥ sanamarīkarotīti || 1.3:18 ||
sumṛta iti suḥ iti pūjāyāṃ yathā vidagdhamukhamaṇḍanabahirlāpikāyām |
pūjāyāṃ kiṃ padaṃ proktamastanaṃ ko bibhartyuraḥ |
ka āyudhatayā khyātaḥ pralambāsuravidviṣaḥ || 1.3:19 ||
sunāsīraḥ iti pūjyaḥ || 1.3:20 ||
mṛta iti viśeṣārthaḥ || 1.3:21 ||
mṛto yathā |
ārdratvaṃ ca ghanatvaṃ ca cāpalyaṃ gurutaijasam |
yasyaitāni na vidyante taṃ vidyānmṛtasūtakam || 1.3:22 ||
iti || 1.3:23 ||
pūjya iti rasendramaṅgale yathā |
abaddhasūtaṃ tu hataṃ pramādātkaroti kaṣṭaṃ prabalaṃ rasendraḥ || 1.3:24 ||
iti || 1.3:25 ||
mūrchanādibandhanaparamparayā yo mṛtaḥ sa pūjyo nānyathā || 1.3:26 ||
yathā ca rasamañjaryām |
ajīrṇaṃ cāpyabījaṃ ca sūtakaṃ yastu ghātayet |
brahmahā sa durācārī mama drohī maheśvari || 1.3:27 ||
iti yukto'yamarthaḥ || 1.3:28 ||
karuṇāparatvaṃ satāṃ svabhāva iti || 1.3:29 ||
yathā |
chede'pi candanataruḥ surabhayati mukhaṃ kuṭhārasya iti || 1.3:30 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.4


suragurugodvijahiṃsāpāpakalāpodbhavaṃ kilāsādhyam |
tadapi ca śamayati yasmātko'nyastasmātpavitrataraḥ || ṅṛht_1.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

suretyādi || 1.4:1 ||
tasmātsūtarājātanyo dvitīya pavitratara atiśayena pavitraḥ kaḥ kila śrūyate na ko'pi || 1.4:2 ||
yasmāddhetoḥ apīti niścayena asādhyaṃ rujaṃ śamayati sarvarūpānvitamasādhyaṃ divyauṣadhibhirapi karmavipākenāpi sādhyate tacca kiṃviśiṣṭamasādhyaṃ suretyādiḥ surāśca guravaśca gāvaśca dvijāśca teṣāṃ yā hiṃsā hananamavamānanaṃ vātādutpanno yaḥ pāpakalāpo duṣkṛtapaṭala etasmādudbhavatīti || 1.4:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.5

tasya svayaṃ hi sphurati prādurbhāvaḥ sa śāṃkaraḥ ko'pi |
kathamanyathā hi śamayati vilasanmātrācca pāparujam || ṅṛht_1.5 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tasyetyādi || 1.5:1 ||
kavinānenānumānena lokapratītaḥ kriyate || 1.5:2 ||
yaḥ pūrvoktaḥ sūtarājastasya ko'pyanirvacanīyaḥ sa sarvadeśīyatvena śāṃkaraḥ prādurbhāvaḥ śamayatīti duḥkhamupaśamayatīti śaṃ prasādaḥ śaṃ karotīti śaṃkaraḥ tasyāyaṃ śāṃkaraḥ duḥkhopaśamāyāyaṃ prādurbhavatīti tātparyārthaḥ || 1.5:3 ||
duḥkhamādhivyādhyātmakena dvividhaṃ punarādhibhautikādhidaivikādhyātmikabhedācca trividham || 1.5:4 ||
sa punarasya sūtarājasya svayaṃ sphurati prakāśata iti || 1.5:5 ||
anyathā anyaprakāreṇa śāṃkaraprādurbhāvaṃ vinā pāparujaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ suragurugodvijahiṃsāpāpakalāpodbhavaṃ kathaṃ śamayati || 1.5:6 ||
kutaḥ vilasanmātrātdṛṣṭigocaratvāt || 1.5:7 ||
rasendramaṅgale yathā |
śatāśvamedhena kṛtena puṇyaṃ gokoṭidānena gajendrakoṭibhiḥ |
suvarṇabhūdānasamānadharme naro labhetsūtakadarśanena iti || 1.5:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.6


rasabandhaśca sa dhanyaḥ prārambhe yasya satatamiva karuṇā |
siddhe rase kariṣye mahīmahaṃ nirjarāmaraṇam || ṅṛht_1.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mūrchitabaddhamṛtasyāvasthayā trividhaṃ sūtarājasya bandhanaṃ praśaṃsati kaviḥ rasa ityādi || 1.6:1 ||
bho janāḥ rasabandhaḥ pāradabandhanaṃ dhanyaḥ yasya prārambhe satataṃ nirantaraṃ karuṇā jāyata iti śeṣaḥ || 1.6:2 ||
kiṃrūpā ahaṃ govindanāmā rase siddhe sati samyagbandhanatvaṃ prāpte sati mahīṃ medinīṃ nirjarāmaraṇaṃ yathā tathā kariṣye || 1.6:3 ||
atisāmīpyādvartamāna eva ḻaṭ || 1.6:4 ||
nirjarāmaraṇamiti kriyāviśeṣaṇam || 1.6:5 ||
jarā pālityaṃ maraṇaṃ prāṇatyāga ābhyāṃ rahitaṃ yathā mahyāṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ na yuktam || 1.6:6 ||
atra mahīpadena mahīmadhikṛtya nivasanti ye manujādayasta eva lakṣaṇāvṛttitvāt || 1.6:7 ||
punā rasāyanavaśājjarāniṣedho bhavediti yuktam || 1.6:8 ||
hitopadeśe yathā |
yajjarāvyādhividhvaṃsi bheṣajaṃ tadrasāyanam |
ādye vayasi madhye vā śuddhakāyaḥ samācared |
iti || 1.6:9 ||
maraṇaniṣedhaḥ kathamauṣadhena anyauṣadhiśaktihrāsato na yuktaḥ raseśvaraśaktyādhikyādyuktaḥ || 1.6:10 ||
rasaratnākare yathā |
mṛtyorjarāviṣadharasya ca vainateya tubhyaṃ namāmi suravanditasūtarāja |
iti || 1.6:11 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.7


ye cātyaktaśarīrā haragaurīsṛṣṭijāṃ tanuṃ prāptāḥ |
vandyāste rasasiddhā mantragaṇāḥ kiṃkarā yeṣām || ṅṛht_1.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

jarāmaraṇaniṣedhatvena kimādhikyaṃ tadāha ya ityādi || 1.7:1 ||
ye evaṃvidharasasiddhāste vandyā abhivādanayogyāḥ stutyāśca || 1.7:2 ||
vadi abhivādanastutyoḥ || 1.7:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭā atyaktaśarīrāḥ na tyaktaṃ śarīraṃ yaiste jīvanmuktā ityarthaḥ || 1.7:4 ||
śarīraṃ dvividhaṃ sthūlasūkṣmabhedātpṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśātmakaṃ sthūlaṃ kośatrayātmakaṃ sūkṣmam || 1.7:5 ||
yathā sūtram |
vijñānamayaṃ manomayaṃ prāṇamayametatkośatrayaṃ militaṃ sūkṣmaśarīramutpadyate || 1.7:6 ||
anye eva sūkṣmaśarīram || 1.7:7 ||
atyaktaśarīrarasasiddhāśca ucyante |
manthānabhairavo yogī siddhabuddhaśca kanthaḍī |
koraṇṭakaḥ surānandaḥ siddhapādaśca carpaṭī || 1.7:8 ||
kaṇerī pūjyapādaśca nityanātho nirañjanaḥ |
kapālī bindunāthaśca kākacaṇḍīśvaro gajaḥ || 1.7:9 ||
āllamaḥ prabhudevaśca ghoḍācolī ca ṭhiṇṭhinī |
bhālukirnāgadevaśca khaṇḍī kāpālikastathā || 1.7:10 ||
ityādayo mahāsiddhā rasabhogaprasādataḥ |
khaṇḍayitvā kāladaṇḍaṃ trilokyāṃ vicaranti te || 1.7:11 ||
iti || 1.7:12 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭāḥ tanuṃ prāptāḥ śarīraṃ grahītāraḥ || 1.7:13 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭāṃ tanuṃ haragaurīsṛṣṭijāṃ haro mahādevaḥ gaurī pārvatī tayoḥ sṛṣṭiḥ sarjanaṃ maithunasaṃyogastajjātā putrā evetyarthaḥ || 1.7:14 ||
punaryeṣāṃ mantragaṇāḥ kiṃkarāḥ mantrasamūhā ājñākarā ityarthaḥ || 1.7:15 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.8-9


sukṛtaphalaṃ tāvadidaṃ sukule yajjanma dhīśca tatrāpi |
sāpi ca sakalamahītalatulanaphalā bhūtalaṃ ca suvidheyam || ṅṛht_1.8 ||
bhūtalavidheyatāyāḥ phalamarthāste ca vividhabhogaphalāḥ |
bhogāḥ santi śarīre tadanityamaho vṛthā sakalam || ṅṛht_1.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tāvaditi sākalye yāvattāvadityetau sākalyāvadhimānavadhāraṇeṣviti prasādataḥ || 1.8-9:1 ||
idaṃ sukṛtaphalaṃ suvihitakarmaphalamidaṃ kiyatsukṛte śubhānvaye janma svatantrā dhīḥ svādhīnabuddhirityarthaḥ || 1.8-9:2 ||
apīti niścayena || 1.8-9:3 ||
sā buddhiḥ sakalamahītalatulanaphalā sakalasya niravaśeṣasya mahītalasya tulanaṃ phalaṃ yasyāḥ sā tathoktā || 1.8-9:4 ||
tulanamiti tulayā svatantrabuddhirūpayā sakalamahītalasya tulanaṃ bhavatyeveti yuktaṃ kimākārā kiyanmānā kaiḥ śritā kairdhṛtā ca bhūriti jyotiṣasiddhāntavidhānādadṛṣṭāntā bhūrbuddhyopalakṣyate || 1.8-9:5 ||
punaḥ sukulajanmasvatantrabuddhibhyāṃ bhūtalaṃ suvidheyaṃ pūjyaṃ jñātavyam || 1.8-9:6 ||
ekadeśagrahaṇātsarvaṃ grāhyaṃ mahāntastokā bhūtalaṃ bahu īdṛśā mahānto yatra tiṣṭhanti tatsthānaṃ pūjyaṃ vettumaśakyatvātsarvamiti || 1.8-9:7 ||
sukulajanmasambandho vyākhyāyate bhūtaletyādi || 1.8-9:8 ||
bhūtalavidheyatāyāḥ bhūtale pṛthivīmaṇḍale yā vidheyatā sarvakarmapravīṇatā tasyā arthāḥ kāryāṇi kathaṃbhūtāḥ vividhabhogaphalāḥ vividhāśca te bhogāśca vividhabhogāḥ nānābhogāḥ phalaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ || 1.8-9:9 ||
te phalaṃ phalarūpāḥ || 1.8-9:10 ||
bhogāḥ śarīre santi bhavanti || 1.8-9:11 ||
kutaḥ yato vedāntasūtraṃ prārabdhakarmaphalabhogāyatanaṃ śarīramiti || 1.8-9:12 ||
ata eva bhogānāmāśrayāḥ śarīram || 1.8-9:13 ||
aho iti kaṣṭe āścarye vā || 1.8-9:14 ||
etaccharīraṃ tu sarvotkṛṣṭamiti tātparyārthaḥ || 1.8-9:15 ||
sā muktiḥ piṇḍapātane iti vacanāt || 1.8-9:16 ||

____________________________________________________
ṅṛht1.10


iti dhanaśarīrabhogānmatvānityān sadaiva yatanīyam |
muktau sā ca jñānāttaccābhyāsātsa ca sthire dehe || ṅṛht_1.10 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sarvasādhanaṃ śarīraṃ matvābhimataṃ diśati bho janāḥ sadā sarvasmin kāle aharniśaṃ yatanīyaṃ kiṃ kṛtvā dhanaśarīrabhogānanityānnaśvarānmatvā yatanīyamiti || 1.10:1 ||
kiṃ yathā śarīraṃ nityasthāyi bhavati śarīre nitye sarvaṃ nityamityarthaḥ || 1.10:2 ||
tasya śarīrasya nityasya jñānātsarvotkṛṣṭenānenaiva śarīraṃ nityaṃ bhavedityavabodhāttasyaivābhyāsācca muktirbhavati || 1.10:3 ||
kva sati sthire dehe sati || 1.10:4 ||
manaso dharmaiḥ śarīrāśritaiḥ ṣaṅkikāraśca dehāsthiratvametanniṣedhatvaṃ dehasthiratvaṃ mokṣaḥ || 1.10:5 ||
tadevāha rāmaṃ prati gurorvacanam |
na mokṣo nabhasaḥ pṛṣṭhe na pātāle na bhūtale |
sarvāśāsaṃkṣayaścetaḥ śamo mokṣamitīkṣate iti || 1.10:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.11


tatsthairyaṃ na samarthaṃ rasāyanaṃ kimapi mūlalohādi |
svayamasthirasvabhāvaṃ dāhyaṃ kledyaṃ ca śoṣyaṃ ca || ṅṛht_1.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tasya dehasya sthairyeṇa sthirabhāvena kṛtvā rasāyanaṃ jarāvyādhināśanaṃ prati samarthaṃ kārakataram || 1.11:1 ||
apīti niścayena || 1.11:2 ||
kiṃ sūtalohādiḥ sūtaḥ pāradaḥ lohāḥ svarṇādayo navakāḥ kṛtrimākṛtrimabhedayuktāḥ rājarītikharparīghoṣāḥ kṛtrimāḥ svarṇatāratāmranāgavaṅgalohā akṛtrimāḥ ādiśabdānmahārasā uparasāśca jñātavyāḥ || 1.11:3 ||
ke ta ime rasāvatāre yathā |
hiṅgūlatāpyavimalācalasasyakāntavaikrāntapakṣipatayaśca mahārasāḥ syuḥ |
sauvīragandhakaśilālaviraṅgadhātukāsīsakāṃkṣyuparasāḥ kathitā rasajñaiḥ iti || 1.11:4 ||
athavā sthairyeṇa samarthe tatdehaṃ prati rasāyanaṃ kiṃ sūtalohādi || 1.11:5 ||
sūte yadabhivyāptaṃ grāsamānena lohādi tattathoktaṃ sūte iti abhivyāpake || 1.11:6 ||
adhikaraṇe saptamī || 1.11:7 ||
taddehaṃ svayamasthiramasthirībhāvatvabhāvaṃ punardāhyaṃ dagdhuṃ śakyaṃ punaḥ kledyamārdrībhāvena śīrṇayituṃ śakyaṃ punaḥ śodhyaṃ śoṣayitumagnijalānilaiḥ dāhyaṃ kledyaṃ śoṣyaṃ ca śarīramityarthaḥ || 1.11:8 ||
sūtalohādinā dehamanityaṃ nityaṃ bhavetayameva yatna iti tātparyārthaḥ || 1.11:9 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.12-13


<āuflsungড়flanśen -> amaratva>
kāṣṭhauṣadhyo nāge nāgaṃ vaṅge vaṅgamapi līyate śulve |
śulvaṃ tāre tāraṃ kanake kanakaṃ ca līyate sūte || ṅṛht_1.12 ||
paramātmanīva niyataṃ layo yatra sarvasattvānām |
eko'sau rasarājaḥ śarīramajarāmaraṃ kurute || ṅṛht_1.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pūrvaśloke sūtalohādikamuktaṃ tatrādhivyāpakādhikaraṇasya kramaṃ darśayati kāṣṭhauṣadhya ityādi || 1.12-13:1 ||
kāṣṭhauṣadhyaḥ kumārikādayaḥ nāge līyante tannāgaṃ vaṅge līyate tadvaṅgamapi śulbe tāmre līyate tacchulbaṃ tāre līyate tattāraṃ rūpyaṃ kanake suvarṇe līyate tatkanakaṃ sūte pārade līyate iti || 1.12-13:2 ||
kathaṃ līyate vidhānata ekaikena yuktaṃ samudāyenāpi ca || 1.12-13:3 ||
dhātūnāmarivargatvānmahārasoparasānāmapi yogamapiśabdāśca || 1.12-13:4 ||
samudāyatvenopavarṇayati paramātmanītyādi || 1.12-13:5 ||
asau eko dhātvādyantarbhūto rasarājaḥ śarīramajarāmaraṃ jarāmaraṇavarjitaṃ kurute || 1.12-13:6 ||
rasairmahārasoparasairantarbhūto rājate śobhate vā prakāśate iti rasarājaḥ athavā raseṣu mahārasoparaseṣu anādarībhūteṣu satsu rājata iti viśeṣārthaḥ || 1.12-13:7 ||
tathā ca sūtraṃ ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyau cānādare iti athavā rasānāṃ mahārasoparasadhātūnāṃ rājā teṣu mukhyatvenopadiṣṭaḥ mukhyatvenāsya grahaṇamityupalakṣaṇam || 1.12-13:8 ||
anenaiva sarvasaṃgrahaṇaṃ jñātavyam || 1.12-13:9 ||
iveti sādṛśye || 1.12-13:10 ||
ātmani brahmaṇi niyataṃ niścitaṃ sarvasattvānāṃ sakalajīvānāṃ layo bhavati layo'ntarbhāvaḥ vā tasminsarve || 1.12-13:11 ||
sattvā līnā eva tiṣṭhantītyātmānaḥ taṭasthasvarūpataitallakṣaṇadvayamuktam || 1.12-13:12 ||
tathā rasarāje'pi darśayati kāṣṭhauṣadhīdhātumahārasoparasādīnāṃ layo jñeyaḥ taṭasthalakṣaṇena layasya krama upadiṣṭaḥ || 1.12-13:13 ||
svarūpalakṣaṇenauṣadhīdhātumahārasoparasādayaḥ pṛthaktvena sthitā api guṇairantarbhūtā eva jñātavyāḥ yataḥ sarveṣāṃ guṇāntarbhūtaḥ sūtastato'nantaguṇa ācāryairupavarṇitaḥ || 1.12-13:14 ||
layaviśeṣādubhayoḥ sāmyamiti rahasyam || 1.12-13:15 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.14


amṛtatvaṃ hi bhajante haramūrtau yogino yathā līnāḥ |
tadvatkavalitagagane rasarāje hemalohādyāḥ || ṅṛht_1.14 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sarvaṃ samīkartumāha amṛtatvamityādi || 1.14:1 ||
te svātmanā ātmanā saha yogakartṛkā yogino yathā haramūrtau mahādevaśarīre līnāḥ santaḥ amṛtatvaṃ bhajante muktatvaṃ prāpnuvanti || 1.14:2 ||
muktiḥ kaivalyanirvāṇaśreyoniḥśreyasāmṛtamityamaraḥ || 1.14:3 ||
tadvattenaiva prakāreṇa rasarāje pārade kavalitagagane grāsīkṛtābhrake sati hemalohādyā līnāḥ santa amṛtatvaṃ pīyūṣabhāvaṃ bhajante amarīkaraṇayogyā bhavanti || 1.14:4 ||
hema ca lohaśca hemalohau ādyau yeṣāṃ vā hemasaṃjñako loha ādyo yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ || 1.14:5 ||
abhrake kavalite sati cāraṇā syānnānyatheti || 1.14:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.15


sthiradeho'bhyāsavaśātprāpya jñānaṃ guṇāṣṭakopetam |
prāpnoti brahmapadaṃ na punarbhavāvāsaduḥkhena || ṅṛht_1.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

adhunā sthiradehasya phalaṃ vyanakti sthiretyādi || 1.15:1 ||
pūrvoktasyaiva rasarājasyābhyāsātsevanāddhetoḥ sthiradehaḥ pumān brahmapadaṃ prāpnoti || 1.15:2 ||
brahmapadaṃ paramānandasvarūpam || 1.15:3 ||
kiṃkṛtvā jñānaṃ prāpya || 1.15:4 ||
kīdṛśaṃ jñānaṃ guṇāṣṭakopetamaṇimādyaṣṭasiddhyupetam || 1.15:5 ||
kathaṃ brahmapadaṃ prāpnoti || 1.15:6 ||
yathā punaraprathamaṃ bhavāvāsaduḥkhe saṃsāranivāsanatāpatrayātmakakaṣṭe na patatītyarthaḥ || 1.15:7 ||
jñānaṃ prāpya brahmapadaṃ prāpnoti || 1.15:8 ||
kathamṛte jñānānna muktiriti || 1.15:9 ||
anyacca kiṃ mokṣatarorbījaṃ samyagjñānaṃ kriyāsahitamiti praśnottararatnamālāyām || 1.15:10 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.16


ekāṃśena jaganti ca viṣṭabhyāvasthitaṃ paraṃ jyotiḥ |
pādaistribhistadamṛtaṃ sulabhaṃ na viraktimātreṇa || ṅṛht_1.16 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

jñānagamyaṃ brahmopavarṇayatyekāṃśenetyādi || 1.16:1 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ brahma paraṃ jyotiḥ prakāśasvarūpaṃ tatparaṃ jyotirjaganti saṃsārāṇi svargamṛtyupātālādīni viṣṭabhya vyāpya sthitaṃ kena ekāṃśena anekabrahmāṇḍanāyakatvātekaikasmin brahmāṇḍe bahūni saṃsārāṇi vartante ata ekāṃśenetyuktaṃ punastatparaṃ jyotīrūpamamṛtaṃ tribhiḥ pādairabhyāsasthiradehajñānasaṃjñakaiḥ sulabhaṃ sukhena labhyamityarthaḥ || 1.16:2 ||
prathamamabhyāso'mṛtasulabhatve || 1.16:3 ||
heturekaḥ sthiradehaśca dvitīyo heturjñānaṃ tṛtīyaheturmokṣe yathāvākyaṃ na viraktimātram || 1.16:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.17


na hi dehena kathaṃcidvyādhijarāmaraṇaduḥkhavidhureṇa |
kṣaṇabhaṅgureṇa sūkṣmaṃ tadbrahmopāsituṃ śakyam || ṅṛht_1.17 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pūrvāparābhyāmabhyāsajñānābhyāṃ sthiradeho heturgarīyāniti sūcayannāha netyādi || 1.17:1 ||
ubhayoḥ sādhakatvāttādṛśadehavyatiriktaṃ kathaṃcidapi kiṃcinna sidhyatītyarthaḥ || 1.17:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭena vyādhijarāmaraṇaduḥkhavidhureṇa vyādhirāmayaḥ jarā pālityaṃ maraṇaṃ prāṇatyāgaḥ duḥkhaṃ mohaśokādikametairvidhuraṃ tāḍitaṃ vyadha tāḍane ityasya ghāto rūpaṃ vidhuramurapratyayāntam || 1.17:3 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭena kṣaṇabhaṅgureṇa kṣaṇavināśinā dehena tadbrahma cidghanānandasvarūpamupāsituṃ sevituṃ kathaṃ kena prakāreṇa śakyaṃ kuto yataḥ sūkṣmamindriyāgrāhyatvāt || 1.17:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.18


nāmāpi yogasiddheḥ ko gṛhṇīyādvinā śarīreṇa |
yadyogagamyamamalaṃ manaso'pi na gocaraṃ tattvam || ṅṛht_1.18 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sarvopāyena śarīraṃ sthiraṃ kāryamityāha nāmetyādi || 1.18:1 ||
dehasiddheḥ śarīravibhūteḥ nāmāpyabhidhānamapi ko gṛhṇīyānna ko'pītyarthaḥ || 1.18:2 ||
kena vinā śarīreṇa śarīramantareṇa siddhirastu paraṃ tannāma kenāpi na gṛhyate śarīranāmagrahaṇamiti tātparyārthaḥ || 1.18:3 ||
anaśvaraṃ śarīraṃ bhavatu cettadamalaṃ nirañjanaṃ tattvaṃ brahmāvaśyaṃ prāpyate || 1.18:4 ||
tattattvaṃ manaso'pi na gocaraṃ cittenāpi na gamyamityarthaḥ || 1.18:5 ||
tarhi kena gamyamubhayormelanamekīkaraṇaṃ yogastenaiva prakṛtipuruṣayorekīkaraṇenetyarthaḥ || 1.18:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.19


yajñāddānāttapaso vedādhyayanāddamātsadācārāt |
atyantaṃ śreyaḥ kila yogavaśādātmasaṃvittiḥ || ṅṛht_1.19 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

adhunā yogasya sarvakarmabhya utkṛṣṭatvaṃ darśayati yajñādityādi || 1.19:1 ||
atyantaṃ śreya iti adhikatarakalyāṇaṃ sarvopadravanivāraṇātmakaṃ bhavedityadhyāhāraḥ || 1.19:2 ||
kutaḥ yajñādaśvamedhādeḥ na kevalaṃ yajñātpunardānātdhanasyārpaṇātpātreṣu punastapasaḥ kṛcchrātikṛcchracāndrāyapasaṃcāgnitapanādeḥ punarvedādhyayanātvedānāmṛgyajuḥsāmātharvaṇāmadhyayanaṃ pāṭhākramastataḥ punardamātvedāntānusāreṇa damastāvatbāhyendriyāṇāṃ tadvyatiriktaviṣayebhyo manasā nirvartanaṃ tadvyatiriktaṃ śravaṇādivyatiriktaṃ tataḥ punaḥ sadācārātbrāhmamuhūrtamārabhya prātaḥsaṃgavamadhyāhnāparāhṇasāyāhnādiṣu śayanaparyantaṃ vedabodhito vidhiḥ sadācārastata iti samudāyaḥ śreyaskaro nātmasaṃvittikaraḥ punarātmasaṃvittiḥ brahmavedanaṃ yogavaśādeva syātyogaḥ pūrvamuktaḥ || 1.19:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.20


galitānalpavikalpasarvārthavivarjitaścidānandaḥ |
sphurito'pyasphuritatanoḥ karoti kiṃ jantuvargasya || ṅṛht_1.20 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

yasya jīvasya yogavaśātsaṃvittirjātā sa kīdṛśa ityāha galitetyādi || 1.20:1 ||
galitānalpavikalpa iti galito dūrībhūto'nalpo bahutaro vikalpo mithyājñānaṃ yasya īdṛksyāt || 1.20:2 ||
punaḥ kīdṛksarvārthavivarjitaḥ sarve ca te arthāśca tairvivarjitāḥ samyagrahito bhavati kāryāṇāṃ smaraṇakaraṇayorabhāva ityarthaḥ || 1.20:3 ||
punaḥ kathaṃbhūtaḥ cidānandaścidā prakāśena ānandaḥ sukhasampattiryasya sa tathoktaḥ || 1.20:4 ||
prakāśahetunā ānandatā bhavetjaḍahetunā tadviparyayaḥ || 1.20:5 ||
īdṛksaḥ sphurito'pi prakāśamāno'pi asphuritatanorjantuvargasya aprakāśaśarīrasya jīvasamūhasya kiṃ karoti pṛcchāṃ karoti || 1.20:6 ||
kimiti pṛcchājugupsayoḥ iti prasādaḥ || 1.20:7 ||
pṛcchati ca hasati ca roditi pramattavanmānavo'pi tallīna iti || 1.20:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.21


bhrūyugamadhyagataṃ yacchikhividyunnirmalaṃ jagadbhāsi |
keṣāṃcitpuṇyakṛtāmunmīlati cinmayaṃ jyotiḥ || ṅṛht_1.21 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

ātmasaṃvitterviralatvaṃ sūcayannāha bhrūyugetyādi || 1.21:1 ||
yatbhrūyugamadhyagataṃ bhrūdvayāntargataṃ satprakāśate tatra dṛṣṭiṃ nidhāya yoginaḥ paśyanti khecaryā mudrayā haṭhapradīpikāyāṃ padyam |
kapālakuhare jihvā praviṣṭā viparītagā |
bhruvorantargatā dṛṣṭirmudrā bhavati khecarī || 1.21:2 ||
iti || 1.21:3 ||
punaḥ śikhividyutsu vahnisaudāminīṣu nirmalaṃ satyatprakāśate punaryatjagadbhāsi jagatsaṃsāraṃ prakāśate tatcinmayaṃ prakāśapracuraṃ jyotiḥ keṣāṃcitpuṇyakṛtāṃ suvihitakarmakartḥṇāmunmīlati prādurbhavati na tu sarveṣāṃ yato nirmalaṃ prakāśaṃ dhyātvā vipulapuṇyena nirmalatvāya jāyate ataḥ prakāśo yuktaḥ || 1.21:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.22
paramānandaikamayaṃ paramaṃ jyotiḥsvabhāvamavikalpam |
vigalitasarvakleśaṃ jñeyaṃ śāntaṃ svayaṃsaṃvedyam || ṅṛht_1.22 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pūrvavarṇitaṃ cinmayaṃ viśeṣayannāha paramānandaikamayamityādi || 1.22:1 ||
tadbrahma īdṛśaṃ jñeyaṃ paramānandaikamayamiti parama utkṛṣṭo'sāvānandaḥ paramānandaḥ sa eva eko'dvitīyastātprācuryaṃ yasmiṃstathoktaṃ punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ paramaṃ jyotiḥsvabhāvaṃ paramaṃ yajjyotiḥ tatsvabhāvaḥ svarūpaṃ yasya tadaśarīratvātsvaprakāśitvācca punaḥ avikalpaṃ mithyājñānaśūnyaṃ punarvigalitasarvakleśaṃ vigalitā viśeṣeṇa dūrīkṛtāḥ sarvakleśāḥ duḥkhāni yasmāttatsvarūpatvātpunaḥ śāntaṃ śamamayaṃ punaḥ svayaṃvedyamanyena veditumaśakyamātmanaiva vedyaṃ tasmānnāparo'stīti bhāvāt || 1.22:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.23


tasminnādhāya manaḥ sphuradakhilaṃ jagatpaśyan |
utsannakarmabandho brahmatvamihaiva cāpnoti || ṅṛht_1.23 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

jñeyopadeśamāha tasminnityādi || 1.23:1 ||
tasminnādhāyeti pūrvanirūpite tasminnevātmani mana ādhāya saṃsthāpya pumānutsannakarmabandho bhavettyaktakarmapāśaḥ syāt || 1.23:2 ||
sa ihaiva janmani brahmatvaṃ prāpnotīti viśeṣaḥ brahmavidbrahmaiva bhavati iti śruteḥ || 1.23:3 ||
sa pumānmana ādadhāti || 1.23:4 ||
kiṃ kurvan sanakhilaṃ jagatsarvasaṃsāraṃ cinmayaṃ prakāśasvarūpaṃ cidvikāraṃ paśyanavalokamāno manaścakṣuṣā kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ jagatsphuratadhyāropāpadeśena dedīpyamānam || 1.23:5 ||
tathā ca bhagavadvacanam |
karmaṇyakarma yaḥ paśyedakarmaṇi ca karma yaḥ |
sa buddhimānmanuṣyeṣu sa yuktaḥ kṛtsnakarmakṛt || 1.23:6 ||
iti || 1.23:7 ||
he pārtha sa pumānmanuṣyeṣu buddhimān || 1.23:8 ||
anena sāmānyatvamuktam || 1.23:9 ||
viśeṣaśca yathā rajjau sarpabhramo yathā śuktau rajatajñānaṃ yathā gandharvanagaraṃ yathā marusthale vāri tathaiva saṃsāro nāsītnāsti na bhaviṣyatīti advaitavādānmithyaiva || 1.23:10 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.24


astaṃ hi yānti viṣayāḥ prāṇāntaḥkaraṇasaṃyogāt |
sphuraṇaṃ nendriyatamasāṃ nātaḥ sphurataśca duḥkhasukhe || ṅṛht_1.24 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

utsannakarmabandhasya viṣayā astaṃ yānti || 1.24:1 ||
prāṇāntaḥkaraṇasaṃyogātna indriyāṇāṃ sphuraṇaṃ bhavet || 1.24:2 ||
tathā ca nyāyaśāstre ātmā manasā saṃyujyate mana indriyeṇa indriyamarthenetīndriyāṇāṃ vastuprāpyaprakāśakāritvaniyamāditi || 1.24:3 ||
ato hetośca duḥkhasukhe na sphurataḥ || 1.24:4 ||
ātmanaḥ prakāśātprāṇāntaḥkaraṇānāṃ prakāśaḥ prāṇāntaḥkaraṇāni tameva prakāśaṃ prāpyendriyāṇi prakāśayanti || 1.24:5 ||
ata ubhayoḥ paraprakāśaḥ || 1.24:6 ||
tadvadgahanatama agrāhyamandhakāraṃ cidbhinnaṃ prakāśena prakāśitaṃ syāditi || 1.24:7 ||
abhāvapadārthatvādindriyatamasorjaḍatvātsāmyam || 1.24:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.25


rāgadveṣavimuktāḥ satyācārā narā mṛṣārahitāḥ |
sarvatra nirviśeṣā bhavanti cidbrahmasaṃsparśāt || ṅṛht_1.25 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

adhunā antaḥkaraṇānāṃ pravṛttiṃ darśayati rāgetyādi cidbrahmasaṃsparśāditi cidbrahmaṇi prakāśasvarūpe ātmani yaḥ sparśaḥ tanniṣṭhā tato hetoḥ puruṣā rāgadveṣaviyuktāḥ snehaśatrutvavirahitāḥ syuḥ || 1.25:1 ||
punaḥ satyaḥ ācāraḥ pravṛttidharmo yeṣāṃ te punarmṛṣārahitāḥ atyācārādyasatyavarjitā ityarthaḥ punaḥ sarvatra nirviśeṣāḥ sarvasminmānāpamānayoḥ samāḥ tathā ca bhagavadvacanam |
samaḥ śatrau ca mitre ca tathā mānāpamānayoḥ || 1.25:2 ||
ityādi || 1.25:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.26


tiṣṭhantyaṇimādiyutā vilasaddehā mudā sadānandāḥ |
ye brahmabhāvamamṛtaṃ samprāptāścaiva kṛtakṛtyāḥ || ṅṛht_1.26 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

ātmani sparśatvamuktiprāptiṃ darśayannāha tiṣṭhantītyādi || 1.26:1 ||
ye brahmabhāvamamṛtaṃ muktisārūpyatvaṃ prāptāste kṛtakṛtyāḥ kṛtasarvakāryāḥ pūrṇatāṃ prāptā ityarthaḥ punaste aṇimādiyutā aṇimādibhiryutā iha jagati tiṣṭhantīti aṇimādayo yathā |
aṇimā mahimā cātha laghimā garimā tathā |
prāptiḥ prākāmyamīśitvaṃ vaśitvaṃ cāṣṭabhūtayaḥ || 1.26:2 ||
iti || 1.26:3 ||
punarvilasaddehāḥ tejaḥprāyaśarīrāḥ punaḥ sadānandāḥ kena mudā harṣeṇa sadā sarvasminkāle ānando yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ paramānande magnatvāt || 1.26:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.27


āyatanaṃ vidyānāṃ mūlaṃ dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇām |
śreyaḥ paraṃ kimanyatśarīramajarāmaraṃ vihāyaikam || ṅṛht_1.27 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

śarīramūlaṃ sarve jñātavyamityāhāyatanamityādi || 1.27:1 ||
ekamajarāmaraṃ jarāmaraṇavarjitaṃ śarīraṃ vihāya tyaktvā anyatparamutkṛṣṭaṃ śreyaḥ kalyāṇasvarūpaṃ kiṃ na kimapītyarthaḥ || 1.27:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ śarīramāyatanaṃ vidyānāṃ vyākaraṇādicaturdaśasaṃkhyākāṅgānāṃ nivāsasthānaṃ punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ mūlaṃ dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ caturṇāṃ padārthānāṃ mūlaṃ hetuḥ dharmādayaścatvāraḥ pratītā eva || 1.27:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.28


pramāṇato'pi pratyakṣādyo na jānāti sūtakam |
adṛṣṭavigrahaṃ devaṃ kathaṃ jñāsyati cinmayam || ṅṛht_1.28 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

ātmano'vekṣayā sūte sugamatvaṃ sūcayannāha pramāṇata ityādi || 1.28:1 ||
yaḥ puruṣaḥ sūtakaṃ rasendraṃ na jānāti kutaḥ pramāṇataḥ pramākaraṇaṃ pramāṇaṃ pramitisādhanaṃ vā tataḥ kimbhūtātpramāṇataḥ pratyakṣāccakṣurindriyagrāhyarūpātsa pumān cinmayamatisūkṣmamātmānaṃ kathaṃ jñāsyati na kathamapītyarthaḥ indriyāgocaratvāt || 1.28:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭamanādivigrahamādiśca vigrahaṃ ca ādivigrahe te na vidyete yatra saḥ tamutpattiśarīrayorabhāvātsthūlajñānābhāva iti tātparyārthaḥ || 1.28:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.29-30


yajjarayā jarjaritaṃ kāsaśvāsādiduḥkhavaśamāptam |
yogyaṃ tanna samādhau pratihatabuddhīndriyaprasaram || ṅṛht_1.29 ||
bālaḥ ṣoḍaśavarṣo viṣayarasāsvādalampaṭaḥ parataḥ |
jātaviveko vṛddho martyaḥ kathamāpnuyānmuktim || ṅṛht_1.30 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

śarīrasya vayovibhāgenāsthiratvaṃ darśayannāha bāla ityādi || 1.29-30:1 ||
ṣoḍaśavarṣa iti ṣoḍaśa varṣāṇi yasya vayasi sa ṣoḍaśābdo bāla ityarthaḥ || 1.29-30:2 ||
granthāntare bālatve'pi vayobhedā vartante || 1.29-30:3 ||
yathā padyam |
ā pañcamācca kaumāraḥ paugaṇḍo navahāyanaḥ |
ā ṣoḍaśācca kiśoro yauvanaṃ ca tataḥ param || 1.29-30:4 ||
iti || 1.29-30:5 ||
bālo vastujñāne aśaktaḥ || 1.29-30:6 ||
punaḥ ṣoḍaśavarṣebhyaḥ paraḥ viṣayarasāsvādalampaṭo bhavati viṣayāḥ śabdasparśarūparasagandhā rasāḥ śṛṅgārahāsyakaruṇaraudravīrabhayānakabībhatsādbhutaśāntāḥ || 1.29-30:7 ||
kecitśāntaṃ rasaṃ na bruvanti nirvikāratvāteteṣāmāsvādaḥ svādastatra lampaṭo vyāsaktaḥ atha vā viṣayānantaraṃ snehastatreti || 1.29-30:8 ||
ataḥ parato jātaviveko bhavati utpannavicāro bhavati || 1.29-30:9 ||
tadā vṛddho'kṣamaḥ paraṃ manuṣyaḥ muktiṃ kaivalyaṃ kathamāpnuyātna kathamapītyartho vayasyupaplavabhāvāt || 1.29-30:10 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.31


asminneva śarīre yeṣāṃ paramātmano na saṃvedaḥ |
dehatyāgādūrdhvaṃ teṣāṃ tadbrahma dūrataram || ṅṛht_1.31 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pūrvapadyābhiprāyaṃ vicārya muktiprāptau praśaṅkitaḥ prāhāsminnityādi || 1.31:1 ||
asmin śarīre vartamāne kṣetrarūpe yeṣāṃ puṃsāmātmasaṃvedo na jātaḥ brahmajñānaṃ na jātaṃ teṣāṃ puṃsāmeva dehatyāgādūrdhvaṃ śarīrotsargataḥ paścāttadbrahma dūrataraṃ dūrāddūrataramityarthaḥ || 1.31:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.32


brahmādayo yajante yasmin divyāṃ tanuṃ samāśritya |
jīvanmuktāścānye kalpāntasthāyino munayaḥ || ṅṛht_1.32 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

adhunā pūrvamataṃ draḍhayati brahmādaya ityādi || 1.32:1 ||
yasmin brahmādayo viṣṇurudrendrādayo brahmavido yajante saṃgatiṃ kurvanti samāpnuvantītyarthaḥ yaja devapūjāsaṃgatikaraṇadāneṣu atra saṃgatikaraṇamartho darśitaḥ || 1.32:2 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā prāpnuvanti divyāṃ tanuṃ paramāṃ samāśritya samprāpya tebhyo brahmādibhyo'pyanye apare munayo nāradādayo jīvanmuktā yajante saṃgatiṃ kurvanti || 1.32:3 ||
te'pi kiṃviśiṣṭāḥ kalpāntasthāyinaḥ pralayānte'pi tiṣṭhantīti bhāvaḥ || 1.32:4 ||
brahmādayastiṣṭhanta eva || 1.32:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.33


tasmājjīvanmuktiṃ samīhamānena yoginā prathamam |
divyā tanurvidheyā haragaurīsṛṣṭisaṃyogāt || ṅṛht_1.33 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

iti bahudhā vicāryeṣṭavastustutimāha tasmādityādi || 1.33:1 ||
yato brahmādayo jīvanmuktāścānye divyāṃ tanuṃ vidhāya muktiṃ prāptāstasmāddhetoryoginā yogayuktena prathamaṃ divyā tanurvidheyā dṛḍhaśarīraṃ kāryamityarthaḥ || 1.33:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭena yoginā jīvanmuktiṃ samīhamānena yoginā divyā tanurvidheyā || 1.33:3 ||
kutaḥ haragaurīsṛṣṭisaṃyogāt || 1.33:4 ||
umeśvarasṛṣṭo rasendrastasya sevanādityarthaḥ || 1.33:5 ||
dṛḍhaśarīreṇa vāñchitaṃ sādhyate na tvanyathā || 1.33:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht1.34


tasyāpi sādhanavidhau sudhiyā pratikarmanirmalāḥ prathamam |
aṣṭādaśasaṃskārā vijñātavyāḥ prayatnena || ṅṛht_1.34 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

divyatanorhetutvādrasendrasya sādhanavidhau sādhanopadeśe sudhiyā pūjyamatinā puṃsā prathamamaṣṭādaśasaṃskārāḥ prayatnena jñātavyāḥ || 1.34:1 ||
saṃskriyanta iti saṃskārāḥ || 1.34:2 ||
puṃsyapi garbhādhānādayaḥ ṣoḍaśa saṃskārā vartante ata eva saṃskārairubhayoḥ sāmyaṃ doṣābhāvatvaṃ guṇavattvaṃ ca syāt || 1.34:3 ||
ato brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrāḥ syuḥ || 1.34:4 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭāḥ saṃskārāḥ pratikarmanirmalāḥ karma karma prati nirdoṣāḥ || 1.34:5 ||



____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.1-2


<18 saṃskāras>

svedanamardanamūrchotthāpanapātananirodhaniyamāśca |
dīpanagaganagrāsapramāṇamatha cāraṇavidhānaṃ ca || ṅṛht_2.1 ||
garbhadrutibāhyadrutijāraṇarasarāgasāraṇaṃ caiva |
krāmaṇavedhau bhakṣaṇamaṣṭādaśadheti rasakarma || ṅṛht_2.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

yasyopajīvyate kīrtiḥ saṅgameva sudhābhujām |
sa śrīmān kārayāmāsa vṛttiṃ mugdhāvabodhinīm || 2.1-2:1 ||
āryādvayena rasāṣṭādaśasaṃskāroddeśaḥ kṛtaḥ || 2.1-2:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.3


<1. svedana>
āsurīpaṭukaṭukatrayacitrārdrakamūlakaiḥ kalāṃśaistu |
sūtasya kāñjikena tridinaṃ mṛduvahninā svedaḥ || ṅṛht_2.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

adhunā saṃskārāṇāṃ sādhane lakṣaṇamāha || 2.3:1 ||
tatra prathamoddiṣṭasya svedanasaṃskārasya sādhanaṃ spaṣṭayannāha āsurītyādi || 2.3:2 ||
sūtasya pāradasya tridinaṃ dinatrayaparimāṇaṃ yathā syāttathā mṛduvahninā svalpāgninā svedaḥ svedanaṃ kāryam || 2.3:3 ||
kena kāñjikena sauvīreṇa || 2.3:4 ||
kaiḥ saha āsurīpaṭukaṭukatrayacitrārdrakamūlakaiḥ saha || 2.3:5 ||
āsurī rājikā paṭu saindhavaṃ lavaṇaviśeṣaḥ kecitpaṭuśabdena kṣāramapi vyācakṣate kaṭukatrayaṃ śuṇṭhīmaricapippalyaḥ citrakaṃ pratītamārdrakaṃ kandaviśeṣo nāgarahetuḥ mūlakaṃ kandaviśeṣaḥ prasiddhaḥ || 2.3:6 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭairetaiḥ kalāṃśaiḥ ṣoḍaśāṃśaiḥ ṣoḍaśāṃśaḥ pratyekaṃ saṃyujyate || 2.3:7 ||
sarvasaṃmatamidaṃ vyākhyānām || 2.3:8 ||
atra viśeṣaḥ kāñjike sarvadhānyāmlasaṃdhānaṃ tuṣavarjye tu kārayet || 2.3:9 ||
uragā triphalā krāntā laghuparṇī śatāvarī || 2.3:10 ||
tena yuktaṃ rasasvinnaṃ tridinaṃ mṛduvahninā || 2.3:11 ||
dolāyantreṇa tīvreṇa mardayitvā punaḥ punaḥ |
iti rasendramaṅgalāt || 2.3:12 ||
kṣārāmlairoṣadhairvāpi dolāyantre sthitasya hi |
pācanaṃ svedanākhyaṃ syānmalaśaithilyakārakam |
iti paribhāṣā || 2.3:13 ||
dravadravyeṇa bhāṇḍasya pūritārdhodarasya ca |
mukhasyobhayato dvāradvayaṃ kṛtvā prayatnataḥ || 2.3:14 ||
tasyopari kṣipeddaṇḍaṃ tanmadhye rasapoṭalīm |
baddhvā tu svedayedetaddolāyantramiti smṛtam |
iti dolāyantralakṣaṇam || 2.3:15 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.4


<2. mardana>
guḍadagdhorṇālavaṇairmandiradhūmeṣṭakāsurīsahitaiḥ |
rasaṣoḍaśāṃśamānaiḥ sakāñjikairmardanaṃ tridinam || ṅṛht_2.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

dvitīyoddiṣṭasya mardanasya sādhanaṃ spaṣṭayannāha guḍetyādi || 2.4:1 ||
etairauṣadhairdinatrayaṃ parimāṇaṃ rasasya mardanaṃ kāryam || 2.4:2 ||
etaiḥ kaiḥ guḍadagdhorṇālavaṇaiḥ guḍa ikṣuvikāraḥ prasiddhaḥ dagdhorṇā dagdhā cāsau ūrṇā ceti samāsaḥ ūrṇā pratītā meṣaromanicayamityarthaḥ lavaṇaṃ saindhavamekaṃ guḍadagdhorṇārajanī iti vā pāṭhaḥ tatra haridrā grāhyā na saindhavam || 2.4:3 ||
rasaṣoḍaśāṃśamānaiḥ rasātṣoḍaśāṃśapramāṇaiḥ || 2.4:4 ||
aṃśa ityasya pratyekaṃ sambandhaḥ || 2.4:5 ||
viśeṣaścātra vastraiścaturguṇairbaddhaḥ sūtaḥ sthāpyaḥ śubhe'hani || 2.4:6 ||
lohārkāśmajakhalve tu tapteṣveva tu mardayet || 2.4:7 ||
uddiṣṭairoṣadhaiḥ sārdhaṃ sarvāmlaiḥ kāñjikairapi |
peṣaṇaṃ mardanākhyaṃ syāttadbahirmalanāśanamiti paribhāṣā || 2.4:8 ||
atra yantraṃ tu khalvākhyaṃ jñeyam || 2.4:9 ||
tallakṣaṇaṃ tu khalvayogyā śilā nīlā śyāmā snigdhā dṛḍhā guruḥ || 2.4:10 ||
ṣoḍaśāṅgulikotsedhā navāṅgulikavistarā iti || 2.4:11 ||
khalvayogyaśilālakṣaṇaṃ prasaṅgāduktam || 2.4:12 ||
utsedhena navāṅgulaḥ khalu kalātulyāṅgulāyāmavān vistāreṇa daśāṅgulo'tha munibhirnimnastathaivāṅgulaiḥ || 2.4:13 ||
pālyāṃ hyaṅgulavistaraśca masṛṇo'tīvārdhacandropamo gharṣo dvādaśakāṅgulaśca tadayaṃ khalvo mato mardane iti || 2.4:14 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.5-6


<3. mūrchana>
malaśikhiviṣābhidhānā rasasya naisargikāstrayo doṣāḥ |
mūrchāṃ malena kurute śikhinā dāhaṃ viṣeṇa mṛtyuṃ ca || ṅṛht_2.5 ||
gṛhakanyā harati malaṃ triphalāgniṃ citrakaśca viṣam |
tasmādebhirmiśrairvārān saṃmūrchayetsapta || ṅṛht_2.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rasasya pāradasya doṣāstrayo vartante || 2.5-6:1 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭāḥ malaśikhiviṣābhidhānāḥ malaśca śikhī ca viṣaṃ ca tānyevābhidhānaṃ nāma yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ || 2.5-6:2 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭāḥ naisargikāḥ nisarga utpattistatsambandhinaḥ sahajā ityarthaḥ || 2.5-6:3 ||
viśeṣaścātra malādyāḥ pañcadoṣāḥ syurbhūjādyāḥ saptakañcukāḥ || 2.5-6:4 ||
kuṣṭānaṣṭau rasāntaḥsthā rase te'nantadoṣadāḥ iti rasasaṃketakalikāyām || 2.5-6:5 ||
yadyapi rasendramaṅgale pañca malādayo naisargikā doṣāḥ kathitāstathāpyatra traya eva anye dve gurutvacapalatve naisargikadoṣarūpe kuto na staḥ tribhiḥ svedanamardanamūrchanātmakaiḥ saṃskārairanivṛtteḥ || 2.5-6:6 ||
naisargikagrahaṇādvaikārikāṇāmapi grahaṇaṃ syāt || 2.5-6:7 ||
atra naisargikā uktā vaikārikāḥ kuto noktāḥ vaikārikāṇāṃ bhāvābhāvāt || 2.5-6:8 ||
raso doṣatrayāvṛtaḥ prāśyamānaḥ kiṃ karoti malena maladoṣeṇa mūrchāmindriyamohaṃ kurute śikhinā vahninā dāhaṃ viṣeṇa mṛtyuṃ maraṇaṃ ceti samuccaye || 2.5-6:9 ||
eṣāmapaharaṇaṃ kāryamiti bhāvaḥ || 2.5-6:10 ||
svarūpasya vināśena piṣṭitvāpādanaṃ hi yat |
vidvadbhirjitasūto'sau naṣṭapiṣṭaḥ sa ucyate || 2.5-6:11 ||
mardanoddiṣṭabhaiṣajyairnaṣṭapiṣṭatvakārakam |
tanmūrchanamiti proktaṃ doṣatrayavināśanamiti || 2.5-6:12 ||
yantramatra khalvameva pūrvoktaṃ yat || 2.5-6:13 ||
tridoṣāpaharaṇaṃ mūrchanaṃ cāha gṛhakanyetyādi || 2.5-6:14 ||
gṛhakanyā gṛhakumārikā malaṃ prathamaṃ doṣaṃ harati || 2.5-6:15 ||
punastriphalā trayāṇāṃ phalānāṃ samāhāraḥ triphalā agniṃ dvitīyaṃ doṣaṃ harati samāhāro yathā ekā harītakī yojyā dvau ca yojyau vibhītakau |
catvāryāmalakānyeva triphaleyaṃ prakīrtitā |
iti || 2.5-6:16 ||
punaścitrako'gniḥ viṣaṃ tṛtīyaṃ doṣaṃ harati dūrīkarotītyarthaḥ || 2.5-6:17 ||
tasmāddhetorebhistribhirgṛhakanyātriphalācitrakairmiśritairekīkṛtai rasaṃ sapta vārānmūrchayetvidhivanmūrchanaṃ kuryāt || 2.5-6:18 ||
viśeṣaścātra |
mūrchanaṃ rasarājasya kartavyaṃ vādibhiḥ sadā |
viṣaistriphalayā pūrvaṃ bṛhatyopaviṣastathā || 2.5-6:19 ||
karkoṭī kṣārakandābhyāṃ citreṇa gṛhakanyayā |
ekenāpyatha saṃmardyo yāmamekaṃ tu pāradaḥ || 2.5-6:20 ||
kiṃnarayantramādāya hyoṣadhyā lepayettalam |
navatārayutaṃ sūtaṃ yantramadhyagataṃ nyaset || 2.5-6:21 ||
dadyādrasopari śarāvaṃ saṃdhilepaṃ dṛḍhaṃ mṛdā |
lavaṇena ca sampūrya dvāraṃ saṃrudhya yatnataḥ || 2.5-6:22 ||
cuhlikopari saṃsthāpya dīptāgniṃ jvālayetsudhīḥ |
yāmaikena taduttārya kartavyaḥ śītalo rasaḥ || 2.5-6:23 ||
yantrāduddhṛtya yatnena sūtamutthāpya mūrchitam |
amūrchitastadā deyaḥ kalāṃśaṃ mūrchite rasaḥ || 2.5-6:24 ||
sindhūtthaṭaṅkaṇābhyāṃ ca mardayenmadhusaṃyutaṃ dolāyantre tataḥ svedyaḥ kṣārāmlalavaṇaiḥ saha |
utthāpya mūrchayetpaścātvāraṃvāraṃ raseśvaram |
punarutthāpitaṃ kuryādekaviṃśativārakam || 2.5-6:25 ||
iti rasasāre || 2.5-6:26 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.7


<saṃskāra:: utthāpana>
amunā vimardanena hi suviśuddho nāgavaṅgaparimuktaḥ |
sūtaḥ pātanayantre samutthitaḥ kāñjikakvāthāt || ṅṛht_2.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sūto nāgavaṅgaparimukto bhavati nāgavaṅgābhyāṃ doṣābhyāṃ virahitaḥ pārado bhavatītyarthaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ san samutthitaḥ san || 2.7:1 ||
pātanayantre sthālīdvayasampuṭe samyagvidhānenotthitaḥ san || 2.7:2 ||
kasmātkāñjikaṃ sauvīraṃ pūrvavarṇitaṃ tatkvāthasaṃyogādityarthaḥ || 2.7:3 ||
hi niścitam || 2.7:4 ||
amunā virecanena uktaśodhanena sūtaḥ suviśuddho bhavetviśeṣaśuddho bhavedityutthāpanam || 2.7:5 ||
anena vidhinā hiṅgulasthasya sūtasyāpi utthāpanaṃ bhavati || 2.7:6 ||
svedanādikayogena svarūpāpādanaṃ punaḥ || 2.7:7 ||
tadutthāpanamityuktaṃ mūrchāvyāpattināśanamiti || 2.7:8 ||
utūrdhvaṃ sthāpanamutthāpanam || 2.7:9 ||
atra yantraṃ tu |
aṣṭāṅgulaparīṇāhamānāhena daśāṅgulam |
caturaṅgulakotsedhaṃ toyādhāro'ṅgulādadhaḥ || 2.7:10 ||
adhobhāṇḍe mukhaṃ ca tasya bhāṇḍasyoparivartinaḥ |
ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistīrṇapṛṣṭhasyāsye praveśayet || 2.7:11 ||
pārśvayormahiṣīkṣīracūrṇamaṇḍūraphāṇitaiḥ || 2.7:12 ||
vilipya śoṣayetsaṃdhiṃ jalādhāre jalaṃ kṣipet |
cullyāmāropayedetatpātanāyantramīritam || 2.7:13 ||
iti || 2.7:14 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.8


<saṃskāra:: pātana:: ūrdhvapātana>
kṛtvā tu śulbapiṣṭiṃ nipātyate nāgavaṅgaśaṅkātaḥ |
tasmindoṣānmuktvā nipatati śuddhastathā sūtaḥ || ṅṛht_2.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pañcamoddiṣṭaṃ pātanasaṃskāraṃ spaṣṭayannāha kṛtvetyādi || 2.8:1 ||
tu punaḥ utthitaṃ sūtaṃ śulbapiṣṭiṃ kṛtvā śulbena tāmreṇa saha tayormelanaṃ yathā syāttathā peṣaṇaṃ vidhāya tasmin pātanayantre nipātyate karmavideti śeṣaḥ || 2.8:2 ||
kutaḥ nāgavaṅgaśaṅkātaḥ nāgavaṅgadoṣaglānitaḥ || 2.8:3 ||
tathā uktavidhānena nipatati sati pātanakarmaṇi kṛte sati śuddhaḥ sūto bhavet || 2.8:4 ||
vāramityanukte granthāntare trisaptaikaviṃśativāraṃ pātanakarmaṇi kṛte sati samyaknāgavaṅgaśaṅkā naśyatīti bhāvaḥ || 2.8:5 ||
viśeṣaścātra pātanayantre |
dvau bhāgau śuddhasūtasya śulbabhāgaikasaṃyutau |
viṃśāṃśaṃ lavaṇaṃ dattvā piṣṭīkuryācca sundaram || 2.8:6 ||
aṣṭāṅgulavistīrṇaṃ dairghyeṇa daśāṅgulaṃ tvadhobhāṇḍam |
kaṇṭhādadhaḥ samantāccaturaṅgulīkṛtajalādhāram || 2.8:7 ||
antaḥ praviṣṭatalabhāṇḍavadanaṃ jalamagnanijamukhaprāntam |
upariṣṭācciṇṭighaṭī deyodaraṣoḍaśāṅgulaviśālā || 2.8:8 ||
tasminnadhordhvabhāṇḍe nipātitaḥ sakaladoṣanirmuktaḥ |
sutarāṃ bhavati rasendro jīrṇagrāso'pi pātyo'sau || 2.8:9 ||
adha ūrdhvaṃ tathā tiryakpātastrividha ucyate |
yatra tiṣṭhati sūtendro vahnistatrānyathā jalam || 2.8:10 ||
uktauṣadhairmarditapāradasya yantrasthitasyordhvamadhaśca tiryak |
niryāpanaṃ pātanasaṃjñamuktaṃ vaṅgāhisamparkajakañcukaghnam || 2.8:11 ||
iti || 2.8:12 ||
ūrdhvapāte rasasyordhvagamanaṃ tatrādhaḥpātre vahniḥ jalamūrdhvapātre adhaḥpāte tu rasasyādhastādgamanaṃ bhavati yantraṃ tadeva paraṃ tu agnijalayorvyatyāsaḥ jalamatrādhaḥpātre agnirūrdhvapātre tiryakpāte tu rasastiryakpatati tatraikapātrapṛṣṭhe jalamanyapātrādho vahniḥ || 2.8:13 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.9-16


<pātanayantra>
aṣṭāṅgulavistāraṃ dairghyeṇa daśāṅgulaṃ tvadhobhāṇḍam |
kaṇṭhādadhaḥ samucchritaṃ caturaṅgulaṃ kṛtajalādhāram || ṅṛht_2.9 ||
antaḥpraviṣṭatalabhāṇḍavadanaṃ jalamagnanijamukhaprāntā |
upariṣṭāccipiṭaghaṭī deyodaraṣoḍaśāṅgulaviśālā || ṅṛht_2.10 ||
tasminnūrdhvabhāṇḍe nipātitaḥ sakaladoṣanirmuktaḥ |
sutarāṃ bhavati rasendro dravye ca rasāyane yogyaḥ || ṅṛht_2.11 ||
<adhaḥpātana>
kṛtvā ca naṣṭapiṣṭiṃ triphalāśikhiśigrurājikāpaṭubhiḥ |
saṃlepya cordhvabhāṇḍe dīptaikapalairadhaḥpātyaḥ || ṅṛht_2.12 ||
athavā dīpakayantre nipātitaḥ sakaladoṣanirmuktaḥ |
<tiryakpātana>
tiryakpātanavidhinā nipātyaḥ sūtarājastu || ṅṛht_2.13 ||
ślakṣṇīkṛtamabhradalaṃ rasendrayuktaṃ tathāranālena |
khalve dattvā mṛditaṃ yāvattannaṣṭapiṣṭatāmeti || ṅṛht_2.14 ||
kuryāttiryakpātanapātitasūtaṃ krameṇa dṛḍhavahnau |
saṃsvedya pātyate'sau na patati yāvaddṛḍhaścāgniḥ || ṅṛht_2.15 ||
<nirodhana>
mardanamūrchanapātaiḥ kadarthito bhajati mandavīryatvāt |
sṛṣṭyambujairnirodhāllabdhāpyāyo na ṣaṇḍhaḥ syāt || ṅṛht_2.16 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

ṣaṣṭhoddiṣṭaṃ nirodhanasaṃskāraṃ spaṣṭayannāha mardanetyādi || 2.9-16:1 ||
etairmardanamūrchanapātaiḥ saṃskāraviśeṣaṃ kṛtvā mandavīryatvātkadarthito bhavati || 2.9-16:2 ||
kutsitavidhānena kadarthito bhavatītyarthaḥ punaḥ sūtaḥ sṛṣṭyambujaiḥ saha mardanānantaraṃ nirodhātmūṣādvayasampuṭe kūpikāyāṃ vā nirodhātrundhanātlabdhāpyāyaḥ prāptabalaḥ sanna ṣaṇḍhaḥ syātna śukrarahito bhavati || 2.9-16:3 ||
sṛṣṭiḥ mūtraśukraśoṇitarūpā ambujaṃ lavaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ kamalamiti mandāḥ || 2.9-16:4 ||
sṛṣṭiryathā |
go'jāvinaranārīṇāṃ mūtraṃ śukraṃ ca śoṇitam |
sṛṣṭireṣā samākhyātā ṣaṇḍhadoṣavināśinī |
iti śaktyavatārāt || 2.9-16:5 ||
yantraṃ yathā |
raktasaindhavakhoṭena mūṣādvaṃdvaṃ prakalpayet |
tatsampuṭe rasaṃ kṣiptvā navasāraṃ sanimbukam || 2.9-16:6 ||
sampuṭasya prayatnena lepayetsaṃdhimuttamam |
vajramṛtsnāṃ samādāya veṣṭayettatprayatnataḥ || 2.9-16:7 ||
chāyāśuṣkaṃ ca tatkṛtvā bhūgarte sthāpayettataḥ |
aṣṭāṅgulapramāṇena mūṣordhvaṃ tatra pūraṇam || 2.9-16:8 ||
trisaptadinaparyantaṃ karīṣāgniṃ ca kārayet |
dine dine prakartavyā mūṣā saindhavanūtanā || 2.9-16:9 ||
svedayettatprayatnena bhūgarbhe sthāpayettataḥ |
athavā kūpikāmadhye sūtaṃ saindhavasaṃyutam || 2.9-16:10 ||
bhūgarbhe ca tataḥ sthāpyamekaviṃśaddināvadhi |
ayaṃ nirodhako nāmnā mahāmukhakaro rase || 2.9-16:11 ||
iti || 2.9-16:12 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.17


<7. niyamana>
iti labdhavīryaḥ samyakcapalo'sau saṃniyamyate tadanu |
phaṇinayanāmbujamārkavakarkoṭīciñcikāsvedāt || ṅṛht_2.17 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

saptamoddiṣṭasaṃskāraṃ spaṣṭayannāha itītyādi || 2.17:1 ||
iti pūrvoktavidhānena yantraṇādinā tadanu rodhanānantaramasau capalaścañcalo raso niyamyate karmavidā saṃniyamanaṃ kriyate || 2.17:2 ||
kasmātphaṇīlaśunāmbujamārkavakarkoṭīciñcikāsvedātphaṇī tāmbūlaṃ laśunaṃ rasonaḥ ambujaṃ lavaṇaṃ mārkavaḥ bhṛṅgarājaḥ karkoṭīti pratītā vandhyā ciñcikā amlikā etābhiḥ saha yaḥ svedaḥ yantre agnitāpaḥ tasmāt || 2.17:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ samyaklabdhavīryaḥ prāptabalo vīryavān rasaḥ capalatvanivṛttaye niyamyata ityarthaḥ || 2.17:4 ||
viśeṣaścātra kācakūpīṃ mṛdā lipya raso madhye vimucyate |
kalāṃśaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ dattvā madhye kiṃcitpradīyate || 2.17:5 ||
dvāramudrā prakartavyā vajramṛttikayā dṛḍhā |
bhūgarbhe kūpikāṃ sthāpya sitayā garbhapūraṇam || 2.17:6 ||
karīṣāgniḥ prakartavya ekaviṃśaddināvadhi |
ayaṃ niyāmako nāma vahnipratyantakārakaḥ || 2.17:7 ||
rodhanāllabdhavīryasya capalatvanivṛttaye |
kriyate yo rasasvedaḥ proktaṃ niyamanaṃ hi tat || 2.17:8 ||
iti || 2.17:9 ||
yantralakṣaṇaṃ tu |
catuḥprasthajalādhāraṃ caturaṅgulakānanam |
ghaṭayantramidaṃ proktaṃ tadāpyāyanakaṃ smṛtamiti || 2.17:10 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.18


<8. dīpana>
bhūkhagaṭaṅkaṇamaricairlavaṇāsurīśigrukāñjikaistridinam |
svedena dīpito'sau grāsārthī jāyate sūtaḥ || ṅṛht_2.18 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

aṣṭamoddiṣṭasya dīpanasaṃskārasya vidhiṃ spaṣṭayannāha bhūkhagetyādi || 2.18:1 ||
asau pūrvasaṃskṛtarasa etairauṣadhaistridinaṃ nirantaraṃ yathā syāttathā svedena dīpitaḥ kṣutpīḍitaḥ san grāsārthī kavalābhilāṣī jāyate || 2.18:2 ||
etairoṣadhaiḥ bhūkhagaṭaṅkaṇamaricaiḥ na kevalametaiḥ punarlavaṇāsurīśigrukāñjikaiḥ || 2.18:3 ||
bhūḥ tuvarī khagaḥ kāsīsaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ maricamūṣaṇametaiḥ || 2.18:4 ||
punaḥ lavaṇaṃ saindhavamāsurī rājikā śigruḥ saubhāñjanaṃ vṛkṣaviśeṣaḥ kāñjikaṃ pūrvoktamamlībhūtametaiśceti || 2.18:5 ||
viśeṣaścātra |
svedanaṃ rasarājasya kṣārāmlaviṣamadyakaiḥ |
bījapūraṃ samādāya vṛntamutsṛjya kārayet || 2.18:6 ||
tasya madhye kṣipetsūtaṃ kalāṃśakṣārasaṃyutam |
dvāraṃ nirudhya yatnena vastramadhye nibandhayet || 2.18:7 ||
dolāsvedaḥ prakartavya ekaviṃśaddināvadhi |
dine dine prakartavyaṃ nūtanaṃ bījapūrakam || 2.18:8 ||
lelihāno hi dhātūṃśca pīḍyamāno bubhukṣayā |
amunaiva prakartavyaṃ rasarājasya dīpanam || 2.18:9 ||
tryahaṃ saptadinaṃ vātha caturdaśaikaviṃśatim |
saṃskāraḥ sūtarāje tu kramātkramataraṃ varam || 2.18:10 ||
iti || 2.18:11 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.19


<cāraṇa>
iti dīpito viśuddhaḥ pracalitavidyullatāsahasrābhaḥ |
bhavati yadā rasarājaścāryo satvādi tadā bījam || ṅṛht_2.19 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

kṛtāṣṭasaṃskārasya pāradasya saṃskārāntarasiddhāṃ parīkṣāmāha itītyādi || 2.19:1 ||
iti pūrvoktaprakāreṇa rasarājo dīpitaḥ san kṣudutpīḍitaḥ san viśuddho bhavati || 2.19:2 ||
yadīdṛśo bhavati rasarājastadā cāryaḥ cāraṇakarma kāryam || 2.19:3 ||
kīdṛkpracalitavidyullatāsahasrābhaḥ prakarṣeṇa calatyaścalanaśīlā yā vidyutastāsāṃ sahasrasyeva bhā dīptiryasya sa tathoktaḥ || 2.19:4 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā cāryaḥ idamagre vakṣyamāṇaṃ kiṃcitdhātūparasamahārasaratnasaṃjñakaṃ dvitīyaṃ rasarājasambandhinaṃ dattvā saṃyojyetyarthaḥ || 2.19:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht2.20


<cāraṇa:: samukha, nirmukha>
pītakriyāsu pītaṃ śvetaṃ tārakriyāsu mukhamādau |
deyaṃ khalve ghṛṣṭo divyauṣadhibhiḥ sa nirmukhaścarati || ṅṛht_2.20 ||

iti paramahaṃsaparivrājakācāryaśrīmadgovindabhagavatpādaviracite rasahṛdayatantre rasaśodhanātmako dvitīyo'vabodhaḥ ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

kiṃ dvitīyamityāśaṅkya taṃ spaṣṭayannāha pītetyādi || 2.20:1 ||
ādau prathamaṃ mukhaṃ vidheyamityadhyāhāraḥ pāradasya mukhaṃ kāryamityarthaḥ || 2.20:2 ||
tataścetpītakriyā bhavanti svarṇasambandhīni kāryāṇi bhavanti tadā pītaṃ svarṇaṃ deyam || 2.20:3 ||
śvetakriyāsu śvetaṃ deyaṃ śvetaṃ tāramityarthaḥ || 2.20:4 ||
svarṇatārādikaṃ khalve dattaṃ kṛtamukho rasaścarati || 2.20:5 ||
punarnirmukho rasaḥ akṛtamukho rasaḥ khalve ghṛṣṭo gharṣitaḥ san pūrvoktaṃ carati bhakṣayati || 2.20:6 ||
kābhiḥ saha divyauṣadhībhirvakṣyamāṇābhiḥ saha || 2.20:7 ||
yathā padyam |
sāsyo rasaḥ syātpaṭuśigrututthaiḥ sarājikairvyoṣaṇakaistrirātram |
piṣṭastataḥ svinnatanuḥ suvarṇamukhānayaṃ khādati sarvadhātūn || 2.20:8 ||
iti || 2.20:9 ||
nirmukhaśca amlavargeṇa saṃyuktaṃ yathālābhena mardayet |
abhrakādīṃśca carate sūtako vāsanāmukhaḥ || 2.20:10 ||
iyanmānasya sūtasya bhojyadravyātmikā mitiḥ |
iyatītyucyate yāsau grāsamānamitīritam || 2.20:11 ||
iti paribhāṣā || 2.20:12 ||
iti śrīmatkuralakulapayodhisudhākaramiśramaheśātmajacaturbhujamiśraviracitāyāṃ mugdhāvabodhinyāṃ rasahṛdayaṭīkāyāṃ rasaśodhanātmako dvitīyo'vabodhaḥ || 2.20:13 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.1


ghanarahitabījacāraṇasamprāptadalādilābhakṛtakṛtyāḥ |
kṛpaṇāḥ prāpya samudraṃ varāṭikālābhasaṃtuṣṭāḥ || ṅṛht_3.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sudhākaragrāsasattvaṃ padmākarasukhāvaham |
tathā vibhūtayaścāsya sajjanānandakārakāḥ || 3.1:1 ||
anena padyena granthasya guṇādhikyaṃ saṃmatatvaṃ ca varṇayannāha ghanetyādi || 3.1:2 ||
ye evaṃvidhāḥ puruṣāste kṛpaṇā eva || 3.1:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭāḥ ghanarahitabījacāraṇasamprāptadalādidravyakṛtakṛtyāḥ ghanenābhrakeṇa rahitaṃ varjitaṃ yadbījacāraṇamanyadhātvādīnāṃ bījakavalanaṃ tena yatprāptaṃ dalādidravyaṃ pattrarañjanavarṇavṛddhitārakṛṣṭī melāpakādikaṃ vastu tena ye kṛtakṛtyāḥ pūrṇāḥ ātmānaṃ manyante ityadhyāhāraḥ || 3.1:4 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭāḥ kṛpaṇāḥ samudraṃ prāpya ratnākaraṃ labdhvā varāṭikālābhasaṃtuṣṭāḥ kapardikālābhena saṃtuṣṭiṃ prāptaḥ daridrabhāvāditi bhāvaḥ || 3.1:5 ||
tathā evaṃvidhāḥ kṛpaṇā idaṃ śāstraṃ ratnākararūpaṃ bahuratnaṃ prāpya dalādidravyeṇa kṛtakṛtyāḥ || 3.1:6 ||
śāstrasamudrayorguṇaratnaiḥ sāmyaṃ varāṭikādalādirdravyayoḥ sāmyaṃ tucchatayā yata ubhāvapi nikṛṣṭāveva || 3.1:7 ||
yata etacchāstraṃ bahupradaṃ mudrikāvedhasparśavedhadhūmavedhaśabdavedhadhāmyavedhādhāmyadhātuvedhapradatvāt || 3.1:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.2


anye punarmahānto lakṣmīkarirājakaustubhādīni |
avadhīrya labdhavantaḥ parāmṛtaṃ cāmarā jātāḥ || ṅṛht_3.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

punaratra granthe guṇādhikyaśataṃ ca varṇayannāhānye ityādi || 3.2:1 ||
punarityaprathamaviśeṣaṇayoriti prasādaḥ || 3.2:2 ||
atra punarviśeṣaṇe || 3.2:3 ||
anye pūrvebhyo mahānto vartante || 3.2:4 ||
kiṃ kurvantaḥ parāmṛtaṃ labdhavantaḥ santa amarā jātā maraṇarahitā jīvanmuktā jātā ityarthaḥ || 3.2:5 ||
paraṃ ca tadamṛtaṃ ceti samāsaḥ mokṣamityarthaḥ || 3.2:6 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā lakṣmīkarirājakaustubhādīni avadhīrya avahelanaṃ vidhāya lakṣmīrharipriyā karirāja airāvata indravāraṇaḥ kaustubho harermaṇiḥ ityādīni caturdaśaratnāni || 3.2:7 ||
satāmayameva svabhāvaḥ daridrāṇāṃ kṛpaṇānāṃ pūrvavat || 3.2:8 ||
ata evaitacchāstraṃ kṛpaṇamahatāṃ nikaṣarūpam || 3.2:9 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.3


kṣārauṣadhipaṭvamlaiḥ kṣudbodho rāgabandhane svedāt |
na punaḥ pakṣacchedo dravatvaṃ vā vinā gaganam || ṅṛht_3.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sarvotkṛṣṭatvena gaganagrāsasādhanamāha kṣāretyādi || 3.3:1 ||
kṣudbodho rasarājasya jāyate iti śeṣaḥ || 3.3:2 ||
kaiḥ kṛtvā kṣārauṣadhipaṭvamlaiḥ kṣārauṣadhayo'himārādayaḥ paṭu saindhavamamlamamlavetasādi etaiḥ kṣudutpattirbhavedityarthaḥ || 3.3:3 ||
kṣārauṣadhayo yathā |
ahimāramapāmārge taṇḍulīyakasaṃyutam |
snuhyarkakaravīraṃ ca lāṅgalīkṣīrakandau || 3.3:4 ||
karkoṭīṃ kañcukīṃ tumbāṃ palāśaṃ cāgnimanthakam |
karīraṃ citrakaṃ śigruṃ varuṇaṃ vetasaṃ vaṭam || 3.3:5 ||
paṭolyarjunakūṣmāṇḍakadalīvajrakandakam |
aśvatthaṃ sūraṇaṃ jālīṃ dahetkandānanekaśaḥ || 3.3:6 ||
antardhūmena sarvāṃśca devadālīṃ dahettathā |
auṣadhikṣāranāmāsau gaṇastu parikīrtitaḥ || 3.3:7 ||
iti || 3.3:8 ||
amlaṃ yathā |
amlavetasajambīraṃ lakucaṃ bījapūrakam |
cāṅgerī caṇakāmlaṃ ca nāraṅgaṃ tittiḍī tathā || 3.3:9 ||
ambaṣṭhā karamardaśca kapitthaḥ karaṇādikaḥ |
pañcāmlasaṃyuto vā syādamlavargaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 3.3:10 ||
caṇakāmlaṃ ca sarveṣāmekameva praśasyate |
amlavetasamekaṃ vā sarveṣāmuttamottamam || 3.3:11 ||
iti || 3.3:12 ||
ityetaiḥ kṣārauṣadhipaṭvamlaiḥ kṣudbodho bhavetrāgabandhane ca bhavetāṃ rāgo rañjanaṃ bandhanaṃ pūrvamupavarṇitam || 3.3:13 ||
kuto hetoḥ etaiḥ pūrvoktaiḥ karaṇarūpaiḥ svedāt || 3.3:14 ||
punariti viśeṣaṇe || 3.3:15 ||
pakṣacchedaḥ rasapakṣāpakartanaṃ yathā sthiro bhavati dravyatvaṃ guṇavattvaṃ vā gaganamabhrakaṃ vinā na bhavatītyarthaḥ || 3.3:16 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.4


abhrakajīrṇo balavān bhavati rasastasya cāraṇe proktāḥ |
saṃdhānavāsanauṣadhinirmukhasamukhā mahāyogāḥ || ṅṛht_3.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

dravyatvamabhrakeṇāhābhrakajīrṇa ityādi || 3.4:1 ||
balarahite atikṣudbodhe ṣaṇḍhatā bhavet || 3.4:2 ||
yathā |
śītatvānmardanābhāvāllohāśuddhasya jāraṇāt |
viḍaprabhūtadānādvā bhuṅkte jīrṇādajīrṇagaḥ || 3.4:3 ||
atyagnito nirāhārātkrāmaṇārahitasya ca |
ityetā vikriyā jñeyā aṣṭabhiḥ ṣaṇḍhatāṃ vrajet || 3.4:4 ||
iti || 3.4:5 ||
ato raso'ṣṭasaṃskārānantaramabhrakajīrṇaḥ kartavyaḥ yato'bhrakajīrṇaṃ balavān bhavati || 3.4:6 ||
abhrakajīrṇe grāsanyāye na jāritaṃ yasmin sa tathoktaḥ || 3.4:7 ||
tasyābhrajāraṇāyogyasya rasasya cāraṇe kevalakavalane ete proktāḥ || 3.4:8 ||
ete ke saṃdhānavāsanauṣadhinirmukhasamukhā eva mahāyogāḥ mahaddravyatvakārakāḥ saṃdhānaṃ sarvadhānyānāmaṣṭauṣadhyādīnāṃ ca saṃdhānaṃ yathā sarvadhānyāni nikṣipya āranālaṃ tu kārayet || 3.4:9 ||
sapattramūlasaṃyuktā auṣadhīstatra niḥkṣipet || 3.4:10 ||
kṣitikāsīsasāmudrasindhutryuṣaṇarājikaiḥ |
saṃyuktaṃ kārayettattu soṣme saptāhasaṃsthitam || 3.4:11 ||
taccāranālasaṃyuktaṃ tāmrabhāṇḍe tu saṃdhayet |
ityabhrakacāraṇārthaṃ saṃdhānam || 3.4:12 ||
vāsanauṣadhyo yathā |
vijñeyamauṣadhīvargaṃ yathā śāstrairudāhṛtam |
jalajaṃ sthalajaṃ caiva samyakjñātvā tu kārayet || 3.4:13 ||
jalajā utpalī padmā sthalajā ca prasāriṇī |
jālinī abhracandrā ca citraparṇī triparṇikā |
rasacandraukasaścaiva tathā ca jalamūlakaḥ |
samaṅgā vāribhūtā ca apāmārgo jalodbhavā || 3.4:14 ||
ajamārī upāmbuśca kumbhikā jalapippalī |
jalapūrvāmbusītā ca kumārī nāginī tathā || 3.4:15 ||
sitajaṅghā svaraścaiva tathā sarpaḥ sugandhikā |
vṛddhā ca bṛhatī tadvanmūrtirmārjārapādikā || 3.4:16 ||
tathā jalacakorī ca mīnākṣī ahilocanā |
jayāṃvica varāhī ca apattrā īśvarī tathā || 3.4:17 ||
kurkurī halinī caiva bṛhatī vajrakandakam |
musalī vanamālā ca vidārī mohinī tathā || 3.4:18 ||
māṇḍūkī lavaṇā caiva ugrā ca uttamā tathā |
śikhipādī kapotī ca nandinī vṛścikālikā |
haṃsapādī śikhā caiva sārivā vāyasī tathā |
dantī gojihvikā caiva garuḍī hemapuṣpikā || 3.4:19 ||
samaṅgā jalajā caiva māṃsī pāṣāṇabhedikā |
alambuṣā meghanādā śukanādā kapotikī || 3.4:20 ||
kṣīrikā tulasī dhānyā meṣikā ca vanārjakā |
vārāhī caṇakāyāsī tathā ca aparājitā || 3.4:21 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭigaṇo hyeṣa auṣadhīnāṃ prakīrtitaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhāṣṭakaprayogena avasthāṃ naiva kārayet || 3.4:22 ||
iti || 3.4:23 ||
ayamoṣadhīgaṇaḥ saṃdhāne'pi yojyaḥ || 3.4:24 ||
saṃdhānaṃ ca vāsanauṣadhayaśca tābhiḥ kṛtvā ye nirmukhasamukhā eva yogāḥ akṛtamukhakṛtamukhā ityarthaḥ || 3.4:25 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.5


<abhra:: preparation forcāraṇa>
niścandrikaṃ hi gaganaṃ kṣārāmlairbhāvitaṃ tathā rudhiraiḥ |
sṛṣṭitrayanīrakaṇātumbarurasamarditaṃ carati || ṅṛht_3.5 ||

------------------
*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

nirmukhatvenābhrakacāraṇopāyamāha niścandrikamityādi || 3.5:1 ||
hi niścitaṃ yadgaganamabhrakaṃ niścandrikaṃ candrarahitaṃ bhavati vajrasaṃjñakamityarthaḥ tadgaganaṃ rucirairnirdoṣairmanoramairvividhaiḥ kṣārāmlairbhāvitaṃ plāvitaṃ kāryaṃ kṣārā yavakṣārasvarjikṣāraṭaṅkaṇakṣārādayo'mlā amlavetasajambīrādyāḥ pūrvoktāḥ || 3.5:2 ||
nanu kṣārā rucirāḥ kathaṃ bhavanti ucyate yathā sarjikācūrṇabhāgaikaṃ viṃśadbhāgaṃ jalasya ca || 3.5:3 ||
tāvatkvāthyaṃ kṣipedbhāṇḍe yāvatphenaṃ sitaṃ bhavet || 3.5:4 ||
kṣīṇe kṣīṇe jalaṃ dattvā śvetaphenaṃ ca gṛhyate || 3.5:5 ||
tadā tu ḍekayantreṇa drāvayedagniyogataḥ || 3.5:6 ||
triḥsaptavāraṃ kartavyaṃ drāvaṇaṃ mūtrasaṃyutam || 3.5:7 ||
svarjikākṣāranāmāyaṃ drāvaṇe paramo mataḥ || 3.5:8 ||
iti viśeṣavidhiḥ || 3.5:9 ||
evamatyuttamāḥ kṣārāḥ syuḥ sampakvāḥ himāḥ || 3.5:10 ||
punaḥ sṛṣṭitrayanīrakaṇātumbarurasamarditaṃ go'jāvinārīṇāṃ mūtraṃ śukraṃ ca śoṇitaṃ sṛṣṭitrayaṃ nīrakaṇā jalapippalī paṭuriti loke tumburu pratītaṃ jalakaṇā ca tumbaruśca anayo rasaḥ sṛṣṭitrayaṃ ca jalakaṇātumbarurasaśca tābhyāṃ mardanaṃ kāryam || 3.5:11 ||
rasaḥ pāradaḥ nirmukho'pi evaṃvidhaṃ gaganaṃ carati grāsīkaroti || 3.5:12 ||
karaṇaṃ granthāntare'sti |
dugdhatrayaṃ kumāryambu gaṅgāputraṃ trimūtrakam |
vaṭaśuṅgamajāraktamebhirabhraṃ sumarditam || 3.5:13 ||
śatadhā puṭitaṃ cāpi jāyate padmarāgavat |
niścandrikaṃ mṛtaṃ tvabhraṃ vṛddhadehe rasāyanam || 3.5:14 ||
kāminīmadadarpaghnaṃ śastaṃ puṃstvopaghātinām |
iti || 3.5:15 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.6


<mercury:: abhracāraṇa>
yavaciñcikāmbupuṭitaṃ tanmūlaśatāvarīgadākulitam |
ghanaravaśigrupunarnavarasabhāvitamabhrakaṃ carati || ṅṛht_3.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhānāntaramāha yavetyādi || 3.6:1 ||
punarabhrakaṃ yavaciñcikāmbupuṭitaṃ kāryaṃ yavaciñcikā pratītā yavaciñciketi loke tasyā ambudravaḥ tena puṭitamātapayogena bhāvitam || 3.6:2 ||
punastanmūlaśatāvarīgadākulitaṃ kāryaṃ tasyā yavaciñcikāyā mūlaṃ tanmūlaṃ śatāvarī śatapādgadaḥ kuṣṭhaḥ etairākulaṃ vyāptaṃ pariplutam || 3.6:3 ||
yadā sarasauṣadhābhāvastadāyaṃ vidhiḥ yathā |
śuṣkadravyamupādāya svarasānāmasaṃbhave |
vāriṇyaṣṭaguṇe sādhyaṃ grāhyaṃ pādāvaśeṣitam || 3.6:4 ||
iti || 3.6:5 ||
punarghanaravaśigrupunarnavarasabhāvitaṃ kāryaṃ ghanaravastaṇḍulīyakaḥ śigru saubhāñjanaṃ suhijanā iti loke punarnavā varṣābhūḥ pratītā eteṣāṃ rasena bhāvitaṃ pariplutamātapayogena śoṣyamityarthaḥ || 3.6:6 ||
evaṃvidhamabhraṃ rasaścarati || 3.6:7 ||
nirmukhena bhāvanāśabdena śatavāraṃ jñātavyaṃ granthāntarasāmyāt || 3.6:8 ||
atra viśeṣaḥ || 3.6:9 ||
somavallīrase piṣṭvā dāpayecca puṭatrayam |
somavallīrasenaiva sapta vārāṃśca bhāvayet || 3.6:10 ||
dāpayenmṛnmaye bhāṇḍe rasena saṃyutam |
mūlaṃ tu śarapuṅkhāyā gavyakṣīreṇa gharṣayet |
kalkena melayetsūtaṃ gaganaṃ tadadhaūrdhvagam || 3.6:11 ||
sthāpayedravitāpe tu nirmukho grasate kṣaṇāt |
jāyate piṣṭikā śīghraṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 3.6:12 ||
iti || 3.6:13 ||
anyacca |
tilaparṇīrasaṃ nītvā gaganaṃ tena bhāvayet |
mardanājjāyate piṣṭī nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 3.6:14 ||
iti || 3.6:15 ||
anyacca |
muṇḍīniryāsake nāgaṃ bahuśastu niṣecayet |
tenābhrakaṃ tu saṃyojya bhūyo bhūyaḥ paṭe dahet || 3.6:16 ||
citrakārdrakamūlānāmekaikena tu saptadhā |
plāvitavyaṃ prayatnena gandhakābhrakacūrṇakam || 3.6:17 ||
nāgamuṇḍīrasākṣiptaṃ rasaluṅgāmlabhāvitam |
ṣoḍaśāṃśena dātavyaṃ dolāyantre caredrasaḥ || 3.6:18 ||
iti nirmukhacāraṇam || 3.6:19 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.7-9


<āranāla forjāraṇa>
sarjīkṣitikhagaṭaṅkaṇalavaṇānvitamarkabhājane tridinam |
paryuṣitamāranālaṃ gaganādiṣu bhāvane śastam || ṅṛht_3.7 ||
tasminnāgaṃ śuddhaṃ pradrāvya niṣecayecchataṃ vārān |
vaṅgaṃ vā tāravidhau rasāyane naiva tadyojyam || ṅṛht_3.8 ||
gaganarasoparasāmṛtaloharasāyasādicūrṇāni |
sarvamanena ha bhāvyaṃ yatkiṃcitcāraṇāvastu || ṅṛht_3.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

samukhacāraṇamāha sarjītyādi || 3.7-9:1 ||
āranālaṃ svedanasaṃskāre yaduktaṃ kāñjikaṃ tat || 3.7-9:2 ||
arkabhājane tāmrapātre || 3.7-9:3 ||
tridinaṃ yāvattāvatparyuṣitaṃ saṃdhānīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt || 3.7-9:4 ||
kīdṛgvidhamāranālaṃ sarjīkṣitikhagaṭaṅkaṇalavaṇānvitaṃ sarjī pratītā sājīti loke kṣitiḥ sphaṭikā khagaḥ kāsīsaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ sohāgā iti loke lavaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ tadabhāve lavaṇāṣṭakeṣu yatra yallabhyaṃ tadeva yojyaṃ lavaṇoddeśaḥ |
saindhavaṃ rucakaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ viḍaṃ sāmudramaudbhidam |
romakaṃ pāṃśujaṃ ceti lavaṇāṣṭakamucyate || 3.7-9:5 ||
iti etairanvitaṃ militaṃ kuryāt || 3.7-9:6 ||
tadāranālaṃ gaganādikabhāvane abhrakādiplāvane śastaṃ pradhānamabhrakādikā agre vakṣyamāṇāḥ || 3.7-9:7 ||
nirmukhā samukhā ceti dvividhā cāraṇā matā nirmukhā cāraṇā proktā bījādhānena bhāgataḥ || 3.7-9:8 ||
śuddhaṃ svarṇaṃ ca rūpyaṃ ca bījamityabhidhīyate || 3.7-9:9 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśato bījaprakṣepo mukhamucyate || 3.7-9:10 ||
evaṃ kṛte raso grāsalolupo mukhavān bhavet |
kaṭhinānyapi lohāni kṣamo bhavati bhakṣaṇe || 3.7-9:11 ||
iyaṃ hi samukhā proktā cāraṇā varavārttikaiḥ |
iti || 3.7-9:12 ||
abhrakādyapadhātūnāṃ nirmukhacāraṇaṃ hemādidhātūnāṃ samukhacāraṇamiti viveko jñeyaḥ || 3.7-9:13 ||
rasāyane śarīrakārye nāgavaṅgau na cāraṇīyau kiṃtu svarṇādikaṃ bījaṃ cāraṇīyamiti bhāvaḥ || 3.7-9:14 ||
ādiśabdena nāgavaṅgayoradhikāraviśeṣamāha tasminnityādi || 3.7-9:15 ||
tasmin pūrvoktasaṃdhāne śuddhaṃ nirmalīkṛtaṃ nāgaṃ sīsakaṃ pradrāvya jalarūpaṃ vidhāya vahniyogātiti śeṣaḥ niṣecayetniṣekaḥ kartavyaḥ vā tatraiva saṃdhāne vaṅgaṃ raṅgaṃ pradrāvya niṣecayet || 3.7-9:16 ||
kativārān śataṃ vārān pratiśatamityarthaḥ || 3.7-9:17 ||
tadvaṅgaṃ tāravidhau rūpyavidhāne yojyaṃ rasena saha militaṃ kāryamityarthaḥ || 3.7-9:18 ||
āsyāmāryāyāṃ svarṇādhikāre'nuktamapi nāgaṃ granthāntarātsamāyojyamiti viśeṣārthaḥ || 3.7-9:19 ||
tannāgaṃ vaṅgaṃ ca rasāyane śarīrasiddhinimittaṃ na yojyamiti yato nāgavaṅgaprabhavāvaupādhikau doṣau galabandhagulmadau kathitau etannāgaṃ vaṅgaṃ ca grāsārthe yojyamiti yuktaṃ yata etenāntargatenānyadapi grāhyaṃ dravyaṃ grasatīti bhāvaḥ || 3.7-9:20 ||
vaṅge viśeṣaḥ |
khurakaṃ miśrakaṃ ceti dvividhaṃ vaṅgamucyate |
khurakaṃ guṇataḥ śreṣṭhaṃ miśrakaṃ na rase hitam || 3.7-9:21 ||
iti rajatakarmaṇi yogyaṃ grāhyam || 3.7-9:22 ||
gaganādikramaṃ spaṣṭayannāha gaganetyādi || 3.7-9:23 ||
anena pūrvoktasaṃdhānena sarvaṃ sakalaṃ bhāvyaṃ bhāvitaṃ kuryāt || 3.7-9:24 ||
sarvamiti kiṃ gaganarasoparasāmṛtaloharasāyasādicūrṇāni || 3.7-9:25 ||
gaganamabhrakaṃ vajrasaṃjñikaṃ rasā mahārasā hiṅgulasvarṇamākṣikarūpyamākṣikaśilājatucapalacumbakavaikrāntakharparagairikasphaṭikakāsīsasaṃjñakā amṛtaṃ viṣaṃ vā amṛtalohā na mṛtā amṛtā amṛtāśca te lohāśca dhāvata iti rasāḥ pūrvoktāḥ āyasā lohāsteṣāṃ saṃyogajāni yāni cūrṇāni kalkāni śulbābhrādīni || 3.7-9:26 ||
ādiśabdāduparasānāmapi grahaṇam || 3.7-9:27 ||
na kevalametānyeva saṃdhānena bhāvyāni kiṃtvanyadapi yatkiṃciccāraṇāvastu cāraṇayogyaṃ dravyaratnādikaṃ tadapyetena saṃdhānena bhāvyaṃ cāraṇārtham || 3.7-9:28 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.10


ādau khalve mṛditāṃ piṣṭīṃ hemnaśca tāṃ rasaścarati |
tārasya tārakarmaṇi dattvā sūte tato gaganam || ṅṛht_3.10 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

svarṇarūpyayoradhikāraviśeṣamāhādāvityādi || 3.10:1 ||
tato'nantaraṃ hemnaḥ svarṇasya piṣṭīṃ khalve mṛditāṃ vakṣyamāṇena iti śeṣaḥ || 3.10:2 ||
tāṃ piṣṭīṃ rasaścarati || 3.10:3 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā piṣṭīṃ dadyātādau prathamataḥ sūteśvare gaganamabhrakaṃ dattvā || 3.10:4 ||
hemno'dhikāro darśitaḥ || 3.10:5 ||
hemakarmaṇi hemaiva tārakarmaṇi tārameva dadyāt || 3.10:6 ||
hemni viśeṣaḥ |
svarṇaṃ pañcavidhaṃ proktaṃ prākṛtaṃ sahajāgnijam |
etatsvarṇatrayaṃ caiva yojyaṃ ṣoḍaśavarṇakam || 3.10:7 ||
khanijaṃ rasavādotthaṃ supattrīkṛtaśodhitam |
taccaturdaśavarṇāḍhyaṃ manujānāṃ rujāpaham || 3.10:8 ||
iti || 3.10:9 ||
tāre'pi viśeṣaḥ |
kailāse sahajaṃ rūpyaṃ khanijaṃ kṛtrimaṃ tathā |
vyutkrameṇa guṇaiḥ śreṣṭhaṃ nāgottīrṇe rase hitam || 3.10:10 ||
iti || 3.10:11 ||
tāramapi pūrvavarṇaṃ cāryam || 3.10:12 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.11

truṭiśo dattvā mṛditaṃ sāre khalve'bhrahemalohādi |
carati rasendraḥ kṣitikhagavetasabījapūrāmlaiḥ || ṅṛht_3.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

piṣṭīmardane pātrauṣadhānyāha truṭiśa ityādi || 3.11:1 ||
abhrahemalohādīni abhramabhrakaṃ pratītaṃ hema kanakaṃ tadeva lohaḥ abhrakaṃ ca hemalohaśca tāvādī yasya tat || 3.11:2 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ dravyaṃ kṣitikhagavetasabījapūrāmlaiḥ kṣitiśca khagaśca vetasaṃ ca bījapūraśca kṣitikhagabījapūrāḥ bījapūro mātuluṅgaḥ teṣāṃ trayāṇāmamlāḥ tairmṛditaṃ gharṣitaṃ satrasendraścarati || 3.11:3 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā mṛditaṃ truṭiśo'lpamātraṃ dattvā || 3.11:4 ||
kasmin sāre khalve || 3.11:5 ||
sārasya tīkṣṇajātasyāyaṃ sārastasminnevaṃvidhe || 3.11:6 ||
sāre viśeṣaḥ |
muṇḍaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ tathā kāntaṃ bhedāstasya trayodaśa |
mṛdu kuṇṭhaṃ ca kaḍāraṃ trividhaṃ muṇḍamucyate || 3.11:7 ||
kharasāraṃ ca hannālaṃ tārāvartaṃ viḍaṃ tathā |
kālalohaṃ gajākhyaṃ ca ṣaḍvidhaṃ tīkṣṇamucyate || 3.11:8 ||
kāntaṃ lohaṃ caturdhoktaṃ romakaṃ bhrāmakaṃ tathā |
cumbakaṃ drāvakaṃ ceti guṇāstasyottarottarāḥ || 3.11:9 ||
iti || 3.11:10 ||
khalvo yathā |
khalvo'śmādyo nirudgāro dviraṅgulakaṭāhakaḥ |
aṣṭāṅgulāvaṭī kāryā dīrghā vā vartulā tathā || 3.11:11 ||
dvādaśāṅguladīrgheṇa mardakaścaturaṅgulaḥ |
mukhaṃ vṛttaṃ tu kartavyaṃ darpaṇodarasaṃnibham || 3.11:12 ||
iti || 3.11:13 ||
aśmādya iti aśmalohārkāṇāṃ jñātavyaḥ || 3.11:14 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.12-13


samukhaṃ nirmukhamathavā tulyaṃ dviguṇaṃ caturguṇaṃ vāpi |
aṣṭaguṇaṃ ṣoḍaśaguṇamathavā dvātriṃśatāguṇitam || ṅṛht_3.12 ||
iti pattrābhrakamuktaṃ tena vidhānena cārayetsūtam |
<cāraṇa>
grāsaḥ piṣṭī garbhastrilakṣaṇā cāraṇā bhavati || ṅṛht_3.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

dhātvādīnāṃ cāraṇāyāṃ parimāṇamāha samukhamityādi || 3.12-13:1 ||
iti pūrvoktaṃ pattrābhrakamuktaṃ pattrābhrakacāraṇamityarthaḥ || 3.12-13:2 ||
samukhaṃ mukhasahitaṃ cāraṇaṃ bhavatu vātha nirmukhaṃ mukhavarjitaṃ cāraṇaṃ bhavatu ubhayatrāpi tulyaṃ samānaṃ sūtaṃ cārayetdhātvādīniti śeṣaḥ || 3.12-13:3 ||
atra vikalpo dviguṇaṃ sūtāddviguṇitaṃ cārayedvā caturguṇitaṃ sūtāccaturguṇitaṃ vā aṣṭaguṇaṃ sūtādaṣṭaguṇitaṃ vā ṣoḍaśaguṇaṃ sūtātṣoḍaśaguṇitaṃ vā dvātriṃśatāguṇitaṃ sūtāddvātriṃśadguṇitaṃ cārayet || 3.12-13:4 ||
tena vidhānena pūrvoktena vidhānena truṭiśo dattvetyādinā || 3.12-13:5 ||
cāraṇā trilakṣaṇā bhavati trīṇi lakṣaṇāni cihnāni yasyāṃ sā tathoktā || 3.12-13:6 ||
kathaṃ grāsaḥ abhrakasya grāsanaṃ nirmukhatvena samukhatvena vāparā piṣṭī rasenābhrādermelanaṃ punargarbho rasasya garbhe rasarūpaṃ gaganaṃ tiṣṭhatīti || 3.12-13:7 ||
ślokadvayānvayasambandhādyugmam || 3.12-13:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.14-15


<wrongideasaboutjāraṇa>
dolanavidhinā yairapi nānāvidhabhaṅgasaṃskṛtaṃ gaganam |
cāraṇavidhau pradiṣṭaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ noddhūyamānaṃ taiḥ || ṅṛht_3.14 ||
anye'pi tucchamatayo gandhakaniṣpiṣṭiśulbapiṣṭirajaḥ |
dolanavidhinoddhūtaṃ rasajīrṇaṃ taditi manyante || ṅṛht_3.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

iti gaganādigrāsapramāṇaṃ kathitamatha cāraṇāvidhānamāha dolanavidhinetyādi || 3.14-15:1 ||
gaganamabhrakaṃ yairauṣadhaiḥ piṣṭaṃ peṣitaṃ bhavati tairevauṣadhairnālpamānairbahumānairnānāvidhabhaṅgasaṃskṛtaṃ kuryāditi śeṣaḥ || 3.14-15:2 ||
nānāvidhā anekaprakārā ye bhaṅgāstaraṃgā āgamābdhijātāstaiḥ saṃskṛtamupaskṛtam || 3.14-15:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ gaganaṃ māraṇavidhinā pañcatvavidhānena uddiṣṭamuddeśitam |
evaṃvidhamapi gaganaṃ dolanavidhinā cāraṇāyāṃ yojyamiti bhāvaḥ || 3.14-15:4 ||
adhunālpamatīnāṃ matamāhānya ityādi |
anye mahadbhyo'pare tucchamatayastucchā stokā matirbuddhiryeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ alpabuddhaya iti yāvat || 3.14-15:5 ||
rasaṃ jīrṇaṃ jāraṇasaṃskāropapannaṃ rasaṃ manyante iti || 3.14-15:6 ||
kiṃ gandhakaniṣpiṣṭiśulbapiṣṭirajo gandhakena yā niṣpiṣṭiḥ piṣṭībhūtā śulbena yā piṣṭiḥ piṣṭībhūtā tāmrapiṣṭītyarthaḥ gandhakaniṣpiṣṭiśca śulbapiṣṭiśca tayoryadrajaḥ pāṃśuḥ gandhakapiṣṭī yathā |
gandhapāṣāṇacūrṇaṃ ca caṇakasya rasena tu |
bhāvayetsaptavāraṃ tu strīraktena ca saptadhā || 3.14-15:7 ||
śuddhasūtaṃ palaikaṃ tu kharpare dāpayettataḥ |
bhāvitaṃ gandhakaṃ dadyānnarapiṇḍena saṃyutam || 3.14-15:8 ||
dolāyantre'pi tāpena piṣṭikā bhavati kṣaṇāt || 3.14-15:9 ||
iti || 3.14-15:10 ||
eva śulbapiṣṭyapi jāyate || 3.14-15:11 ||
kimbhūtaṃ gandhakaniṣpiṣṭiśulbapiṣṭirajaḥ dolanavidhinodbhūtaṃ dolikāyantravidhānenotpannam || 3.14-15:12 ||
iti yaduktaṃ tadasamañjasamiti bhāvaḥ || 3.14-15:13 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.16


<golakamukha>
tailādikataptarase hāṭakatārādigolakamukhena |
carati ghanaṃ rasarājo hemādibhireti piṇḍatvam || ṅṛht_3.16 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

samukhacāraṇāntarbhūtaṃ vāsanāmukhacāraṇaṃ darśayannāha tailetyādi || 3.16:1 ||
rasarājaḥ pārado hāṭakatārādi svarṇarūpyādi dhātudravyaṃ kṛtrimākṛtrimātmakanavasaṃkhyākaṃ pūrvamuktaṃ carati bhakṣati || 3.16:2 ||
kena golakamukhena || 3.16:3 ||
golakaśca mukhaviśeṣaḥ tena viḍasya golakenetyarthaḥ || 3.16:4 ||
kva sati tailādikataptarase sati || 3.16:5 ||
tailamādiḥ yeṣāṃ te tailādikāstailavasāmūtraśukrapuṣpāḥ etaistapto yo rasa uṣṇatvaṃ nīto yo'sau pāradastasmin satyevaṃ ghanamabhrakaṃ carati rasendraḥ || 3.16:6 ||
punarhemādibhirnavakairgrāsīkṛtaiḥ piṇḍatvameti nibiḍatvaṃ prāpnoti || 3.16:7 ||
tailāni yathā |
kaṅguṇītumbinīghoṣākarañjaśrīphalodbhavam |
kaṭuvātārisiddhārthasomarājīvibhītajam || 3.16:8 ||
atasījaṃ mahākālīnimbajaṃ tilajaṃ tathā |
apāmārgo devadālī dantītumbaruvigrahāḥ || 3.16:9 ||
aṅkolonmattabhallātaphalebhyastailasambhavaḥ |
iti || 3.16:10 ||
vasā yathā |
ajoṣṭrakharameṣāṇāṃ mahiṣasya vasā tathā |
iti || 3.16:11 ||
mūtrapuṣpaśukrāṇi yathā |
mūtrāṇi hastikarabhamahiṣīkharavājinām |
striyaḥ puṃsastathā mūtraṃ puṣpaṃ vīryaṃ ca yojayet |
iti || 3.16:12 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.17


<mukha:: śukapicchamukha>
anye svacchaṃ kṛtvā śukapicchamukhena cārayanti ghanam |
siddhopadeśavidhinā āśitagrāse na śuṣkeṇa || ṅṛht_3.17 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tasminnabhiprāye'nyamatamāhānya ityādi || 3.17:1 ||
eke uktavidhānena cāraṇāṃ kurvanti anye apare rasaṃ pāradaṃ svacchaṃ kṛtvā svedanādyaṣṭasaṃskāropasaṃskṛtaṃ vidhāya vā hiṅgulotthaṃ ghanamabhrakaṃ cārayanti abhrakasya cāraṇāṃ kurvanti || 3.17:2 ||
kena śukapicchamukhena śukapicchaṃ saṃdhānaviśeṣaḥ mukhaṃ viśeṣo yeṣāṃ saṃdhānānāṃ tena || 3.17:3 ||
kva sati cārayanti āśitagrāse sati bhuktakavale sati punaścāryamityarthaḥ || 3.17:4 ||
kena siddhopadeśavidhinā siddhā rasasiddhā nityanāthavīranāthādayaḥ pūrvoktāḥ teṣāṃ ya upadeśavidhistena || 3.17:5 ||
na śuṣkeṇa saṃdhānenārdrībhāvātnīrasatāṃ prāptena punaścāraṇā na syāt || 3.17:6 ||
śukapicchaṃ yathā |
bhasmakṣārān suśuṣkāṃstu kṣārāṃśca lavaṇāni ca |
āloḍya hyamlavargeṇa śulbabhāṇḍe nidhāpayet || 3.17:7 ||
yāvacca śukapicchābhamabhrakaṃ tena bhāvayet |
grasate tatkṣaṇātsūto golakastu vidhīyate || 3.17:8 ||
iti || 3.17:9 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.18

<mercury:: cāraṇa>
athavā mākṣikagaganaṃ samabhāgaṃ paṭuyutaṃ pakvam |
prakṣipya lohapātre svedāntaścarati kṛṣṇābhram || ṅṛht_3.18 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyamatābhiprāye prakārāntaramāhāthavetyādi || 3.18:1 ||
pūrvoktaṃ vidhānaṃ kuryātathavā pakṣāntare idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ kuryāt || 3.18:2 ||
mākṣikagaganamiti mākṣikena yuktaṃ gaganamabhrakaṃ samabhāgaṃ dvayaṃ tulyabhāgaṃ puṭitaṃ bhāvitaṃ yatpaṭu saindhavaṃ lavaṇaṃ śāstrāntarasāmyādamlavargeṇa puṭitaṃ tena yutaṃ militaṃ satpakvaṃ vahnipuṭitaṃ kuryātiti śeṣaḥ || 3.18:3 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā lohapātre muṇḍādibhājane prakṣipya madhye sthāpya || 3.18:4 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ kṛṣṇābhraṃ svedāntarvahnitāpamadhye rasaḥ pāradaścarati grasati mākṣikasaṃyogātkṣipramiti bhāvaḥ || 3.18:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.19


taṃ pravakṣyāmyupadeśaṃ gandhābhrakasaṃpraveśanaṃ yena |
pakṣachinnaśca raso yogyaḥ syādrasarasāyanayoḥ || ṅṛht_3.19 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

upadeśavidhānamāha tamityādi |
yenopadeśena gandhābhrakapraveśanaṃ gandhapāṣāṇasaṃyogādyadabhrapraveśena bhavati abhrasya pāradāntaḥpraveśo bhavati tamupadeśamahaṃ kavirvakṣyāmi kathayiṣye || 3.19:1 ||
atisāmīpyādvartamāna eva ḻaṭ || 3.19:2 ||
tu punaḥ || 3.19:3 ||
pakṣachinnaśca raso rasarasāyanayoryogyaḥ rase jvarādiroganāśake jvarāṅkuśādau rasāyane ca jarāvyādhināśane prayoge yogyaḥ samarthaḥ || 3.19:4 ||
yathā mañjaryām |
mārito dehaśuddhyarthaṃ mūrchito vyādhināśanaḥ |
rasabhasma kvacidroge dehārthe mūrchitaṃ kvacit || 3.19:5 ||
baddhaṃ dvābhyāṃ prayuñjīta śāstradṛṣṭena karmaṇā || 3.19:6 ||
iti || 3.19:7 ||
gandhābhrakapraveśena pakṣachinno'calo bhavediti bhāvaḥ || 3.19:8 ||
____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.20


<sulfur:: specialrole inalchemy>
rasarājarāgadāyī bījānāṃ pākajāraṇasamarthaḥ |
sūtakapakṣacchedī rasabandhe gandhako'bhihitaḥ || ṅṛht_3.20 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

gandhakaṃ viśeṣayannāha rasarājetyādi || 3.20:1 ||
rasabandhane pāradabandhane gandhako'bhitaḥ lelinakaḥ sarvotkṛṣṭaḥ || 3.20:2 ||
kuto yataḥ sūtakapakṣacchedī gandhakaḥ sūtasya pāradasya pakṣau chinatti || 3.20:3 ||
punaḥ kuto rasarājarāgadāyī rasarājaḥ pāradaḥ tasya rāgaṃ rañjanaṃ dadātīti || 3.20:4 ||
punaḥ kuto bījānāṃ pākajāraṇasamarthaḥ pākaśca jāraṇaṃ ca pākajāraṇe || 3.20:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.21-25


<mercury:: māraṇa, pakṣacheda>
dattvā khalve truṭiśo gandhakamādau rasaṃ ca truṭiśo'pi |
tāvacca mardanīyaṃ yāvatsā piṣṭikā bhavati || ṅṛht_3.21 ||
tadanu ca drutabalivasayā samabhāganiyojitaṃ tathā gaganam |
truṭiśo rasaṃ ca dattvā kurvīta yathepsitāṃ piṣṭim || ṅṛht_3.22 ||
sāpi ca dīptairupalairnipātyate'dho'tha dīpikāyantre |
tadanu ca nirmuktamalo nikṛntapakṣo'bhragandhābhyām || ṅṛht_3.23 ||
bhasmākāraśca raso hemnā saha yujyate sa ca dvaṃdve |
gandhakābhrakābhyāṃ rasapakṣāpakartanaṃ yathā syāttathāha dattvetyādi || 3.21-23:1 ||
ādau prathamaṃ khalve lohārkāśmamaye gandhakaṃ truṭiśo dattvā alpamātraṃ vāraṃ vāraṃ gandharasau dattvā tāvanmardanīyaṃ yāvatsā piṣṭikā ekaśarīratā bhavati kajjaliketi vyaktārthaḥ || 3.21-23:2 ||
tadanu tatpaścātrasagandhakapiṣṭīkaraṇānantaraṃ tatra rasagandhakapiṣṭyā gaganamabhrakaṃ samabhāganiyojitaṃ gandharasābhyāṃ tulyāṃśaṃ militaṃ kāryamityarthaḥ || 3.21-23:3 ||
kayā kṛtvā drutabalivasayā drutā dravībhūtā yā balivasā bhekamatsyakarkaṭaśiśumārāṇāṃ tailarūpā śarīrajātā tayā || 3.21-23:4 ||
samabhāgābhrakaniyojanānantaraṃ balivasayā mardanaṃ kāryamiti tātparyārthaḥ || 3.21-23:5 ||
balivasā yathā |
bhekamatsyabhavā yā tu karkaṭasya vasāthavā |
bhāvyamebhiḥ kramādgandhaṃ śiśumāravasāpi vā || 3.21-23:6 ||
etāsvekā balivasā samyaksūtasya bandhinī |
rañjanaṃ caiva kurute maṇimūṣavidhikramāt || 3.21-23:7 ||
eṣā balivasā nāma kṣaṇādbadhnāti sūtakam |
iti || 3.21-23:8 ||
rasagandhābhrapiṣṭiṃ kurvītetyarthaḥ || 3.21-23:9 ||
sā pūrvoktā rasagandhābhrapiṣṭirathetyanantaraṃ dīpikāyantre'dhaḥpātane raso nirmuktamalastyaktadoṣo bhavati || 3.21-23:10 ||
tasminnirmuktamale sati nikṛntapakṣaḥ chinnapakṣo bhavati || 3.21-23:11 ||
kābhyāmabhragandhābhyāmabhraṃ ca gandhaśca abhragandhau tābhyāṃ gandhakāntaḥ saṃyogātsukhaṃ rasābhrapiṣṭirbhavetyato gandhako dvaṃdvamelanasamarthaḥ kiṃ punarbalivasayeti tṛtīyaślokārthaḥ || 3.21-23:12 ||
kulakamiti || 3.21-23:13 ||
pūrvasaṃskṛtarasasyākāraṃ kāryāntarasampattiṃ cāha bhasmetyādi || 3.21-23:14 ||
ca punaḥ || 3.21-23:15 ||
tato'dhaḥpātanādraso bhasmākāro bhasmasadṛśo bhavet || 3.21-23:16 ||
sa ca bhasmākāro rasaḥ hemnā svarṇena sārdhamubhayamelane yujyate karmavidā iti śeṣaḥ || 3.21-23:17 ||
athavā samuccaye avyayo rekārthasambandhātpakṣāntare ca || 3.21-23:18 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.24-25


<mercury:: māraṇa, compoundwithgold>
athavā gandhakapiṣṭiṃ paktvā drutagandhakasya madhye tu || ṅṛht_3.24 ||
sā cāpi hemapiṣṭirvipacyate gandhake bhūyaḥ |
itthaṃ hemnā sūto milati dvaṃdve tathā kṣaṇānmriyate || ṅṛht_3.25 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

athavā hemnā saha pūrvarasavidhānena gandhapiṣṭiṃ kuryātathavā drutagandhakasya dravībhūtagandhakasya madhye paktvā vahniyogena supakvaṃ kṛtvā rasaṃ piṣṭivatrasahemagandhapiṣṭiṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 3.24-25:1 ||
pūrvoddiṣṭasya dvaṃdvamelanasya vidhānamāha setyādi || 3.24-25:2 ||
ca punaḥ || 3.24-25:3 ||
sā pūrvoktā hemapiṣṭiḥ rasahemagandhakṛtaretasoḥ sambandhaḥ sadātanaḥ || 3.24-25:4 ||
hemnā militā yā piṣṭimelanaviśetsā hemapiṣṭī bhūyaḥ punaḥ gandhake vipacyate yuktyā pākaḥ kāryaḥ pūrvavadgandhake || 3.24-25:5 ||
itthamuktavidhānena hemnā saha sūtaḥ pārado milati granthimeti kva sati dvaṃdve sati ubhayasaṃyoge sati || 3.24-25:6 ||
tathoktavidhānena dvaṃdve milati sati tatkālādalpakālato mriyate pañcatvamupayāti || 3.24-25:7 ||
alpakriyāntarakaraṇānmṛto bhavatīti bhāvaḥ || 3.24-25:8 ||
iti pakṣacchedavidhiḥ || 3.24-25:9 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.26


itare pakṣacchedaṃ dvaṃdve rasamāraṇaṃ na vāñchanti |
bījānāmapi pākaṃ hṛṣyanti ca tadanu tapyanti || ṅṛht_3.26 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

etāvatā rasapakṣakartanena nālaṃ bhavitavyamityāha itare ityādi || 3.26:1 ||
ye puruṣā iti uktavidhānena pakṣacchedaṃ rasapakṣāpakartanaṃ vāñchanti punaḥ dvaṃdve rasamāraṇaṃ dvaṃdvena pūrvoktena rasahemagandhakena kṛtvā yadrasamāraṇaṃ tanna vāñchanti punarbījānāmapi raktābhrahemarasakādīnāmapi pākaṃ vahniyogena supakvakaraṇaṃ na vāñchanti te puruṣāḥ pūrvaṃ pakṣacchedaṃ jñātvā hṛṣyanti harṣayuktā bhavanti pakṣacchedaṃ vinānyakāryasiddhiṃ jñātvetyarthastadanu ca kāryāsiddhau tapyanti paritāpayuktā bhavanti || 3.26:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.27


itthamanekadoṣairbahuśramairgaganacāraṇaṃ matvā |
nirdiśyate prakāraḥ karmaṇi śāstre'pi saṃvādī || ṅṛht_3.27 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

gaganacāraṇaṃ nirdiśyānyasaṃskāraṃ stuvannāha itthamityādi || 3.27:1 ||
mayā granthakartrā asmin śāstre karmaṇyati saṃvādī prakāraḥ agrimaprakaraṇe sattvaniṣkāsanarūpaḥ nirdiśyate nirdeśaḥ kriyate || 3.27:2 ||
saṃvedyate saṃskriyate saṃskāravidbhiriti saṃvādī || 3.27:3 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā itthamuktaprakāreṇa anekairdoṣaiḥ anekakaṣṭaiḥ bahuśramairbahvāyāsairgaganacāraṇaṃ matvā abhrakacāraṇaṃ jñātvā || 3.27:4 ||
gaganacāraṇānantaramanyotkṛṣṭaprakāro nirdiśyate iti bhāvaḥ || 3.27:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht3.28


agrāhyo nirlepaḥ sūkṣmagatirvyāpako'kṣayo jīvaḥ |
yāvadviśati na yonau tāvadbandhaṃ kuto bhajate || ṅṛht_3.28 ||
