iti paramahaṃsaparivrājakācāryaśrīmadgovindabhagavatpādaviracite rasahṛdayākhye tantre nirmukhavāsanāmukhāntarbhūtasamukhapattrābhrakacaraṇātmakastṛtīyo'vabodhaḥ ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

prakārāntaraṃ darśayannāha agrāhya ityādi || 3.28:1 ||
evaṃvidho harajo yāvadyonau abhrake na viśati na milati yāvadbandhaṃ bandhanaṃ kuto bhajate prāpnoti na kuto'pi yonāvapraviśati sati na bandhanamāpnotītyarthaḥ || 3.28:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭo harajaḥ agrāhyaḥ haraḥ kathamapi na gṛhyate'navayavatvātharajastadguṇa eva kāraṇānurūpaṃ kāryamiti nyāyāt |
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭo nirlepaḥ vaikārikairdoṣaguṇairna lipyata iti doṣaguṇanivṛtteḥ || 3.28:3 ||
punaḥ sūkṣmagatiḥ sūkṣmā gatirgamanaṃ pravartanaṃ vā yasya saḥ dhūmarūpāvalokanatvāt || 3.28:4 ||
punarvyāpakaḥ dehalohādervyāpakatvāt || 3.28:5 ||
punarakṣayaḥ na kṣayo yasyetyakṣayaḥ sarvadā bhāvarūpatvāt || 3.28:6 ||
punarjīvaḥ ajīve prakāśatvābhāvaḥ svatvaṃ vihāya malinopādhikatvānnirupādhāvupādhisampattiriti tātparyārthaḥ || 3.28:7 ||
tadāśrayā tadviṣayā anādyavidyeti vedāntavacanāt || 3.28:8 ||
iti śrīmatkuralavaṃśapayodhisudhāramiśramaheśātmajacaturbhujaviracitāyāṃ mugdhāvabodhinyāṃ rasahṛdayaṭīkāyāṃ nirmukhavāsanāmukhāntarbhūtasamukhapattrādhrakacāraṇātmakastṛtīyo'vabodhaḥ || 3.28:9 ||



____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.1


kṛṣṇo raktaḥ pīto yo'bhraḥ syātsthūlatārakārahitaḥ |
vajrī sa pītakarmaṇi pātitasattvo ghano yojyaḥ || ṅṛht_4.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

adhunābhrasattvacāraṇaṃ tatra pītakarmaṇi gaganavarṇabhedānāha kṛṣṇa ityādi || 4.1:1 ||
kṛṣṇo ghano rakto ghanaḥ pīto ghanaśceti trividhaḥ pātitasattvaḥ pātitaṃ sattvaṃ yasyeti samāsaḥ || 4.1:2 ||
evaṃvidho vajrī pītakarmaṇi suvarṇakārye yojyaḥ || 4.1:3 ||
vajriṇo lakṣaṇaṃ dhmāto'pi haṭhāgnau saṃyojito'pi yaḥ sthūlatārakārahito bhavati sthūlāśca tāstārakāśca tābhī rahitaḥ dalasamuccayarūpāḥ sthūlatārakāḥ || 4.1:4 ||
anukto'pi śvetavarṇo ghanaḥ śvetakarmaṇi yojyaḥ granthāntarasāmyādayamabhiprāyaḥ || 4.1:5 ||
yathā granthāntare raktaṃ pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ śastaṃ hemakriyāsu gaganaṃ hi || 4.1:6 ||
tārakriyāsu śuklaṃ rasāyane sarvameva tu śreṣṭhamiti || 4.1:7 ||
abhre'pi vṛttikṛdviśeṣamāha kadācidgirijā devī haraṃ dṛṣṭvā manoharam || 4.1:8 ||
amocayattadā vīryaṃ tajjātaṃ śvetamabhrakam || 4.1:9 ||
śvetaṃ raktaṃ tathā pītaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ tadbhūmisaṃgamāt |
pinākaṃ darduraṃ nāgaṃ vajrābhraṃ ca caturvidham || 4.1:10 ||
dhmātaṃ vahnau dalacayaṃ pinākaṃ visṛjatyalam |
phūtkāraṃ bhujagaḥ kuryāddarduraṃ bhekaśabdavat || 4.1:11 ||
caturthaṃ khecaraṃ vajraṃ naivāgnau vikṛtiṃ bhajet |
tasmādvajrābhrakaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ vyādhivārdhakyamṛtyujititi || 4.1:12 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.2


niścandrikaṃ hi gaganaṃ vāsitamapi vāsanābhiriha śatadhā |
tadapi na carati rasendraḥ sattvaṃ kathamatra yatnataḥ prabhavet || ṅṛht_4.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

cāraṇāyāmabhrapattre vaiṣamyaṃ sattve ca sugamatvaṃ sūcayannāha niścandrikamityādi || 4.2:1 ||
ihāsmin cāraṇāsaṃskāre niścandrikaṃ gaganaṃ tārakārahitamabhraṃ vāsanābhiḥ pūrvoktābhirvāsanauṣadhibhiḥ śatadhā śataprakāraṃ vāsitaṃ mathitamapi rasendraḥ pāradastadapi bahuśramaiḥ saṃskṛtamapyabhraṃ na carati grāsīkaroti || 4.2:2 ||
atra cāraṇe sattvaṃ yatnataḥ prayatnātkathaṃ prabhavetkathamapi samarthībhavetsattvaṃ yatnataḥ samarthībhavedityarthaḥ || 4.2:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.3


muktvaikamabhrasattvaṃ nānyaḥ pakṣāpakartanasamarthaḥ |
tena niruddhaprasaro niyamyate badhyate ca sukham || ṅṛht_4.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sugamatvādguṇādhikatvācca sattvaṃ praśaṃsati muktvetyādi || 4.3:1 ||
abhrasattvamekaṃ muktvā tyaktvā anyo'paro rasapakṣāpakartanasamartho na pāradapakṣacchettā na tena sattvena sukhaṃ yathā syāttathā rasaḥ pārado niyamyate pāradasya niyamanaṃ bhavedityarthaḥ badhyate ca bandhanaṃ prāpyate raso baddho bhavatītyarthaḥ || 4.3:2 ||
kimbhūtaḥ sanniyamanaṃ bandhanaṃ ca pnoti niruddhaprasaraḥ sanniruddha ārundhittaḥ prasaraḥ prasaraṇaṃ prakṛṣṭena gamanaṃ yasya tādṛśaḥ san || 4.3:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.4


<pakṣaccheda>
pakṣacchedamakṛtvā rasabandhaṃ kartumīhate yastu |
bījaireva hi sa jaḍo vāñchatyajitendriyo mokṣam || ṅṛht_4.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rasabandhane'dhikāritvaṃ darśayannāha pakṣacchedamityādi || 4.4:1 ||
yo vādī abhrasattvagrasanena vinā pakṣacchedaṃ kṛtvā pakṣāpakartanamavidhāya rasabandhaṃ kartuṃ pāradabandhanaṃ vidhātumīhate ceṣṭate sa vādī na kiṃtu jaḍa evamapaṇḍita iti bhāvaḥ || 4.4:2 ||
kaḥ kṛtvā rasabandhanaṃ kartumīhate bījaiḥ raktābhrahemarasakairityādibhiḥ || 4.4:3 ||
evāvyayamanyadravyavyavacchedī || 4.4:4 ||
pakṣacchedanaṃ kṛtvā bandhanaṃ kāryamayaṃ vidhiḥ anyathā tvavidhiḥ || 4.4:5 ||
avidherdṛṣṭāntamāha yathā ajitendriyo lampaṭaḥ pumānmokṣaṃ vāñchati || 4.4:6 ||
ajitendriyajaḍayoḥ sāmyamiti || 4.4:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.5


nādhaḥ patati na cordhvaṃ tiṣṭhati yantre bhavedanudgārī |
abhrakajīrṇaḥ sūtaḥ pakṣacchinnaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ || ṅṛht_4.5 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pakṣacchinnapāradasya lakṣaṇamāha netyādi || 4.5:1 ||
evaṃvidhaḥ pāradaḥ sūto yaḥ sa pakṣacchinnaḥ pakṣau chinnau cheditau yasyeti samāsaḥ || 4.5:2 ||
sa kaḥ yo nādhaḥ patati adhaḥpātane kṛte ūrdhvato'dhobhāgo na patati punaradhobhāgata ūrdhvapātane kṛte ūrdhvaṃ na yāti anudgārī acañcalo bhavetyantre svastha eva tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ || 4.5:3 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ abhrajīrṇaḥ abhrakaṃ jīrṇaṃ niḥśeṣatāmāptaṃ yasmin rasa iti samāsaḥ || 4.5:4 ||
grāsamātreṇa pakṣacchedo na jāyate yāvanna cāritamabhrakaṃ jaratīti dhvanyarthaḥ || 4.5:5 ||
sa pakṣacchinnaḥ sūta iti || 4.5:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.6


<abhra:: subtypes>
śvetādicaturvarṇāḥ kathitāste sthūlatārakārahitāḥ |
<abhra:: subtypes:: reaction to heating>
vajrī satvaṃ muñcatyapare dhmātāśca kācatāṃ yānti || ṅṛht_4.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vajrābhraṃ vihāya pinākanāgabhekāḥ satvamocane'samarthā iti darśayannāha śvetetyādi || 4.6:1 ||
ye dhmātāḥ sthūlatārakārahitāḥ pattracayena varjitā raktapītakṛṣṇāḥ kathitāḥ pūrvaṃ varṇitāste śvetādicaturvarṇā bhavanti || 4.6:2 ||
trayāṇāṃ raktapītakṛṣṇavarṇābhrāṇāṃ cetśvetavarṇa ādau yujyate tadā caturvarṇā bhavantītyarthaḥ || 4.6:3 ||
teṣāṃ caturvarṇānāṃ madhye yo vajrī vajrasaṃjñako ghanaḥ sa satvaṃ muñcati dhmātaḥ san satvaṃ tyajati nānye || 4.6:4 ||
apare pinākanāgabhekāhvayāḥ dhmātāḥ santaḥ kācatāṃ yānti kācākāratvamāpnuvanti na ca sattvanirgama iti || 4.6:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.7


<abhra:: subtypes:: potency>
sitaraktāsitapītā ye kecidudāhṛtā ghanā loke |
alpabalā niḥsattvā vajrī śreṣṭhastu sarveṣām || ṅṛht_4.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sasattvabalavatvābhyāmutkṛṣṭatvādvajrābhraṃ punaḥ praśaṃsati sitetyādi || 4.7:1 ||
loke saṃsāre ye kecitghanā udāhṛtāḥ kathitāste alpabalāḥ alpaṃ balaṃ yeṣu te tathoktāḥ || 4.7:2 ||
niḥsattvāḥ satvavarjitāḥ dhmāteṣu teṣu satvābhāva ityarthaḥ || 4.7:3 ||
te ke ghanāḥ sitaraktāsitapītāḥ śvetaraktakṛṣṇapītavarṇāḥ nānye varṇāḥ santīti bhāvaḥ || 4.7:4 ||
sarveṣāṃ caturvarṇānāṃ madhye vajrī vajrasaṃjñakaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ pradhānaḥ || 4.7:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.8


<abhra:: inedible variants>
sūte'pi rasāyanināṃ yojyaṃ parikīrtitaṃ paraṃ satvam |
trividhaṃ gaganamabhakṣyaṃ kācaṃ kiṭṭaṃ ca pattrarajaḥ || ṅṛht_4.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

deyamabhramāha sūte'pītyādi || 4.8:1 ||
rasāyanināṃ rasāyanaṃ jarāvyādhividhvaṃsibheṣajaṃ vidyate yeṣāṃ yeṣu vā te rasāyaninaḥ teṣāṃ paraṃ pradhānaṃ satvamabhrasatvaṃ parikīrtitaṃ saṃkathitaṃ yathā granthāntare |
satvasevī vayaḥstambhaṃ kṛtaśuddhirlabhetsudhīḥ |
iti || 4.8:2 ||
abhrasatvaṃ sūte'pi pārade'pi paramamutkṛṣṭaṃ pakṣacchedanasamarthaṃ baladaṃ ca || 4.8:3 ||
punastrividhaṃ gaganamabhakṣyamabhojyaṃ rasāyanināṃ sūte'pi || 4.8:4 ||
kiṃ tattrividhamekaṃ kācaṃ vahnau dhamanātkācākāratāṃ nītaṃ dvitīyaṃ kiṭṭaṃ yaddhamanātkiṭṭasvarūpaṃ prāptaṃ tṛtīyaṃ pattrarajaḥ patrāṇāṃ samāhitaṃ yadrajastadevaṃ trividhamabhakṣyaṃ sadoṣatvāt || 4.8:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.9


<abhra:: sattva>
muñcati satvaṃ dhmātastṛṇasāravikārakairghanaḥ svinnaḥ |
parihṛtya kācakiṭṭaṃ grāhyaṃ sāraṃ prayatnena || ṅṛht_4.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

satvapātanavidhānaṃ darśayannāha muñcatītyādi || 4.9:1 ||
ghanastṛṇasāravikārakaiḥ svinnaḥ tṛṇameva sāro yeṣāṃ te tṛṇasārāḥ teṣāṃ ye vikārakā viśeṣāstairauṣadhaiḥ sūryāvartakādibhiḥ kṛtvā svinnau vahnau dhmāto ghanaḥ satvaṃ muñcati sattvapātaṃ vidadhāti || 4.9:2 ||
punaḥ kācaṃ kiṭṭaṃ ca parihṛtya sattvaṃ patitakācakiṭṭayuktaṃ yadā bhavati tadā prayatnena grāhyamityarthaḥ || 4.9:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.10


<abhra:: sattva:: pātana>
svedya baddhvā piṇḍaṃ māhiṣadadhidugdhamūtraśakṛdājyaiḥ |
atha paṃcagavyayuktaḥ satvaṃ pātayati lohanibham || ṅṛht_4.10 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

adhunā viśeṣaprakārāntaramāha svedya ityādi |
svedyo ghanaḥ pūrvoktaistṛṇasāravikāraiḥ sveditamabhraṃ svedavidhiruktaḥ punarghanasya piṇḍaṃ baddhvā kaiḥ saha māhiṣadadhidugdhamūtraśakṛdājyaiḥ kṛtvā mahiṣyā idaṃ māhiṣamevaṃbhūtaṃ yaddadhi dugdhaṃ mūtraṃ śakṛdviṣṭhā ājyaṃ ghṛtaṃ caitaiḥ piṇḍaṃ baddhvā || 4.10:1 ||
atheti samuccaye || 4.10:2 ||
pañcagavyaiḥ gavāṃ dugdhadadhimūtraśakṛdājyaiḥ saṃyuktaḥ kāryaḥ piṇḍaṃ baddhvetyarthaḥ || 4.10:3 ||
etatpiṇḍaṃ lohanibhaṃ muṇḍaprabhaṃ sattvaṃ pātayati pūrvasaṃbadhātghanasya ityadhyāhāraḥ || 4.10:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.11


<sattva:: extractfroṃ coolminerals>
sūryātapapītarasāḥ svalpaṃ muñcanti dhātavaḥ satvam |
svasthānasthāḥ santo muñcanti ta eva bhūyiṣṭham || ṅṛht_4.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sūryātapapītarasā iti sūryātape savitṛgharme pītāḥ śoṣitā rasā dravā yaiḥ evaṃvidhā dhātavo dhmātāḥ santaḥ svalpamīṣanmātraṃ sattvaṃ muñcanti tyajanti || 4.11:1 ||
punasta eva sūryātapapītarasā dhātavaḥ svasthānasthāḥ svakīyaṃ yatsthānaṃ draveṇa sthānapiṇḍaṃ rūpaṃ tasmin tiṣṭhantīti evaṃvidhāḥ santo bahalaṃ bhūyiṣṭhaṃ satvaṃ muñcanti dravantītyarthaḥ || 4.11:2 ||
sūryātapapītarasā iti keṣāṃ sūryāvartakadalīvandhyākarkoṭakyādīnāṃ drāvakauṣadhīnām || 4.11:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.12


bahugambhīraṃ dhmāto varṣati meghaḥ suvarṇadhārābhiḥ |
devamukhatulyamamalaṃ patitaṃ satvaṃ tathā vindyāt || ṅṛht_4.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

patitasattvalakṣaṇamāha bahugambhīramityādi || 4.12:1 ||
megho ghano bahugambhīraṃ yathā syāttathā dhmātaḥ san suvarṇadhārābhiḥ śobhanavarṇadhārābhiḥ vā suvarṇavatkanakavatvarṇo yāsāṃ tābhiḥ nirmalatvātprakāśakatvācca varṣati dhārāpātaṃ vidadhāti || 4.12:2 ||
kathaṃbhūtaṃ devamukhatulyaṃ vahninā tulyaṃ samamamalaṃ nirmalaṃ haritapītaraktādidhūmarahitatvātpatitaṃ satvaṃ tathā vindyātghanasyetyarthaḥ || 4.12:3 ||
tathā granthāntare |
bhastrānalena tīvreṇa mahājvāle hutāśane |
atidīpte bhavedbuddhā aṅgārāḥ kṣayamāgatāḥ || 4.12:4 ||
punaranyā na cenmūrdhni dvistrirvārāṇi buddhimān |
yadā dīpto bhavedvahniḥ śuddhajvālo mahābalaḥ || 4.12:5 ||
patitaṃ tu tadā vindyāttatsattvaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
patitaṃ tu pṛthakkāryaṃ kiṭṭāṅgāravivarjitam || 4.12:6 ||
nirguṇaṃ lakṣayitvā tu punardhāmyo yathāvatā |
dvistrirevamaśeṣaṃ tu dhmātaḥ sattvaṃ vimuñcati || 4.12:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.13-14


<abhra:: sattva:: "eaten" withouta mukha>
yadi lohanibhaṃ patitaṃ jātaṃ gaganasya tadrasaścarati |
milati ca sarvadvandve hyauṣadhibhiścarati vināpi mukhaiḥ || ṅṛht_4.13 ||
<abhra:: sattva:: producesa mukha>
mākṣikasahitaṃ gaganaṃ dhmātaṃ satvaṃ mukhapradaṃ bhavati |
tadanu ca nāgairvaṅgaiḥ sahitaṃ ca mukhapradaṃ satvam || ṅṛht_4.14 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mākṣikasatvāntarasaṃyogātsattvasya guṇādhikyaṃ darśayannāha mākṣiketyādi || 4.13-14:1 ||
gagane bhavaṃ gaganamaṇpratyayāntaṃ ṇito vā iti sūtreṇa na vṛddhiḥ gaganamabhrasatvam || 4.13-14:2 ||
mākṣike bhavaṃ mākṣikaṃ mākṣikasatvaṃ tena sahitaṃ pūrvasatvaṃ cetdhmātaṃ tadubhayasatvaṃ mukhapradaṃ bhavati rasasya iti śeṣaḥ || 4.13-14:3 ||
mukhaṃ prakṛṣṭena dadātīti samāsaḥ || 4.13-14:4 ||
tadanu ca tatpaścācca nāgairvaṅgaiḥ sahitaṃ pūrvaṃ yadgaganamabhrasatvaṃ mukhapradamityarthaḥ || 4.13-14:5 ||
vā sukhapradamiti pāṭhaḥ asmin yoge bahukleśaṃ vihāya sukhena cāraṇaṃ bhavatīti vikalpārthaḥ || 4.13-14:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.15
<abhra:: sattva:: inductionof garbhadruti, rañjana andbandhana>
mākṣikasatve yogādghanasatvaṃ carati sūtako nikhilam |
niyataṃ garbhadrāvī sa rajyate badhyate caivam || ṅṛht_4.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mākṣikasatvaṃ mukhyatvenāha mākṣiketyādi || 4.15:1 ||
sūtakaḥ pārado ghanasatvamabhrasatvaṃ nikhilaṃ samastaṃ carati || 4.15:2 ||
kasmātmākṣikasatve yogātmākṣikaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ tatsatve yo yogastasmāt || 4.15:3 ||
niyataṃ niścitam || 4.15:4 ||
mākṣikasattvayogādghanasattvaṃ carati raso garbhadrāvī garbhe drāvayati sattvaṃ dravarūpaṃ vidhatte yaḥ sa tathoktaḥ || 4.15:5 ||
sūtasyodare mākṣikābhrasatvaṃ drutirūpaṃ tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ || 4.15:6 ||
evamamunā vidhānena saha sūtakaḥ pārado rajyate rāgavān bhavati badhyate baddhaśca bhavatītyarthaḥ || 4.15:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.16


<abhra:: sattva:: forbandhana of mercury>
satvaṃ ghanasya kāntaṃ tālakayuktaṃ surundhitaṃ dhmātam |
vāraistribhiriha satvaṃ bhavati rasendrabandhakāri param || ṅṛht_4.16 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mākṣikayogānantaramaparo'bhidhīyate sattvamityādi || 4.16:1 ||
ghanasyābhrasya satvaṃ tathā kāntaṃ lohaviśeṣaṃ tālakayuktaṃ tālakena haritālena yuktaṃ surundhitaṃ dhmātaṃ sattrayamapi satvarūpaṃ bhavati yadaikavāradhamanena satvaṃ na milati tathā punardvistrivelābhirdhamanaṃ kāryam || 4.16:2 ||
samabhāgatālakayojanaṃ ghanasatvamākṣikasatvayogadrāvaṇāddhamitādatyarthaṃ tatsatvaṃ rakhe pārade bandhakāri bhavati paramamutkṛṣṭaṃ bandhanapradaṃ bhavati || 4.16:3 ||
tatsattvasya cāraṇato raso bandhanamavāpnotīti bhāvaḥ || 4.16:4 ||
kāntalakṣaṇam |
pātre yasmin praviśati jale tailabindurna sarpethiṅgurgandhaṃ visṛjati nijaṃ tiktatāṃ nimbakalkaḥ |
pāke dugdhaṃ bhavati śikharākāratāṃ naiva bhūmau kāntaṃ lohaṃ viduriti ca tallakṣaṇoktaṃ na cānyat |
iti || 4.16:5 ||
____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.17


<abhra:: sattva:: inductionof garbhadruti>
lohaṃ cābhrakasatvaṃ tālakasamabhāgasāritaṃ carati |
abhiṣavayogāccāṅgulimṛditaṃ garbhe ca taddravati || ṅṛht_4.17 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

kāntābhrasatvālayogakaraṇamāha lohamityādi || 4.17:1 ||
ceti samuccaye || 4.17:2 ||
lohaṃ pūrvoktalakṣaṇaṃ muṇḍādikamabhrasatvaṃ ca tālakasamabhāgasāritaṃ tālakasya samabhāgena pūrvavidhānena mukhādinā yatsāritamekaśarīratāṃ nītaṃ sṛ gatāvityasya dhāto rūpaṃ sāritaṃ pramilitamityarthaḥ evaṃvidhaṃ kāntābhrasatvālaṃ rasaścarati || 4.17:3 ||
ca punaḥ aṅgulimṛditamaṅgulinā marditaṃ tatkāntābhrasatvālaṃ garbhe rasodare dravati tatsvarūpatvena milati || 4.17:4 ||
kasmātabhiṣavayogātabhiṣavaḥ saṃmardanaṃ tadyogāt || 4.17:5 ||
lohasya trayo daśabhedānāṃ madhyātkenāpi bhedena sahayogaḥ kārya iti lohaśabdena dhvanitam || 4.17:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.18


<abhra:: sattva:: inductionof cāraṇa>
vaṃgamatho ghanasatvaṃ tālakaṣaḍbhāgasāritaṃ carati |
abhiṣavayogāccarati vrajati raso nātra sandehaḥ || ṅṛht_4.18 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

lohayogamuktvā vaṅgayogamāha vaṅgamityādi || 4.18:1 ||
atha lohakathanānantaraṃ vaṅgaṃ khurasaṃjñakamabhrakaṃ ca etaddvayaṃ tālakaṣaḍbhāgasāritaṃ tālakasya ṣaḍaṃśena ekaśarīratāṃ nītaṃ tatsvarūpaṃ rasaścarati || 4.18:2 ||
kasmātabhiṣavayogātabhiṣavaḥ saṃmardanaṃ tadyogātṣuñabhiṣave ityasya dhāto rūpamabhiṣavaḥ || 4.18:3 ||
punaḥ rasaścarati milati || 4.18:4 ||
cāritaṃ yattaddravati taddrutaṃ rase vrajati pṛthaktvātsaṃ milati nātrasandehaḥ niḥ saṃdigdhamiva || 4.18:5 ||
vaṅgasya laghudravitvātṣaḍaṃśayogastālakasya yuktaḥ lohajāteḥ kāṭhiṇyātsamabhāgatvamuditam || 4.18:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.19-20


<abhra:: sattva:: inductionof cāraṇa>
bahalaṃ suvarṇavarṇaṃ niculapuṭaiḥ patati pañcabhiḥ satvam |
vaṭakīkṛtamṛtagaganaṃ nirañjanaṃ kiṭṭarahitaṃ ca || ṅṛht_4.19 ||
taccūrṇīkṛtya tataḥ kṣārāmlairbhāvitaṃ ghanaṃ bahuśaḥ |
sṛṣṭitrayanīrakaṇātumbururasamarditaṃ carati || ṅṛht_4.20 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

abhrasatvavidhānamāha bahalamityādi || 4.19-20:1 ||
vaṭakīkṛtaṃ ca tanmṛtaṃ ca gaganaṃ tathoktaṃ na vaṭako vaṭakaḥ kriyata iti vaṭakīkṛtamatra abhūtatadbhāve cviḥpratyayaḥ || 4.19-20:2 ||
pañcabhirniculapuṭaiḥ pañcasaṃkhyābhirvetasavṛkṣadravabhāvanābhirbhāvitaṃ yanmṛtagaganaṃ mṛtābhraṃ vaṭakīkṛtaṃ satsatvaṃ patati taddrāvakauṣadhayogaṃ vidhāya vahninā vidhamanāditi śeṣaḥ || 4.19-20:3 ||
kiyanmānaṃ satvaṃ patati bahalaṃ bahu anyavidheradhikam || 4.19-20:4 ||
kīdṛśaṃ svarṇavarṇaṃ pītaśvetaṃ prakāśākhyaṃ punarnirañjanaṃ nirmalaṃ kiṭṭarahitaṃ ca || 4.19-20:5 ||
asmin satve kācakiṭṭābhāvaḥ || 4.19-20:6 ||
sarvotkṛṣṭavidhirayaṃ pūrvamuditātbahalasatvapātādityarthaḥ || 4.19-20:7 ||
kevalasatvacāraṇavidhānamāha tadityādi || 4.19-20:8 ||
tatastadabhrasatvapātanavidheranantaraṃ tadevābhrasatvaṃ cūrṇīkṛtya kalkaṃ vidhāya kṣārāmlairbhāvitaṃ kuryātkṣārāḥ svarjikādayaḥ amlā jambīrādayaḥ tairbahuśo bahuvāraṃ gharmapuṭitaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 4.19-20:9 ||
punaḥ sṛṣṭitrayanīrakaṇātumbururasamarditaṃ sṛṣṭiḥ pūrvoktā tattrayaṃ mūtraśukraśoṇitamiti nīrakaṇā jalapippalī tumbaru pratītameteṣāṃ rasena marditaṃ kuryāt || 4.19-20:10 ||
abhrasattvamevaṃ kṛtaṃ satcarati grasati rasaḥ ityadhyāhāraḥ || 4.19-20:11 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.21-22


<śulvābhra:: production>
ghanasatvaśulbamākṣikasamabhāganiyojitaṃ tathā militam |
tacchulbābhraṃ kathitaṃ carati raso jīryati kṣipram || ṅṛht_4.21 ||
iti tāpyaśulbasahitaṃ ghanasatvaṃ lohakhalvake mṛditam |
carati rasendraḥ kāñjikavetasajambīrabījapūrāmlaiḥ || ṅṛht_4.22 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha śulbābhramāha ghanetyādi || 4.21-22:1 ||
ghanasatvaśulbamākṣikasamabhāganiyojitaṃ ghanasatvamabhrasatvaṃ śulbaṃ tāmraṃ mākṣikaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ samabhāgena tulyabhāgena niyojitaṃ prayuktaṃ tanmilitaṃ satśulbābhraṃ kathitam || 4.21-22:2 ||
atra śulbaṃ kīdṛśaṃ prayojyaṃ tadāha |
dvāvarkau mlecchanepālau rase nepāla uttamaḥ |
ghanaghātasahaḥ snigdho raktapatro'malo mṛduḥ || 4.21-22:3 ||
mlecchastu kṣālitaḥ kṛṣṇo rūkṣasnigdho ghanāsahaḥ |
miśrito nāgalohābhyāṃ na śreṣṭho rasakarmaṇi |
iti ato nepālakaṃ grāhyamityarthaḥ || 4.21-22:4 ||
tacchulvābhraṃ rasaḥ kṣipraṃ carati punaḥ kṣipraṃ śīghraṃ tacchulbaṃ jīryati jāraṇamāpnoti || 4.21-22:5 ||
atra mākṣikayogaḥ śulbābhrasatvamelanārthaṃ rasaprītyeti bhāvaḥ || 4.21-22:6 ||
śulbābhracāraṇavidhānamāha itītyādi || 4.21-22:7 ||
iti pūrvoktaṃ tāpyaśulbasahitaṃ tāpyaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ śulbaṃ tāmraṃ nepālasaṃjñakaṃ tābhyāṃ sahitaṃ miśritaṃ ghanasatvaṃ taptalohakhalvake mṛditaṃ kāryaṃ mardanīyaṃ kaiḥ kṛtvā kāñjikavetasajambīrabījapūrāmlaiḥ kāñjikamuktavidhānaṃ sauvīraṃ vetasaṃ cukraṃ jambīraṃ prasiddhaṃ bījapūro mātuluṅgaḥ eteṣāmamlaiḥ dravarūpaiḥ || 4.21-22:8 ||
tataḥ śulvābhraṃ rasendraḥ pāradaścarati grasati || 4.21-22:9 ||
lohakhalvasya kathanāttaptakhalvake mardanaṃ kuryāditi tātparyārthaḥ || 4.21-22:10 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.23


<bhasman (of diff. metals):: rañjana>
iti tīkṣṇaśulbanāgaṃ mākṣikayuktaṃ ca tatkṛtaṃ khoṭam |
tadbhasma ca puṭavidhinā nirvyūḍhaṃ satvarañjakaṃ bhavati || ṅṛht_4.23 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

prakārāntaramāha itītyādi || 4.23:1 ||
iti pūrvoktalakṣaṇaṃ tīkṣṇaśulbanāgaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ tāmraṃ nāgaṃ ca etattrayaṃ mākṣikayuktaṃ svarṇamākṣikayutaṃ samaṃ tulyabhāgaṃ kuryāt || 4.23:2 ||
tatkṛtaṃ khoṭaṃ taiḥ sarvaiḥ piṣṭīstambhena khoṭabhasma kāryam || 4.23:3 ||
puṭavidhinā vahnipuṭavidhānena tatkṛtaṃ khoṭaṃ bhasma kāryaṃ punaḥ tadbhasma satve nirvyūḍhaṃ nirvāhitaṃ satsatvarañjakaṃ bhavati khasatve abhrasatve rāgadāyi bhavati rañjitaṃ tatsatvaṃ rasarañjakaṃ bhavediti || 4.23:4 ||
____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.24


cāryaṃ yatnena rase ghanasatvaṃ tadvidhaṃ ghanaṃ tasya |
saṃyojya sarvabījaṃ nirvāhya dvandvasaṃkarataḥ || ṅṛht_4.24 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rañjitaghanasatvasya cāraṇamāha cāryamityādi || 4.24:1 ||
tadvidhaṃ pūrvarañjitaṃ ghanasatvamabhrasatvaṃ ca ghanaṃ kevalaghanodbhavaṃ satvamarañjitaṃ rase pārade cāryaṃ grāsagrasanamānenaitadabhrasatvaṃ rase saṃyojyam || 4.24:2 ||
vā satve sarvabījaṃ sarvaṃ rāgadāyi dravyaṃ saṃyojya nirvāhyaṃ nirvāhitaṃ kuryāt || 4.24:3 ||
kutaḥ dvandvasaṃkarataḥ dvandvānāṃ saṃkaro melāpaḥ saṅkaro'vakara ityamaraḥ || 4.24:4 ||
nirvāhyamiti vaha prāpaṇe ityasya rūpaṃ pūrvavat || 4.24:5 ||
niravyayaṃ niścayārthaṃ nirniścayaniṣedhayoḥ ityamaraḥ || 4.24:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.25


abhrakacāraṇamādau garbhadruticāraṇaṃ ca hemno'nte |
yo jānāti na vādī vṛthaiva so'rthakṣayaṃ kurute || ṅṛht_4.25 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha rasacāraṇe jñeyamāha abhraketyādi || 4.25:1 ||
yo vādī rasakartā ādau prathamamabhracāraṇaṃ na jānāti yathā raso'bhrakaṃ carati grasati punaḥ tatpaścātgarbhadruticāraṇaṃ yadrasagarbhe drutaṃ dravarūpaṃ tiṣṭhatyabhrādikaṃ tasya cāraṇaṃ grasanaṃ punarante hemnaḥ svarṇasya cāraṇaṃ grasanaṃ na jānāti sa vṛthaiva mithyaiva arthakṣayaṃ dhananāśaṃ kurute kāryasiddherabhāvāt || 4.25:2 ||
sarvajña eva rasakriyāyāṃ pravarteteti bhāvaḥ || 4.25:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht4.26

<abhra:: sattva:: grāsa:: onlyabhra partis"eaten">
gaganagrāsarahasyaṃ vakṣyāmyekaṃ ghanārkasaṃyogāt |
kevalamabhrakasatvaṃ grasate yatnānna sarvāṅgam || ṅṛht_4.26 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

grāsarahasyamāha gaganetyādi || 4.26:1 ||
ahaṃ śrīmadgovindabhagavatpūjyapādācāryaḥ ekamadvitīyaṃ gaganagrāsarahasyamabhrakakavalane grāsakautukaṃ vakṣyāmi kathayāmi || 4.26:2 ||
kasmātghanārkasaṃyogātghanamabhrakasatvamarkastāmrametayoḥ saṃyogaḥ tasmātubhayasatvakṛtakhoṭaṃ cāryam || 4.26:3 ||
athaśabdaḥ pakṣāntarasūcakaḥ || 4.26:4 ||
abhrasatvasya yasya dhāto rūpaṃ tena saha yasya dhātorvā saṃyogo bhavati dvandvabhāvātsaṅkarataḥ tatsaṃyuktamabhidhānaṃ bhavati yathā śulvābhraṃ nāgābhraṃ vaṅgābhraṃ mākṣikābhraṃ hemābhramiti evaṃ sarvatra saṃyogānnāmaniṣpattiḥ || 4.26:5 ||
grastasya drāvaṇaṃ garbhe garbhadrutirudāhṛtā bahireva dravīkṛtya ghanasatvādikaṃ khalu |
jāraṇāya rasendrasya sā bāhyadrutirucyate || 4.26:6 ||



____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.1


yadi ghanasatvaṃ garbhe na patati no vā dravanti bījāni |
na ca bāhyadrutiyogastatkathamiha badhyate sūtaḥ || ṅṛht_5.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vāco marīcibhistaptaṃ toṣaya janakairavam |
kumudāni ca kāsārodvartīni cirabhāsayā || 5.1:1 ||
atha garbhadrutibāhyadrutipraśaṃsanamāha yadītyādi || 5.1:2 ||
yadi cedghanasatvamabhrasatvaṃ garbhe pāradasyāntarna patati dravatvaṃ nāpnoti vā bījāni śulbābhrādīni pāradasyodare no dravanti na rasarūpā bhavanti ca punaḥ bāhyadrutirna yujyate cedevaṃ na syāttarhi iha asyāṃ kriyāyāṃ prāptāyāṃ satyāṃ sūto rasaḥ kathaṃ badhyate anyathā na ko'pyupāyaḥ || 5.1:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.2


garbhadrutyā rahito grāsaścīrṇo'pi naikatāṃ yāti |
ekībhāvena vinā na jīryate tena sā kāryā || ṅṛht_5.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

garbhadruterādhikyaṃ darśayannāha garbhetyādi || 5.2:1 ||
cīrṇo'pi grāsaḥ cāraṇatāṃ prāpto'pi kavalaḥ yadi garbhadrutyā rahito bhavetrasasyodare rasarūpakaraṇavarjito bhavettadā ekatāṃ na yāti rasarūpo na bhavati || 5.2:2 ||
punarekībhāvena vinā grāso na jīryate jāraṇatvaṃ nāpnoti || 5.2:3 ||
tena hetunā garbhadrutipūrvikā jāraṇā kāryā rasabandhe jāraṇaṃ heturiti bhāvaḥ || 5.2:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.3


bījānāṃ saṃskāraḥ kartavyaḥ ko'pi tādṛśaḥ prathamam |
yena dravanti garbhe rasarājasyāmlavargeṇa || ṅṛht_5.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

jāraṇāyāṃ prathamaṃ kartavyamāha bījānāmityādi || 5.3:1 ||
bījānāṃ śulbābhrādīnāṃ ko'pyanirvacanīyaḥ saṃskāro garbhe drutikārakaḥ prathamaṃ kartavyaḥ saṃskriyata iti saṃskāraḥ || 5.3:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ tādṛśaḥ taiḥ bījaiḥ sadṛśaḥ yathā bījāni śaktimanti santi tathā saṃskāro'pi śaktimān kartavya ityarthaḥ || 5.3:3 ||
saṃskāraḥ kena kartavyaḥ amlavargeṇa jambīrādinā || 5.3:4 ||
yena saṃskāreṇa rasarājasya garbhe rasodare bījāni dhātūpadhātujātāni śulbābhrādīni dravantītyarthaḥ || 5.3:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.4


<garbhadrutiyogyabīja (1)>
samamākṣikakṛtavāpaṃ samamākṣikasatvasaṃyutaṃ hema |
garbhe dravati ca jarati ca jaritaṃ badhnāti nānyathā sūtam || ṅṛht_5.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

hemabījapraśaṃsanamāha sametyādi || 5.4:1 ||
samamākṣikakṛtavāpaṃ samabhāgaṃ tulyāṃśaṃ hemnā yanmākṣikaṃ tasya kṛtvā vāpo vāraṃ vāramākṣepo'gniyogādyasmin tathoktaṃ punastaddhema samamākṣikasatvasaṃyutaṃ hemnā samaṃ tulyaṃ yanmākṣikasatvaṃ tena saṃyutaṃ kṛtakhoṭaṃ kuryāt || 5.4:2 ||
tatkhoṭarūpaṃ hema garbhe pāradāntardravati || 5.4:3 ||
punastaddrāvitaṃ hema jarati jīrṇatāmāpnoti || 5.4:4 ||
taddhema jaritaṃ satsūtaṃ raseśvaraṃ badhnāti || 5.4:5 ||
anyathā anyaprakāreṇa sūto bandhanaṃ nāpnoti || 5.4:6 ||
mākṣikasya vāpo hemno varṇotkarṣaprada iti bhāvaḥ || 5.4:7 ||

____________________________________________________
ṅṛht5.5


<tārāriṣṭa>
mākṣikasatvaṃ hemnā pādādikajāritaṃ drutaṃ sūte |
tārāriṣṭaṃ kurute varakanakaṃ pattralepena || ṅṛht_5.5 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mākṣikasatvacāraṇādrase guṇotkarṣamāha mākṣikasatvamityādi || 5.5:1 ||
mākṣikasatvaṃ vahnyauṣadhayogadrutaṃ yaddhemamākṣikasāraṃ hemnā kanakena saha sūte pārade pūrvaṃ yadgutaṃ punaḥ pādādikajāritaṃ pādādikavibhāgena pādārdhasatvena niḥśeṣatāmāptaṃ satayaṃ sūtaḥ tārāriṣṭaṃ tāraṃ rūpyādi ariṣṭaṃ śubhaṃ varakanakaṃ kurute pūrṇavarṇamityarthaḥ || 5.5:2 ||
kena vidhānena pattralepanena patraṃ kaṇṭakabhedi tatra yo'sau lepaḥ vahniyogāditi śeṣaḥ tena || 5.5:3 ||
hemamākṣikasatvajāritasya rūpyapatralepena kanakaṃ syāditi vyaktārthaḥ || 5.5:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.6


<garbhadruti:: testfor~>
samarasatāṃ yadi yāto vastrādgalito'dhikaśca tulanāyām |
grāso drutaḥ sa garbhe drutvāsau jīryate kṣipram || ṅṛht_5.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

garbhadruterlakṣaṇamāha sametyādi || 5.6:1 ||
yadi grāsaḥ samarasatāṃ yāto bhavedrasatulyarūpatāṃ prāpto bhavetpunarvastrādgalito bhavetcaturguṇaśvetavastrānniḥsṛto bhavetpunastulanāyāṃ tulākarmaṇi yadādhiko'pi syāttadā garbhe pāradasyāntardruto grāso jñātavyaḥ garbhadruto raso veditavya iti vyaktārthaḥ || 5.6:2 ||
punarasau raso drutvā dravarūpaṃ śīghraṃ prāpto jīryati dhātūnapi vidhāneneti śeṣaḥ || 5.6:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.7


<ṛolle von tāpyasattva>
na biḍairnāpi kṣārairna snehairdravati hemaṃ tāraṃ vā |
mākṣikasatvena vinā tridinaṃ nihitena raktena || ṅṛht_5.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mākṣikasatvotkarṣamāha netyādi || 5.7:1 ||
mākṣikasatvena vinā svarṇamākṣikasāramantareṇa hema kanakaṃ vā tāraṃ rūpyaṃ na dravati || 5.7:2 ||
kaiḥ kṛtvā viḍaiḥ kṛtvā śaṅkhacūrṇārkakṣārādikṛtapiṇḍaiḥ kṛtvā granthāntare ca |
lavaṇakṣāroparasairebhiramlairbiḍo mataḥ |
same garbhe tu saṃsthāpyo hyanenaiva dravībhavet |
iti || 5.7:3 ||
na kevalaṃ biḍaiḥ kiṃtu kṣārairapi na kṣāraiḥ svarjikāyavakṣāraṭaṅkaṇādyaiḥ || 5.7:4 ||
na kevalaṃ kṣāraiḥ kiṃtu snehairapi na snehāni yathā |
kaṅguṇikaṃ vinādoṣākarañjaśrīphalodbhavam |
kaṭuvātārisiddhārthasomarājīvibhītajam |
atasījaṃ mahākālanimbajaṃ tilajaṃ tathā |
apāmārgadevadālīdantītumbaravigrahā |
aṅkolonmattabhallātaphalebhyastailasaṃbhavaḥ |
iti || 5.7:5 ||
tailairapi na dravati |
punarmākṣikasatvena garbhe drutirjāyate || 5.7:6 ||
kiṃviśeṣeṇa mākṣikasatvena raktena raktavargeṇa || 5.7:7 ||
tridinaṃ dinatrayaṃ nihitena raktavargāntaḥsthāpitena || 5.7:8 ||
raktavargo yathā |
dāḍimaṃ kiṃśukaṃ caiva bandhūkaṃ ca kusumbhakam |
samāñjiṣṭho haridrādyo lākṣārasasamanvitaḥ |
raktacandanasaṃyukto raktavargaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
iti || 5.7:9 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.8-12


<mercury:: cāraṇa, garbhadruti>
lavaṇaṃ devīsvarasaplutamahipatraṃ vicūrṇitaṃ śilayā |
etatpuṭanatritayātsumṛtaṃ saṃsthāpayedayaḥpātre || ṅṛht_5.8 ||
vihitārdhāṃgulanimnā sphuṭavikaṭakaṭorikā mukhādhārā |
tasyoparyādeyā kaṭorikā cāṅgulotsedhā || ṅṛht_5.9 ||
vihitacchidratritayā śastā caturaṃgulordhvachidreṣu |
lohaśalākā yojyāstatrāpi ca hemapatrāṇi || ṅṛht_5.10 ||
saṃsthāpya vidhūpyante yantrādhastātpradīpayedagnim |
dhūmopalepamātrādbhavanti kṛṣṇāni hemapatrāṇi || ṅṛht_5.11 ||
tānyagnitāpitāni ca paścādyantre mṛtāni dhūmena |
pācitahemavidhānāccarati rasendro dravati garbhe ca || ṅṛht_5.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

svarṇajāraṇayantravidhānamāha lavaṇamityādi || 5.8-12:1 ||
prathamaṃ lavaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ devīsvarasaplutaṃ kuryātbrāhmīsvakīyarasena saṃmiśraṃ kuryāt || 5.8-12:2 ||
punarbrāhmīrasaplutaṃ lavaṇaṃ ca ahipatraṃ tāmbūlidalaṃ tacca dvayaṃ śilayā vitatagrāveṇa cūrṇitaṃ peṣitaṃ kuryāt || 5.8-12:3 ||
punaretadayaḥpātre lohabhājane saṃsthāpayet || 5.8-12:4 ||
etatsthāpitaṃ dravyaṃ puṭatritayāttripuṭakaraṇādagnisaṃyogena sumṛtaṃ syāt || 5.8-12:5 ||
tasya devīsvarasaplutasya sumṛtasaindhavasya mukhādhārā sphuṭavikaṭakaṭorikā pātrī kāryā mukhameva ādhāro yasyāḥ sā evaṃvidhā sphuṭā prakaṭā vikaṭā viparītā adhomukhetyarthaḥ sā kaṭorikā vihitā kāryā ayaḥpātrasya || 5.8-12:6 ||
tasyāḥ kaṭorikāyā upari ūrdhvabhāge eṣā kaṭorikā ādeyā sthāpyā || 5.8-12:7 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭā aṅgulotsedhā aṅgula utsedhaḥ parimāṇaṃ yasyāḥ sā tathoktā || 5.8-12:8 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭā ardhāṅgulanimnā ardhāṅgulaparimāṇanimnā madhyagā || 5.8-12:9 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭā vihitachidratritayā vihitāni kṛtāni chidratritayāni yasyāṃ sā evaṃvidhā śastā ca saṃtulavihitachidratritayā || 5.8-12:10 ||
caturaṅgulordhvā taduparibhāge kaṭorikā caturaṅgulipramāṇonnateti bhāvaḥ || 5.8-12:11 ||
punaśchidreṣu triṣu śalākā yojyā lohaśalākāḥ kṣepyāḥ punastatrāpi chidreṣu hemapatrāṇi kaṇṭakavedhīni kanakapatrāṇi yojyānīti || 5.8-12:12 ||
evaṃvidhe pūrvaṃ nirmite yantre hemapatrāṇi sthāpya vidhūpyante || 5.8-12:13 ||
tato'gniṃ pradīpayetyantrādhastādyantrādhobhāge vahniṃ prajvālayettadā tāni hemapatrāṇi kṛṣṇāni śyāmavarṇāni bhavanti || 5.8-12:14 ||
kasmātdhūmopalepamātrātdhūmaścāsāvupalepaśca dhūmopalepastanmātrāttatpramāṇāt || 5.8-12:15 ||
yenauṣadhena dhūpo niruktastenauṣadhenopalepaḥ kāryaḥ patreṣviti || 5.8-12:16 ||
punaḥ kiṃbhūtāni hemapatrāṇi rasendro jarati agnitāpitāni santi vahniyogāttaptāni kṛtāni || 5.8-12:17 ||
svarṇajāraṇamidaṃ gaditam || 5.8-12:18 ||
iti pañcabhiḥ ślokaiḥ kulakam || 5.8-12:19 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.13

<silver:: garbhadruti (inmercury)>
tenaiva tārapatraṃ vidhinā saṃsvedya yantrayogena |
jāyeta kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tattāraṃ dravati garbhe ca || ṅṛht_5.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha rūpyajāraṇamāha tenetyādi || 5.13:1 ||
tenaiva vidhinā pūrvapidhānena tārapatraṃ rūpyadalaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ śyāmalaprabhaṃ jāyate || 5.13:2 ||
kena saṃsvedya yantrayogena saṃsvedaḥ prabalāgnistasyedaṃ sambandhi yadyantraṃ yasya yo yogastena || 5.13:3 ||
tattārapatraṃ punaḥ garbhe rasodare dravati jalatvamāpnoti || 5.13:4 ||
punastasmin yantre dravati caśabdātrasendrastatpatraṃ jarati tārakṛṣṇīti || 5.13:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.14


<gold, silver:: garbhadruti (~)>
athavā balinā vaṅgaṃ nāgābhidhānena yantrayogena |
hemāhvaṃ tāraṃ vā dravati ca garbhe na sandehaḥ || ṅṛht_5.14 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha yogāntaramāha athavetyādi || 5.14:1 ||
athaveti samuccaye ekārthaniṣṭhatvāt || 5.14:2 ||
balinā gandhena saha vaṅgaṃ yantrayogena kṛṣṇaṃ jāyeta || 5.14:3 ||
kevalaṃ vaṅgaṃ punarnāgābhidhānena saha nāgavidhānamapyevaṃ syāditi vyaktiḥ || 5.14:4 ||
nāgābhidhāneneti nāganāmnā ākhyāhve abhidhānaṃ ca nāmadheyaṃ ca nāma ca ityamaraḥ || 5.14:5 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ vaṅgaṃ vā nāgaṃ vā hemāhvaṃ hemapatraṃ vā tāraṃ tārapatraṃ vā etatsarvaṃ balinā kṛṣṇaṃ jāyeta mṛtaṃ ca sarvaṃ garbhe rasāntare dravati nātra saṃdehaḥ asaṃdigdhamidamuktam || 5.14:6 ||
caśabdājjarati niḥśeṣatvamāpnoti || 5.14:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.15


<rasaka:: garbhadruti (~)>
rasakaṃ balinā yuktaṃ pūrvoktavidhānayogena |
pakvaṃ cūrṇaṃ yāvadbhavati bhṛśaṃ dravati garbhe ca || ṅṛht_5.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

prayogāntaramāha rasakamityādi || 5.15:1 ||
rasakaṃ kharparikaṃ balinā gandhena saha yuktaṃ satmilitaṃ satsamabhāgena iti śeṣaḥ kena kṛtvā pūrvoktavidhānayogena pūrvoktaṃ yadvidhānaṃ yantrādikaṃ tasya yo'sau yogastena kṛtvā tāvadbhṛśamatyarthaṃ pakvaṃ kāryaṃ yāvadaśarīratāṃ yāti taccūrṇaṃ garbhe rasodare dravati garbhadrutirbhavati caśabdājjarati ca || 5.15:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.16


<gold:: garbhadruti>
vyūḍho'tha gandhakāśmā śataguṇasaṃkhyaṃ tathottame hemni |
sūte ca bhavati piṣṭirdravati hi garbhe na vismayaḥ kāryaḥ || ṅṛht_5.16 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha vyūḍha ityādi || 5.16:1 ||
athetyanantaram || 5.16:2 ||
gandhakāśmā gandhapāṣāṇaḥ śataguṇasaṃkhyaṃ yathā syāttathā uttame hemni pūrṇavarṇe vyūḍho nirvāhyaḥ tadgandhavyūḍhaṃ hema sūte pārade piṣṭirbhavati hi niścitaṃ garbhe rasāntardravati garbhadrutirbhavatītyatravismayo || 5.16:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.17


<gold:: bīja forgarbhadruti>
athavā śatanirvyūḍhaṃ rasakavaraṃ śuddhahemni varabījam |
bījaṃ jarati rasendre dravati ca garbhe na sandehaḥ || ṅṛht_5.17 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha athavetyādi || 5.17:1 ||
athaveti samuccaye rasakavaraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ kharparikaṃ śuddhahemni pūrṇavarṇe śatanirvyūḍhaṃ kuryātśataguṇanirvāhaḥ kāryaḥ ekaguṇasvarṇe śataguṇanirvāha iti yuktamevaṃ kṛte sati varabījaṃ śreṣṭhabījaṃ bhavati tadbījaṃ garbhe rasodare dravati garbhe drutirbhavati rasendre pārade śīghramavilambitaṃ jarati ca niḥśeṣatāmāpnoti vidhānena iti śeṣaḥ atra dravaṇe jaraṇe ca na sandehaḥ || 5.17:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.18


<gold:: bīja forgarbhadruti>
athavā tālakasatvaṃ śilayā vā tacca hemni nirvyūḍham |
śataguṇamatha mūṣāyāṃ jarati rasendro dravati garbhe ca || ṅṛht_5.18 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pūrvārthe vidhyantaramāha athavetyādi || 5.18:1 ||
tālakasatvaṃ haritālasāraṃ śataguṇaṃ śataguṇitaṃ hemni kanake nirvyūḍhamandhamūṣāyāṃ vā prakāśamūṣāyāṃ vahniyogena iti śeṣaḥ tālakasatvasya hemni nirvāhaḥ kārya iti vyaktiḥ tacca tatsattvaṃ kevalaṃ vā śilayā manaḥśilayā sārdhaṃ nirvyūḍhaṃ kāryaṃ taddhema garbhe rasodare dravati atha rasendro rasarājaḥ drutaṃ jarati vidhāneneti || 5.18:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.19-21


<lead, tin:: combinedmāraṇa>
rasadaradābhrakatāpyavimalāmṛtaśulbalohaparpaṭikā |
snuhyarkadugdhapiṣṭaṃ kaṃkuṣṭhaśilāyutaṃ nāgam || ṅṛht_5.19 ||
abhrakatālakaśaṅkharasasahitaṃ tatpunaḥ punaḥ puṭitam |
ciñcākṣāravimiśraṃ vaṅgaṃ nirjīvatāṃ yāti || ṅṛht_5.20 ||
vidhinānena ca puṭitaṃ mriyate nāgaṃ nirutthatāṃ ca gatam |
vaṅgaṃ ca sarvakarmasu niyujyate tadapi gatajīvam || ṅṛht_5.21 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha vidhyantaramāha rasetyādi || 5.19-21:1 ||
rasadaradābhrakatāpyamiti rasaḥ pāradaḥ darado hiṅgulaḥ abhrakaṃ pratītaṃ tāpyaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ vimalā rukmamākṣikaṃ mṛtaṃ yacchulbaṃ tāmraṃ loho muṇḍādiśca eteṣāṃ rasādīnāṃ rasaparpaṭīvatparpaṭikā kāryā || 5.19-21:2 ||
tayā yutaṃ nāgaṃ punaḥ kaṅkuṣṭhaśilāyutaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ haritapītavarṇo viṣaharapāṣāṇajātiḥ śilā manohvā tābhyāṃ yutaṃ miśritaṃ yannāgaṃ sīsakaḥ snuhyarkadugdhapiṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ snuhī sehuṇḍaḥ arkaḥ prasiddho viṭapī tayordugdhena piṣṭaṃ pāṃśubhūtaṃ mṛtaṃ yannāgaṃ kukkuṭapuṭavidhāneneti śeṣaḥ etadapi bījaṃ siddhaṃ garbhe dravati ca pūrvasaṃbandhāt || 5.19-21:3 ||
atha vaṅgabījavidhānamāha abhraketyādi || 5.19-21:4 ||
ciñcākṣāravimiśraṃ yadvaṅgamamlikākṣārayuktaṃ vaṅgamabhrakatālakaśaṅkharasasahitamabhrakaṃ pratītaṃ tālakaṃ haritālaṃ śaṅkhaṃ kambugrīvaṃ rasaḥ pāradaḥ etaiścaturbhiḥ sahitaṃ yathā syāttathā punaḥ punaḥ vāraṃ vāraṃ nirutthaṃ yāvattāvatpuṭitaṃ kuryāt || 5.19-21:5 ||
etadauṣadhasyāṃśabhāgena saha puṭanādvaṅgaṃ nirjīvatāṃ yāti pañcatvamāpnoti etadapi bījaṃ siddhaṃ garbhadrāvaṇe jāraṇārthe ca pūrvasaṃbandhāt || 5.19-21:6 ||
nāgavaṅgayoretadauṣadhaṃ kāraṇamityāha vidhinetyādi || 5.19-21:7 ||
anena vidhinā uktavidhānena nāgaṃ sīsakaṃ puṭitaṃ satmriyate mṛtaṃ bhavatīti vāmunā vidhānenaiva nirutthatāṃ gatamaśarīratāṃ prāptaṃ vaṅgaṃ sarvakarmasu cāraṇajāraṇabhakṣaṇādikāryeṣu niyujyate rasajñairiti śeṣaḥ || 5.19-21:8 ||
nāgavaṅgamāraṇamekavidhamevoktamatastadbhakṣaṇādiṣu parasparaṃ guṇādhikayogyaṃ natu jāraṇādiṣu || 5.19-21:9 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.22


<gold:: bīja forgarbhadruti>
mṛtanāgaṃ mṛtavaṅgaṃ mṛtavaraśulvaṃ mṛtaṃ tathā tīkṣṇam |
ekaikaṃ hemavare śatanirvyūḍhaṃ dravati garbhe ca || ṅṛht_5.22 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

nāgavaṅgaśulvatīkṣṇānāṃ jāraṇavidhānamāha mṛtanāgamityādi || 5.22:1 ||
mṛtanāgamiti mṛtaṃ nirjīvatāṃ gataṃ yannāgaṃ sīsakaṃ tathānena vidhānena mṛtaṃ vaṅgaṃ tathā mṛtaṃ nirutthatāṃ gataṃ varaśulbaṃ tāmraṃ tathā ca mṛtaṃ tīkṣṇamarivargeṇeti śeṣaḥ eṣāṃ madhye ekaikaṃ nāgaṃ vā vaṅgaṃ vā śulbaṃ vā tīkṣṇaṃ vā pṛthaktvena hemavare pūrṇavarṇe svarṇe śatanirvyūḍhaṃ hemnaḥ śataguṇanirvāhitaṃ kuryāttatsiddhaṃ garbhe rasodare dravati caśabdājjarati ca || 5.22:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.23


<gold:: mahābīja>
samagarbhe drutikaraṇaṃ hemno vakṣyāmyahaṃ paraṃ yogam |
bhrāmakasasyakacūrṇaṃ śatanirvyūḍhaṃ mahābījam || ṅṛht_5.23 ||
------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mahābījaprabhāvaṃ darśayannāha samagarbha ityādi || 5.23:1 ||
ihāsmiñśāstre vidhinā śāstroktarītyā yāni bījānyuktāni tāni kartavyāni sāmānyeneti bhāvaḥ paraṃ garbhadrutyarthamayaṃ vārttikendro yogaḥ vārttāsu kuśalā vārtikāḥ śāstropadeśarahitā ityarthaḥ atra bhāvādyarthe ikpratyayaḥ teṣu vārtikeṣu indraḥ pravaro yogaḥ tathānenaiva prakāreṇa ekamataścāyaṃ śāstropadeśikānāṃ śāstropadeśarahitānāṃ ca abhimata ityarthaḥ || 5.23:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.24-26


<garbhadruti>
athavā gandhakadhūmaṃ tālakadhūmaṃ śilāhvarasakasya |
dattvādhomukhamūṣāṃ dīrghatamāṃ kharparasyārdhe || ṅṛht_5.24 ||
ūrdhvaṃ lagnā piṣṭī sudṛḍhā ca yathā tathā ca kartavyā |
dattvā kharparapṛṣṭhe daityendraṃ dāhayettadanu || ṅṛht_5.25 ||
stokaṃ stokaṃ dattvā karṣāgnau dhmāpayenmṛdā liptām |
garbhe dravati hi bījaṃ mriyate tathādhike dāhe || ṅṛht_5.26 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rasodare bījadrāvaṇavidhānamāha athavetyādi || 5.24-26:1 ||
athaveti samuccaye || 5.24-26:2 ||
ūrdhvaṃ dīrghatamamūṣāyantrasya talabhāge piṣṭī rasendrabījayornirmitā piṣṭikā ca punaḥ sudṛḍhā yathā syāttathā lagnā kartavyā || 5.24-26:3 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā kharparasyārdhe mṛnmayapātrasya khaṇḍārdhe khaṇḍaikadeśa ityarthaḥ dīrghatamāmadhobhāgamukhīmadhobhāge mukhaṃ yasyāḥ sā tathoktā tāṃ dattvā || 5.24-26:4 ||
punaḥ kiṃ kṛtvā gandhakadhūmaṃ dattvā vā stokaṃ stokamalpamalpaṃ tālakadhūmaṃ dattvā vā śilāhvarasakasya śilāhvā manaḥśilā rasakaḥ kharparaḥ cakavadbhāvasamāsaḥ tasya dhūmaṃ dattvā || 5.24-26:5 ||
punastatkharparamadhomukhamukhāṃ ca mṛdā liptāṃ mṛdveṣṭitāṃ karīṣāgnau dhmāpayetkarīṣavahnāvityarthaḥ || 5.24-26:6 ||
evamadhomukhāṃ kharparaṃ ca dattvā daityendraṃ balināmānaṃ prastāvādgandhakaṃ tadanu tatkaraṇapaścāddāhayet || 5.24-26:7 ||
evaṃ kṛte sati rasendramilitaṃ yadbījaṃ tadgarbhe rasendrāntardravati || 5.24-26:8 ||
punarbījasahito rasendro'dhike dāhe sati mriyata ityarthaḥ || 5.24-26:9 ||
ślokatrayasaṃbandhādviśeṣakam || 5.24-26:10 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.27

<viḍa:: production>
gandhakatālakaśailāḥ sauvīrakarasakagairikaṃ daradam |
kṣārāmlalavaṇāni viḍo mākṣikavaikrāntavimalasamabhāgaiḥ || ṅṛht_5.27 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha suvarṇajāraṇārthaṃ biḍamāha gandhaketyādi || 5.27:1 ||
gandhakatālakaśailā iti gandhakaṃ pratītaṃ tālakaṃ haritālaṃ śailaḥ śilājatuḥ dvaṃdvaḥ samāsaḥ tena tathoktāḥ samabhāgāḥ kāryāḥ || 5.27:2 ||
punaḥ sauvīrakaṃ śuklāñjanaṃ rasakaṃ kharparikaṃ gairikaṃ dhātugairikaṃ daradaṃ hiṅgulamatraikavadbhāvasamāsaḥ tattathoktaṃ samabhāgaṃ kāryam || 5.27:3 ||
punaḥ kṣārāmlalavaṇāni kṣārā yavakṣārādayaḥ amlaṃ jambīrādi lavaṇāni saindhavādīni etānyapi samabhāgāni || 5.27:4 ||
kaiḥ saha mākṣikavaikrāntavimalasamabhāgaiḥ saha mākṣikaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ vaikrāntaṃ vajrabhūmijaṃ rajaḥ vimalaṃ rukmamākṣikametāni samabhāgāni tairbiḍa ucyate sarvaiḥ samabhāgaiḥ sumarditairbiḍaḥ kārya ityarthaḥ || 5.27:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.28


<viḍa:: forgarbhadruti>
kṛtvā suvarṇapiṣṭīṃ mṛditāṃ ca suveṣṭitāmanenaiva |
tripuṭaistapte khalve mṛditā garbhe tathā dravati || ṅṛht_5.28 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

biḍayogādyathā bījaṃ garbhe dravati tathāha kṛtvetyādi || 5.28:1 ||
pūrvoktā yā piṣṭī tāmanenaivoktabiḍayogena tapte khalve taptasaṃbandhāllohamaye tripuṭaiḥ karīṣāgnyātmakairmṛditā gharṣitā sati anenaiva ca veṣṭitā kāryā || 5.28:2 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā suvarṇapiṣṭīṃ kanakapiṣṭīṃ vā anyasyāpi dhātoḥ suvarṇapiṣṭīṃ śobhanavarṇāṃ piṣṭīṃ kṛtvā || 5.28:3 ||
khalve mṛditā satī tathā tenaiva prakāreṇa natvanyaprakāreṇa garbhe rasodare dravati salilarūpā tiṣṭhati || 5.28:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.29


<viḍa:: forgarbhadruti>
rakte śatanirvyūḍhaṃ netrahitaṃ bhasma vaikrāntakaṃ cātha |
vimalaṃ śatanirvyūḍhaṃ grasati samaṃ dravati garbhe ca || ṅṛht_5.29 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha vaikrāntagarbhadrutimāha rakta ityādi || 5.29:1 ||
vaikrāntabhasma vajrabhūmijaṃ rajastadudbhavaṃ bhasma rakte raktagaṇe śatanirvyūḍhaṃ śatavāraṃ nirvāhitaṃ kuryāt || 5.29:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ netrahitaṃ netrahitaśabdena maṇitvaṃ darśitaṃ maṇayo netrahitā iti || 5.29:3 ||
vimalaṃ rūpyamākṣikaṃ śvetavarṇaṃ yanmākṣikaṃ raktagaṇe śatanirvyūḍhaṃ kuryāt || 5.29:4 ||
tadubhayaṃ vaikrāntaṃ vimalaṃ ca raktaśatanirvyūḍhaṃ satraso grāsavidhānaṃ vihāya samaṃ grasati kavalayati stadgrasitaṃ garbhe rasāntardravati jarati ca iti caśabdārthaḥ || 5.29:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.30


<viḍa:: forgarbhadruti:: immermitśatanirvāhaṇa>
ye kecidviḍayogāḥ kṣārāmlalavaṇāni dīptavargāśca |
sarve śatanirvyūḍhā garbhadrutikārakāḥ kathitāḥ || ṅṛht_5.30 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

raktagaṇādhikyaṃ darśayannāha ya ityādi || 5.30:1 ||
ye kecidviḍayogā atra granthāntareṣvapi ca kathitāḥ tathā kṣārāmlalavaṇāni kathitāni kṣārā yavakṣārādayaḥ atha ca vṛkṣauṣadhisamudbhavāḥ amlā jambīrādayaḥ amlavṛkṣaśākasamudbhavāśca yānyetāni kathitāni ca punarye dīptavargāḥ kathitā dīptikarā yogā abhihitāḥ te sarve biḍakṣārāmlalavaṇadīptavargāḥ śatanirvyūḍhā garbhadrutikārakāḥ garbhe rasodare drutaṃ dravarūpaṃ kurvanti dhātumaṇiratnādīnīti śeṣaḥ || 5.30:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.31-32


<ṃenge nirvāhaṇa => grāsa, derassimiliertwird>
śatanirvyūḍhe ca samaṃ pādonaṃ pañcasaptativyūḍhe |
pañcāśati tadardhaṃ pādaḥ syātpañcaviṃśatike || ṅṛht_5.31 ||
aṣṭāṃśaṃ tu tadardhe ṣoḍaśāṃśaṃ tadardhanirvyūḍhe |
tasyārdhe dvātriṃśaccatuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśaṃ tadardhanirvyūḍhe || ṅṛht_5.32 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

śatanirvāhitādau samādividhānamāha śatetyādi || 5.31-32:1 ||
śatanirvyūḍha iti śatavāraṃ nirvāhite raktagaṇe iti śeṣaḥ samaṃ tulyaṃ grasati rasa iti śeṣaḥ || 5.31-32:2 ||
punaḥ pañcasaptativyūḍhe sati pādonaṃ caturthāṃśavarjitaṃ samagraṃ grasatīti || 5.31-32:3 ||
punaḥ pañcāśannirvyūḍhe sati tadardhaṃ samasyārdhamiti || 5.31-32:4 ||
punaḥ pañcaviṃśatike sati pādaścaturthāṃśam || 5.31-32:5 ||
nyūnādhike nirvyūḍhe sati nyūnādhikāṃśo jñeya iti viśeṣārthaḥ || 5.31-32:6 ||
alpanirvyūḍhakramamāha aṣṭāṃśamityādi || 5.31-32:7 ||
tu punastadardhe sārdhadvādaśake nirvyūḍhe sati aṣṭāṃśaṃ tadardhe ṣaḍvāranirvyūḍhe sati ṣoḍaśāṃśamiti punastasyārdhe trivāranirvyūḍhe sati dvātriṃśadaṃśaṃ tadardhanirvyūḍhe ekadvivāranirvyūḍhe sati catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśaṃ raso grasatītyarthaḥ || 5.31-32:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.33


<garbhadruti>
iti gaditāṃ garbhadrutimabhiṣavayogena cāmlavargeṇa |
svedanavidhinā jñātvā mṛditāṃ tapte tu khalvatale || ṅṛht_5.33 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

garbhadrutau satyāṃ kartavyamāha itītyādi || 5.33:1 ||
ityuktavidhānena gaditāṃ kathitāṃ garbhadrutiṃ jñātvā tapte khalvatale lohamaye karīṣāgninā uṣṇatāṃ nīte mṛditāṃ kuryāt || 5.33:2 ||
kena abhiṣavayogena abhiṣavaḥ saṃmardanaṃ tasya yogena na kevalamanena amlavargeṇa ca jambīrādinā na kevalamanenāpi svedanavidhinā ca svedanavidhiḥ svedanasaṃskāroktatvānnātrābhihitaḥ jāraṇahetoriti śeṣaḥ || 5.33:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.34


<ḍurchfṃhrungvon saṃskāra gemīß den verwendeten bījasusw.>
jñātvā bījabalābalamardanayogaṃ kṛtaṃ ca rasarāje |
svedavidhānaṃ ca puṭaṃ yantraṃ vā vihitarasakarma || ṅṛht_5.34 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mardanasvedanayoḥ pūrvopakaraṇaṃ darśayannāha jñātvetyādi || 5.34:1 ||
bījabalābalamardanayogaṃ kṛtaṃ jñātvā bījānāṃ dhātūpadhātuyogajanitānāṃ balābale nyūnādhike yo'sau mardanayogastameva kṛtaṃ jñātvā viditvā rasarāje svedavidhānaṃ kuryātvā puṭaṃ vahniyogaṃ kuryātvā yantraṃ vihitarasakarma kuryātvihitaṃ kṛtaṃ rasasya karma saṃskārarūpaṃ yatra tathoktaṃ garbhayantrādikamityarthaḥ || 5.34:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.35


sūtavaraṃ lakṣayate bījaṃ nopekṣatāṃ yathā yāti |
tadvatkāryaṃ vidhinā sukarma gurupādanirdiṣṭam || ṅṛht_5.35 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sūtakarmaṇo durbodhatvādgurupādaṃ stuvannāha sūtetyādi || 5.35:1 ||
yathā yena prakāreṇa sūtavaraṃ pāradaḥ lakṣayate jñāyate karmakṛteti śeṣaḥ punaryathā bījamupekṣatāṃ na yāti samyakmilati tadvattenaiva prakāreṇa gurupādanirdiṣṭaṃ karma ācāryavaryadarśitaṃ pūjyaṃ saṃskārarūpaṃ vidhinā ācāryoktavidhānena kuryāt || 5.35:2 ||
gurupādalakṣaṇam |
sarvaśāstraviśeṣajñaḥ kuśalo rasakarmaṇi |
evaṃlakṣaṇasaṃyukto rasavidyāgururbhavet |
iti || 5.35:3 ||
iti garbhadrutijāraṇaprakaraṇam || 5.35:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.36


<bāhyadruti>
bāhyadrutirati vimalā sphurati hi keṣāṃcideva siddhānām |
tebhyaḥ samyakjñātvā kalanāḥ kāryāstathā drutayaḥ || ṅṛht_5.36 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

bāhyadrutiṃ praśaṃsannāha bāhyadrutirityādi || 5.36:1 ||
drutirdvidhoktā garbhadrutirbāhyadrutiśceti || 5.36:2 ||
garbhadrutiḥ pūrvamuktā bāhyadrutiradhunābhidhīyate || 5.36:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭā ativimalā nirmalatarā || 5.36:4 ||
eṣā ca punaḥ keṣāṃcideva siddhānāṃ sphurati siddhā rasavidyāpāragā nityanāthādayaḥ teṣāṃ te jānantīti || 5.36:5 ||
duṣprāpyatvātbāhyadrutiriha viralā || 5.36:6 ||
tebhyaḥ samyakjñātvā tathā tenaiva siddhodeśavidhānena drutayo bāhyadrutayaḥ || 5.36:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.37-38


<jāraṇa of lead>
varanāgaṃ rasarājaṃ bījavaraṃ sāritaṃ tathā tritayam |
gandhakaśilālasahitaṃ nirnāgaṃ dīpavartito bhavati || ṅṛht_5.37 ||
baddhvā sudṛḍhe vastre poṭalikāyāṃ śikhīkṛto dīpaḥ |
taile magnaṃ kṛtvā nirnāgaṃ jāyate kṣipram || ṅṛht_5.38 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha nāgajāraṇamāha varanāgamityādi || 5.37-38:1 ||
varanāgaṃ śreṣṭhajāti sīsakaṃ jāraṇayogyaṃ rasarājamuktasaṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaṃ pāradaṃ bījavaraṃ hemabījametattrayaṃ sāritaṃ militaṃ kāryaṃ punargandhakaśilālasahitaṃ gandhakaṃ pratītaṃ śilā manaḥśilā ālaṃ haritālaṃ dvandvastāni taiḥ sahitaṃ ca kāryametatsarvaṣaṭkaṃ dīpavartitaḥ prajvālitadīpavartiyogātnirnāgaṃ nāgavarjitaṃ bhavati nāgaṃ jaratītyarthaḥ || 5.37-38:2 ||
yathā nirṇāgaṃ syāttathā vidhānamāha baddhvetyādi || 5.37-38:3 ||
tatpūrvoktaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ sudṛḍhe vastre nūtane vastre atropaśleṣike'dhikaraṇe saptamī poṭalikāyāṃ baddhvā punastaile tilodbhave tatṣaṭkaṃ magnaṃ nimajjitaṃ kṛtvā tadadhaḥ śikhīkṛto dīpo'vadhāryaḥ na śikhī śikhāyuktaḥ kṛtaḥ śikhīkṛtaḥ śikhāvānityarthaḥ || 5.37-38:4 ||
anena vidhinā kṣipraṃ śīghraṃ nirnāgaṃ syāditi || 5.37-38:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.39-40

<jāraṇa of lead>
kṛtvātra dīrghamūṣāṃ sudṛḍhāṃ dhmātaṃ tu bhasmagartāyām |
kṣiptvā śilālacūrṇaṃ paścātsūtaṃ tataḥ śilācūrṇam || ṅṛht_5.39 ||
saṃsthāpya bhasmanāto dhmātaṃ syātsvāṃgaśītalaṃ yāvat |
ākṛṣya tatra sūtaṃ jñātvā nāgaṃ subhakṣitaṃ sakalam || ṅṛht_5.40 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

nirnāgakaraṇe vidhānamāha kṛtvetyādi || 5.39-40:1 ||
tu punaḥ dīrghāṃ gostanākārāṃ sudṛḍhāṃ nirvraṇavajropamāṃ mūṣāṃ kṛtvā tāṃ mūṣāṃ prati śilālacūrṇaṃ kṣiptvā śilā manohvā ālaṃ haritālametayoścūrṇaṃ paścātsūtaṃ pūrvoktaṃ pāradaṃ kṣiptvā tato'nantaraṃ śilācūrṇaṃ kṣiptvā tāmeva rasasaṃyuktāṃ mūṣāṃ bhasmagartāyāṃ bhasmanā yuktā yā gartā tasyāṃ dhmātaṃ kuryātpunastāvadbhasmanā ācchādya yāvatsvāṅgaśītalaṃ svayameva śītalaṃ syāt || 5.39-40:2 ||
tatra tasyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ sakalaṃ samastaṃ nāgaṃ subhakṣitaṃ jīrṇatāṃ gataṃ jñātvā sūtamākṛṣya uddhārya nirnāgakaraṇavidhānametat || 5.39-40:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.41


<bīja of nāgajīrṇapārada>
jñātvā nāgaṃ truṭitaṃ punarapi dadyādyathā bhavettriguṇam |
paścācchuddhaṃ kṛtvā bījavaraṃ yojayettadanu || ṅṛht_5.41 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

nirnāgānantaraṃ yatkartavyaṃ tadāha jñātvetyādi || 5.41:1 ||
nāgaṃ sīsakaṃ truṭitaṃ buddhvā punarapi nāgaṃ dahyātpūrvoktavidhānena pārade iti śeṣaḥ || 5.41:2 ||
yathā nāgaṃ triguṇitaṃ bhavettathaiva kuryāt || 5.41:3 ||
paścātsūtaṃ śuddhaṃ kṛtvā pāradaṃ nirnāgaṃ vidhāya tadanu nāgajāraṇānantaraṃ bījavaraṃ pūrvoktaṃ yojayet || 5.41:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.42


<mercury:: jāraṇa of tin>
athavā tāraṃ vaṅgaṃ sūtaṃ saṃsārya vaṅgaparihīnam |
tālakayogena tathā nirvaṅgaṃ yantrayogena || ṅṛht_5.42 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha tārayogamāha athavetyādi || 5.42:1 ||
athaveti vidhānāntaram || 5.42:2 ||
tāraṃ vaṅgaṃ sūtamiti tāraṃ rūpyaṃ vaṅgaṃ khurakaṃ sūtaṃ saṃskṛtapāradametattritayaṃ saṃsārya melanaṃ vidhāya vaṅgaparihīnaṃ kuryāttathā tenaiva vidhānena tālasya yo'sau yogastena yantrayogena ca dīrghamūṣāyogena ca nirvaṅgaṃ vaṅgavivarjitaṃ kuryāt || 5.42:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.43


<mercury:: jāraṇa of tin>
athavā vastranibaddhaṃ girijatusahitaṃ suveṣṭitaṃ māṣaiḥ |
pakvaṃ taile vaṭakaṃ nirvaṅgaṃ jāyate nūnam || ṅṛht_5.43 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha athavetyādi || 5.43:1 ||
athavā tāraṃ vaṅgaṃ vaṭakākāraṃ taile tilodbhave pakvaṃ kuryātnūnaṃ niścitaṃ tadvaṭakaṃ tathā pakvaṃ kuryādyathā nirvaṅgaṃ jāyate vahniyogena iti śeṣaḥ || 5.43:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.44-46


<bīja:: forgarbhadruti>
piṣṭīstambhaṃ kṛtvā bījavareṇaiva sāritaṃ tadanu |
<bīja:: forgarbhadruti>
athavā baddharasena tu sahitaṃ bījaṃ surañjitaṃ kṛtvā || ṅṛht_5.44 ||
gandhakanihitaṃ sūtaṃ nihitānihitaṃ ca śṛṅkhalāyāṃ tat |
yojitanirvyūḍharase garbhadrutikārakaṃ nūnam || ṅṛht_5.45 ||
sūtakabhasmavareṇa tu bījaṃ kṛtvā rasendrake garbhe |
mṛditā piṣṭī vidhinā hyabhiṣavayogāddravati garbhe ca || ṅṛht_5.46 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhānāntaramāha piṣṭītyādi || 5.44-46:1 ||
bījavareṇaiva pūrvakanakabījenaiva sāritaṃ militaṃ satpiṣṭīstambhaṃ khoṭastambhaṃ kṛtvā tadanu tatpaścātathaveti prakārāntaraṃ darśayati tu punaḥ baddharasena khoṭabaddharasena sahitaṃ surañjitaṃ śobhanavidhānena varṇavṛddhīkṛtaṃ bījaṃ svarṇabījaṃ saṃyutaṃ kuryātyoga eva kārya iti dvividhānamuktam || 5.44-46:2 ||
anyaccāha gandhaketyādi || 5.44-46:3 ||
gandhakanihitaṃ gandhake nihitaṃ sthāpitaṃ sūtamūrdhvādho gandhakaṃ dattvā sūtaṃ madhyasthaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 5.44-46:4 ||
ca punarevaṃvidhaṃ sūtaṃ śṛṅkhalāyāṃ śṛṅkhalīkaraṇayoge nihitānihitaṃ yannihitaṃ tadanihitamajāritaṃ kāryaṃ nihitānihitasaṃyogātśṛṅkhaleyaṃ nūnaṃ niścitaṃ yojitanirvyūḍharase pūrvaṃ yojitaṃ paścānnirvāhitaṃ nirvyūḍhaṃ yatra tattasminnevaṃvidharase garbhadrutiḥ pāradasyodare bījāni dravanti || 5.44-46:5 ||
piṣṭīvidhinā jāraṇamāha sūtaketyādi || 5.44-46:6 ||
tu punaḥ rasendrake garbhe rasendrakṛto yo'sau garbhastasmin bījaṃ kṛtvā vidhinā piṣṭīrvidheyā sā piṣṭī mṛditā kāryā kasmātabhiṣavayogātsaṃmardanayogātkena saha sūtakabhasmavareṇa saha sūtakasya yadbhasmavaraṃ tena sā piṣṭī garbhe dravati caśabdājjarati ca || 5.44-46:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.47-49


<bīja:: forgarbhadruti>
patrābhrakaṃ ca satvaṃ kāṃkṣī vā kāntamākṣikaṃ puṭitam |
nirguṇḍī gṛhakanyā cāṅgerī palāśaśākaiśca || ṅṛht_5.47 ||
tāvatpuṭitaṃ kṛtvā yāvatsindūrasaprabhaṃ bhavati |
tatpādaśeṣalavaṇaṃ haṇḍikapākena pācitaṃ sudṛḍham || ṅṛht_5.48 ||
ekaikaṃ śatavyūḍhaṃ bījavaraṃ jārayedrasendrasya |
garbhe dravati ca kṣipraṃ hyabhiṣavayogena mṛditamaṅgulyā || ṅṛht_5.49 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

bījavaravidhānamāha patrābhrakamityādi || 5.47-49:1 ||
patrābhrakamiti abhrakasya patrāṇi vābhrakasya satvaṃ punaḥ kāṃkṣī saurāṣṭrī kāntamākṣikaṃ kāntaścumbakaḥ mākṣikaṃ svarṇamākṣikameteṣāṃ dvandva ekatvaṃ punaretatnirguṇḍīgṛhakanyācāṅgerīpalāśaśākaiḥ puṭitaṃ nirguṇḍī sephālikā gṛhakanyā kumārī cāṅgerī amlaśākaḥ palāśo brahmavṛkṣaḥ śāko vṛkṣaviśeṣaḥ eteṣāṃ dvandvasamāsaḥ eteṣāṃ rasaṃ gṛhītvā pūrvauṣadhapuṭitaṃ kuryātgharme iti śeṣaḥ || 5.47-49:2 ||
tadauṣadhaṃ puṭitaṃ kṛtvā punastatpuṭitamauṣadhaṃ tatpādaśeṣaṃ caturthāṃśaṃ lavaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ dattvā haṇḍikāpākena haṇḍikāyāṃ mṛdbhājane yaḥ pākastena pācitaṃ vahnau puṭitaṃ tāvatkuryādyāvatsindūrasaṃprabhaṃ sindūratulyavarṇaṃ bhavati || 5.47-49:3 ||
kathaṃ pācitaṃ kuryātsudṛḍhaṃ yathā syāttathā ekaikaṃ śatavyūḍhamiti || 5.47-49:4 ||
pūrvoktānāṃ sindūrīkṛtānāmuparasānāmadharātekaikamekamekaṃ pṛthaktvena śatavyūḍhaṃ śatavāraṃ vāhitaṃ bījavaraṃ jāyate kanake iti śeṣaḥ tadbījaṃ rasendrasya garbhe dravati caśabdātkṣipraṃ śīghraṃ jarati ca || 5.47-49:5 ||
kathamaṅgulyā anāmikayā yo'bhiṣavayogaḥ saṃmardanayogasteneti viśeṣakam || 5.47-49:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.50


<gold:: bīja withlead>
āvṛtte'pyāvartyaṃ hemavare kṣepyamujjvale nāgam |
triguṇaśilāprativāpaṃ hyahibījaṃ tatsamuddiṣṭam || ṅṛht_5.50 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

nāgena bījakaraṇamāha ujjvalahemavare svarṇaśreṣṭhe āvartye samyagdrute nāgaṃ śuddhasīsakamāvartyaṃ pradrāvyaṃ kiṃ kṛtvā samaṃ svarṇasamabhāgakṣepaṃ kṣiptvā punarnāgopari triguṇaśilāprativāpaṃ triguṇā yā śilā tasyā nirvāpaṃ kuryāt || 5.50:1 ||
śilā manaḥśilā || 5.50:2 ||
tatsiddhamahibījaṃ nāgayogena bījaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ rasavidbhiḥ iti śeṣaḥ || 5.50:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.51


<gold, silver:: bīja withtin>
vaṅgaṃ tu tena vidhinā hemavare kṣepya tālavāpena |
tāre vā nirvyūḍhaṃ bījavaraṃ truṭitasaṃyogāt || ṅṛht_5.51 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha vaṅgayogena bījamāha vaṅgamityādi || 5.51:1 ||
tena pūrvoktena vidhinā vidhānena tu punaḥ hemavare pūrvavarṇite vaṅgaṃ kṣepya tālavāpena haritālanikṣepeṇa nirvyūḍhaṃ kuryātvā tāre āvartye vaṅgaṃ nikṣipya nirvyūḍhaṃ nirvāhitaṃ sadbījavaraṃ bhavet || 5.51:2 ||
kasmāttruṭitasaṃyogāttruṭitaṃ bhavati tathā bījavaramiti vaṅgabījaṃ tatpūrvavatdravati jarati ca || 5.51:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.52


<rasāṅkuśa>
yo niḥsṛto bhujaṅgādrasakeśarīvajrapañjaraḥ sa punaḥ |
phaṇihemaguṇātkuṭilo rasāṅkuśo nāma vikhyātaḥ || ṅṛht_5.52 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

suvarṇābhidhānabījapraśaṃsanamāha ya ityādi || 5.52:1 ||
bhujaṅgātsīsakātniḥsṛtaḥ bhujaṅgaśilāvāpena kṣayaṃ nītvā yaḥ pṛthagbhūtaḥ sa rasakesarīvajrapañjaraḥ kathitaḥ rasa eva kesarī siṃhaḥ tadarthaṃ vajrapañjaraḥ vajreṇa vyadhitaḥ pañjaro'tidṛḍhatvātsiṃharakṣaṇasamartha ityarthaḥ || 5.52:2 ||
punarviśeṣeṇocyate ayaṃ vajrapañjaro na kiṃtu ayaṃ rasāṅkuśaḥ raso gajarūpaḥ tasyāṅkuśaḥ vaśīkaraṇasamarthaḥ || 5.52:3 ||
nāma saṃbhāvanāyām || 5.52:4 ||
vikhyātaḥ prakathitaḥ || 5.52:5 ||
kasmātkuṭilātkimapi vastuharaṇātkuṭilo vakro bhavati duṣṭasvabhāva eva anena hemnā nāgaharaṇaṃ kṛtam || 5.52:6 ||
kathaṃbhūtātkuṭilātphaṇī bhujaṅgaḥ hema svarṇaṃ tayorguṇā vidyante yasminevaṃvidhātkuṭilādeśo yuktaḥ aṅkuśo'pi vakro bhavatīti || 5.52:7 ||
atra bhrāntimānalaṅkāraḥ hemabīje vajrapañjaragajāṅkuśadarśanādbhrāntirjāteti || 5.52:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht5.53-58


<jāraṇa withoutprecedinggarbhadruti; bāhyadruti??>
evaṃ pakvaṃ vidhinā bījavaraṃ sūtarāṭtathāmlena |
kartavyaḥ saṃsvedyo yāvatpiṣṭī bhavecchlakṣṇā || ṅṛht_5.53 ||
tailena tena vidhinā svinnā piṣṭī bhavedakhilam |
athavā ślakṣṇaṃ śilayā nighṛṣṭabījaṃ bhavetpiṇḍī || ṅṛht_5.54 ||
pāko vaṭakavidhinā kartavyastailayogena |
krāmaṇapiṇḍe kṣiptvā māṣaiśca syātsudṛḍhapiṇḍatvam || ṅṛht_5.55 ||
mṛdvagninā supakvaṃ dagdhaṃ yāvanna bhavetpiṇḍam |
ākṛṣya cātha sūtaṃ piṇḍe śeṣaṃ tathā punaḥ pācyam || ṅṛht_5.56 ||
athavāpyauṣadhapiṇḍe dolātapte kharpare vidhinā |
punarapi piṇḍe kṣepyaṃ garbhe yāvaddrutirbhavati || ṅṛht_5.57 ||
evaṃ drutaṃ hi garbhe bījavaraṃ jarati rasarāje |
garbhadrutyā rahitaṃ biḍayogairjarati garbhe ca || ṅṛht_5.58 ||
------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

bījasyāsya piṣṭīkaraṇamāha evamityādi || 5.53-58:1 ||
evamamunā prakāreṇa vidhinārthādupadeśena pakvaṃ yadbījavaraṃ tathā pūrvasaṃskṛtaḥ sūtarāṭpāradaḥ amlena jambīrādinā saṃsvedyaḥ svedākhyo vidhiḥ kartavyaḥ || 5.53-58:2 ||
atra kimavadhistatkartavyo yāvatślakṣṇā spaṣṭā piṣṭī ekaśarīratā bhavetrasabījayoriti śeṣaḥ || 5.53-58:3 ||
vidhyantaramāha tailenetyādinā || 5.53-58:4 ||
tena pūrvoktena vidhinā vadhavidhānena tilatailena svinnā sveditā satī piṣṭirbhavati vahnāviti śeṣaḥ || 5.53-58:5 ||
athaveti vidhānāntaraṃ darśayati || 5.53-58:6 ||
śilayā dṛṣanmayayā nighṛṣṭabījaṃ nighṛṣṭaṃ gharṣitaṃ yadbījaṃ nāgākhyaṃ tatsūteneti śeṣaḥ akhilaṃ samastaṃ yathā syāttathā piṣṭirbhavatīti || 5.53-58:7 ||
yathā vaṭakaḥ pācyastathāha pāka ityādi || 5.53-58:8 ||
vaṭakavidhinā māṣavaṭakavidhinā tailayogena pākaḥ kartavyaḥ vahnau pācanaṃ vidheyamityarthaḥ || 5.53-58:9 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā krāmaṇapiṇḍe kṣiptvā biḍapiṇḍamadhye sthāpya ca punarmāṣairannaviśeṣairdṛḍhapiṇḍatvaṃ syātmāṣacūrṇaveṣṭitaṃ krāmaṇapiṇḍaṃ dṛḍhaṃ bhavediti vyaktiḥ || 5.53-58:10 ||
tadvidhānamāha mṛdvityādi || 5.53-58:11 ||
māṣacūrṇitajātaṃ krāmaṇapiṇḍaṃ tāvatsupakvaṃ kartavyaṃ yāvaddagdhaṃ na bhavet || 5.53-58:12 ||
kena supakvaṃ mṛdvagninā komalavahninā puṭaḥ tatpiṇḍataḥ śeṣaṃ śiṣṭaṃ taṃ nirmalapāradamākṛṣya gṛhītvā piṇḍamanyasmin piṇḍe tathā pūrvaprakāreṇa pācyamiti || 5.53-58:13 ||
aparaṃ cāha athavetyādi || 5.53-58:14 ||
athaveti prakārāntare || 5.53-58:15 ||
punarapi piṣṭīrdolātapte auṣadhapiṇḍe dolayottapte uṣṇatāṃ nīte krāmaṇauṣadhānāṃ piṇḍe kṣepya madhye sthāpya kasyopari kharpare mṛnmayapātropari || 5.53-58:16 ||
kena vidhinā pūrvoktena tailena vā amleneti || 5.53-58:17 ||
kiyatkālaṃ yāvadgarbhe rasodare piṣṭī dravati tāvadbiḍāntare piṇḍāntare kṣepyeti tātparyārthaḥ || 5.53-58:18 ||
aparaṃ cāha evamityādi || 5.53-58:19 ||
evamamunā prakāreṇa garbhe rasodare jarati niḥśeṣatvaṃ rasodare prāpnoti ca punargarbhadrutyā rahitaṃ draveṇa varjitaṃ bījavaraṃ biḍairjarati drutabījamāraṇasamartho biḍa ityarthaḥ || 5.53-58:20 ||
drutagrāsaparimāṇo biḍayantrādiyogataḥ |
jāraṇetyucyate tasyāḥ prakārāḥ santi koṭiśaḥ |
prakārā yantrāṇāmiti śeṣaḥ || 5.53-58:21 ||
jāraṇabhedāstu |
grāsasya cāraṇaṃ garbhadrāvaṇaṃ jāraṇaṃ tathā |
iti trirūpā nirdiṣṭā jāraṇā varavārtikaiḥ |
iti || 5.53-58:22 ||



____________________________________________________

ṅṛht6.1-7


<jāraṇa:: method>

grāsamiti cārayitvā garbhadrutiṃ tato bhūrje |
lavaṇakṣārāmlasudhāsurabhīmūtreṇa kṛtalepe || ṅṛht_6.1 ||
dṛḍhavastrabāhyabaddhe dolāsvedena jārayedgrāsam |
sauvīreṇārdhapūrṇe kumbhe sakṣāramūtrakairathavā || ṅṛht_6.2 ||
amunā krameṇa divasaistribhistribhirjārayedgrāsam |
garbhadrutijāraṇamāha grāsamityādi || 6.1-2:1 ||
pūrvoktaprakāreṇa grāsaṃ kavalaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ cārayitvā punargarbhadrutiṃ kṛtvā tatastadanantaraṃ tadgarbhadrutaṃ sūtaṃ bhūrje bhūrjavṛkṣatvakpuṭake sthāpayedityarthaḥ || 6.1-2:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭe bhūrje lavaṇakṣārāmlasudhāsurabhimūtreṇa kṛtalepe lavaṇāni saindhavādīni kṣārāḥ svarjikādayaḥ amlo jambīrādiḥ sudhā śukticūrṇaṃ surabhī dhenustanmūtrametena yogena kṛtvā kṛto lepo yasmin || 6.1-2:3 ||
udaradrutiyuktaḥ sūtaḥ sthāpya ityarthaḥ || 6.1-2:4 ||
jāraṇavidhānamāha dṛḍhetyādi || 6.1-2:5 ||
dṛḍhavastrabāhyabaddhe iti dṛḍhaṃ nūtanaṃ ghanaṃ ca yadvastraṃ tena bāhye sarvato baddhe saṃyate pūrvoktena dolāsvedena dolāyantravidhinā yaḥ svedastaṃ kṛtvā grāsaṃ rasāntardrutaṃ kavalaṃ jārayet || 6.1-2:6 ||
kutra kumbhe kalaśe kiṃviśiṣṭe sauvīreṇārdhapūrṇe sauvīreṇa svedanasaṃskāroditakāñjikena kṛtvā ardhapūrṇe || 6.1-2:7 ||
athaveti vidhyantare || 6.1-2:8 ||
sakṣāramūtrakaiḥ saha kṣāraḥ svarjikādibhiḥ vartante yāni mūtrāṇi go'jāvinārīṇāmiti śeṣaḥ etaiḥ ardhabhṛte kumbhe jārayedityarthaḥ || 6.1-2:9 ||
atha rasajāraṇe kālasaṃkhyāmāha amunetyādi || 6.1-2:10 ||
amunā krameṇeti uktaprakāreṇa tribhistribhirdivasaiḥ tribhiḥ saṃkhyākairdivasaiḥ grāse jāte anyagrāsaḥ kriyate garbhadrutagrāsaḥ kriyate garbhadrutagrāsaḥ tridivasairjaratīti bhāvaḥ || 6.1-2:11 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht6.3-7


jīrṇasya lakṣaṇamatho jñeyaṃ yantrātsamuddhṛtya || ṅṛht_6.3 ||
<ūntersuchung, objāraṇa stattgefunden hat>
uddhṛtamātraṃ pātre prakṣālya kāṃjikenātaḥ |
samalaṃ ca kāṃjikamato haraṇārthaṃ vastrayogena || ṅṛht_6.4 ||
tadanu sukhoṣṇe pātre saṃmardyo'sau yathā na hīye |
tāvadyāvacchuṣyati tallagnaṃ kāñjikaṃ sakalam || ṅṛht_6.5 ||
itthaṃ ca śoṣitajalaḥ karamardanataḥ sunirmalībhūtaḥ |
pīḍyaḥ pātrasyopari vastreṇa caturguṇenaiva || ṅṛht_6.6 ||
yadi parigalitaḥ sakalo vastrādgrāsena caikatāṃ yātaḥ |
na bhavati yadi daṇḍadharo jīrṇagrāsastadā jñeyaḥ || ṅṛht_6.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atheti tridinasvedānantaraṃ jīrṇasya rasasya lakṣaṇaṃ jñeyaṃ grāso jīrṇo na veti jñātavyaḥ || 6.3-7:1 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā yantrāddolikābhidhānāduddhṛtya yantrādbahirgṛhītveti || 6.3-7:2 ||
rasamalāpanayanamāha uddhṛtetyādi || 6.3-7:3 ||
uddhṛtamātramiti yantrādbahirgṛhītamātraṃ tatpātre bhājane mṛnmaye kāñjikena prakṣālya || 6.3-7:4 ||
punarato rasātsamalaṃ malasaṃyutaṃ kāñjikaṃ haraṇīyaṃ bahiḥ kāryam || 6.3-7:5 ||
kena kṛtvā vastrayogena vastre kṣiptaṃ sattadeva tiṣṭhati na kāñjikam || 6.3-7:6 ||
tasyaiva vidhānaṃ cāha tadanvityādi || 6.3-7:7 ||
tadanu vastrayogānantaraṃ sukhoṣṇe pātre asau rasaḥ saṃmardyaḥ || 6.3-7:8 ||
kathaṃ yathā na hīyate nāśaṃ nāpnuyātatyauṣṇyātvā kāṃsyatāmranāgavaṅgakanakatārapātrātvā dṛḍhakaraghātādraso hīna eva syātato'sau pāradastāvan saṃmardyo yāvallagnakāñjikaṃ rasasaṃsargasauvīraṃ śuṣyati niḥśeṣatāṃ yātītyarthaḥ || 6.3-7:9 ||
tasyaiva vidhānaṃ cāha itthamityādi || 6.3-7:10 ||
itthamamunā prakāreṇa karamardanataḥ hastatalamardanataḥ sunirmalībhūto malarahitaḥ śoṣitajalo rasaścaturguṇena vastreṇa kṛtvā pānasyopari pīḍyaḥ || 6.3-7:11 ||
jīrṇagrāsalakṣaṇamāha yadītyādi || 6.3-7:12 ||
tadā pārado jīrṇagrāso jñeyaḥ jīrṇo niḥśeṣatvamāpanno grāso yasmin sa tathoktaḥ || 6.3-7:13 ||
yadi sakalaḥ sarvaḥ vastrātparigalitaḥ caturguṇavastrātcyuto bhavati pātre iti śeṣaḥ || 6.3-7:14 ||
punaryadi grāsena saha ekatāṃ yātaḥ sanmilitaḥ san raso daṇḍadharo na bhavati sthirarūpo na syāttadā jīrṇagrāso jñātavya ityarthaḥ || 6.3-7:15 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht6.8


<grāsājīrṇa:: treatment>
grāsādajīrṇapiṣṭīṃ sūtāduddhṛtya pātayedyantre |
svastho bhavati rasendro grāsaḥ pakvaḥ punarjarati || ṅṛht_6.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rasājīrṇalakṣaṇamāha grāsādityādi || 6.8:1 ||
grāsātkavalasaṃyogātajīrṇapiṣṭīmajīrṇā aparipakvā yā piṣṭī pūrvoktalakṣaṇā tāṃ sūtātrasātyantre pātanakarmocite pātayet || 6.8:2 ||
evaṃ grāsātpṛthakkṛtya patitaḥ svastho nirmalo bhavati || 6.8:3 ||
punargrāsaḥ pakvo vahnitale dattvā pakvaḥ kṛtastaṃ rasendro jaratīti || 6.8:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht6.9


dolāyāṃ catvāro grāsā jāryā yathākrameṇaiva |
śeṣāḥ kacchapayantre yāvaddviguṇādikaṃ jarati || ṅṛht_6.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

grāsajāraṇāyāṃ yantrādikaraṇamāha dolāyāmityādi || 6.9:1 ||
yathākrameṇaiva catuḥṣaṣṭyādinaiva catvāro grāsā dolāyāṃ jāryāḥ śeṣā grāsāścatvāraḥ asaṃkhyā vā kacchapayantre jalayantre ca jāryāḥ || 6.9:2 ||
śeṣāścatvāraḥ kutaḥ yato'nyaśāstreṣu aṣṭaiva grāsāḥ || 6.9:3 ||
jāraṇe kimavadhiḥ yāvaddviguṇādikaṃ jarati pāradāddviguṇitamādiśabdena dviguṇānnyūnaṃ na kāryamadhikamadhikaṃ ca bhavatu || 6.9:4 ||
śaktyavatare'ṣṭau bṛhacchāstre kvacidaṣṭau grāsā uktāḥ kvacidviṃśatigrāsā iti || 6.9:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht6.10-12


<īussere ṃerkmale desḥgnachjāraṇa einerbest. ṃenge abhra>
nādau kartuṃ śakyo'tra grāsapramāṇaniyamastu |
grasate na hi sarvāṅgaṃ gaganamato lakṣaṇairjñeyam || ṅṛht_6.10 ||
yadi hi catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśān grasati rasastadā dhareddaṇḍam |
catvāriṃśadbhāgapraveśataḥ pāyasākāraḥ || ṅṛht_6.11 ||
bhavati jalaukākārastriṃśadbhāgādavipluṣaśca viṃśatyā |
chedīva ṣoḍaśāṃśādata ūrdhvaṃ durjaro grāsaḥ || ṅṛht_6.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:
grāse'nirdiṣṭasaṃkhyatvaṃ darśayannāha netyādi || 6.10-12:1 ||
atrāsmin śāstre ādau prathamaṃ grāsapramāṇaniyamaḥ kartuṃ na śakyaḥ || 6.10-12:2 ||
yataḥ kāraṇātgaganamabhraṃ sarvāṅgaṃ na grasate rasa iti śeṣaḥ || 6.10-12:3 ||
tatsarvāṅgagrastaṃ gaganamabhraṃ lakṣaṇaireva jñātavyamityarthaḥ || 6.10-12:4 ||
catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśādigrāse rasākāramāha yadītyādi || 6.10-12:5 ||
yadi cedrasaḥ catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśān pramāṇato grāsaṃ grasati hi niścitaṃ tadā daṇḍaṃ dhārayetvastrānna kṣaratītyarthaḥ || 6.10-12:6 ||
punaścatvāriṃśadbhāgapraveśato rasodare iti śeṣaḥ tadā pāyasākāraḥ kvathitadugdhākāro bhavetnibiḍatvāt || 6.10-12:7 ||
tadevāha bhavatītyādi || 6.10-12:8 ||
viṃśadbhāgāttriṃśadbhāgasya jāraṇato jalaukākāro bhavetrasa ityadhyāhāraḥ || 6.10-12:9 ||
punaḥ viṃśatyā viṃśadbhāgajāraṇena avipluṣo bhavedāsanānna calati || 6.10-12:10 ||
punaḥ ṣoḍaśāṃśātṣoḍaśāṃśabhāgajāraṇataḥ chedī bhavetkṣurikādibhiḥ chede kṛte pṛthaktvamāpnoti || 6.10-12:11 ||
ataḥ ṣoḍaśabhāgādūrdhvaṃ grāso durjaro bhavet || 6.10-12:12 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht6.13


pañcabhirebhirgrāsairghanasatvaṃ jārayitvādau |
garbhadrāve nipuṇo jārayati bījaṃ kalāṃśena || ṅṛht_6.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha pañcabhirityādi || 6.13:1 ||
evamuktaprakāreṇa punargarbhadrāve nipuṇaḥ rasodare abhradhātvādīnāṃ drutikaraṇe pravīṇaḥ pumān kalāṃśena grāsaṃ yojayet || 6.13:2 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā pañcabhiḥ pūrvoktaiḥ grāsaiścāru yathā syāttathā ghanasatvamādau jārayitvā pañcabhirgrāsairghanasatvajāraṇānantaraṃ ṣoḍaśabhāgena bījaṃ jārayedityarthaḥ || 6.13:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht6.14-15


<Änderungvon phys. Eig. von ḥgnachjāraṇa>
dhūmraściṭiciṭiśabdo maṇḍūkagatistathā sakampaśca |
niṣkampo bhavati raso vijñātavyo'bhrajīrṇastu || ṅṛht_6.14 ||
kapilo'tha nirudgārī vipluṣabhāvaṃ ca muñcate sūtaḥ |
niṣkampo gatirahito vijñātavyo'bhrajīrṇastu || ṅṛht_6.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

abhrakajīrṇarasalakṣaṇamāha dhūmra ityādi || 6.14-15:1 ||
abhrajīrṇarasa evaṃvidho bhavati abhraṃ jīrṇaṃ yasmin sa tathoktaḥ || 6.14-15:2 ||
vahniyogātprathamaṃ dhūmro dhūmrābho bhavati punaściṭiciṭiśabdo bhavati tato maṇḍūkagatirbhavati punastathā tena prakāreṇa dhṛte sati sakampo bhavati punarvahnau niṣkampaḥ svastho bhavati abhrasatvadrutasya lakṣaṇamiti || 6.14-15:3 ||
tadevāha kapila ityādi || 6.14-15:4 ||
punarevaṃvidho raso'bhrajīrṇo vijñātavyaḥ || 6.14-15:5 ||
kīdṛśaḥ kapilaḥ varṇataḥ nirudgārī sthirabhāvaḥ punaḥ sa raso vipluṣabhāvaṃ cañcalatvaṃ muñcate || 6.14-15:6 ||
punarniṣkampaḥ svasthaḥ punargatirahitaḥ pakṣacchinnaḥ iti lakṣaṇānyabhrajīrṇasya bhavanti || 6.14-15:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht6.16-18


<kacchapayantra>
jalapūrṇapātramadhye dattvā ghaṭakharparaṃ suvistīrṇam |
tadupari madhyagataḥ sūtaḥ sthāpyastataḥ kuḍye || ṅṛht_6.16 ||
laghulohakaṭorikayā kṛtapaṭamṛtsandhilepayācchādya |
pūrṇaṃ tadghaṭakharparamaṅgāraiḥ karīṣatuṣamiśraiḥ || ṅṛht_6.17 ||
svedanato mardanataḥ kacchapayantrasthito raso jarati |
agnibalenaiva tato garbhadrutiḥ sarvalohānām || ṅṛht_6.18 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

kacchapayantramāha jaletyādi || 6.16-18:1 ||
jalapūrṇapātramadhye iti jalapūrṇaṃ yatpātraṃ tasya madhye suvistīrṇaṃ sundarāyataṃ ghaṭakharparaṃ kumbhakhaṇḍaṃ dattvā tadupari kharparopari biḍamadhyagataḥ sūtaḥ sthāpyaḥ || 6.16-18:2 ||
kutra kuḍye mṛdā vinirmite viśālamukhe laghulohakaṭorikayā atilaghvī yā lohasya muṇḍādeḥ kaṭorikā pātraviśeṣaḥ tayā viḍāvṛtaṃ sūtamācchādya ā samantātsaṃrudhya || 6.16-18:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭayā kṛtapaṭamṛtsandhilepayā kṛtaḥ paṭamṛdbhyāṃ sandherlepo rodho yasyāḥ sā tayā || 6.16-18:4 ||
punastatkuḍyāntargataghaṭakharparamaṅgāraiḥ pūrṇaṃ kiṃviśiṣṭaiḥ karīṣatuṣamiśraiḥ karīṣo gomayasya cūrṇaṃ tuṣāḥ śālyāderdhānyasya tairmiśritairiti || 6.16-18:5 ||
kacchapayantre yadvihitaṃ tadāha svedanata ityādi || 6.16-18:6 ||
kacchapayantrastho raso jarati grāsamiti śeṣaḥ || 6.16-18:7 ||
kutaḥ svedanataḥ vahnau paritāpataḥ || 6.16-18:8 ||
na kevalaṃ svedanato mardanataśca viḍādinā ityadhyāhāraḥ || 6.16-18:9 ||
tato'gnibalenaiva sarvalohānāṃ svarṇādīnāmasminnantarāle garbhadrutirbhavati atrāgnibalameva mukhyam || 6.16-18:10 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht6.19


evaṃ dattvā jīryati na kṣayati raso yathā tathā kāryaḥ |
kṣayameti kṣāraviḍaiḥ sa tūparasairgrāsamudgirati || ṅṛht_6.19 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

jāraṇāyāṃ vidhidvayamāha evamityādi || 6.19:1 ||
evamamunā prakāreṇa kacchapayantre nidhāya jīryati grāsaṃ rasa iti śeṣaḥ || 6.19:2 ||
yathā raso na kṣayati kṣayaṃ nāpnoti tathā kāryo vidhiḥ iti śeṣaḥ || 6.19:3 ||
kāryasyāśrayarakṣaṇāyeti bhāvaḥ || 6.19:4 ||
punaḥ kṣārabiḍaiḥ kṣayameti nāśamāpnoti grāsa ityadhyāhāraḥ || 6.19:5 ||
punaḥ sa rasa uparasairgandhādibhiḥ grāsamutprāvalyena girati gilatītyarthaḥ || 6.19:6 ||
kṣārairamlaiśca gandhādyairmūtraiḥ paṭubhireva ca |
rasagrāsasya jīrṇārthaṃ biḍaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ |
iti paribhāṣā || 6.19:7 ||
biḍaśabdena piṇḍaḥ || 6.19:8 ||



____________________________________________________

ṅṛht7.1


<viḍa:: positive effects>

grāsaṃ na muñcati na vāñchati taṃ ca bhūyaḥ kāṃścidguṇānbhajati bhuktavibhuktimātrāt |
yajjīryate pracurakevalavahniyogāttasmādviḍaiḥ suniviḍaiḥ saha jāraṇā syāt || ṅṛht_7.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

biḍairjāraṇāyāḥ sugamatvamāha grāsamityādi || 7.1:1 ||
grāsaṃ na muñcati ca punastamevānyagrāsaṃ na vāñchati bhūyaḥ punaḥ bhuktavibhuktimātrātkāṃścidguṇānnityaṃ bhajati dadhāti grāsagrasanamātrātguṇaṃ karotītyarthaḥ || 7.1:2 ||
yato hetorbiḍairvakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaiḥ kṛtvā jīryate grāsamityarthaḥ || 7.1:3 ||
kutaḥ pracurakevalavahniyogātpracura udagro yaḥ kevalavahniyogaḥ śuddhāgniyogastasmāt || 7.1:4 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaiḥ sunibiḍaiḥ ghanaiḥ || 7.1:5 ||
yato biḍāḥ śreṣṭhāstasmādviḍaireva jāraṇā syātsugamatvāditi bhāvaḥ || 7.1:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht7.2


<viḍa:: forjāraṇa of copperleafs>
sauvarcalakaṭukatrayakākṣīkāsīsagandhakaiśca viḍaiḥ |
śigro rasaśatabhāvyaistāmradalānyapi jārayati || ṅṛht_7.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

biḍavidhānamāha sauvarcaletyādi || 7.2:1 ||
tāmradalānyapi jārayati buddhimāniti śeṣaḥ svarṇabījādīnāṃ kā katheti bhāvaḥ || 7.2:2 ||
kaiḥ kṛtvā biḍaiḥ kṛtvā || 7.2:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭairbiḍaiḥ sauvarcalakaṭukatrayakāṃkṣīkāsīsagandhakaiḥ sauvarcalaṃ rucakaṃ kaṭutrayaṃ śuṇṭhīmaricapippalyātmakaṃ kākṣī saurāṣṭrī kāsīsaṃ puṣpakāsīsaṃ gandhakaṃ lelitakametānyauṣadhāni yeṣu viḍeṣu santi te tathoktāḥ taiḥ || 7.2:4 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaiḥ śigro rasaśatabhāvyaiḥ śigroḥ saubhāñjanasya rasena śatavāraṃ bhāvyāḥ ye biḍāstaiḥ evaṃ niṣpannairviḍairniścitaṃ jāraṇā syāditi bhāvaḥ || 7.2:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht7.3


<viḍa:: forjāraṇa of gold>
sarvāṅgadagdhamūlakabhasma pratigālitaṃ surabhimūtreṇa |
śatabhāvyaṃ balivasayā tatkṣaṇato jāryate hema || ṅṛht_7.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

biḍāntaramāha sarvetyādi || 7.3:1 ||
sarvāṅgadagdhamūlakabhasma pratigālitamiti sarvāṅgena mūlatvakpatrapuṣpaphalena saha dagdhaṃ bhasmatāṃ prāptaṃ yanmūlakakandaṃ tadbhasma surabhimūtreṇa gojalena gālitaṃ kāryaṃ kṣāro grāhya ityarthaḥ || 7.3:2 ||
punarbalivasayā balamukhyā yā jalaukā maṇḍūkādīnāṃ vasā yathā ca |
bhekamatsyavasā yā tu kukkuṭasya vasāthavā |
bhāvyamebhiḥ kramādgandhaṃ śiśumāravasāpi vā || 7.3:3 ||
ekā balivasā samyaksūtasya bandhinī param |
rañjanaṃ caiva kurute maṇimūṣāvidhikramāt |
iti || 7.3:4 ||
balivasayā śataṃ śatavāraṃ kṣārabhūtaṃ bhasma bhāvyaṃ punaḥ tacchatabhāvyamauṣadhaṃ tatkṣaṇataḥ tatkālato hema svarṇaṃ jāryate raso grāsabhūtaṃ hema jaratīti biḍayogāditi bhāvaḥ || 7.3:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht7.4-7


<viḍa:: forjāraṇa>
kadalīpalāśatilaniculakanakasuradālivāstukairaṇḍāḥ |
varṣābhūvṛṣamokṣakasahitāḥ kṣāro yathālābham || ṅṛht_7.4 ||
ānīya kṣāravṛkṣān kusumaphalaśiphātvakpravālairupetān kṛtvātaḥ khaṇḍaśastān vipulataraśilāpiṣṭagātrātiśuṣkān |
dagdhvā kāṇḍaistilānāṃ karisurabhihayāmbhobhirāsrāvya vastrairbhasma tyaktvā jalaṃ tanmṛduśikhini pacedvaṃśapākena bhūyaḥ || ṅṛht_7.5 ||
tacchuṣyamāṇaṃ hi sabāṣpabudbudān yadā vidhatte kṣaṇabhaṅgurān bahūn |
tadā kṣipettryūṣaṇahiṃgugandhakaṃ kṣāratrayaṃ ṣaḍlavaṇāni bhūkhagau || ṅṛht_7.6 ||
dravyāṇi saṃmiśrya nivṛtya bhūtale vyavasthitaṃ śastrakaṭorikāpuṭe |
saṃsthāpayetsaptadināni dhānyagataṃ prayojyaṃ rasajāraṇādikam || ṅṛht_7.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha kṣāravṛkṣagulmauṣadhiviśeṣānāha kadalītyādi || 7.4-7:1 ||
yathālābhaṃ lābhamanatikramya eṣāṃ kṣāraḥ kartavyaḥ || 7.4-7:2 ||
te ke kadalīpalāśatilaniculakanakasuradālivāstukairaṇḍāḥ kadalī rambhā palāśo brahmavṛkṣaḥ tilāḥ pratītāḥ niculo vetasavṛkṣaḥ kanako dhattūraḥ suradālī devadālī vāstukaṃ kṣāraśākameraṇḍo vātāriḥ ete kṣārasaṃbhavāḥ || 7.4-7:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭā ete varṣābhūvṛṣamokṣakasahitāḥ varṣābhūḥ punarnavā vṛṣo vāsakaḥ mokṣako mokhāvṛkṣa iti pratītaḥ etaiḥ saṃyutā ityuddeśaḥ || 7.4-7:4 ||
kṣārakaraṇavidhānamāhānīyetyādi || 7.4-7:5 ||
prathamaṃ kṣāravṛkṣān pūrvoktānānīya vanāntarādgṛhītvā || 7.4-7:6 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭān kusumaphalaśiphātvakpalāśairupetān kusumāni prasūnāni pratītāni śiphā mūlaṃ tvaktvacā pravālā nūtanapallavāḥ etaiḥ pañcāṅgāhvayairupetān saṃyutān || 7.4-7:7 ||
khaṇḍaśaḥ bahuśakalān kṛtvā || 7.4-7:8 ||
na kevalaṃ khaṇḍaśaḥ vipulataraśilāpiṣṭagātrātiśuṣkān kṛtvā vipulatarā ativistīrṇā yā śilā tasyāṃ piṣṭāni gātrāṇi yeṣāṃ te'śuṣkā nirasāḥ tānevaṃvidhān kṛtvā || 7.4-7:9 ||
punastāneva ca tilānāṃ kāṇḍairnālaiḥ saha dagdhvā || 7.4-7:10 ||
punaḥ karisurabhihayāmbhobhiḥ hastigo'śvānāṃ mūtrairāsrāvya āplutya tadbhasma tyaktvā vastrairjalaṃ grāhyamiti śeṣaḥ || 7.4-7:11 ||
tajjalaṃ mṛduśikhini komalāgnau pacetkena vaṃśapākena vaṃśānāṃ samavahnitvāt || 7.4-7:12 ||
kṣārajalapākalakṣaṇamāha tadityādi || 7.4-7:13 ||
tajjalaṃ kṣārapānīyaṃ śuṣyamāṇaṃ satniścitaṃ yadā sabāṣpabudbudān vidhatte saha bāṣpeṇa jalātyayadhūmena vartante ye budbudāstān tadā kṣāro niṣpanno jñeyaḥ || 7.4-7:14 ||
punastadā tryūṣaṇaṃ śuṇṭhīmaricapippalyaḥ hiṅgu rāmaṭhaṃ gandhakaṃ lelītakaṃ punaḥ kṣāratrayaṃ sarjikāyavāgrajaṭaṅkaṇāhvayaṃ lavaṇāni ṣaṭsaindhavādīni bhūḥ tuvarī khagaṃ kāsīsametāni kṣipetetatkṣāreṇārdreṇa saha miśraṃ kāryamityarthaḥ || 7.4-7:15 ||
tathā ca dravyāṇi tryūṣaṇādīni saṃmiśrya ekīkṛtya nivṛtya ca saṃmardya śastrakaṭorikāpuṭe lohamayapātrasaṃpuṭe vyavasthitaṃ saptadināni dhānyagataṃ kasyaciddhānyasya madhyagataṃ sthāpayetkutra bhūtale pṛthivyā āsthāne tato'nantaraṃ tatsiddhaṃ rasajāraṇādikaṃ prati prayojyametadbiḍarūpaṃ rasajāraṇādiṣu praśastamityarthaḥ || 7.4-7:16 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht7.8


<viḍa:: forgarbhadruti>
jambīrabījapūrakacāṅgerīvetasāmlasaṃyogāt |
kṣārā bhavanti nitarāṃ garbhadrutijāraṇe śastāḥ || ṅṛht_7.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

jāraṇāyāṃ kṣāravidhānamāha jambīretyādi || 7.8:1 ||
kṣārā uktavṛkṣodbhavāḥ nitarāmatiśayena garbhadrutijāraṇe rasāntargrāsajāraṇe śastā utkṛṣṭā bhavanti || 7.8:2 ||
kutaḥ jambīrabījapūrakacāṅgerīvetasāmlasaṃyogātjambīraḥ pratītaḥ bījapūrako mātuluṅgaḥ cāṅgerī amlapatrikā vetasāmlaṃ cukrakameṣāṃ yo rasastasya saṃyogātetairbhāvitāḥ kṣārā biḍavatkāryakarā iti bhāvaḥ || 7.8:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht7.9


<viḍa:: how to use>
viḍamadharottaramādau dattvā sūtasya cāṣṭamāṃśena |
kuryājjāraṇamevaṃ kramakramādvardhayedagnim || ṅṛht_7.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rase viḍayojanamāha biḍamityādi || 7.9:1 ||
ādau prathamaṃ sūtasya rasasyāṣṭamāṃśena pūrvanirmitaṃ viḍamadharottaramadha uparibhāgaṃ ca dattvā evamamunā prakāreṇa jāraṇaṃ kuryātpunaḥ kramyate aneneti kramo biḍarūpaḥ tatkramaḥ paraṃparā tasmātagniṃ vivardhayetkarmakṛtityadhyāhāraḥ vāraṃvāraṃ biḍasaṃprayogādagnirvardhate || 7.9:2 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.1-2


<rañjana:: colouracc. to abhra>

jīrṇābhrako rasendro darśayati ghanānurūpiṇīṃ chāyām |
kṛṣṇāṃ raktāṃ pītāṃ sitāṃ tathā saṃkarairmiśrām || ṅṛht_8.1 ||
kṛṣṇābhrakeṇa balavadasitarāgairyujyate rasendrastu |
śvetai raktaiḥ pītairvahneḥ khalu varṇato jñeyaḥ || ṅṛht_8.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

dyotate divi candro'sau jīrṇe'bhre kāntimattayā |
tatheha tāmupāśritya śālate manujeṣu ca || 8.1-2:1 ||
susiddhabiḍadhātvādijāraṇena rasasya hi |
pītādirāgajananaṃ rañjanaṃ parikīrtitam |
iti paribhāṣā || 8.1-2:2 ||
abhrasya chāyāviśeṣamāha jīrṇābhraka ityādi || 8.1-2:3 ||
rasendro yadā jīrṇābhro bhavati tadā ghanānurūpiṇīṃ jīrṇābhrasamavarṇāṃ chāyāṃ darśayati || 8.1-2:4 ||
kṛṣṇe jīrṇe kṛṣṇāṃ rakte'bhre jīrṇe raktāṃ pīte pītāṃ tathā site śubhre sitāmevaṃ caturvidhāṃ chāyāṃ darśayati || 8.1-2:5 ||
punardvayostrayāṇāṃ vā caturṇāṃ saṃkare melāpe sati dvitricaturṇāmanurūpiṇīṃ chāyāṃ darśayatītyarthaḥ || 8.1-2:6 ||
abhrayogādvarṇaviśeṣamāha kṛṣṇetyādi || 8.1-2:7 ||
kṛṣṇābhrakeṇa jīrṇena raso balavān bhavettu punaḥ asitarāgaiḥ kṛṣṇarāgairyujyate || 8.1-2:8 ||
tathā śvetena śvetai rāgairyujyate raktena raktaiḥ pītena pītaiḥ kapilāgnerdivyasyāpi sarvasyādarśanarūpaṃ kṣayakarasya balairdravyāṇāṃ tādṛśo varṇo rasavarṇato varṇakovidairjñeyaḥ || 8.1-2:9 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.3


<mercurytransfersown colouronothersubstances>
atha nijakarme varṇaṃ na jahāti yadā sa rajyate rāgaiḥ |
kramaśo hi vakṣyamāṇairnirṇikto raṃjanaṃ kurute || ṅṛht_8.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rañjitarasapraśaṃsāmāha athetyādi || 8.3:1 ||
athānantaraṃ rasaḥ rasendro yadā vakṣyamāṇaiḥ śvetādibhiḥ rāgaiḥ rajyate tadā nijakarme varṇaṃ svakīyameva svābhāvikaṃ rūpaṃ na jahāti na tyajati punastaireva rāgaiḥ nirṇikto raktaḥ san rañjanaṃ kurute rāgadāyī bhavatīti || 8.3:2 ||
tathā no rakto na rañjanaṃ kurute avidyamānatvānniṣedhaḥ || 8.3:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.4


<(ṣtoffe, die versch. saṃskārasinduśieren)>
balamāste'bhrakasatve jāraṇarāgāḥ pratiṣṭhitāstīkṣṇe |
bandhaśca sāralohe sārakamatha nāgavaṃgābhyām || ṅṛht_8.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

abhrasatvādīnāṃ yoge rase vyavasthāmāha balamityādi || 8.4:1 ||
abhrakasatve'dhikaraṇe balamāste abhrasatvasaṃyogena raso balamāpnotītyarthaḥ || 8.4:2 ||
punastīkṣṇe lohabhede jāraṇarāgā jāraṇena tīkṣṇasthā rāgāḥ pratiṣṭhitā bhavantītyarthaḥ || 8.4:3 ||
sāraṇamatha nāgavaṅgābhyāmiti nāgavaṅgābhyāṃ duḥsaraṇaṃ sāraṇadravyaṃ sarata iti || 8.4:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.5


<potencyof tīkṣṇaloha>
krāmati tīkṣṇena rasastīkṣṇena ca jīryate kṣaṇādgrāsaḥ |
hemno yonistīkṣṇaṃ rāgān gṛhṇāti tīkṣṇena || ṅṛht_8.5 ||

------------------
*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sarvakāraṇaṃ tīkṣṇamāha krāmatītyādi || 8.5:1 ||
tīkṣṇena lohabhedena rasaḥ krāmati krāmaṇaṃ vidadhāti punastīkṣṇena kṛtvā grāsaḥ kṣaṇādalpakālato jīryate jāraṇamāpnoti punarhemnaḥ suvarṇasya yonirutpattisthānaṃ tīkṣṇamasti punaḥ rāgān rañjanabhāvān tīkṣṇena kṛtvā raso gṛhṇāti svasmin rāgān dadhātītyarthaḥ || 8.5:2 ||
