____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.6


<tīkṣṇaloha:: cāraṇa, jāraṇa>
tadapi ca daradena hataṃ kṛtvā mākṣikeṇa ravisahitam |
vāsitamapi vāsanayā ghanavaccāryaṃ ca jāryaṃ ca || ṅṛht_8.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tīkṣṇasya hiṅgulayogena guṇādhikyamāha tadapītyādi || 8.6:1 ||
api niścayena tattīkṣṇaṃ daradena hiṅgulena hataṃ māritaṃ vā mākṣikena svarṇamākṣikena ravisahitaṃ tāmrasaṃyutaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ hataṃ māritaṃ punarvāsanayā vāsanauṣadhena vāsitaṃ paribhāvitaṃ ghanavadabhravatcāryaṃ jāryaṃ ca satvābhravatnānyathā || 8.6:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.7


<rañjana:: suitable substances>
kāntaṃ vā tīkṣṇaṃ vā kāñcīṃ vā vajrasasyakādīnām |
ekatamaṃ sarvaṃ vā rasaraṃjane saṃkaro'bhīṣṭaḥ || ṅṛht_8.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tīkṣṇavadetānāha kāntamityādi || 8.7:1 ||
rasarañjane rasendre rāgakarmaṇi kāntaṃ cumbakapāṣāṇotthaṃ lohaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ veti samuccaye tīkṣṇaṃ lohabhedo vā kāñcīṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ vā vajrasasyakādīnāṃ vajrasasyakāvādiryeṣāṃ te teṣāṃ hīrakacapalādīnāmekatamaṃ tanmadhyādekatamaṃ sarvaṃ vā atrābhīṣṭaśabdasya pratyekaṃ saṃbandhaḥ hīrakādīni ratnāni sasyakādyā upadhātavaḥ || 8.7:2 ||
vā rasarañjane ayameva saṃkaraḥ sarveṣāṃ kāntādīnāṃ melāpaḥ sarvatrābhīṣṭaḥ || 8.7:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.8-9


<(chem. ড়otenśen versch. ṣtoffe)>
kuṭile balamabhyadhikaṃ rāgastīkṣṇe tu pannage snehaḥ |
rāgasnehabalāni tu kamale śaṃsanti dhātuvidaḥ || ṅṛht_8.8 ||
<mercury:: rañjana>
sarvairebhirlohairmākṣikanihataistathā drutairgarbhe |
viḍayogena tu jīrṇo rasarājo rāgamupayāti || ṅṛht_8.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sve sve vikāre vakṣyamāṇamāha balamityādi || 8.8-9:1 ||
dhātuvido rasavaidyā iti śaṃsanti || 8.8-9:2 ||
iti kiṃ kuṭile balamabhyadhikaṃ sarvādhikaṃ punastīkṣṇe'bhyadhiko rāgaḥ rañjanaṃ tu punaḥ pannage nāge'bhyadhikaṃ snehaḥ snigdhatvaṃ tu punaḥ rāgasnehabalāni trīṇyevoktāni kamale tāmre kuṭilatīkṣṇapannagānāṃ jāraṇādrase yathā balarāgasnehā bhavanti tathaikatāmrajāraṇāttrayo bhavantītyarthaḥ || 8.8-9:3 ||
rasabandhanopāyamāha sarvairityādi || 8.8-9:4 ||
biḍayogena pūrvoktena jīrṇo jāraṇamāpanno rasarājo bandhamupayāti bandhanamādatte || 8.8-9:5 ||
kaiḥ saha jīrṇaḥ ebhiḥ pūrvoktaiḥ sarvairlohairdhātubhiḥ || 8.8-9:6 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaiḥ mākṣikanihataiḥ svarṇamākṣikamāritaiḥ || 8.8-9:7 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaiḥ garbhe rasodare drutairvidrutairiti || 8.8-9:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.10-11


<mercury:: rañjana (witha kṛṣṭi)>
tālakadaradaśilābhiḥ snehakṣārāmlalavaṇasahitābhiḥ |
samakadviguṇatriguṇān puṭo vahedvaṃgaśastrādīn || ṅṛht_8.10 ||
raktasnehaniṣekaiḥ śeṣaṃ kuryādrasasya kṛṣṭiriyam |
cāraṇajāraṇamātrātkurute rasamindragopanibham || ṅṛht_8.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vakṣyamāṇadhātūnāṃ māraṇavidhānamāha tālaketyādi || 8.10-11:1 ||
vaṅgaśastrādīn vaṅgaṃ trapuṣaṃ śastraṃ tīkṣṇaṃ te ādiryeṣāṃ te tān || 8.10-11:2 ||
kābhiḥ saha puṭo vahettālakadaradaśilābhiḥ tālakaṃ haritālaṃ daradaṃ hiṅgulaṃ śilā manaḥśilā tābhiḥ || 8.10-11:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭābhiḥ snehakṣārāmlalavaṇasahitābhiḥ snehaḥ tailaṃ kaṅguṇitumbinyādīnāṃ kṣāraḥ svarjikādiḥ amlaṃ jambīrādi lavaṇāni saindhavādīni etaiḥ sahitābhiḥ || 8.10-11:4 ||
ekadhātuto dvādaśāṃśādārabhya yāvatsamakadviguṇatriguṇabhāgāḥ samāpyante tāvatpuṭo vahediti vyaktiḥ || 8.10-11:5 ||
puṭitadhātukṛtyamāha raktetyādi || 8.10-11:6 ||
uktadhāturgarbhitaṃ rasaṃ raktasnehaniṣekaiḥ rakto raktavargaḥ snehaḥ kaṅguṇyādīnāmanayorniṣekāḥ siñcanāni taiḥ śeṣaṃ dhātuvarjitaṃ kuryātiyaṃ rasasya kṛṣṭiḥ rasasya guṇākarṣaṇaṃ punariyaṃ kṛṣṭiḥ rasendramindragopanibhaṃ kurute atiriktavarṇaṃ kurute || 8.10-11:7 ||
kutaḥ cāraṇajāraṇamātrātpunaḥ puṭitadhātūnāṃ cāraṇaṃ ca jāraṇaṃ jīrṇaṃkaraṇaṃ ca tanmātrātvā cāraṇasya dravyasya jāraṇaṃ tanmātrātubhayoḥ pakṣayoreka evārthaḥ paramuktiviśeṣaḥ || 8.10-11:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.12


<mercury:: rañjana>
athavā kevalamamalaṃ kamalaṃ daradena vāpitaṃ kurute |
triguṇaṃ hi cīrṇajīrṇaṃ lākṣārasasannibhaṃ sūtam || ṅṛht_8.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mukhyatvena tāmrapraṃśasanamāha athavetyādi || 8.12:1 ||
athaveti vidhānāntare kevalaṃ śuddhaṃ vānyasaṃyogena varjitamamalaṃ jātapūrvaśodhanaṃ triguṇaṃ cīrṇajīrṇaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ pūrvaṃ cīrṇaṃ cāraṇamāptaṃ paścājjīrṇaṃ jāraṇamāpannamevaṃbhūtaṃ tāmraṃ sūtaṃ lākṣārasasannibhamalaktakaprabhaṃ kurute || 8.12:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.13


<mercury:: rañjana>
raktagaṇagalitapaśujalabhāvitatāpyagandhakaśilānām |
ekena vāpitamṛtaṃ kamalaṃ rañjayati rasarājam || ṅṛht_8.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha raktetyādi || 8.13:1 ||
raktagaṇena dāḍimakiṃśukabandhūkādinā pūrvoktena galitaṃ yatpaśujalaṃ gomūtraṃ tena bhāvitā yāstāpyagandhakamanaḥśilāstāsāṃ madhyādekena tāpyena svarṇamākṣikena vā gandhakena vā śilayā vāpitamṛtaṃ satkamalaṃ tāmraṃ rasaṃ rañjayati rāgaṃ dadātītyarthaḥ || 8.13:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.14


<durationof colouringacc. to substance>
bāhyo gandhakarāgo vilulitarāge manaḥśilātāle |
mākṣikasatvarasakau dvāveva hi rañjane śastau || ṅṛht_8.14 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rāgādhikārigandhakādīnāha || 8.14:1 ||
tatra gandhakaḥ kīdṛśaṃ rāgaṃ dadāti tatsvarūpamāha bāhya ityādi || 8.14:2 ||
gandhakarāgo bāhyo bahirbhavaḥ punarmanaḥśilātāle manaḥśilā manohvā tālaṃ haritālaṃ tāvubhe vilulitarāge cañcalarāge punarmākṣikasatvarasakau svarṇamākṣikasatvakharparikau dvāveva rañjane rasarāge śastau gandhakamanaḥśilātālebhyaḥ pradhānau atyadhikāvityarthaḥ || 8.14:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.15


<rasarañjak(15)>
kramavṛttau ravirasakau saṃśuddhau mūkamūṣikādhmātau |
triguṇaṃ cīrṇo jīrṇo hemābho jāyate sūtaḥ || ṅṛht_8.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pradhānayostāmrakharparayoḥ kṛtyamāha kramavṛttāvityādi || 8.15:1 ||
kramavṛttau ravirasakau saṃśuddhau viśeṣavidhānena śodhitau vā uttamajātīyau mūkamūṣikādhmātau andhamūṣāyāṃ dhmātau vahniyogīkṛtau kāryau etattriguṇaṃ yathā syāttathā cīrṇo jīrṇaśca sūtaḥ hemanibho jāyate etena cāraṇamāpannaḥ paścāttenaiva jāraṇāmāpanno rasaḥ svarṇaprabho bhavedityarthaḥ || 8.15:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.16


<rasarañjak(16)>
atha kṛṣṇābhrakacūrṇaṃ puṭitaṃ raktaṃ bhavettathā sakalam |
triguṇaṃ cīrṇo jīrṇo hemadrutisannibhaḥ sūtaḥ || ṅṛht_8.16 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

abhrakayogamāha athetyādi || 8.16:1 ||
atha rasakayogānantaraṃ kṛṣṇavarṇābhrakacūrṇaṃ śyāmavarṇābhrakarajaḥ tathā ravirasakavidhānena kharparakeṇa sahitaṃ puṭitaṃ satsakalaṃ samastaṃ raktaṃ bhavettadraktabhūtamabhraṃ triguṇaṃ yathā syāttathā cīrṇaḥ cāraṇamāpannastato jīrṇo jāraṇamāpannaśca san sūto hemadrutisannibhaḥ svarṇadravasadṛśo bhavedityarthaḥ || 8.16:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.17-18


<mercury:: rañjana>
triguṇena mākṣikeṇa tu kanakaṃ ca mṛtaṃ rasakatālayutam |
paṭusahitaṃ tatpakvaṃ haṇḍikayā yāvadindragopanibham || ṅṛht_8.17 ||
taccūrṇaṃ sūtavare triguṇaṃ cīrṇaṃ hi jīrṇaṃ tu |
drutahemanibhaḥ sūto rañjati lohāni sarvāṇi || ṅṛht_8.18 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha svarṇamāraṇamāha triguṇenetyādi || 8.17-18:1 ||
tu punaḥ triguṇena mākṣikeṇa svarṇatriguṇitena tāpyena yatkanakaṃ mṛtaṃ tatkanakamindragopako varṣākālīno raktavarṇo jīvaviśeṣaḥ tadvannibhā dīptiryasya tadindragopanibhaṃ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ || 8.17-18:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ kanakaṃ mṛtaṃ rasakatālayutaṃ rasakaṃ kharparaṃ tālaṃ haritālaṃ tābhyāṃ yutaṃ miśritaṃ satyanmṛtaṃ pañcatvamāptamityarthaḥ || 8.17-18:3 ||
punaḥ paṭusahitaṃ lavaṇamiśritaṃ punarhaṇḍikayā bhājanena pakvaṃ vahnipuṭitaṃ tadapi pūrvavat || 8.17-18:4 ||
mṛtakanakacūrṇaṃ sūtavare pārade triguṇaṃ cīrṇaṃ jīrṇaṃ cāritaṃ jāritaṃ ca satsūto drutahemanibho bhavetgalitasvarṇaprabha ityarthaḥ || 8.17-18:5 ||
evaṃ rañjito rasaḥ sarvalohāni dhātūni kṛtrimākṛtrimāni navavidhāni rañjati svarṇarūpāṇi karotītyarthaḥ || 8.17-18:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht8.19


<bījjāraṇkā mahattv>
patrādaṣṭaguṇaṃ satvaṃ satvādaṣṭaguṇā drutiḥ |
druteraṣṭaguṇaṃ bījaṃ tasmādbījaṃ tu jārayet || ṅṛht_8.19 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sarveṣāṃ dhāturasānāmuttarottaraṃ viśeṣatvamāha patrādityādi || 8.19:1 ||
patrādaṣṭaguṇaṃ satvamabhrapatre jīrṇe sati rase yo guṇastasmādaṣṭaguṇo guṇastatsatve ityarthaḥ punaḥ sattvātdrutistaddravarūpā aṣṭaguṇā punardruterbījaṃ dhātūparasasaṃyogajanitaṃ pūrvopavarṇitaṃ tadaṣṭaguṇaṃ tataḥ sarvotkṛṣṭatvādbījaṃ jārayennatvanyat || 8.19:2 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.1


<bīja (ḍefinition)>

iti rakto'pi rasendro bījena vinā na karmakṛdbhavati |
dvividhaṃ tatpītasitaṃ niyujyate siddhamevaitat || ṅṛht_9.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

himāṃśuriva dīptyāsau caṇḍāṃśuriva tejasā |
niśāhnoriva kartā ca durjanaiḥ sahanālayaḥ || 9.1:1 ||
nirvāpaṇaviśeṣeṇa tadvadvarṇaṃ bhavedyadā |
mṛdulaṃ citrasaṃskāraṃ tadbījamiti kathyate || 9.1:2 ||
iti paribhāṣā || 9.1:3 ||
bījapraśaṃsanamāha itītyādi || 9.1:4 ||
ityuktavidhānena rakto'pi rasendro bījena vinā karmakṛnna bhavati bījenaiva karmakārī syādityarthaḥ || 9.1:5 ||
tadbījaṃ dvividhaṃ dviprakāraṃ pītasitamekaṃ pītamaparaṃ sitaṃ śvetaṃ svarṇarūpyakriyāyogyamityarthaḥ || 9.1:6 ||
tadbījaṃ siddhaṃ sarvalakṣaṇopetaṃ rase pārade niyojyaṃ nāsiddhamiti || 9.1:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.2


<bīja:: śodhana necessary>
tasya viśuddhirbahudhā gaganarasoparasalohacūrṇaiśca |
dvividhaṃ bījaṃ tairapi nāśuddhaiḥ śudhyate vai tat || ṅṛht_9.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:
tasyetyādi || 9.2:1 ||
tasya bījasya viśuddhiḥ śodhanaṃ bahudhā bahuprakāraiḥ kṛtvā rasoparasadhātūnāṃ bahuvidhatvāt || 9.2:2 ||
kaiḥ kṛtvā gaganarasalohacūrṇaiḥ gaganamabhraṃ rasā vaikrāntādayo'ṣṭau vakṣyamāṇāḥ uparasā gandhakādayaḥ lohā dhātavaḥ teṣāṃ cūrṇāni taiḥ || 9.2:3 ||
ca punaḥ tairgaganarasoparasalohacūrṇairaśuddhaiḥ śuddhivarjitaistadbījaṃ na śudhyate śuddhihīnaṃ syātkāraṇānurūpaṃ kāryamitinyāyāt || 9.2:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.3


yaḥ punaretaiḥ kurute karmāśuddhairbhavedrasastasya |
avyāpakaḥ pataṃgī na rase rasāyane yogyaḥ || ṅṛht_9.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

aśuddhabījaprabhāvamāha ya ityādi || 9.3:1 ||
punarviśeṣeṇa yaḥ saṃskārakṛdetairgaganādyairaśuddhaiḥ kṛtvā rasasya karma kurute tasya puruṣasya rasaḥ pārado'vyāpako'saraṇaśīlo bhavetpataṅgī ūrdhvagāmī ca bhavetyantrasyādhobhāge na tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ || 9.3:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.4


<mahārasa>
vaikrāntakāntasasyakamākṣikavimalādridaradarasakāśca |
aṣṭau rasāstathaiṣāṃ sattvāni rasāyanāni syuḥ || ṅṛht_9.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

ete vakṣyamāṇā aṣṭau rasāḥ rasasaṃjñakāḥ syuḥ || 9.4:1 ||
ete ke vaikrāntakāntasasyakamākṣikavimalādridaradarasakāśceti vaikrāntaṃ vajrabhūmijaṃ rajaḥ kāntaṃ cumbakotthaṃ sasyakaṃ capalaṃ mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ vimalā raupyamākṣikamadri śilājatu daradaṃ hiṅgulaṃ rasakaḥ kharparikaḥ ete rasasaṃjñikā jñeyāḥ || 9.4:2 ||
tathā eṣāṃ satvāni sārāṇi rasāyanāni jarāvyādhināśanāni syuriti || 9.4:3 ||

____________________________________________________
ṅṛht9.5-6


<uparasa>
gandhakagairikaśilālakṣitikhecaramañjanaṃ ca kaṃkuṣṭham |

uparasasaṃjñakānāha gandhaketyādi || 9.4:1 ||
idaṃ vakṣyamāṇamuparasasaṃjñakaṃ syāt || 9.4:2 ||
kimidaṃ gandhakagairikaśilālakṣitikhecaramiti gandhakaṃ pratītaṃ gairikaṃ dhātugairikaṃ śilā manohvā ālaṃ haritālaṃ kṣitiḥ sphaṭikā khecaraṃ kāsīsametatsarvamiti ca punaḥ añjanaṃ nīlāñjanaṃ punaḥ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viraṅgamityaṣṭau uparasasaṃjñakā ityarthaḥ || 9.4:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.5-6


<sāraloha>
uparasasaṃjñakamidaṃ syātśikhiśaśinau sāralohākhyau || ṅṛht_9.5 ||
<pūtiloha>
tāmrāratīkṣṇakāntābhrasattvalohāni vaṅganāgau ca |
kathitāstu pūtisaṃjñāsteṣāṃ saṃśodhanaṃ kāryam || ṅṛht_9.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

śikhiśaśinau svarṇatārakau sāralohākhyau sāralohasaṃjñakāvityarthaḥ || 9.5-6:1 ||
pūtilohasaṃjñānāha tāmretyādi || 9.5-6:2 ||
tāmrāratīkṣṇakāntābhrasatvalohānīti tāmraṃ nepālakamāraṃ rājarītiḥ tīkṣṇaṃ sāraṃ kāntaṃ cumbakodbhavamabhrasatvaṃ gaganasāraṃ lohaṃ muṇḍametānīti punarvaṅganāgau ete pūtisaṃjñakāḥ kathitāḥ || 9.5-6:3 ||
teṣāṃ pūtilohasaṃjñakānāṃ śodhanaṃ samyaṅmalāpanayanaṃ kāryamiti || 9.5-6:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.7


<ṣaḍlavaṇa>
sauvarcalasaindhavakacūlikasāmudraromakabiḍāni |

lavaṇakṣārasaṃjñe āha sauvarcaletyādi || 9.6:1 ||
etāni vakṣyamāṇāni lavaṇasaṃjñānyāhuḥ ācāryāḥ iti śeṣaḥ || 9.6:2 ||
kāni sauvarcalasaindhavakacūlikasāmudraromakabiḍānīti sauvarcalaṃ rucakaṃ saindhavaṃ maṇikamanthāhvayaṃ cūlikaṃ kācalavaṇaṃ sāmudraṃ kṣārābdhijaṃ romakaṃ pratītaṃ biḍaṃ lavaṇaviśeṣaḥ etānīti || 9.6:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.7


<trikṣāra>
ṣaṭlavaṇānyetāni tu svarjīṭaṅkaṇayavakṣārāḥ || ṅṛht_9.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

punaḥ svarjī sarjikā ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ yavakṣāraḥ pratītaḥ ete kṣārāḥ kṣārasaṃjñikāḥ rasakarmaṇi ityadhyāhāraḥ || 9.7:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.8-9


<uparasa:: śodhana, sattvapātana>
sūryāvartaḥ kadalī vandhyā kośātakī ca suradālī |
śigruśca vajrakando nīrakaṇā kākamācī ca || ṅṛht_9.8 ||
āsāmekarasena tu lavaṇakṣārāmlabhāvitā bahuśaḥ |
śudhyanti rasoparasā dhmātāḥ sattvāni muñcanti || ṅṛht_9.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sadrāvakaṃ śodhakagaṇamāha sūryāvarta ityādi || 9.8-9:1 ||
sūryaṃ prati āvartako bhramaṇaṃ yasyetyevaṃvidhaḥ kadalī rambhā vandhyā phalarahitā karkoṭī kośātakī jālinī suradālī devadālī śigruḥ saubhāñjanaṃ vajrakando vadasūraṇakandaḥ nīrakaṇā jalapippalī kākamācī vāyasī iti gaṇaḥ śodhanadrāvaṇayogya iti || 9.8-9:2 ||
śodhanadrāvakāṇāṃ śodhanadrāvaṇavidhānamāha āsāmityādi || 9.8-9:3 ||
āsāṃ pūrvauṣadhīnāṃ madhyātekarasena ekasyā rasena rasoparasā vaikrāntādayo'ṣṭau rasāḥ gandhakādayo'ṣṭāvuparasāḥ bahuśo'nekavāraṃ bhāvitā gharmapuṭitāḥ kāryāḥ punarlavaṇakṣārāmlabhāvitāśca lavaṇāni sauvarcalādīni ṣaṭkṣārāḥ svarjikādayaḥ amlā jambīrādayaḥ tairbahuvāraṃ bhāvitāstīvragharmapuṭitā rasoparasāḥ śudhyanti doṣavarjitā bhavanti punaste dhmātāḥ santaḥ satvāni svīyasārāṇi muñcanti tyajantīti || 9.8-9:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.10-11


<vaikrānta:: śodhana>
svinnaṃ sakṣārāmlairdhmātaṃ vaikrāntakaṃ haṭhāddravati |

rasānāṃ krameṇa śodhanamāha svinnamityādi || 9.9:1 ||
sakṣārāmlairvaikrāntakaṃ svinnaṃ dolābhidhānena sveditaṃ kuryāttatsvinnaṃ vaikrāntaṃ haṭhātprābalyātdhmātaṃ satdravati sāraṃ muñcati drutamātraṃ satvanirgamamātrameva śudhyati pūrvasaṃbandhātdravati || 9.9:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.10-11


<kānta (= cumbaka):: śodhana>
taddrutamātraṃ śudhyati kāntaṃ śaśaraktabhāvanayā || ṅṛht_9.10 ||
<sasyaka:: śodhana>
sasyakamapi raktagaṇaiḥ subhāvitaṃ sneharāgasaṃsiktam |
śudhyati vāraiḥ saptabhirataḥ paraṃ yujyate kārye || ṅṛht_9.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha sasyakamityādi || 9.10-11:1 ||
sasyakamapi capalamapi raktagaṇairdāḍimakiṃśukabandhūkādibhiḥ subhāvitaṃ kuryāt || 9.10-11:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ sneharāgasaṃsiktaṃ snehaḥ kaṅguṇitumbunyādīnāṃ rāgo raktavarṇadravaḥ tābhyāṃ vahnau taptaṃ sasyakaṃ saṃsiktaṃ secitamiti ghṛtaiḥ saṃsiktaṃ komalaṃ bhāvanāyogyaṃ syāt || 9.10-11:3 ||
katibhirvāraiḥ subhāvitaṃ kuryātsaptabhiḥ saptasaṃkhyākaiḥ || 9.10-11:4 ||
ataḥ paraṃ kārye bījādike yujyate || 9.10-11:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.12


<vimala, rasaka, darada, mākṣika:: śodhana>
kṣāraiḥ snehairādau paścādamlena bhāvitaṃ vimalam |
śudhyati tathā ca rasakaṃ daradaṃ mākṣikamapyevam || ṅṛht_9.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha kṣārairityādi || 9.12:1 ||
vimalaṃ raupyamākṣikamādau prathamaṃ kṣāraiḥ svarjikādibhiḥ snehaistailaiḥ kaṅguṇyādīnāṃ bhāvitaṃ kuryātpaścādamlena jambīrādinā bhāvitaṃ kuryātevaṃvidhaṃ kṛtaṃ satśudhyati || 9.12:2 ||
tathā tenaiva vidhinā rasakaṃ kharparakaṃ śudhyati daradaṃ hiṅgulaṃ caivaṃ mākṣikamapyeva śudhyati || 9.12:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.13


<copper:: rañjana, śodhana>
tanurapi patraṃ liptaṃ lavaṇakṣārāmlaravisnuhikṣīraiḥ |
dhmātaṃ nirguṇḍīrasasaṃsiktaṃ bahuśo bhaveddhi raktaṃ ca || ṅṛht_9.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

svarṇarūpyayoḥ śodhanamāha tanurityādi || 9.13:1 ||
lavaṇetyādi || 9.13:2 ||
lavaṇāni sauvarcalādīni kṣārāḥ svarjikādayaḥ amlāḥ jambīrādayaḥ ravirarkaḥ snuhī sudhā tayoḥ kṣīrāṇi etaiḥ tanurapi sūkṣmamapi patraṃ dalaṃ sāralohākhyayoḥ iti śeṣaḥ liptaṃ dhmātaṃ satbahuśo'nekavāraṃ nirguṇḍīrase saṃsiktaṃ śephālīdrave siñcitaṃ kuryāt || 9.13:3 ||
tarhi svarṇaṃ rūpyaṃ ca raktamāraktachaviyutaṃ bhavedityarthaḥ caśabdācchudhyati || 9.13:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.14


<lead, tin, bronśe, copper:: śodhana>
śudhyati nāgo vaṃgo ghoṣo raviṇā ca vāramapi munibhiḥ |
nirguṇḍīrasasekaistanmūlarajaḥ pravāpaiśca || ṅṛht_9.14 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

lohaśodhanamāha śudhyatītyādi || 9.14:1 ||
nāgaḥ sīsakaḥ nirguṇḍīrasasekaiḥ śephālīrasaṃsecanaiḥ śudhyati nirdoṣo bhavati vaṅgaśca śudhyati || 9.14:2 ||
evaṃ raviṇā tāmreṇa saha ghoṣo'pi kāṃsyamapi śudhyati || 9.14:3 ||
kativāraṃ secanaiḥ munibhiḥ saptasaṃkhyākaiḥ tanmūlarajaḥ nirguṇḍīśiphācūrṇaṃ tatpravāpaiḥ galiteṣu nāgavaṅgaravighoṣeṣu rajo nikṣepaṇaiśca catvāraḥ śudhyantīti || 9.14:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.15


<tīkṣṇaloha:: rañjana>
raktagaṇagalitapaśujalabhāvitapuṭitaṃ hi rajyate tīkṣṇam |
<tīkṣṇaloha:: śodhana>
śudhyati kadalīśikhirasabhāvitapuṭitaṃ tribhirvāraiḥ || ṅṛht_9.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha raktagaṇetyādi || 9.15:1 ||
tīkṣṇaṃ sārākhyaṃ raktagaṇagalitapaśujalabhāvitaṃ puṭitaṃ satraktagaṇena saha galitaṃ militaṃ yatpaśujalaṃ gomūtraṃ tena bhāvitaṃ tato vahnipuṭitaṃ satrajyate rāgamāpnoti || 9.15:2 ||
punastīkṣṇaṃ kadalīśikhirasabhāvitapuṭitaṃ vā rambhācitrakarasabhāvitaṃ gharmapuṭitaṃ tato vahnipuṭitaṃ ca sattribhirvāraiḥ śudhyatītyarthaḥ || 9.15:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht9.16


<metals:: śodhana>
sarvaṃ śudhyati loho rajyati suragopasannibho vāpāt |
mākṣikadaradena bhṛśaṃ śulvaṃ vā gandhakena mṛtam || ṅṛht_9.16 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sāmānyena sarvalohānāṃ śodhanamāraṇamāha sarva ityādi || 9.16:1 ||
sarvo loho dhātuvargaḥ śudhyati mṛtaśca bhavati punaḥ rajyati ca || 9.16:2 ||
kena bhṛśamatyarthaṃ yathā syāttathā mākṣikadaradena tāpyahiṅgulena kṛtvā yo vāpaḥ galiteṣu loheṣu mākṣikadaradaprakṣepaṇaṃ tasmātsuragopasaṃnibha indragopasadṛśaḥ sarvo loho bhavet || 9.16:3 ||
vā śulbaṃ tāmraṃ mākṣikadaradavāpena suragopasannibhaṃ syātvā gandhena gandhakavāpena mṛtamapyeva syādityarthaḥ || 9.16:4 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.1


atha satvanirgamamabhidhāsyate |

<sattva:: importance of ~>

vaikrāntakāntasasyakamākṣikavimalādayo vinā satvam |
śuddhā api no dvandve milanti na ca tān raso grasati || ṅṛht_10.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

dattavānsatataṃ saukhyaṃ śuddhebhyaśca khalaṃ tathā |
duḥkhamṛtyapyamātmānaḥ kopasya samakārakam || 10.1:1 ||
vaikrāntādīnāṃ rasasaṃjñikānāṃ sattvapraśaṃsanamāha vaikrāntamityādi || 10.1:2 ||
vaikrāntakāntasasyakamākṣikavimalādayaḥ śuddhā api dvandve na milanti || 10.1:3 ||
vaikrāntaṃ vajrabhūmijaṃ rajaḥ kāntaṃ cumbakaṃ sasyakaścapalaḥ mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ vimalā raupyamākṣikamityādayo gandhakādayaścoparasaṃjñakā na milanti ekaśarīratāṃ nāpnuvanti || 10.1:4 ||
kva dvandve ubhayamelāpe || 10.1:5 ||
punastān śuddhānapi rasaḥ sūto na grasati || 10.1:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.2-3


<??>
nāganāsikābhidhānaṃ candrodakamamṛtamāptakāṭhinyam |
rasavaikrāntakamevaṃ badhnāti rasaṃ svasattvena || ṅṛht_10.2 ||
<śailodaka>
nānāvidhasaṃsthānaṃ nirjaraśikhariśikharasambhūtam |
dhārodambhasi śreṣṭhaṃ tadaśma śailodakaṃ prāpya || ṅṛht_10.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vaikrāntaprādhānyamāha nāgetyādi || 10.2-3:1 ||
evaṃlakṣaṇaṃ vaikrāntakaṃ jñātavyam || 10.2-3:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ nāganāsikābhidhānaṃ nāgānāṃ phaṇināṃ nāsikā eva abhidhānaṃ saṃjñā yasya tat || 10.2-3:3 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ candrodakaṃ candramasaḥ sambandhi yadudakaṃ balaṃ tasmādevāmṛtam || 10.2-3:4 ||
āptakāṭhinyaṃ prāptaṃ kāṭhinyaṃ yena pūrvametacca mṛdūtpannamityarthaḥ || 10.2-3:5 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ rasavaikrāntaṃ rasasaṃjñakaṃ vaikrāntaṃ rasaṃ sūtaṃ badhnāti || 10.2-3:6 ||
kena svasatvena svīyasāreṇeti || 10.2-3:7 ||
vaikrāntaprakāramāha nānetyādi || 10.2-3:8 ||
pūrvamupavarṇitaṃ vaikrāntaṃ nānāvidhasaṃsthānamasti nānāvidhamanekaprakāraṃ saṃsthānaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ yasya tatsaṃsthānaṃ vyañjanaṃ liṅgaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ cihnamākṛtiḥ iti mādhavanidānaṃ sitāsitaraktapītavarṇatvānnānāvidhasaṃsthānamityarthaḥ || 10.2-3:9 ||
punarnirjaraśikhariśikharasambhūtaṃ nirjarāṇāṃ devānāṃ yaḥ śikharī parvatastasya śikharaṃ śṛṅgaṃ tatra sambhūtamutpannam || 10.2-3:10 ||
nānāvidhasaṃsthānaṃ kutaḥ dhārodambhasi dhārābhirudanta unmattamambho yatra samaye tasmin varṣākāle śailodakaṃ śilāsaṃbandhi yadudakaṃ jalaṃ tatprāpya śreṣṭhaṃ tadaśma vaikrāntābhidhānaṃ nānāvarṇaṃ bhavati yataḥ śilodakasya nānāvidhatvam || 10.2-3:11 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.4-5


<vaikrānta:: sattva:: pātana>
bhastrādvayena haṭhato dhmātavyaṃ pañcamāhiṣasubaddham |
dattvā daśāṃśasvarjikapaṭuṭaṃkaṇaguñjikākṣārān || ṅṛht_10.4 ||
tadgacchati kaṭhinatvaṃ muñcati satvaṃ sphuliṅgakākāram |
muktānikaraprāyaṃ grāhyaṃ tatkācamadhivarjya || ṅṛht_10.5 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vaikrāntasatvapātanamāha bhastretyādi || 10.4-5:1 ||
tadvaikrāntaṃ pañcamāhiṣasubaddhaṃ dadhidugdhājyamūtraśakṛdbhiḥ pañcasaṃkhyākairmāhiṣaiḥ saha subaddhaṃ piṇḍākṛti kṛtaṃ satbhastrādvayena khallayugmena haṭhato balātdhmātavyam || 10.4-5:2 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā daśāṃśasarjikapaṭuṭaṅkaṇaguñjikākṣārān dattvā daśāṃśavibhāgena sarjikālavaṇasaubhāgyaraktikāyavakṣārān piṣṭavaikrānte kṣepyetyarthaḥ || 10.4-5:3 ||
dhmātaṃ satkiṃ syāttadāha tadityādi || 10.4-5:4 ||
sphuliṅgakākāraṃ vahnikaṇanibhaṃ satvaṃ sāraṃ muñcati || 10.4-5:5 ||
punastatsatvaṃ kaṭhinatvaṃ gacchati kaṭhinaṃ syādityarthaḥ || 10.4-5:6 ||
tatsatvamākārato muktānikaraprāyaṃ mauktikarāśisadṛśaṃ syātevaṃvidhaṃ satvaṃ kācamadhivarjya dūrīkṛtya tatnirmalaṃ grāhyamityarthaḥ || 10.4-5:7 ||
yugmam || 10.4-5:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.6


<vaikrānta:: bandhana of mercury>
rasavaikrāntakamevaṃ milati dvandvānvitaṃ samaṃ hemnā |
nirvyūḍhaṃ ghanasatvaṃ tena raso bandhamupayāti || ṅṛht_10.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vaikrāntasatvayogamāha rasetyādi || 10.6:1 ||
tadrasavaikrāntakaṃ sattvaṃ hemnā samaṃ svarṇena samabhāgaṃ dvandvānvitaṃ satdvandvamelāpakauṣadhasahitaṃ satevamamunā vidhānena milati rase iti śeṣaḥ || 10.6:2 ||
punastena satvena saha ghanasatvamabhrasāraṃ nirvyūḍhaṃ nirvāhitaṃ sattenobhayasatvasaṃyogena rasaḥ sūto bandhamupayāti bandhanamāpnoti || 10.6:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.7


<minerals:: sattvapātana>
vajrābhrakāntasasyakamākṣikaprabhṛtisakaladhātūnām |
pātayati satvameṣāṃ piṇḍī dhmātā dṛḍhāṅgāraiḥ || ṅṛht_10.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

satvapātanavidhānamāha vajretyādi || 10.7:1 ||
dṛḍhāṅgārairiti dṛḍhakathanātkhadirādīnāṃ pūrvoktatvādbhastrādvayena ca dhmātā satī vajrābhrakāntasasyakamākṣikaprabhṛtisakaladhātūnāṃ vajrasaṃjñakaṃ yadabhraṃ tadvajrābhraṃ kāntaṃ cumbakaṃ sasyakaṃ capalā mākṣikaṃ svarṇamākṣikamitiprabhṛtayaḥ sakaladhātavaḥ sarvoparasāsteṣāṃ piṇḍī satvaṃ pātayati || 10.7:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.8

<mākṣika:: sattva:: specialalchem. potency>
hitvā mākṣikasatvaṃ nānyeṣāṃ śaktirasti lohaghnī |
na patati tāvatsatvaṃ bhastrānte na yāvadāhriyet || ṅṛht_10.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

śaktimattvena mākṣikasatvapraśaṃsanamāha hitvetyādi || 10.8:1 ||
mākṣikasatvaṃ tāpyasāraṃ hitvā tyaktvā anyeṣāṃ rasoparasānāṃ śaktiḥ sāmarthyaṃ nāsti || 10.8:2 ||
kiṃbhūtā śaktiḥ lohaghnīti lohān hantīti vigrahaḥ || 10.8:3 ||
punastāvatsattvaṃ na patati yāvadbhastrā ante satvasamīpe na āhrīyeta na prāpyeta tasmādalpenāgninā satvāpravṛttirityarthaḥ || 10.8:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.9


<mākṣika:: sattva:: phys. properties>
raktaṃ mṛdu nāgasamaṃ satvaṃ yasmāddhi mākṣikātpatitam |
gandhāśmano'pi tadvatkāryaṃ yatnena mṛdubhāvam || ṅṛht_10.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mākṣikasatvamudāsyānyasatvapravṛttimāha raktamityādi || 10.9:1 ||
raktaṃ lohitaṃ nāgasamaṃ sīsakatulyaṃ mṛdu komalamevaṃvidhaṃ satvaṃ yasmāddhetormākṣikātpatati tāpyātnirgacchati tadvattasmāddhetorvā tasmādvidhānataḥ gandhāśmano gandhakasya yatnena mṛdubhāvaṃ kāryaṃ yathā gandhako'pi mṛdurbhavatītyarthaḥ || 10.9:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.10


<mākṣika:: sattva:: pātana>
lavaṇāmlena supuṭitaṃ mākṣikamamlena marditaṃ vidhinā |
muñcati soṣṇe grāsamāyasapātre tu piṣṭikā bhavati || ṅṛht_10.10 ||
------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mākṣikasatvavidhānamāha lavaṇetyādi || 10.10:1 ||
mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ lavaṇāmlena lavaṇaṃ mukhyatvātgranthāntarasāmyācca saindhavamamlo jambīrādiḥ tena marditaṃ punaramlena jambīrādinā vidhinā uktarītyā puṭitaṃ vahnau pratāpitaṃ satmuñcati pūrvaślokasaṃbandhātsattvamiti śeṣaḥ || 10.10:2 ||
itthaṃ patitaṃ satvaṃ grāhyam || 10.10:3 ||
lavaṇāmlena gandhakamapi mṛdu syāt || 10.10:4 ||
tu punaḥ soṣṇe āyasapātre vahnau tāpite lohapātre piṣṭikā bhavati raktavarṇarajorūpetyarthaḥ || 10.10:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.11


<sattva:: colourof diff. ~>
tutthāddhi tāpyajasamaṃ samasṛṣṭaṃ patati vai satvam |
abhravaikrāntakāntaprabhṛtīnāṃ tatra lohanibham || ṅṛht_10.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tutthādīnāṃ satvapātanamāha tutthādityādi || 10.11:1 ||
tutthāttutthaṃ śikhigrīvaṃ tasmāttāpyajasamamiti mākṣikasatvavatmākṣikasatvavidhānenāsya satvapāta ityarthaḥ || 10.11:2 ||
samasṛṣṭaṃ samaṃ tāpyena tulyaṃ varṇamārdavābhyāṃ sṛṣṭaṃ kathitamityarthaḥ || 10.11:3 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ tutthakasatvaṃ patati || 10.11:4 ||
abhraketyādi abhrakaṃ pratītaṃ vaikrāntaṃ rasavaikrāntaṃ kāntaṃ cumbakamitiprabhṛtīnāmityādīnāṃ tattvaṃ patrasatvapātanayoge lohanibhaṃ muṇḍavarṇamityarthaḥ || 10.11:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.12


<svarṇamākṣika (?):: sattvapātana (?)>
strīvajrīdugdhabhāvitameraṇḍasnehabhāvitaṃ śataṃ dhmātam |
evaṃ tribhiriha vāraiḥ śulvasamaṃ bhavati rañjakaṃ haimam || ṅṛht_10.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

punarmākṣikavidhānāntaramāha strītyādi |
tāpyaṃ strīvajrīdugdhabhāvitaṃ strī nārī vajrī sehuṇḍaḥ tayordugdhaṃ tena bhāvitaṃ gharmapuṭitaṃ kuryāt || 10.12:1 ||
punareraṇḍasnehena śataṃ śatavāraṃ bhāvitaṃ ca kuryāt || 10.12:2 ||
tato vāraistribhireva dhmātaṃ sathaimaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ tasyedaṃ haimaṃ satvaṃ śulbasamaṃ tāmranibhaṃ bhavati rañjakaṃ rase rāgadāyī syātkanake'pi ca || 10.12:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.13


<mākṣika, rasaka:: sattva:: pātana>
kadalīrasaśatabhāvitamadhvairaṇḍatailaparipakvam |
tāpyaṃ muñcati satvaṃ rasakaṃ caivaṃ trisantāpaiḥ || ṅṛht_10.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha kadalītyādi || 10.13:1 ||
tāpyaṃ mākṣikaṃ kadalīrasaśatabhāvitamadhvairaṇḍatailaparipakvamiti prathamaṃ rambhādraveṇa śatavāraṃ bhāvitaṃ paścātmadhvairaṇḍatailābhyāṃ saha paripakvaṃ samyakpācitaṃ satsatvaṃ muñcati || 10.13:2 ||
kaiḥ kṛtvā trisaṃtāpaiḥ trivāraṃ dhamanaiḥ || 10.13:3 ||
evaṃ rasakaṃ kharparakamapi satvaṃ muñcatīti || 10.13:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.14


<minerals:: sattva:: pātana>
ūrṇāṭaṅkaṇaguḍapuralākṣāsarjarasaiḥ sarvadhātubhiḥ piṣṭaiḥ |
chāgīkṣīreṇa kṛtā piṇḍī śastā hi satvavidhau || ṅṛht_10.14 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

satvapātane piṇḍīmāha ūrṇetyādi || 10.14:1 ||
ūrṇā iti ūrṇā meṣaroma ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ guḍaḥ pratītaḥ puro gugguluḥ lākṣā jatu sarjaraso rālaḥ etaiḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaiḥ sarvadhātubhiḥ rasoparasairvā svarṇādibhiḥ saha piṣṭaiḥ peṣitaiḥ punaḥ chāgīkṣīreṇa ajāpayasā kṛtā yā piṇḍī sā satvavidhau satvapātanakarmaṇi śastā pradhānā || 10.14:2 ||
dhāturasoparasānāṃ satvaṃ pātayatyeveti || 10.14:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht10.15-17


<minerals:: sattva:: pātana>
cūrṇitasatvasambhāraṃ triṃśatpalamādareṇa saṃgṛhya |
ṭaṃkaṇapalasaptayutaṃ guṃjāpalatritayayojitaṃ caiva || ṅṛht_10.15 ||
tilacūrṇakakiṭṭapalairmatsyairāloḍya dviraṃśayuktaiśca |
godhūmabaddhapiṇḍī gopañcakabhāvitā bahuśaḥ || ṅṛht_10.16 ||
koṣṭhakadhamanavidhinā tīvraṃ bhastrānalena tatpatati |
saṃdravati cābhrasattvaṃ tathaiva sarvāṇi satvāni || ṅṛht_10.17 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha cūrṇitetyādi || 10.15-17:1 ||
cūrṇitasatvasamānaṃ cūrṇitaṃ piṣṭaṃ yatsatvaṃ sāraṃ tatsamānaṃ viśuddhatvātsatvasamānaṃ dhāturasoparasacūrṇamiti śeṣaḥ || 10.15-17:2 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ viśuddhaṃ cūrṇamādareṇa prītyā ādau saṃgṛhya ṭaṅkaṇapalasaptayutaṃ kuryātsaubhāgyasya palaiḥ saptasaṃkhyākaiḥ sahitaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 10.15-17:3 ||
viśuddhe cūrṇe triṃśatpale saptapalaṃ saubhāgyaṃ yojyamevaṃ ṭaṅkaṇavidhānena ca punaḥ guñjāpalatritayena raktikāpalatritayaparimāṇena yojitaṃ kuryāditi || 10.15-17:4 ||
tilacūrṇakakiṭṭapalaiḥ tilaṃ pratītaṃ teṣāṃ cūrṇakaṃ kiṭṭaṃ muṇḍādīnāṃ malaṃ tayoḥ palaiḥ palamānairgodhūmabaddhapiṇḍī bahuśo bahuvāraṃ gopañcakabhāvitaṃ gavāṃ kṣīrājyadadhimūtraviṭkena bhāvitā kiṃ kṛtvā matsyairāloḍya matsyaiḥ kṣudrajalacarairāloḍya saṃmiśryetyarthaḥ || 10.15-17:5 ||
koṣṭhake koṣṭhikāyantre dhamanavidhinā utkṣipyotkṣipya dhamanena bhastrānalena tatsatvaṃ patati pūrvasaṃbandhāttāpyādīnāmiti śeṣaḥ || 10.15-17:6 ||
ca punarabhrasatvamabhrakātsāraṃ saṃdravati || 10.15-17:7 ||
tathaivoktavidhānena sarvāṇi samastāni satvāni sārāṇi patanti anuktānāmiti śeṣaḥ || 10.15-17:8 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht11.1-2


atha bījanirvāhaṇamārabhyate |

<svarṇamākṣika:: forproductionof gold(?)>

svīkṛtya sarvasarito gaṅgā jaladhau yathā tathā haimam |
praviśati rase gṛhītvā saṃmiliti sarvalohaguṇān || ṅṛht_11.1 ||
jīryati milati ca śulbe tatsatvaṃ kiṭṭatāṃ yāti |
suvṛttaḥ sadrṛtārambhaḥ sujñaḥ saṃjñānadarśakaḥ |
abhūcca dhanadharmajño hārī kuñjanasaṃpadām || 11.1:1 ||
ādau haimapraśaṃsāmāha svīkṛtyetyādi || 11.1:2 ||
haimaṃ svarṇamākṣikasatvaṃ saṃmiliti kiṃkṛtvā sarvalohaguṇān sarvaloheṣu samastadhātuṣu saṃmilitā miśritāḥ ye guṇāstān gṛhītvā tathā rase praviśati yathā gaṅgā sarvā nadyaḥ saritaḥ svīkṛtya aṅgīkṛtya jaladhau samudre praviśati || 11.1:3 ||
tāpyasatvādhikāramāha jīryatītyādi || 11.1:4 ||
tathaimaṃ tāpyasatvaṃ śulbe tāmre milati sati sattvaṃ jīryati jāraṇatvamāpnoti rase iti śeṣaḥ || 11.1:5 ||
tasmin satve śulbe milati sati kiṭṭatāṃ yāti lohamalasadṛśaṃ syāt || 11.1:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht11.2


<passende ṣtoffe frsvarṇa-, tārakriyā>
hemakriyāsu kariṇā trapuṇā tārakriyāsu nirvyūḍham || ṅṛht_11.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

prathamaṃ tatsatvaṃ kariṇā nāgena saha hemakriyāsu svarṇakāryeṣu nirvyūḍhaṃ rase nirvāhitaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 11.2:1 ||
punastrapuṇā vaṅgena saha tārakriyāsu rūpyakāryeṣu nirvyūḍhaṃ kuryātnāgavaṅgau sarvatra pītasitakāryeṣu praśastāvityarthaḥ || 11.2:2 ||

____________________________________________________
ṅṛht11.3


<ḥerstellungeinesbījas>
ghanasatvaṃ khalu raviṇā rasāyane dvaṃdvakaṃ yojyam |
raktagaṇapātabhāvitagirijatumākṣikagairikadaradaiḥ || ṅṛht_11.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

abhrasatvayogamāha ghanasatvamityādi || 11.3:1 ||
khalu niścaye vākyālaṅkāre vā ghanasatvamabhrasāraṃ raviṇā tāmreṇa saha rasāyane jarāvyādhināśane dvaṃdvakaṃ ghanasatvatāmraṃ yojyaṃ punaḥ raktagaṇapātabhāvitagirijatumākṣikagairikadaradaiḥ raktagaṇasya yaḥ pātaḥ pātanaṃ nikṣepo vā tena bhāvitāni gharmapuṭitāni girijatumākṣikagairikadaradāni girijatu śilājatu mākṣikaṃ pratītaṃ daradaṃ hiṅgulametairbījaśeṣaṃ kuryādityāgāmiślokājjñeyam || 11.3:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht11.4


<gold:: bīja>
mṛdulatāmrakāntaghanasatvaṃ mṛtanāgatīkṣṇakanakaṃ ca |
kurvīta bījaśeṣaṃ daradaśilātālamākṣikairvāpāt || ṅṛht_11.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha mṛduletyādi || 11.4:1 ||
mṛdulaṃ nepālasaṃjñikaṃ tāmraṃ kāntaṃ lohajāti ghanasatvamabhrasāraṃ punarmṛtaṃ nāgaṃ sīsakaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ lohajāti kanakaṃ hema etattrayaṃ bījaṃ śulbāditrayaṃ ca bījasaṃjñakaṃ daradaśilātālamākṣikairvāpātdaradaṃ hiṅgulaṃ śilā manohvā tālaṃ haritālametaiḥ kṛtvā vāpaḥ vahnitapte parikṣepaḥ tasmātbījaśeṣaṃ kurvīta ubhayorbīje abhrasatvahemnaḥ śeṣe kuryādityarthaḥ vā evaṃ kṛte yaccheṣaṃ tiṣṭhati tadbījamiti || 11.4:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht11.5


<gold, silver:: bīja>
mṛtanāgaṃ vaṅgaṃ vā śulvaṃ ghanasatvatārakanakaṃ vā |
dhmātaṃ tadeva sarvaṃ giriṇādhikaśodhanairvāpāt || ṅṛht_11.5 ||
------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha mṛtaṃ nāgamityādi || 11.5:1 ||
mayannāgaṃ sīsakaṃ tanmṛtanāgaṃ ca mṛtaṃ yadvaṅgaṃ raktagaṇāvāpamṛtaṃ ca yacchulvaṃ vā ghanasatvatārakanakaṃ ghanasatvamabhrasāraṃ tāraṃ rūpyaṃ kanakaṃ hema etannadugaṇasarvaṃ vā pratyekaṃ pṛthakgiriṇā śilājatunā saha dhmātaṃ kuryātadhikaśodhanaiḥ daradaśilātālairvāpātbījaśeṣaṃ kuryāditi pūrvasaṃbandhaḥ || 11.5:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht11.6-7


<gold:: bija (?)>
raktagaṇaṃ pītaṃ vā mākṣikarājāvartamatho vimalam |
ekatamaṃ vā gairikakunaṭīkṣitigandhakakhagairvā || ṅṛht_11.6 ||
nirvyūḍhaireva raso rāgādi gṛhṇāti bandhamupayāti |
mṛtalohoparasādyairnirvyūḍhaṃ bhavati śṛṅkhalābījam || ṅṛht_11.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

raktagaṇaṃ dāḍimakiṃśukādikaṃ vā pītagaṇaṃ yathā |
kiṃśukaḥ karṇikāraśca haridrādvitayaṃ tathā |
pītavargo'yamuddiṣṭo rasarājasya karmaṇi iti || 11.6-7:1 ||
vā mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ vā rājāvartaṃ rājavarto lājavarada iti bhāṣāyāmatha vimalaṃ tāramākṣikamityekatamaṃ sarvameva vā gairikakunaṭīkṣitigandhakakhagaiḥ gairikaṃ pratītaṃ kunaṭī manohvā kṣitiḥ sphaṭakī gandhakaḥ pratītaḥ khagaḥ kāsīsametairiti || 11.6-7:2 ||
etaiḥ pūrvoktaireva rase nirvyūḍhe raso rāgādi rañjanādi gṛhṇāti ādiśabdātsāraṇaṃ ca vijñeyaṃ punarbandhamupayāti bandhanamāpnoti punaḥ mṛtalohoparasādyaiḥ mṛtāśca te lohāśca dhātavaśca ta eva uparasā gandhakādyāḥ ādyaśabdātrasā api tairnirvyūḍhaiḥ kṛtvā śṛṅkhalābījamuttarottaraṃ rañjakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ || 11.6-7:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht11.8


<bīja forrañjana andbandhana of mercury>
āyasaśalākikābhyāmadvandvākhyaiśca saṅkarākhyaiśca |
nirvyūḍhaṃ rasalohairjāraṇakarmocitaṃ bhavati || ṅṛht_11.8 ||

------------------
*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rasalohairiti rasā vaikrāntādayo lohā dhātavaḥ pratītāstairnirvyūḍhaṃ kiṃviśiṣṭaiḥ advandvākhyaiḥ ekātmaiḥ saṃkarairvā sarvaiḥ saṃkaro'vakare ityamaraḥ evaṃ niṣpanne bījaṃ jāraṇayogyaṃ sadityarthaḥ || 11.8:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht11.9


bījamidaṃ raktagaṇe niṣecitaṃ tena kṛtavāpam |
cāritajāritamātraṃ sūtaṃ rañjayati badhnāti || ṅṛht_11.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣavidhyantaramāha bījamityādi || 11.9:1 ||
idaṃ niṣpannabījaṃ raktagaṇe niṣecitaṃ kuryāt || 11.9:2 ||
punastena raktagaṇena kṛtavāpaṃ kṛto vāpo yasmin tat || 11.9:3 ||
punastadbījaṃ cāritajāritamātraṃ pūrvaṃ ca paścātjāritaṃ santaṃ sūtaṃ rañjayati rāgaṃ prāpayati badhnāti ceti || 11.9:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht11.10


<bīja:: furtherprocessing>
raktasnehaviśodhitamṛtaloharasādibhistu sarveṣām |
bījānāṃ kuru vāpaṃ raktasnehe niṣekaṃ ca || ṅṛht_11.10 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha raktetyādi || 11.10:1 ||
raktasneha iti raktagaṇo dāḍimakiṃśukādikaḥ snehaḥ kaṅguṇyādīnāmetairviśodhitāḥ paścānmṛtā ye dhātavo rasādayaśca rasoparasāstaiḥ sarveṣāṃ bījānāṃ pūrvoktānāṃ vāpaṃ kuru rase iti śeṣaḥ vā rakte raktavarṇe snehe snehavarge niṣekaṃ ca vidhānadvayamidam || 11.10:2 ||

____________________________________________________
ṅṛht11.11


<silver:: bījas>
vaṅgābhramabhratāraṃ sitaśailamalāhatau ca sitavaṅgau |
raktaṃ sitatāpyahataṃ ramati nirvyūḍhavaṅgābhram || ṅṛht_11.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pītakriyāyāṃ bījānyuktāni atha śvetakriyāyāṃ bījānyāha vaṅgetyādi || 11.11:1 ||
vaṅgābhramiti vaṅgaṃ raṅgamabhraṃ gaganamete bījahetave iti śeṣaḥ || 11.11:2 ||
punarabhratāramabhraṃ gaganaṃ tāraṃ rūpyamiti ca sitaṃ śvetaṃ yacchailamalaṃ śilājatu tena āhatau samyakmṛtau sitavaṅgau tāraraṅgau kāryau punaḥ raktaṃ hema sitaṃ tāraṃ tāpyaṃ mākṣikaṃ tābhyāṃ hataṃ māritaṃ kuryāt || 11.11:3 ||
punastāraṃ rūpyaṃ nirvyūḍhaṃ vaṅgaṃ cābhraṃ ca yatra tadevaṃvidhaṃ kuryāt || 11.11:4 ||
etatsarvaṃ rase tārakriyāsu yojyamityarthaḥ || 11.11:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht11.12


nirvāhaṇavidhireṣaḥ prakāśito'śeṣadoṣaśamanāya |
bījānāmapyevaṃ ghanasatvaṃ yujyate prathamam || ṅṛht_11.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

bīje'bhrasatvaṃ pradhānamāha nirvāhaṇetyādi || 11.12:1 ||
eṣaḥ kimarthaḥ aśeṣadoṣaśamanāya dhātvādīnāṃ samastadoṣanāśanāyetyarthaḥ || 11.12:2 ||
evamamunā prakāreṇa yathā dhātunirvāhaṇavidhistathā bījānāṃ rase nirvāhaṇaṃ kuryātsarvabījanirvāhaṇe abhrakasatvaṃ prathamaṃ nirvāhyamiti jñeyam || 11.12:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht11.13


<crucible forbījanirvāhaṇa>
chāgāsthibhasmanirmitamūṣāṃ kṛtvaiva mallakākārām |
dalayoge ghanarandhrāṃ ṭaṅkaṇaviṣaguñjākṛtalepām || ṅṛht_11.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

nirvāhaṇavidhānamāha chāgetyādi || 11.13:1 ||
evaṃvidhāṃ mūṣāṃ kṛtvā dhātunirvāhaṇaṃ kuryātiti śeṣaḥ || 11.13:2 ||
kiṃbhūtāṃ chāgāsthibhasmanirmitamūṣāmiti chāgo bastastasyāsthīni tadbhasmanā nirmitā kṛtā yā mūṣā tām || 11.13:3 ||
punaḥ kiṃbhūtāṃ mallakākārāṃ gostanasadṛśīm || 11.13:4 ||
punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭāṃ ghanarandhrāṃ nibiḍachidrām || 11.13:5 ||
punaḥ ṭaṅkaṇaviṣaguñjākṛtalepāṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ viṣaṃ saktukaṃ guñjā raktikā tābhiḥ kṛto lepo yasyāṃ sā tām || 11.13:6 ||
evaṃbhūtā mūṣā dalayoge patramelane kāryetyarthaḥ || 11.13:7 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.1


atha dvandvamelanamabhidhāsyate |

<ṃotivation frdvandvamelana>

yāvannāṅgāṅgatayā na milanti lohāni sarvasattveṣu |
tāvatsarvāṅgaṃ na ca carati raso dvandvayogena || ṅṛht_12.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vācāṃ vilāsena sudhānukārī nu jagatkaroti |
kiṃcitsvayaṃ yatpuruṣatvameva sudhādvijihvāśritamityadoṣaḥ || 12.1:1 ||
atha dvandvayogaprakāramāha yāvadityādi || 12.1:2 ||
yāvadityavadhau || 12.1:3 ||
lohāni hemādīni nāgāṅgatayā bhujaṅgaśarīratayā na milanti sugamatvena ekaśarīratāṃ nāpnuvanti || 12.1:4 ||
keṣu sarvasattveṣu abhrādīnāṃ sāreṣu sattvasya kāṭhinyādvinopāyaṃ naikatāṃ yānti lohāni || 12.1:5 ||
atastāvadrasaḥ sarvāṅgaṃ na carati || 12.1:6 ||
kena kṛtvā satveṣu lohāni milanti dvandvayogena daradādinā vā guḍapuraṭaṅkaṇādineti || 12.1:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.2


<metals:: preparation fordv.~>
mākṣīkarasakasasyakadaradānyatamena vāpitaṃ loham |
saṃtyajati nibiḍabhāvaṃ satve saṃmilati sudhmātam || ṅṛht_12.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sattvamelanavidhānamāha mākṣiketyādi || 12.2:1 ||
lohaṃ svarṇādi || 12.2:2 ||
mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ rasakaṃ kharparikaṃ sasyakaṃ capalā daradaṃ hiṅgulametena catuṣkeṇa vā ebhyo'nyatamenoktarasoparasena vāpitaṃ sudhmātaṃ śobhanayuktyā dhmātaṃ ca satniviḍabhāvaṃ saṃtyajati sattve saṃmilati ca || 12.2:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.3


<dvandvamelāpaka:: preparation>
guḍapuraṭaṅkaṇalākṣāsarjarasairdhātakīsamāyuktaiḥ |
strīstanyena tu piṣṭaiḥ rasāyane dvaṃdvitaṃ yojyam || ṅṛht_12.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha guḍetyādi || 12.3:1 ||
idaṃ dvaṃdvitaṃ dvaṃdvīkṛtaṃ lohaṃ sattvena militaṃ rasāyane jarāvyādhināśane yojyam || 12.3:2 ||
kairdvandvamelāpakaiḥ kṛtvā dvaṃdvitamityāha guḍetyādi || 12.3:3 ||
guḍaḥ pratītaḥ puro gugguluḥ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ lākṣā jatu sarjaraso rālā etaiḥ dhātakīsamāyuktaiḥ dhātakī pratītā tatsamāyuktaiḥ punaḥ strīstanyena nārīdugdhena piṣṭairmarditaiḥ etairdvandvamelāpakaiḥ kṛtvā || 12.3:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.4


<dvandvamelāpaka>
ūrṇāṭaṅkaṇagirijatukarṇākṣimalendragopakarkaṭakaiḥ |
nārīpayasā piṣṭaiḥ sarve dvandveṣu hi milanti || ṅṛht_12.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sarve dvandveṣu satvaṃ prati dvandveṣu milanti ekībhavanti lohāni iti śeṣaḥ || 12.4:1 ||
etaiḥ kaiḥ ūrṇādibhiḥ || 12.4:2 ||
ūrṇā pratītā ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ girijatu śilājatu karṇākṣimalaṃ manuṣyasya indragopako jīvaviśeṣaḥ karkaṭakaścakulīraḥ syātkulīraḥ karkaṭakaḥ ityamaraḥ etaiḥ || 12.4:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaiḥ nārīpayasā strīdugdhena piṣṭaiḥ kalkitaiḥ trayo'pi dvandvamelāpakayogā iti || 12.4:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.5


<dva.~ of rasavaikrānta andgold=> bandhana of mercury>
rasavaikrāntakamevaṃ milati dvandvānvitaṃ samaṃ hemnā |
nirvyūḍhaṃ tatsatvaṃ tena raso bandhamupayāti || ṅṛht_12.5 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha rasetyādi || 12.5:1 ||
evamuktavidhānena rasavaikrāntakaṃ dvandvānvitaṃ svakīyadvandvasahitaṃ milati pṛthagbhāvaṃ tyajati || 12.5:2 ||
kena samaṃ hemnā saha || 12.5:3 ||
tadrasavaikrāntasattvaṃ hemnā saha nirvyūḍhaṃ kuryāttena rasavaikrāntasattvahemayogena raso bandhamupayāti baddho bhavatīti || 12.5:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.6


<dvandvamelāpaka>
śastaṃ sarvadvandve girijatulelītakendragopādyaiḥ |
mahiṣīkarṇamalādyaiḥ syādbījaṃ ṭaṅkaṇālaviṣaiḥ || ṅṛht_12.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha viśeṣavidhyantaramāha śastamityādi || 12.6:1 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ bījaṃ śastaṃ kva sarvadvandve || 12.6:2 ||
punaretaiḥ kṛtvā bījaṃ śastaṃ syāt || 12.6:3 ||
kaiḥ girijatulelītakendragopādyaiḥ girijatu śilājatu lelītako gandhakaḥ indragopaḥ surendragopo jīvaviśeṣaḥ ete ādyā yeṣāṃ taiḥ || 12.6:4 ||
na kevalametairmahiṣīkarṇamalādyaiśca mahiṣyāḥ karṇayormala ādyo yeṣāṃ te ādyaśabdānnāsākṣimalaṃ ca || 12.6:5 ||
punaṣṭaṅkaṇālaviṣaiḥ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyamālaṃ haritālaṃ viṣaṃ kandajametaiḥ piṣṭairdvandvamelāpaḥ syāditi punaḥ saṃbandhaḥ || 12.6:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.7


<dvandvamelāpaka>
madhusahitairapyetaistārābhraṃ milati tāpyakanakaṃ ca |
eraṇḍatailaṭaṅkaṇakaṃkuṣṭhaśilendragopaistu || ṅṛht_12.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha madhvityādi || 12.7:1 ||
etaiḥ pūrvoktairyogaiḥ madhusahitaiḥ kṣaudrayutaiḥ tārābhraṃ rūpyagaganaṃ milati || 12.7:2 ||
ca punastāpyakanakaṃ mākṣikasvarṇamidaṃ dvandvaṃ ca milati || 12.7:3 ||
na kevalaṃ pūrvoktayogairmilati punaretaireraṇḍatailaṭaṅkaṇakaṅkuṣṭhaśilendragopaiśca eraṇḍatailaṃ vātārisnehaḥ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viraṅgaṃ śilā manohvā indragopako jīvaviśeṣaḥ etaiśca madhusahitaiḥ kṛtvā dvandvaṃ milatītyavaśyam || 12.7:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.8


<melāpana of mercury, gold, andabhra>
sūtena śuddhakanakaṃ niṣpiṣya samābhrayojitaṃ kṛtvā |
pādena tu pūrvoktadvandvānyatamakaṃ kalpyam || ṅṛht_12.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tadvidhānamāha sūtenetyādi || 12.8:1 ||
prathamaṃ sūtena rasena saha śuddhakanakaṃ niṣpiṣya saṃmardya punaḥ samābhrayojitaṃ kṛtvā samaṃ ca tadabhraṃ ca tena yojitaṃ kṛtvā paścātpādena caturthāṃśavibhāgena pūrvoktadvandvānyatamakaṃ kalpyaṃ pūrvoktadvandvameraṇḍatailādikaṃ girijatvādikaṃ ca tebhyo'nyatamakaṃ dvandvaṃ yojyamiti || 12.8:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.9-10


<melāpana of goldandabhra>
rasoparasasya hemno dviguṇaṃ śuddhamākṣikaṃ dattvā |
svarasena kākamācyā rambhākandena mṛdnīyāt || ṅṛht_12.9 ||
kṛtamityetatpiṇḍaṃ hemābhraṃ milati vajramūṣāyām |
vidhyantaramāha rasetyādi || 12.9:1 ||
rasoparasasya vaikrāntagandhakādermadhye śuddhamākṣikaṃ nirdoṣaṃ tāpyaṃ hemno dviguṇaṃ kanakāddviguṇitaṃ dattvā dviguṇamākṣikayutaṃ hema dattvetyarthaḥ || 12.9:2 ||
etadrasoparasādikaṃ kākamācyā vāyasyāḥ svarasena mṛdnīyātmardanaṃ kuryāt || 12.9:3 ||
rambhākandena ca kadalīkandenāpītyarthaḥ || 12.9:4 ||
etatpūrvauṣadhaṃ piṇḍaṃ golākāraṃ kuryāt || 12.9:5 ||
itividhānena hemābhraṃ milati hematāpyaṃ ceti || 12.9:6 ||
kva vajramūṣāyām || 12.9:7 ||
tathāha |
mṛdastribhāgāḥ śaṇaladdibhāgau bhāgaśca nirdagdhatuṣopalādeḥ |
kiṭṭārdhabhāgaṃ parikhaṇḍya vajramūṣāṃ vidadhyātkhalu satvapāte |
iti || 12.9:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.10


<melāpana of abhrasattva withdiff. substances>
raviśaśitīkṣṇairevaṃ milanti gaganādisatvāni || ṅṛht_12.10 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

evamamunā vidhinā raviśaśitīkṣṇaiḥ saha ravistāmraṃ śaśī rūpyaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ lohajātiḥ etaiḥ sārdhaṃ gaganādisatvāni abhrādīnāṃ sārāṇi milantīti yugmam || 12.10:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.11

<melāpana forsaṅkarabījas>
saṅkarabījānāmapi vidhānamityādi gaganasatvayogena |
mākṣīkayogādanyaṃ yojyamavaśyaṃ tu sarvatra || ṅṛht_12.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

abhrasatvasyādhikāramāha saṃkaretyādi || 12.11:1 ||
saṅkarabījānāmapi vidhānaṃ kartavyārthopadeśa iti yāvat || 12.11:2 ||
ityādi pūrvoktaṃ tu punaḥ gaganasatvayogena abhrakasattvena sārdhaṃ mākṣīkayogādanyaṃ yojyamabhrasatvena saha mākṣīkaṃ na syāditi vyaktiḥ || 12.11:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht12.12


kāntamukhaṃ sarveṣāṃ sattvānāṃ melakaṃ prathamam |
pūrvoktakalkasahitaṃ mākṣīkamṛtanāgatālaśilam || ṅṛht_12.12 ||

iti paramahaṃsaparivrājakācāryaśrīmadgovindabhagavatpādaviracite rasahṛdayatantre dvandvādhikārātmako dvādaśo'vabodhaḥ ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

kāntetyādi || 12.12:1 ||
mākṣikeṇa mṛtaṃ yannāgaṃ tālaṃ haritālaṃ śilā manohvā ca tattathā pūrvoktakalkasahitaṃ yatkalkaṃ rasoparasādīnāṃ tena sahitaṃ yuktaṃ kāntamukhaṃ yathā |
abhāve'bhrakasattvasya kāntasattvaṃ pradāpayet |
kāntasatvasya vābhāve tīkṣṇalohaṃ tu dāpayet |
iti || 12.12:2 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht13.1


mākṣīkakāntatīkṣṇaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ mākṣīkamabhrakaṃ bījam |
mākṣīkakāntaśulbaṃ tīkṣṇābhrakaṃ mahābījam || ṅṛht_13.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

madhuni mādhava eva madhuvrataḥ prakurute mudito madhuraṃ ravam |
vararavo'pi satāṃ ca samāgamaṃ śabalatā kimupaiti na cārutām || 13.1:1 ||
saṃkarabījānāṃ madhye mahābījasaṃjñānyāha mākṣiketyādi || 13.1:2 ||
etattrikaṃ mahābījaṃ kimetatmākṣīkakāntatīkṣṇaṃ mākṣīkakāntaśulbaṃ tīkṣṇābhrakaṃ ca mākṣīkaṃ tāpyaṃ kāntaṃ cumbakaṃ śulbaṃ tāmraṃ tīkṣṇaṃ lohajātirabhrakaṃ gaganametattritayaṃ bījaṃ mahābījametattritrimukhaṃ pratyekaṃ mahāsaṃjñam || 13.1:3 ||
viśeṣo'tra |
bījapākaṃ pravakṣyāmi jāraṇārthaṃ rasasya tu |
sūtrakramo'yaṃ bījena samajīrṇena śudhyati |
iti || 13.1:4 ||
avāntaratvena ca pratyekaṃ dravyaṃ bījasaṃjñābhimatam || 13.1:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht13.2


mākṣīkakāntaśulbaṃ śulbābhrakamākṣikaṃ cāpi |
kāntābhrakamākṣīkaṃ tāpyakaśulbābhrakaṃ mahābījam || ṅṛht_13.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha mākṣīketyādi || 13.2:1 ||
mākṣīkakāntaśulbaṃ mākṣīkaṃ tāpyaṃ kāntaṃ cumbakaṃ śulbaṃ tāmrametadapi mahābījaṃ jñeyam || 13.2:2 ||
punaḥ śulbābhrakamākṣīkaṃ punaḥ kāntābhrakamākṣīkaṃ kāntaṃ kāntapāṣāṇamabhrakaṃ gaganaṃ mākṣīkaṃ tāpyaṃ tathā tāpyakaśulbābhrakametadapi ca mahābījaṃ jñeyamiti || 13.2:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht13.3


mākṣīkatīkṣṇaśulbaṃ tīkṣṇaśulbābhrakaṃ mahābījam |
mākṣīkakāntakanakaṃ kanakāruṇamākṣikaṃ mahābījam || ṅṛht_13.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha mākṣīketyādi || 13.3:1 ||
mākṣīkatīkṣṇaśulbaṃ mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ sāralohajātiḥ śulbaṃ tāmram || 13.3:2 ||
punastīkṣṇaśulbābhrakaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ sāraṃ śulbaṃ tāmramabhrakaṃ gaganam || 13.3:3 ||
punarmākṣīkakāntakanakaṃ mākṣīkaṃ tāpyaṃ kāntaṃ kāntapāṣāṇaṃ kanakaṃ svarṇam || 13.3:4 ||
punaḥ kanakāruṇamākṣikaṃ kanakaṃ svarṇamaruṇaṃ tāmraṃ mākṣikaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ ceti catuṣṭayaṃ mahābījaṃ pravarabījamityarthaḥ || 13.3:5 ||
caturṇāṃ pratyekaṃ mahābījasaṃjñeti || 13.3:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht13.4


mākṣīkatīkṣṇatāraṃ tārāruṇamākṣikaṃ caivam |
kāntaṃ tu śulbatāpyaṃ śulbābhratāpyakāṃcanaṃ cāpi || ṅṛht_13.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha mākṣīkatīkṣṇatāramiti || 13.4:1 ||
mākṣīkaṃ tāpyaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ sāraṃ tāraṃ rūpyam || 13.4:2 ||
punastārāruṇamākṣikamevamuktavidhānena idamapi tāraṃ rūpyamaruṇaṃ tāmraṃ mākṣīkaṃ tāpyam || 13.4:3 ||
punaḥ kānte cumbake'bhivyāpake adhikaraṇe śulbaṃ tāmraṃ tāpyaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ yatprayuktam || 13.4:4 ||
apīti niścayena || 13.4:5 ||
śulbābhratāpyakāñcanaṃ vā śulbaṃ tāmramabhraṃ gaganaṃ tāpyaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ kāñcanaṃ hema etaccatuṣṭayamapi mahābījaṃ jñeyam || 13.4:6 ||
mahābījasaṃbandhaḥ pratyekamiti vyaktiḥ || 13.4:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht13.5


kāntendusasyatāpyaṃ kāntābhrakatīkṣṇamākṣikaṃ caiva |
hemābhraśulbatāpyaṃ hemābhrakaśulbamākṣikaṃ vāpi || ṅṛht_13.5 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha kāntendusasyatāpyamiti || 13.5:1 ||
kāntaṃ kāntapāṣāṇamindustāraṃ sasyaṃ capalā tāpyaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ ceti || 13.5:2 ||
punaḥ kāntābhrakatīkṣṇamākṣikaṃ tathā hemābhraśulbatāpyaṃ punarhemābhrakaśulbamākṣikaṃ vā hema kanakamabhrakaṃ gaganaṃ śulbaṃ tāmraṃ mākṣīkaṃ tāpyametaccatuṣṭayamapi mahābījaṃ jñeyam || 13.5:3 ||
pratyekadravye bījasaṃjñā ceti dhvanyarthaḥ || 13.5:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht13.6


kāntābhraśulbatāpyaṃ saṅkarabījaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ || ṅṛht_13.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha kāntetyādi || 13.6:1 ||
kāntābhraśulbatāpyaṃ kāntaṃ cumbakamabhrakaṃ gaganaṃ śulbaṃ tāmraṃ tāpyaṃ mākṣikamityapi mahābījam || 13.6:2 ||
eteṣāṃ mahābījānāṃ cetsaṃkarabījaṃ pratyekaṃ dravyasya bījasaṃjñā sā tarhi catuḥṣaṣṭipramāṇā syādityarthaḥ || 13.6:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht13.7

<mahābīja:: production>
sarveṣāṃ bījānāmādau kṛtvā yathoktasaṃyogam |
śatavāpyaṃ yadvahnau drāvitaṃ hi bījaṃ viśuddhamidam || ṅṛht_13.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

bījavidhānamāha sarveṣāmityādi || 13.7:1 ||
ādau prathamaṃ sarveṣāṃ bījānāṃ yathoktaṃ saṃyogaṃ catuḥṣaṣṭīnāmuktasaṃjñānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ saṃyogamekatrīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā yadekatrīkṛtaṃ vahnau drāvitaṃ bhavati tatsarvaṃ śatavāpyaṃ bījaṃ siddhaṃ prayatnena syāditi śabdārthaḥ || 13.7:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht13.8


<cāraṇa, garbha- andbāhyadruti necessaryforbandhana>
na patati yadi ghanasatvaṃ garbhe no vā dravanti bījāni |
na ca bāhyadrutiyogastatkathamiha badhyate sūtaḥ || ṅṛht_13.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sūtabandhane hetūnāha netyādi || 13.8:1 ||
yadi garbhe rasodare ghanasattvamabhrakasāraṃ na patati na prāpnoti vā garbhe bījāni asminnadhyāye abhihitāni mākṣikakāntaśulbādīni yāvanno dravanti ca punarbāhyadrutistasyā yogo rase drutimelanaṃ na syāttattasmāddhetoḥ sūta ihāsyāṃ kriyāyāmasatyāṃ kathaṃ badhyate ghanatvaṃ dhatte || 13.8:2 ||
garbhadrutibāhyadrutibhyāṃ sūto badhyate niyatamityabhiprāyaḥ || 13.8:3 ||



____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.1


<productionof gold:: preparingthe mercury>

samādadhi ca yajjīrṇaṃ bījaṃ tenaiva cāvartatā kāryā |
kartavyaṃ tatkaraṇaṃ yasmātkhalu jāyate hema || ṅṛht_14.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

śaradi śāradamegho varṣati varṣāsu vārṣiko vārdaḥ |
madhusamaye parapuṣṭaḥ pravarajavaḥ śobhate satatam || 14.1:1 ||
bījajāraṇātkiṃ bhavati tadāha samādītyādi || 14.1:2 ||
samaṃ sūtatulyamadhiśabdādaparimitaṃ samādadhikaṃ yajjīrṇaṃ jāraṇamāptaṃ bījaṃ niṣpannabījaṃ tenaiva jīrṇena bījena saha āvartatā kāryā āvarta iti āvartaḥ || 14.1:3 ||
kiṃ tatyasmādvidhānāddhema kanakaṃ jāyate khalu niścayena tatkaraṇaṃ tasya vidhānasya karaṇam || 14.1:4 ||
samādijīrṇasya sūtasya rūpyādiṣu prayogātkanakaṃ bhavediti vyaktiḥ || 14.1:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.2-9


<rasabīja:: production>
pradrāvya śastrapātre gandhapādena sūtakaṃ dadyāt |
svarasena cauṣadhīnāṃ vaṭikāṃ niṣpiṣya kurvīta || ṅṛht_14.2 ||
saṃsthāpya lohaphalake chāyāśuṣkāṃ tu tāṃ vaṭikām |
laghulohakaṭorikayā sthagayitvā lepayetsudṛḍham || ṅṛht_14.3 ||
lavaṇārdramṛdā liptāṃ sudṛḍhaṃ kurvīta dhūmrarodhāya |
dattvā sudṛḍhāṃgārān bhastrādvayavahninaiva nirdhūme || ṅṛht_14.4 ||
tāvadyāvaddhmātā raktābhā khoṭikā bhavati |
apanīya tato'ṅgārān svabhāvaśītāṃ kaṭorikāṃ matvā || ṅṛht_14.5 ||
utkhanyotkhanya tataḥ kaṭorikāyā raso grāhyaḥ |
eṣaḥ mṛtasūtarājo golakavadbhavati ca sa sukhādhmātaḥ || ṅṛht_14.6 ||
śikhigalatāmekaraso'tidhmātaḥ kācaṭaṃkaṇataḥ |
triguṇaṃ vaṅgaṃ dadyātkrameṇa nāgamalpālpadānena || ṅṛht_14.7 ||
paścāddhemnā yojyaṃ rasabījaṃ sūtabandhakaram |
sūtamāraṇavidhānamāha pradrāvyetyādi || 14.2-7:1 ||
śastrapātre tīkṣṇamayapātre aupaśleṣike'dhikaraṇe saptamī || 14.2-7:2 ||
gandhapādena gandhasya turyāṃśavibhāgena sūtakaṃ dadyāttatpātroparibhāge dattaṃ pradrāvya vahninā iti śeṣaḥ || 14.2-7:3 ||
punaḥ oṣadhīnāṃ svarasena auṣadhyo granthāntare yathā |
mṛdnīyātkhalu tāvatpiṣṭamañjanasadṛśaṃ bhavedyāvat |
tadanu ca niyāmakānāṃ śatāvarīkandukīsudhādīnām |
iti || 14.2-7:4 ||
śatāvaryādīnāṃ svakīyarasena niṣpiṣya pramardya vaṭikāṃ badarākarāṃ kurvīteti || 14.2-7:5 ||
taccāha saṃsthāpyetyādi || 14.2-7:6 ||
tāṃ pūrvoktāṃ vaṭikāṃ chāyāśuṣkāṃ lohaphalake śastrapātre saṃsthāpya punaḥ laghulohakaṭorikayā pūrvoktalohaphalakātlaghvī yā lohakaṭorikā tayā sthagayitvā ācchādya dṛḍhaṃ gāḍhaṃ yathā syāttathā lepayetvakṣyamāṇeneti śeṣaḥ || 14.2-7:7 ||
taccāha lavaṇetyādi || 14.2-7:8 ||
tāṃ pūrvoditāṃ laghulohakaṭorikāṃ sudṛḍhaṃ yathā syāttathā lavaṇārdramṛdā lavaṇena saindhavādinā yutā yā ārdrā jalasiktā mṛttayā liptāṃ kurvīta || 14.2-7:9 ||
kimarthaṃ dhūmrarodhāya yathā yantrādbahirdhūmodgamo na syāt || 14.2-7:10 ||
punaḥ sudṛḍhāṅgārān khadirādīnāṃ dattvā bhastrādvayavahninā khalu dvayāgninā dhamyāditi agrimaślokasaṃbandhāt || 14.2-7:11 ||
kva sati nirdhūme sati rase dhūmaniḥsaraṇavarjite sati || 14.2-7:12 ||
taccāha tāvadityādi || 14.2-7:13 ||
sandhiliptā pūrvoktā lohaśarāvikā tāvadavadhau dhmātā kāryā yāvatkālapramāṇaṃ raktābhā raktadyutiyuktā khoṭikā bhavati khoṭasyeva ākṛtiryasyāḥ sā khoṭikā || 14.2-7:14 ||
tato'nantaraṃ kaṭorikāṃ svabhāvaśītalāṃ svato himāṃ matvā jñātvā punaraṅgārānapanīya apasārya kaṭorikāmutkhanya raso grāhya iti śeṣaḥ āgamiślokasaṃbandhāt || 14.2-7:15 ||
taccāha utkhanyetyādi || 14.2-7:16 ||
tato'nantaraṃ laghulohakaṭorikāṃ pūrvavarṇitāmutkhanyotkhanya prabalatvenotpāṭya rasaḥ sūto grāhyaḥ || 14.2-7:17 ||
utkhanyotkhanyeti kaṭhinataratvādvā atyādareṇa vīpsā || 14.2-7:18 ||
kutaḥ kaṭorikāsakāśāteṣa itthamutpanno mṛtasūtarājo jñeyaḥ || 14.2-7:19 ||
sa ca sukhādhmātaḥ san golakavadbhavati vajramūṣāyāmiti śeṣaḥ || 14.2-7:20 ||
kācaḥ pratītaḥ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ tataḥ raso'ti maryādāmatikramya dhmātaḥ sanekaraso bhavati samarasa ityarthaḥ || 14.2-7:21 ||
keṣāṃ śikhigalatāṃ śikhini galantīti vigrahaḥ śikhigalatāṃ dhātūnāmevaṃ galite rase triguṇaṃ vaṅgaṃ raṅgaṃ dadyāttato vaṅgadānānantaraṃ krameṇa alpamalpadānena nāgaṃ sīsakaṃ ca dadyāditi || 14.2-7:22 ||
taccāha paścādityādi || 14.2-7:23 ||
paścāttriguṇasīsakadānānantaraṃ hemnā kanakena saha rasabījaṃ mahābījaṃ yojyaṃ hemnā sārdhaṃ mahābījaṃ kīdṛgbhavati || 14.2-7:24 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.8-9


<mercury:: māraṇa>
tālakasūtenāpi ca kṛtvā vaṭikāṃ niyāmakauṣadhibhiḥ || ṅṛht_14.8 ||
evaṃ nigṛhya dhūmaṃ sudhiyā rasamāraṇaṃ kāryam |
rasabandhakaraṃ pāradabandhapradaṃ ca punaḥ tālakaṃ haritālaṃ sūto rasaḥ tenāpi niyāmakauṣadhibhiśca śatāvaryādibhiḥ pūrvoktābhirguṭikāṃ kṛtvā nigṛhya dhūmaṃ rundhitadhūmaṃ yathā syāttathā sudhiyā matimatā rasajñena evamamunā vidhinā rasamāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ pāradabandhaḥ kārya ityarthaḥ || 14.8:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.9


<mercury:: māraṇa>
athavā śilayā sūto mākṣikayogena vā siddhaḥ |
jāyeta śuklavarṇo dhūmarodhena tābhyāṃ vā || ṅṛht_14.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha athavetyādi || 14.9:1 ||
athavā vidhyantare śilayā manohvayā kṛtvā vā mākṣikayogena tāpyasaṃyogaṃ kṛtvā sādhitastālakayogavatsūto rasaḥ śuklavarṇo jayeta || 14.9:2 ||
kena dhūmarodhena saindhavārdramṛdā lepena vā tābhyāṃ śilāmākṣikābhyāmubhābhyāṃ tālakayogavatsādhitaḥ san sūtaḥ śuklavarṇo bhavediti || 14.9:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.10


<mercury:: mṛta:: medic. use>
mṛtaśulvatāpyacūrṇaṃ kāntayutaṃ tena rañjayetkhoṭam |
nirvyūḍhaṃ ghanasatvahemayutaṃ tadrasāyane yojyam || ṅṛht_14.10 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha mṛtetyādi || 14.10:1 ||
mṛtaśulbatāpyacūrṇaṃ mṛtaṃ ca yatśulbaṃ tāmraṃ tāpyaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ ca taccūrṇaṃ kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ kāntayutaṃ cumbakamiśritaṃ tena mṛtaśulbatāpyacūrṇena kāntayutena pūrvaṃ niṣpannaṃ khoṭaṃ rañjayet || 14.10:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ ghanasattvahemayutamabhrasatvasvarṇamiśritam || 14.10:3 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ tatkhoṭaṃ rasāyane jarāvyādhināśane yojyam || 14.10:4 ||
____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.11-12


<??>
balinā triguṇena rasātparpaṭikayutena marditaṃ sūtam |
niyāmakadivyauṣadhibhiśchāyāśuṣkā kṛtā vaṭikā || ṅṛht_14.11 ||
<??>
mūṣādhṛtaparpaṭikāmadhye saṃchādya nigūḍhasudṛḍhena |
dhmātaṃ gacchati khoṭaṃ hemayutaṃ sūtabandhakaram || ṅṛht_14.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha balinetyādi || 14.11-12:1 ||
triguṇena balinā gandhakena saha rasaṃ sūtaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ yathā syāttathā marditaṃ kuryātityadhyāhāraḥ || 14.11-12:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭena balinā parpaṭikayutena parpaṭiko lohaparpaṭikaḥ pratītastena yutena militena niyamasaṃskāroktāḥ niyamakāḥ divyauṣadhayaḥ śatāvarīpramukhāstābhiḥ tato vaṭikā chāyāśuṣkā kāryā chāyāgharmarūpā śuṣkā nīrasā tathā kāryā iti || 14.11-12:3 ||
anyaccāha mūṣetyādi || 14.11-12:4 ||
mūṣādhṛtaparpaṭikā mūṣāyāṃ yā parpaṭikā pūrvoktalohaparpaṭikā sā nigūḍhasudṛḍhena nigūḍhaścāsau sudṛḍhaśca tena mūlakādikṣārabiḍena kṛtvā madhye svāntaḥ ācchādya dhmātaṃ kriyate punastadūdhmātaṃ satkhoṭaṃ gacchati khoṭatvamāpnoti || 14.11-12:5 ||
tatkhoṭaṃ hemayutaṃ svarṇamilitaṃ sūtabandhakaraṃ syātrasabandhanapradamityarthaḥ || 14.11-12:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.13-14


<mercury:: māraṇa, khoṭa>
baliyuktā parpaṭikā mṛditā snuhyarkabhāvitā guṭikā |
madhye gartā kāryā sūtabhṛtācchāditā tadanu || ṅṛht_14.13 ||
bāhye dattvā nigaḍaṃ suliptamūṣodare dṛḍhaṃ nyastam |
sūtaḥ puṭito mriyate dhmātaḥ khoṭaṃ bhavatyeva || ṅṛht_14.14 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha balītyādi || 14.13-14:1 ||
pūrvoktā yā parpaṭikā lohaparpaṭikā baliyuktā gandhakamiśritā snuhyarkabhāvitā ca snuhī vajrī arko mandārastābhyāṃ bhāvitā plutā etayoḥ payaseti bhāvaḥ mṛditā ca gharṣitā ca evaṃ kṛtavidhānā parpaṭikā sati guṭikā vaṭikā kāryā madhye guṭikāntaḥ gartā kāryā sā gartā tataḥ sūtabhṛtā sūtapūritā satī tadanu gartakaraṇānantaramācchāditā kāryā parpaṭikayeti bhāvaḥ || 14.13-14:2 ||
taccāha bāhya ityādi || 14.13-14:3 ||
bāhye sūtodaraguṭikopari nigaḍaṃ dattvā suliptamūṣodare suliptā sāraṇakarmābhihitauṣadhīriti śeṣaḥ evaṃvidhā yā mūṣā tasyā yadudaraṃ tasmindṛḍhaṃ yathā syāttathā nigaḍaṃ nyastaṃ sthāpitaṃ kuryāditi śeṣaḥ || 14.13-14:4 ||
mūṣodaraguṭikāntasthaḥ sūtaḥ puṭito vahniyogātmriyate pañcatvamāpnoti || 14.13-14:5 ||
punaḥ dhmātaḥ san khoṭo bhavati || 14.13-14:6 ||
evāvyayamanyaniṣedhavāci || 14.13-14:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.15


<mercury:: māraṇa, khoṭa>
evaṃ tālaśilābhyāṃ mākṣikarasakaiśca daradaśikhisahitaiḥ |
mriyate puṭasaṃyogāddhmātaṃ khoṭaṃ kṛtaṃ vimalam || ṅṛht_14.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha evamityādi || 14.15:1 ||
evaṃ lohaparpaṭikāvidhānena tālaśilābhyāṃ dhmātaṃ satyutaṃ yatkhoṭaṃ tadvimalaṃ malavarjitaṃ syāt || 14.15:2 ||
ca punaḥ mākṣikarasakaistāpyakharparikaiḥ daradaśikhisahitaiśca hiṅgulaśikhimilitaiśca karaṇarūpairvimalaṃ ca punaḥ puṭayogādvahnisaṃparkātdhmātaṃ mriyate || 14.15:3 ||
ādau malalakṣaṇamuktam || 14.15:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.16


<mercury:: māraṇa>
kiṭṭakapurasaṃyogāddhmātaiḥ kiṭṭastu kiṭṭataḥ satvam |
nipatati satvaṃ rasasākaṃ janayati tadbhasma tasyāpi || ṅṛht_14.16 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:
taccāha kiṭṭapurasaṃyogāt || 14.16:1 ||
kiṭṭaṃ lohamalaṃ puro gugguluḥ tayoḥ saṃyogātdhmātaiḥ mākṣikarasadaradarūpaiḥ pūrvoktaiḥ kiṭṭo bhavetpunaḥ kiṭṭato rasasākaṃ sūtamiśritaṃ sattvaṃ sāraṃ nipatati tatsattvaṃ bhasma janayati utpādayati || 14.16:2 ||
tasyāpi bhasmanaḥ sattvaṃ ca nipatatītyarthaḥ || 14.16:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.17


<rañjana withsmoke andwithoutmercury(????)>
vaṅgarasagandhatālaṃ khaṭikāyā yogataḥ suparpaṭikā |
rañjayati satvatālaṃ dhūmena vināpi sūtam || ṅṛht_14.17 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha vaṅgarasagandhatālamiti || 14.17:1 ||
vaṅgaṃ trapu rasaḥ sūtaḥ gandhako baliḥ tālaṃ haritālametaccatuṣṭayaṃ khaṭikāyā yogataḥ khaṭikā citrakarajastasyā yogataḥ suparpaṭikāṃ pūrvoktāṃ lohaparpaṭikāṃ rañjayati sūtena vināpi kimuta rasamilitena tālasattveneti vyaktiḥ || 14.17:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht14.18


<bīja:: forrañjana>
evaṃ khoṭaṃ bījaṃ kṛtvā rañjanavidhinā surañjanaṃ kāryam |
triguṇaṃ rasasya hema saṃyojyaṃ tasya varabījam || ṅṛht_14.18 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mahābījānāṃ bījānāṃ ca viśeṣavidhānamāha evamityādi || 14.18:1 ||
evamuktavidhānena bījaṃ vidhāya rañjanavidhinā rañjanavidhānena surañjanaṃ kāryam || 14.18:2 ||
tatkārye rasasya sūtasya triguṇaṃ hema saṃyojyaṃ punastasya hemnaḥ triguṇaṃ varabījaṃ yojyamiti viśeṣavidhiḥ || 14.18:3 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.1


<abhra:: sattva:: druti:: causesbandhana of mercury>

vakṣye tvabhrakasatvādvimaladrutimakhilaguṇagaṇādhārām |
sā hi nibadhnāti rasaṃ saṃmilitā milati ca sukhena || ṅṛht_15.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

bhāratī bharatakhaṇḍamaṇḍitā pacaremānandamañjarī |
kastayā na rasamalaṃ kuto jayā vakrapadmamadhisthayā sadā || 15.1:1 ||
bāhyadrutipraśaṃsāmāha vakṣya ityādi || 15.1:2 ||
ahaṃ kaviḥ abhrakasattvādgaganasārato vimaladrutiṃ pakṣe vimalā cāsau drutiśceti vigrahaḥ || 15.1:3 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭāmakhilaguṇagaṇādhārāmakhilāśca te guṇagaṇāśca guṇapaṭalāśca teṣāṃ yā ādhārā tāṃ bahavo guṇāstiṣṭhantyasyāmiti vyaktiḥ || 15.1:4 ||
sā rasabhūtā drutiḥ rasaṃ sūtāṃ nibadhnāti niścayena badhnātītyarthaḥ || 15.1:5 ||
kiṃbhūtā satī || 15.1:6 ||
militā satī tulyamiśritā satī punaḥ drutiḥ sukhena milati patrāderdurmilāpatvāt || 15.1:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.2


<abhra:: sattva:: druti:: production>
vajravallyāḥ svarasena gaganaṃ sauvarcalānvitaṃ piṣṭam |
paripakvaṃ niculapuṭairnirlepaṃ bhavati rasarūpam || ṅṛht_15.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

drutividhānamāha vajravallyā ityādi || 15.2:1 ||
vajravallyāḥ svarasena svakīyena rasena gaganamabhrasattvaṃ sauvarcalānvitaṃ rucakasahitaṃ piṣṭaṃ kuryātiti śeṣaḥ || 15.2:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ gaganaṃ niculapuṭairvetasadravabhāvanābhiḥ pakvaṃ vahnipuṭitaṃ tatpakvaṃ sannirlepaṃ saṃparkavarjitaṃ rasarūpaṃ bhavati pāradasya rūpamityarthaḥ || 15.2:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.3


<abhra:: sattva:: druti:: production>
ajajalaśatapariplāvitakapitindukacūrṇavāpamātreṇa |
drutajātamabhrakasatvaṃ mūṣāyāṃ rasanibhaṃ bhavati || ṅṛht_15.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha ajetyādi || 15.3:1 ||
drujātarabhrakasattvamabhrakasatvaṃ gaganasāraṃ mūṣāyāṃ vajrasaṃjñāyāṃ drutaṃ satrasasaṃnibhaṃ bhavati pāradabhūtamityarthaḥ || 15.3:2 ||
kena ajajalaśataparibhāvitakapitindukacūrṇavāpamātreṇa ajaḥ chāgastasya jalena mūtreṇa śataṃ śatavāraṃ paribhāvitaṃ gharmapuṭitaṃ yatkapitindukacūrṇaṃ tasya vāpamātreṇa drute'bhrasattve vāpe || 15.3:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.4


<abhra:: sattva:: druti:: production>
nijarasaśataplāvitakañcukikandotthacūrṇakṛtaparivāpam |
drutamāste'bhrakasattvaṃ tadvatsarvāṇi lohāni || ṅṛht_15.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

abhrakasattvaṃ vahniyogena drutamāste dravarūpamevāvatiṣṭhate || 15.4:1 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ sattvaṃ nijarasaśataparibhāvitetyādi nijarasena svakīyadraveṇa paribhāvitaṃ yatkañcukikandotthacūrṇaṃ tasya āvāpena yathā śatabhāvitakañcukikandotthacūrṇena sattvaṃ drutamāste tadvatsarvāṇi lohāni drutāni tiṣṭhanti || 15.4:2 ||

____________________________________________________
ṅṛht15.5


<abhra:: sattva:: druti:: production>
gaganaṃ cikuratailaghṛṣṭaṃ gomayaliptaṃ ca kuliśamūṣāyām |
sudhmātamatra sattvaṃ plavati jalākāramacireṇa || ṅṛht_15.5 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha gaganetyādi || 15.5:1 ||
gaganamabhrasāraṃ cikuratailaghṛṣṭaṃ cikuratailaṃ keśatailaṃ pratītaṃ grantheṣu tena ghṛṣṭaṃ marditaṃ gomayaliptaṃ gomayena liptaṃ yathā syāttathā kuliśamūṣāyāṃ vajrābhidhānāyāṃ sudhmātaṃ satacireṇālpakālena jalākāraṃ bhavatītyanvayaḥ || 15.5:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.6


<abhra:: sattva:: druti:: generalrule>
gaganadrutiriha satve jñeyo hi rasasya sampradāyo'yam |
prathamaṃ nipātya satvaṃ deyo vāpo drute tasmin || ṅṛht_15.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sāmānyenābhradrutividhānamāha gaganetyādi || 15.6:1 ||
ihāsmin śāstre sattve gaganasāre jāte sāṅgatayā gaganadrutiḥ bhavatītyadhyāhāryam || 15.6:2 ||
ayaṃ pratyakṣāntargataḥ hi niścitaṃ rasasya sūtasya saṃpradāyo jñeyaḥ || 15.6:3 ||
punaḥ prathamādau sattvamabhrasāraṃ nipātya tasmindrute sattve vahninā dravarūpe sati vāpaḥ kāryaḥ kathitauṣadhīnāmiti śeṣaḥ || 15.6:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.7


<gold:: druti>
suragopakadeharajaḥ suradāliphalaiḥ samāṃśakairdeyaḥ |
vāpo drute suvarṇe drutamāste tadrasaprakhyam || ṅṛht_15.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha suvarṇadrutividhānamāha suragopakadeharaja iti || 15.7:1 ||
indragopaśarīracūrṇaṃ suradālīphalaiḥ samāṃśakaiḥ suragopacūrṇatulyabhāgaiḥ kṛtvā vāpo deyaḥ drute satyuparikṣepa iti suvarṇe vāpe kṛte suvarṇaṃ drutamāste kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ rasaprakhyaṃ jalatulyamityarthaḥ || 15.7:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.8


<gold:: druti>
atha nijarasaparibhāvitasuradālīcūrṇavāpamātreṇa |
drutamevāste kanakaṃ labhate bhūyo na kaṭhinatvam || ṅṛht_15.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha athetyādi || 15.8:1 ||
athendragopadevadālīyogakathanānantaraṃ kanakaṃ hema nijarasaparibhāvitaṃ yatsuradālīcūrṇaṃ tasya vāpamātreṇa galite hemni kṣepamātreṇa drutamevāste galitamevāvatiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ punaḥ kanakaṃ kāṭhinyaṃ sthiratvaṃ na labhate iti cirakālaprayojanam || 15.8:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.9


<tīkṣṇaloha:: druti>
suradālībhasma galitaṃ triḥsaptakṛtvātha gojalaṃ śuṣkam |
vāpena salilasadṛśaṃ kurute mūṣāgataṃ tīkṣṇam || ṅṛht_15.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:
atha tīkṣṇavidhānamāha suradālītyādi || 15.9:1 ||
suradālībhasmagalitaṃ suradālī devadālī tasyāḥ bhasma dāhasambhūtaṃ tena galitaṃ trisaptakṛtvā ekaviṃśativāraṃ gojalaṃ surabhimūtraṃ bhāvitaṃ kuryādityadhyāhāraḥ || 15.9:2 ||
atha mūṣāgataṃ vajrasaṃjñāyāṃ sthitaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ sāraṃ vāpena nikṣepaṇena jalasadṛśaṃ jalatulyaṃ kurute karmaviditi śeṣaḥ || 15.9:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.10


<mākṣikasattvadruti>
kūrmāsthiśilājatukameṣīmṛgago'sthivāpitā kāñcī |
jalasadṛśī bhavati sadā vāpo deyo drutāyāṃ tu || ṅṛht_15.10 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha mākṣikadrutividhānamāha kūrmetyādi || 15.10:1 ||
kūrmāsthi śilājatukaṃ pratītaṃ meṣī meṣapatnī mṛgo hariṇaḥ gauḥ pratītā pratīto vā teṣāṃ yānyasthīni tairnirvāpitā yā kāñcī svarṇamākṣikaṃ sā jalasadṛśī bhavati kiyatkālaparimāṇaṃ sadā nityaṃ punaḥ drutāyāṃ galitāyāṃ vāpo deyaḥ vāpo nikṣepaṇam || 15.10:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.11


<abhra:: druti:: alchem. properties>
abhrakadrutiraviśeṣā nirlepā yojitā samāsāttu |
āroṭaṃ rasarājaṃ badhnāti hi dvandvayogena || ṅṛht_15.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sāmānyābhradruteradhikāramāha abhradrutirityādi || 15.11:1 ||
gaganadravaḥ aviśeṣā sāmānyāpi vidhānena kṛtā nirlepā asparśā samā sūtatulyabhāgayojitā satī āroṭaṃ rasanajaṃ pūrvasaṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaṃ sūtaṃ badhnāti kena dvandvayogena ubhayamelāpakauṣadhena || 15.11:2 ||
hiśabdo yuktārtha iti || 15.11:3 ||

____________________________________________________
ṅṛht15.12


<receipe fordvandvana of druti andmercury>
kṛṣṇāgarunābhisitai rasonasitarāmaṭhairimā drutayaḥ |
soṣṇe milanti rasena mṛditāḥ strīkusumapalāśabījarasaiḥ || ṅṛht_15.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pūrvoktānāṃ melanamāha kṛṣṇetyādi || 15.12:1 ||
imā drutayaḥ soṣṇatuṣakarīṣādinā tāpite khalve mṛditāḥ satyo milanti rasena saha tathā kāryam || 15.12:2 ||
kaiḥ kṛtvā kṛṣṇāgarunābhisitaiḥ || 15.12:3 ||
kṛṣṇāgarukastūrikāghanasāraiḥ kṛtvā na kevalametaiḥ rasonasitarāmaṭhaiśca laśunaśarkarāhiṅgubhiḥ punaḥ strīkusumapalāśabījarasaiḥ strīkusumaṃ ca palāśasya bījāni ca rasaśceti dvaṃdvaḥ etaistribhiryogaiḥ pṛthagbhūtairmilanti sarvaiśceti || 15.12:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.13


<mercury:: baddha:: causeslongevity>
iti baddho rasarājo guñjāmātropayojito nityam |
ekenaiva palena tu kalpāyutajīvitaṃ kurute || ṅṛht_15.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

itthaṃ baddharasarājasya māhātmyamāha itītyādi || 15.13:1 ||
iti pūrvoktena drutividhānena baddho rasarājaḥ sūtaḥ ekena palena ṣoḍaśikayā kalpāyutaṃ jīvitaṃ kurute kalpānāmayutaṃ sahasraparimāṇaṃ jīvitamiti || 15.13:2 ||
kasmātnityaṃ yathā syāttathā guñjāmātropayogataḥ dinaṃ dinaṃ prati raktikāparimāṇasya rasasya yo'sau upayogastena || 15.13:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.14


atha pūrvoktagrāsakramājjarate raso vidhivat |
etāḥ pūrvadrutayo bhavanti rasarājaphaladāśca || ṅṛht_15.14 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhinā grāsajārito raso guṇavānityāha athetyādi || 15.14:1 ||
atha drutiyogānantaraṃ rasaḥ sūtaḥ pūrvoktagrāsakramātyojitakavalakramātvidhivatśāstroktavidhānena biḍādinā jarate ca punaretāḥ pūrvoktadrutayo rasarājaphaladā bhavanti sūte prayuktāḥ phaladāḥ syurityarthaḥ || 15.14:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.15


<drutijāraṇa => vedha potency>
samajīrṇaḥ śatavedhī dviguṇena rasaḥ sahasravedhī ca |
kramaśo hi koṭivedhī dviguṇadviguṇadruteścaraṇāt || ṅṛht_15.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

adhikadruterjāraṇādadhikaguṇe raso bhavatītyāha samajīrṇa iti || 15.15:1 ||
iti samā tulyabhāgā drutirjīrṇā yasminniti || 15.15:2 ||
drute dviguṇā yā drutiḥ tasyāścaraṇātkramaśaḥ koṭivedhī koṭyaṃśena vedhakaḥ syāt || 15.15:3 ||
drutibhāgo vṛddhau hyadhikaḥ syāditi vyaktiḥ || 15.15:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht15.16


ṣoḍaśa vā dvātriṃśadvā grāsā jīrṇāścatuḥṣaṣṭiḥ |
vidhyati tadā rasendro lohaṃ dhūmāvalokanataḥ || ṅṛht_15.16 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

grāsavṛddhyā guṇānāha ṣoḍaśetyādi || 15.16:1 ||
yadā ṣoḍaśagrāsā vā dvātriṃśadgrāsā vā catuḥṣaṣṭigrāsā jīrṇā jāraṇamāpannā bhavanti tadā rasendraḥ sūtaḥ lohaṃ dhātusaṃjñakaṃ vidhyati vedhaṃ karoti kutaḥ dhūmāvalokanataḥ dhūmasya yadavalokanaṃ darśanam || 15.16:2 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.1


<sāraṇa:: motivation>

iti rakto'pi rasendro jāritabījo'pi sāraṇārahitaḥ |
vyāpī na bhavati dehe loheṣvapyathavāpi hi ṣaṇḍhatāṃ yāti || ṅṛht_16.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

mano manīṣāyatamāyatātmanā samācaretkarma paropakārī |
arcīva śobhāṃ labhate parātparāṃ parāpavādādapi saṃnivṛttaḥ || 16.1:1 ||
sūte satailayantrasthe svarṇādikṣepaṇaṃ ca yat |
vedhādhikyakaraṃ lohe sāraṇaṃ tatprakīrtitam |
iti paribhāṣā || 16.1:2 ||
sāraṇamutkṛṣṭaṃ matvā stuvannāha itītyādi || 16.1:3 ||
iti pūrvoktena vidhānena rakto'pi rāgavānapi rasendraḥ sūtaḥ jaritabījo'pi jāritāni bījāni yasminniti sāraṇarahitaḥ sāraṇā vakṣyamāṇasaṃskārastena varjitaḥ vyāpī na bhavati dehe lohe ca vyāpako na syāthi niścitamathavāpi sāraṇārahito rasendraḥ ṣaṇḍhatāṃ yāti nirvīryatvamāpnoti || 16.1:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.2-5


<sāraṇātaila>
maṇḍūkamatsyakacchapameṣajalaukāhisūkarādīnām |
saṃyojyaikasya vasāṃ tataḥ pacetsāraṇātailam || ṅṛht_16.2 ||
jyotiṣmatīvibhītakakarañjakaṭutumbītailamekasmāt |
dviguṇitaraktakaṣāyaṃ kṣīreṇa caturguṇena pacet || ṅṛht_16.3 ||
<raktavarga>
dāḍimapalāśabandhukakusumarajanībhiraruṇasahitābhiśca |
mañjiṣṭhālākṣārasacandanasahito'pi raktavargo'yam || ṅṛht_16.4 ||
vidrumabhūnāgamalaṃ viṇmakṣikādhvāṅkṣaśalabhānāṃ ca |
karṇamalaṃ mahiṣīṇāṃ krameṇa kalkaṃ kalāṃśena || ṅṛht_16.5 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sāraṇāya vasātailamāha maṇḍūketyādi || 16.2-5:1 ||
maṇḍūko bhekaḥ matsyo jalacaraviśeṣaḥ kacchapaḥ kamaṭhaḥ pratītaḥ jalaukāḥ pratītāḥ ahiḥ sarpaḥ sūkaro varāhaḥ ādiśabdādgomahiṣagajoṣṭrakharanarakarkaṭaśiśumārā api grāhyāḥ || 16.2-5:2 ||
atha teṣāṃ madhye ekaikasya pṛthaktvena vasāṃ saṃyojya sāraṇaṃ tailaṃ sāraṇameva tailaṃ tatpacediti vahninā iti śeṣaḥ || 16.2-5:3 ||
sāraṇatailaviśeṣamāha jyotiṣmatītyādi || 16.2-5:4 ||
jyotiṣmatīvibhītakakarañjakaṭutumbītailaṃ jyotiṣmatī kaṅguṇī vibhītakaḥ kalidrumaḥ karañjaḥ pratītaḥ kaṭutumbī kaṭukā yā tumbī etāsāṃ tailamekamato dviguṇito yo raktakaṣāyaḥ raktagaṇasya kvāthaḥ taṃ niyojya pūrvasaṃbandhāt || 16.2-5:5 ||
kena saha caturguṇavasayā tathā tailataḥ caturguṇitena dugdhena saha pacetpākaṃ kuryāditi || 16.2-5:6 ||
sāraṇatailavidhānāya raktavargamāha dāḍimetyādi || 16.2-5:7 ||
dāḍimaṃ pratītaṃ palāśo brahmavṛkṣaḥ bandhūkapuṣpaṃ madhyāhnavikāśikusumaṃ rajanī haridrā etābhiḥ aruṇasahitābhiḥ aruṇamāraktaṃ yaddravyaṃ kārpāsakusumādikaṃ tatsahitābhiḥ || 16.2-5:8 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭo'yaṃ raktavargaḥ mañjiṣṭhālākṣārasacandanasahitaḥ mañjiṣṭhā pratītā lākṣārasaḥ alaktakaḥ candanaṃ raktacandanam || 16.2-5:9 ||
sāraṇataile kalkamāha vidrumetyādi || 16.2-5:10 ||
vidrumaṃ latāmaṇirbhūnāgamalaṃ gaṇḍūpadapurīṣaṃ makṣikādhvāṅkṣaśalabhānāṃ makṣikā jīvaviśeṣaḥ dhvāṅkṣāḥ kākāḥ śalabhaḥ pataṅgaḥ iti haimaḥ teṣāṃ viṭśakṛtpunarmahiṣīṇāṃ karṇamalaṃ krameṇa kalāṃśena ṣoḍaśāṃśena kalkaṃ prativāpaṃ dattvā pūrvatailamuttārayet || 16.2-5:11 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.6-8


<sāraṇa>
paṭagālitaṃ gṛhītvā sūtaṃ sampūrṇadīrghamūṣāyām |
tadanu khalu taptataile pradrāvya samaṃ kṣipedbījam || ṅṛht_16.6 ||
mūṣāvaktraṃ sthagayellatādvayaprotavitatanaddhena |
tailārdrapaṭena tato bījaṃ prakṣipya samakālam || ṅṛht_16.7 ||
piśitānuguṇaṃ bījaiḥ sāraṇavidhinā niyojitaḥ sūtaḥ |
akṣīyamāṇo milati ca bījairbaddho bhavatyeva || ṅṛht_16.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

siddhatailakṛtyamāha paṭetyādi || 16.6-8:1 ||
tatsiddhatailaṃ paṭagālitaṃ vastrapūtaṃ gṛhītvā tadanu tatpaścāt || 16.6-8:2 ||
sampūrṇadīrghamūṣāyāṃ gostanākārāyāṃ taptataile koṣṇasāraṇataile sūtaṃ kṣipet || 16.6-8:3 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā bījaṃ samaṃ samabhāgaṃ rasatulyaṃ yadbhāvyaṃ mahābījaṃ tatbhāvitaṃ kṛtvetyarthaḥ || 16.6-8:4 ||
sāraṇayantrasya vidhānamāha mūṣetyādi |
samakālamekakālaṃ yathā syāttathā bījaṃ mūṣāntarnikṣipya tato'nantaraṃ mūṣāvaktraṃ sthagayetācchādayet || 16.6-8:5 ||
kena tailārdrapaṭena sāraṇatailārdravastreṇa || 16.6-8:6 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭena latādvayena vastracīradvayena protaṃ ca tadvitataṃ vistīrṇaṃ ca tatnaddhaṃ baddhaṃ tena || 16.6-8:7 ||
akṣīyamāṇo milati na kṣīyata ityarthaḥ || 16.6-8:8 ||
sūte milati baddho jñeyaḥ bījaiḥ saha milito baddho bhavatītyarthaḥ || 16.6-8:9 ||
pītādivarṇakathanenāpi kartuṃ sūcitam || 16.6-8:10 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.9


<mercury:: pratisāraṇa>
tadvadgabhīramūṣe sāraṇatailārdrameva rasarājam |
sūtāddviguṇaṃ kanakaṃ dattvā pratisārayettadanu || ṅṛht_16.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sūtabījasāraṇānantaraṃ kanakasāraṇamāha tadvadityādi || 16.9:1 ||
tadvatpūrvavidhānena gabhīramūṣe dīrghamūṣāyāṃ sāraṇatailārdraṃ sāraṇatailāplutameva niścayena rasarājaṃ kuryāditi śeṣaḥ || 16.9:2 ||
tadanu tatpaścātsūtāddviguṇaṃ yatkanakaṃ hema tadatra dattvā pratisārayetsāraṇaṃ kuryātpūrvavat || 16.9:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.10


<mercury:: anusāraṇa>
bījena triguṇena tu sūtakamanusārayetprakāśastham |
vidhyantaramāha bījenetyādi || 16.9:1 ||
prakāśasthaṃ prakāśamūṣāgataṃ sūtaṃ triguṇena bījena anusārayetpratisārayet || 16.9:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.10


īṣannāgaṃ deyaṃ trividhāyāṃ sāraṇāyāṃ tu || ṅṛht_16.10 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

kiṃ kṛtvā īṣadalpaṃ nāgaṃ dattvā trividhāyāṃ sāraṇāyāmevaṃ vidheyamiti || 16.10:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.11-12


<sāraṇāyantra>
kṛtvā mūṣāṃ dīrghāṃ bandhitatribhāgapraṇālikāṃ tāṃ ca |
tasyāgre prakaṭamūṣā sacchidrā sudṛḍhamṛttikāliptā || ṅṛht_16.11 ||
tasminprakṣipya rasaṃ sāraṇatailānvitaṃ tapte |
pradrāvya tulyakanakaṃ kṣipte'sminmilati rasarājaḥ || ṅṛht_16.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sāraṇayantramāha kṛtvetyādi || 16.11-12:1 ||
prathamaṃ dīrghāṃ mūṣāṃ kṛtvā ca punaḥ tāṃ bandhitatribhāgapraṇālikāṃ bandhitā tribhāge praṇālikā yasyāḥ sā tāṃ ca kṛtvā tasyāgre yantrasyāgre praṇālikāyāṃ mūṣāntarityarthaḥ || 16.11-12:2 ||
sudṛḍhamṛttikāliptā sacchidrā randhrasahitā prakaṭamūṣā prakāśamūṣā kāryeti yantram || 16.11-12:3 ||
tasminyantre sāraṇatailānvitaṃ rasaṃ prakṣipya tato'nantaraṃ tulyaṃ kanakaṃ pradrāvya gālayitvā tasminneva tapte yantre kṣipte sati raso milati ekatāṃ yāti || 16.11-12:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.13-16


<sāraṇāyantra>
kṛtvā nalikāṃ dīrghāṃ ṣaḍaṃgulāṃ dhūrtakusumasaṃkāśām |
mūṣāpyadho vilagnā kartavyā vai mṛdā lepyā || ṅṛht_16.13 ||
aparā sūkṣmā nalikā kāryā saptāṃgulā sudṛḍhā |
madhye praviśati ca yathā tadvatkāryā ca dṛḍhamukhā || ṅṛht_16.14 ||
tasminsūtaḥ kṣiptaḥ sāraṇatailānvito madanaruddhamukhaḥ |
tadanu bṛhattamayā hema pradrāvya hemakoṣṭhikayā || ṅṛht_16.15 ||
tasminmadhye kṣiptvā nalikāgramadhomukhīṃ kuryāt |
antarūrdhvaṃ bhārākrāntāṃ sarati raso nātra saṃdehaḥ || ṅṛht_16.16 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyadyantramāha kṛtvetyādi || 16.13-16:1 ||
pūrvavaddīrghāṃ dhūrtakusumasaṃkāśāṃ dhattūrapuṣpasaṃkāśāṃ pūrvayantranalikāyāḥ sthāne evaṃvidhāṃ ṣaḍaṅgulāṃ nalikāṃ kuryāditi vyaktiḥ || 16.13-16:2 ||
mūṣāpi adho vilagnā nalikāyāstalabhāge mūṣā vilagnā saṃlagnā kāryā || 16.13-16:3 ||
sā ca mṛdā mṛtsnayā lepyā || 16.13-16:4 ||
punarapi aparā sūkṣmā nālikā saptāṅgulā saptāṅgulaparimāṇā sudṛḍhā manoharakaṭhinā kāryā yathā madhye ṣaḍaṅgulanālikāntaḥ praviśati tadvattathā kāryā || 16.13-16:5 ||
kiṃbhūtā dṛḍhamukhā dṛḍhaṃ mukhaṃ yasyāḥ sā evaṃrūpā tasmin saṃsiddhe yantre sāraṇatailānvitaḥ sūtaḥ kṣiptaḥ sanmadanaruddhamukhaḥ kāryaḥ madanena sikthakena ruddhaṃ mudritaṃ mukhaṃ yasya saḥ || 16.13-16:6 ||
tadanu tatpaścāthemakoṣṭhikayā hemno yā koṣṭhī eva koṣṭhikā tayā hema svarṇaṃ pradrāvya drāvayitvā tatra kṣipetityadhyāhāryam || 16.13-16:7 ||
tasminyantre madhye'ntaḥ nalikāgraṃ nalikāyāḥ saptāṅgulāyā agrabhāgaṃ kṣiptvā adhomukhīṃ kuryātpunarūrdhvaṃ bhārākrāntāṃ kuryāt || 16.13-16:8 ||
iti kṛte sati rasaḥ sarati hemnā milati na saṃdehaḥ niyatamityarthaḥ || 16.13-16:9 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.17-18


<sāraṇāyantra>
kṛtvāṣṭāṃgulamūṣāṃ dhūrtakusumopamāṃ dṛḍhāṃ ślakṣṇām |
aparā madhyagatāpi ca sacchidrā ca saptāṃgulā kāryā || ṅṛht_16.17 ||
niruddhatāṃ ca kṛtvā sūtaṃ prakṣipya tailasaṃyuktam |
nirdhūmaṃ karṣāgnau sthāpya mūṣāṃ susaṃdhitāṃ kṛtvā || ṅṛht_16.18 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyadyantravidhānamāha kṛtvetyādi || 16.17-18:1 ||
