aṣṭāṅgulamūṣāmaṣṭāṅgulaparimāṇadīrghāṃ dhūrtakusumopamāṃ kanakapuṣpasadṛśāṃ dṛḍhāṃ kaṭhināṃ ślakṣṇāṃ masṛṇāmevaṃvidhāṃ mūṣāṃ kṛtvā aparā dvitīyā saptāṅgulā saptāṅgulaparimāṇadīrghā sacchidrā randhrayuktā sā madhyagatā antaḥpraviṣṭā kāryā apītyavaśyamiti mūṣādvayayantraṃ siddham || 16.17-18:2 ||
taccāha pūrvoktāyāmantaḥpraviṣṭāyāṃ saptāṅgulāyāṃ sūtaṃ tailasaṃyuktaṃ sāraṇatailasahitaṃ prakṣipya niruddhatāṃ ca kṛtvā nirdhūmaṃ yathā syāttathā karṣāgnau mūṣāṃ sthāpya punaḥ kiṃ kṛtvā susaṃdhitāṃ sandhimudritāṃ kṛtvā pūrvavatsārayedityarthaḥ || 16.17-18:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.19-21


<sāraṇāyantra>
vitastimātranalikāpi kāryā sudṛḍhe tadagrato mūṣe |
uttānaikā kāryā niśchidrā chidramudritā ca tanau || ṅṛht_16.19 ||
dattvā sūtaṃ pūrvaṃ sāraṇatailānvitaṃ nidhāpya bhuvi |
uttānāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ tasyāṃ bījaṃ samāvṛtya || ṅṛht_16.20 ||
svacchaṃ jñātvā ca tatastadbījaṃ chidrasaṃsthitaṃ kuryāt |
bījaṃ sūtasyopari nipatati badhnātyasaṃdeham || ṅṛht_16.21 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyayantravidhānamāha mūṣe kārye || 16.19-21:1 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭe tadagrato vitastimātranalike vitastiparimāṇe nalike yayoste evaṃvidhe sudṛḍhe ubhe kārye ityabhiprāyaḥ || 16.19-21:2 ||
tayormadhye ekā mūṣā uttānā kāryā aparā nimneti bhāvaḥ || 16.19-21:3 ||
uttānā kiṃviśiṣṭā niśchidrā nirvraṇā chidramudritā chidraṃ mudritaṃ yasyāṃ tanau mūṣāśarīre iti || 16.19-21:4 ||
taccāha pūrvaṃ prathamaṃ sūtaṃ yantre pūrvokte sāraṇatailānvitaṃ dattvā bhuvi nidhāpya tasyāmuktāyāmuttānāyāṃ mūṣāyāṃ bījaṃ mahābījaṃ samāvṛtya dravīkṛtya dattvetyarthaḥ || 16.19-21:5 ||
tato'nantaraṃ bījaṃ svacchamamalaṃ dravarūpaṃ jñātvā chidrasaṃsthitaṃ kuryātchidrāntaḥ kṣipedityabhiprāyaḥ chidrāntaḥkṣepaṇātbījaṃ rasasyopari patati sati sūtamasaṃdehaṃ yathā syāttathā badhnāti bīje chidrāntaḥkṣepaṇānantaraṃ chidramacchidraṃ syādityarthaḥ || 16.19-21:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.22-23


<sāraṇā (6)>
sā ca prakāśamūṣā nyubjā kāryārdhāṅgulasaṃniviṣṭā |
nalikā kāryā vidhinā ūrdhve sūtastvadho bījam || ṅṛht_16.22 ||
mūṣāṃ nirudhya vidhinā dhmātā koṣṭhe drutaṃ bījam |
jñātvā parivartya tato nibadhnāti sūtarājaṃ ca || ṅṛht_16.23 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

setyādi || 16.22-23:1 ||
punaḥ sā vitastimātranalikā prakāśamūṣā ardhāṅgulasuniviṣṭā mūṣāntaḥ praviṣṭā nyubjā adhomukhī kāryā tasyāḥ prakāśamūṣāyāḥ nalikā praṇālikā vidhinā śāstravārtikasaṃpradāyena kāryā yathordhve sūto bhavedadho bījamityarthaḥ mūṣāmityādi || 16.22-23:2 ||
mūṣāṃ nirudhya randhraṃ dūrīkṛtya vidhinā koṣṭhe koṣṭhīyantre sā mūṣā dhmātā kāryā drutaṃ dravarūpaṃ kṛtaṃ bījaṃ jñātvā parivartya ca mūṣāyāṃ bījasya parivartanaṃ kṛtvā tato bījaṃ sūtarājaṃ badhnātīti || 16.22-23:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.24


<sāraṇā:: withḍamaruyantra>
athavā ḍamarukayantre sāraṇavidhinā niyojitaḥ sūtaḥ |
sarati rasendro vidhinā jñātvā tatkarmakauśalyam || ṅṛht_16.24 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

athānyadyantramāha athavetyādi || 16.24:1 ||
vidhinā sāraṇavidhānena ḍamarukayantre uktalakṣaṇapātanakaraṇocite yantre sūto niyojitaḥ san sarati bījena milati || 16.24:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.25


<jāraṇā aftersāraṇā => vedha potency>
tatsāritaṃ rasendraṃ grāsavidhānena jārayettadanu |
punarapi sāritasūto vidhyati koṭyaṃśataḥ śulbam || ṅṛht_16.25 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

kiṃ kṛtvā rasendro niyojitaḥ jñātvā tatkarmakauśalyaṃ rasendrakarmaprāvīṇyaṃ jñātveti || 16.25:1 ||
____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.26


<sāraṇā:: withkrāmaṇāvasā>
krāmaṇavasādiyogādvidhinā sūtaḥ saratyeva |
capalatvātilaghutvādbījaṃ yato'tha vipluṣaḥ kāryaḥ || ṅṛht_16.26 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pūrvoktaṃ dṛḍhīkartumāha krāmaṇetyādi || 16.26:1 ||
sūto vidhinoktavidhānena krāmaṇocitā yā vasā maṇḍūkādīnāṃ tā eva ādayo yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ yogātsarati sāraṇā syātpunarbījayuto'pi sūtaḥ capalatvātilaghutvātcapalatvaṃ cañcalatvaṃ ca atilaghutvaṃ ca tasmāddhetoḥ avipluṣaḥ sthiraḥ kāryaḥ || 16.26:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.27


<sāraṇa, krāmaṇa:: withleadetc.>
sarati sukhena ca sūto dahati mukhaṃ naiva hastapādādi |
kramati rasaḥ phaṇiyogānmākṣikayutahemagairikayā || ṅṛht_16.27 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pūrvoktaguṇānāha saratītyādi || 16.27:1 ||
pūrvavidhānā sūtaḥ sarati sāritaḥ sūto mukhaṃ na dahati hastapādādi ca aṅgavibhāgaṃ naiva dahati || 16.27:2 ||
punaḥ phaṇiyogātnāgasaṃyogataḥ mākṣikayutahemagairikayā saha tāpyamilitasvarṇagairikayā sārdhaṃ krāmati || 16.27:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.28


<druti, sāraṇā>
mākṣīkasattvayogātphaṇiyogānnāgavaddravati śīghram |
dravati ca kanake sūtaḥ saṃsāryate vidhinā || ṅṛht_16.28 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sukhena sāraṇavidhānamāha mākṣiketyādi || 16.28:1 ||
kanakaṃ hema mākṣikasattvayogātphaṇisaṃyogānnāgasaṃyogācca śīghraṃ dravati kanake dravati sati vidhinā sāraṇatailādinā saṃsāryate sāraṇā kriyata iti || 16.28:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.29


<mercury:: preparation forvedha>
tasmāddravyavidhāyī sūto bījena sārito laghunā |
samasāritaḥ subaddho mūṣāyāṃ syātsamāvartaḥ || ṅṛht_16.29 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sāraṇayā pāradaguṇānāha tasmādityādi || 16.29:1 ||
tasmāddhetoḥ sūto dravyavidhāyī syātiti śeṣaḥ dravyakartetyartha || 16.29:2 ||
alaghunā bījena analpaparimāṇena mahābījena sāritaḥ san samasāritaḥ san baddho bhavetityadhyāhāraḥ || 16.29:3 ||
mūṣāyāṃ samāvarto dravaṇaṃ syātvahnidhamanātiti śeṣaḥ || 16.29:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.30


<(prati-, anu-)sāraṇā>
sāritavartitasūtaḥ samānabījena milati yaḥ sāryaḥ |
dviguṇena pratisāryaḥ sa cānusāryaśca triguṇena || ṅṛht_16.30 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sāraṇakramamāha sarityādi || 16.30:1 ||
sāritavartitasūtaḥ sāritaścāsau vartitaśceti vigrahaḥ || 16.30:2 ||
yaḥ samānabījena tulyamahābījena milati sa sāryaḥ yo dviguṇena milati saḥ pratisāryaḥ yaśca triguṇena so'nusārya iti sāraṇākramo darśitaḥ || 16.30:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.31


<sāraṇa <=> vedha potency>
śatavedhī sāryaḥ pratisāritaḥ syātsahasravedhī ca |
anusārito'yutena ca vidhināpi balābalaṃ jñātvā || ṅṛht_16.31 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sāraṇakramasya guṇānāha śatetyādi || 16.31:1 ||
yaḥ sāryaḥ samasāritaḥ sa śatavedhī syādityabhiprāyaḥ || 16.31:2 ||
punaḥ pratisāritaḥ dviguṇabījena sārito yaḥ sūtaḥ sa sahasravedhī syāt || 16.31:3 ||
ca punaḥ anusāritaḥ triguṇabījena sāritaḥ sa ayutena ayutavedhī syāditi vyaktiḥ || 16.31:4 ||
vidhinā ityuktavidhānena || 16.31:5 ||
balābalaṃ jñātvā nyūnādhikyaṃ matvā vidhinā yukta iti śeṣaḥ || 16.31:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.32


anusāritena tu samaḥ svacchaḥ sūtaḥ sāritastadanu |
sa bhavati lakṣavedhī pratisāritaḥ prayutavedhī ca || ṅṛht_16.32 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

sāritapratisāritādanusāritasya viśeṣamāha anvityādi || 16.32:1 ||
tu punaḥ svacchaḥ pradhānasaṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ sūtaḥ anusāritena samaḥ triguṇabījena sārito'nusāritastena tulyo yadi syātsa ca lakṣavedhī syāt || 16.32:2 ||
evaṃ pratisāritaḥ sūtaḥ samena sārito'yutavedhī syāt || 16.32:3 ||
evaṃ svecchātisvacchavṛddhau vedhasyāpi vṛddhiḥ syāditi rahasyam || 16.32:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.33


koṭiṃ vidhyati sūto'pyanusāritaḥ sarati bījena |
pratisārito'nusārito daśakoṭiṃ vidhyate sūtaḥ || ṅṛht_16.33 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tathottarasattvena guṇādhikyamāha koṭimityādi || 16.33:1 ||
sūte sarito bījena grāsanyāyaṃ vihāya samena bījenānusārito yaḥ sa koṭisaṃkhyāṃ dhātūnāmiti śeṣaḥ sūto vidhyati || 16.33:2 ||
yathoktasārite sūte daśaguṇavṛddhiḥ sarvatraiveti veditavyam || 16.33:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.34


pratisāritastathābjaṃ tvanusāritaḥ kharvavedhī ca |
evaṃ sāraṇayogātkurute vedhaṃ yathepsitaṃ vidhinā || ṅṛht_16.34 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣamāha pratītyādi || 16.34:1 ||
pratisāritaḥ sūto dviguṇabījena vāraikena sārito rasaḥ abjasaṃkhyāṃ vidhyati || 16.34:2 ||
tu punaranusāritaḥ triguṇena bījena vāraikena sāritaḥ sūtaḥ kharvavedhī syātkharvasaṃkhyāke dravyasaṃbandhaṃ karotītyabhiprāyaḥ || 16.34:3 ||
evamuktaprakāreṇa vidhinā śāstrajñavārtikasaṃpradāyena sāraṇayogātyathepsitaṃ vedhaṃ kurute yathāvāñchitamityarthaḥ || 16.34:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.35


anusāritena sārito vidhyati śulbaṃ nikharvasaṃkhyākam |
pratisāritastu vidhyati padmaṃ svanusāritaḥ śaṅkham || ṅṛht_16.35 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

punarviśeṣamāha anvityādi || 16.35:1 ||
anusāritena triguṇaprathamagrāsanyāyena bījena sāritaḥ sūto nikharvasaṃkhyākaṃ śulbaṃ vidhyati tu punaḥ pratisāritaḥ ṣaḍguṇaprathamagrāsanyāyena sāritaḥ sūtaḥ padmasaṃkhyākaṃ vidhyati || 16.35:2 ||
punaranusārito navaguṇagrāsanyāyena bījena sāritaḥ sūtaḥ śaṅkhasaṃkhyāṃ vidhyatīti || 16.35:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht16.36


<lead(?):: vedha (?)>
vidhyeddviguṇaṃ dravyaṃ nāgaṃ dattvānuvāhayecchanakaiḥ |
tāvadyāvatkanakaṃ divyaṃ pronmīlayetsakalam || ṅṛht_16.36 ||

iti paramahaṃsaparivrājakācāryaśrīmadgovindabhagavatpādaviracite rasahṛdayatantre sāraṇātmakaḥ ṣoḍaśo'vabodhaḥ ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

punarviśeṣamāha vidhyedityādi || 16.36:1 ||
kanakaṃ hema dattvā śanakairnīcaistāvadanuvāhayetyāvaddivyaṃ pravaraṃ kanakaṃ bhavediti śeṣaḥ || 16.36:2 ||
punaryāvatsakalaṃ samastaṃ nāgaṃ pronmīlayenniḥśeṣaṃ kuryādityabhiprāyaḥ || 16.36:3 ||
punastatkanakaṃ dviguṇaṃ svato dravyaṃ vidhyetvā sūtakena sāritaṃ satdravyaṃ kanakaṃ śatasahasrādisaṃkhyāto dviguṇasaṃkhyākaṃ dravyaṃ śulbādikaṃ vidhyediti rahasyam || 16.36:4 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht17.1


iti kṛtasāraṇavidhirapi balavānapi sūtarāṭkriyāyogāt |
saṃveṣṭya tiṣṭhati lohaṃ no viśati krāmaṇārahitaḥ || ṅṛht_17.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

susaṃskṛtā mukhāntaḥsthā viśadāśca hitārthakāḥ |
tathā parocitāḥ pūtā bhavantyamarajā giraḥ || 17.1:1 ||
atha krāmaṇapraśaṃsāmāha itītyādi || 17.1:2 ||
uktavidhānena kṛtaḥ sāraṇasya vidhiryasmin sūtarāje evaṃvidhiḥ sūtarāṭbalavān bhavediti śeṣaḥ || 17.1:3 ||
kutaḥ kriyāyogātkṛtyakaraṇāt || 17.1:4 ||
yādṛkkṛtyaṃ kriyate tādṛkbalavān syādityabhiprāyaḥ || 17.1:5 ||
evaṃvidho'pi krāmaṇārahitaḥ krāmaṇavarjito lohaṃ na viśati lohāntaḥpraveśaṃ na karoti tato hetorlohaṃ dhātuṃ saṃveṣṭya pariveṣṭanaṃ kṛtvā tiṣṭhati bāhyarāgadāyī syāditi || 17.1:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht17.2


annaṃ vā dravyaṃ vā yathānupānena dhātuṣu kramate |
evaṃ krāmaṇayogādrasarājo viśati loheṣu || ṅṛht_17.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

dehalohayoḥ sādṛśyamāha annamityādi || 17.2:1 ||
yatheti sādṛśye || 17.2:2 ||
annaṃ godhūmādikaṃ vā dravyamauṣadhamanupānena saha jalādinā sārdhaṃ dhātuṣu māṃsādiṣu saptasu kramate vyāpnoti tathā amunā vakṣyamāṇavidhānena krāmaṇayogātkrāmaṇāya yogaḥ kunaṭīmākṣikaviṣādistataḥ sūtarājo loharūpyādiṣu viśati bāhyābhyantaraṃ vidhyatītyarthaḥ || 17.2:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht17.3-5


<krāmaṇayoga (1)>
kāntaviṣarasakadaradai raktailendragopikādyaiśca |
krāmaṇametacchreṣṭhaṃ lepe kṣepe sadā yojyam || ṅṛht_17.3 ||
<krāmaṇayoga (2)>
kunaṭīmākṣikaviṣaṃ nararudhiraṃ vāyasasya viṣṭhā ca |
mahiṣīṇāṃ karṇamalaṃ strīkṣīraṃ krāmaṇe balakṛt || ṅṛht_17.4 ||
<krāmaṇayoga (3)>
ṭaṅkaṇakunaṭīrāmaṭhabhūmilatāsaṃyutaṃ mahārudhiram |
krāmaṇametatkathitaṃ lepe kṣepe sadā yojyam || ṅṛht_17.5 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

krāmaṇayogamāha kāntaviṣetyādi || 17.3-5:1 ||
kāntaṃ cumbakaṃ viṣaṃ kandajaṃ viṣaṃ kandaviṣāṇi kālakūṭādīni trayodaśa daradaṃ hiṅgulaṃ taiḥ ca punaḥ raktatailendragopādyaiḥ rakto raktakavargaḥ tailaṃ kaṅguṇyādeḥ indragopo jīvaviśeṣaḥ ityādyāḥ krāmaṇocitāstacca || 17.3-5:2 ||
etatśreṣṭhaṃ sarvottamaṃ krāmaṇamanena sūtaḥ krāmati viśati loheṣviti vyāptiḥ tatkrāmaṇaṃ kathitam || 17.3-5:3 ||
tallepe kṣepe ca yojyamityarthaḥ || 17.3-5:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht17.6


<krāmaṇayoga (4): frṅold-|ṣilberherstellung>
śilayā nihato nāgo vaṅgaṃ vā tālakena śuddhena |
kramaśaḥ pīte śukle krāmaṇametatsamuddiṣṭam || ṅṛht_17.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha śilayetyādi || 17.6:1 ||
nāgaḥ sīsakaḥ śilayā manohvayā nihato māritaḥ punaḥ vaṅgaṃ śuddhena doṣavarjitena tālena nihataṃ kramaśaḥ krameṇa pīte hemakarmaṇi śukle rūpyakarmaṇi etatkrāmaṇaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ samyakprakāśitaṃ pītakarmaṇi nāgaḥ śuklakarmaṇi vaṅgaṃ ca niyojitavyamityarthaḥ || 17.6:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht17.7


<krāmaṇa>
tīkṣṇaṃ daradena hataṃ śulbaṃ vā tāpyamāritaṃ vidhinā |
krāmaṇametatkathitaṃ kāntamukhaṃ mākṣikairvāpi || ṅṛht_17.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha tīkṣṇamityādi || 17.7:1 ||
daradena hiṅgulena hataṃ māritaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ sāro vidhinā arivargavidhānena tāpyena svarṇamākṣikena māritaṃ śulbaṃ tāmrametadapi krāmaṇaṃ kathitaṃ vā kāntamukhaṃ kāntaṃ lohajāti uktaṃ granthādau tatmukhaṃ pradhānaṃ yasya tatmākṣikairvā māritaṃ niyojyamiti śeṣaḥ || 17.7:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht17.8


mākṣikasattvaṃ nāgaṃ vihāya na krāmaṇaṃ kimapyasti |
dalasiddhe rasasiddhe vidhāvasau bhavati khalu saphalaḥ || ṅṛht_17.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣavidhānamāha mākṣikasattvamityādi || 17.8:1 ||
mākṣikasattvaṃ tāpyasāraṃ nāgaḥ sīsakaḥ taṃ vihāya nānyatkimapyasti krāmaṇaṃ na krāmaṇamiti bhāvaḥ || 17.8:2 ||
khalviti jijñāsāyām || 17.8:3 ||
dalasiddhe vidhau saṃskāraiḥ pūrṇatāṃ nīte sati asau vidhiḥ saphalaḥ || 17.8:4 ||




____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.1


<vedhamahattva>

anayā khalu sāraṇayā krāmaṇena ca viśati yojito vidhivat |
asati vedhavidhau na rasaḥ svaguṇānprakāśayati || ṅṛht_18.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vyavāyibheṣajopete dravye kṣipto rasaḥ khalu |
vedha ityucyate tajjñaiḥ sa ca naikavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ |
iti paribhāṣā || 18.1:1 ||
krāmaṇasaṃskārādhikāramāha anayetyādi || 18.1:2 ||
anayā uktayā sāraṇayā saha krāmaṇasaṃskāre kṛte sati raso viśati krāmati punarvedhavidhau kṛte sati rasaḥ svaguṇān prakāśayatīti veditavyam || 18.1:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.2


<hemakṛṣṭi>
rasadaradatāpyagandhakamanaḥśilārājavarttakaṃ vimalam |
puṭamṛtaśulbaṃ tāre nirvyūḍhaṃ hemakṛṣṭiriyam || ṅṛht_18.2 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vedhavidhānamāha rasetyādi || 18.2:1 ||
rasaḥ sūtaḥ daradaṃ hiṅgulaṃ tāpyaṃ mākṣikaṃ gandhakaḥ pratītaḥ manaḥśilā manohvā rājavarttakaṃ rājāvartaṃ vimalaṃ raupyamākṣikamekavadbhāvadvandvaḥ || 18.2:2 ||
puṭamṛtaśulbaṃ rasādīnāṃ puṭena mṛtamityarthaḥ || 18.2:3 ||
evaṃvidhaṃ śulbaṃ tāre nirvāhitamiyaṃ hemakṛṣṭiḥ svarṇakaraṇamityarthaḥ || 18.2:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.3


<śatāṃśavidhi|-vedha (1)>
aṣṭānavatirbhāgāstārastveko'pi kanakabhāgaḥ syāt |
sūtasyaiko bhāgaḥ śatāṃśavidhireṣa vikhyātaḥ || ṅṛht_18.3 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vedhavidhānamāha aṣṭānavatirityādi || 18.3:1 ||
eṣa rasadaradetyādiśatāṃśavedhavidhiḥ kathamatra śatāṃśe aṣṭānavatirbhāgāstārasya rūpyasya punariha kanakabhāgaḥ syāteka eveti punaḥ sūtasya daradādimilitarasasyaiko bhāgaḥ ekāṃśaḥ iti sarve śatāṃśāḥ śatāṃśena vedha iti || 18.3:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.4


<śatāṃśavidhi (2)>
ekonapañcāśadbhāgāstārasyeha tathaiva śulvasya |
kanakasyaiko bhāgo vedhaścaikena sūtasya || ṅṛht_18.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha ekonapañcāśadityādi || 18.4:1 ||
ekonapañcāśadbhāgāḥ tārasya rūpyasya kāryāḥ tathaiva śulvasya tāmrasya ekonapañcāśadbhāgāśca kāryāḥ punaḥ kanakasya hemnaśca eko bhāgaḥ kāryaḥ sūtasya ca ekena bhāgena vedha iti eṣo'pi śatāṃśavidhiḥ || 18.4:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.5

<ḍefinition sahasravedhinundhṇher>
evaṃ sahasravedhī niyujyate koṭivedhī ca |
jāraṇabījavaśena tu sūtasya balābalaṃ jñātvā || ṅṛht_18.5 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pūrvoktavidherviśeṣamāha evamityādi || 18.5:1 ||
evaṃ śatāṃśavedhanyāyena daśavṛddhivibhāgena sahasravedhī syāt || 18.5:2 ||
evaṃ ca jāraṇabījavaśena jāraṇāyāṃ yadbījaṃ kiyadguṇajāritamiti bhāvaḥ tadvaśena koṭivedhī ca syāt || 18.5:3 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā tenaiva jāraṇabījavaśena yattasya balābalaṃ nyūnādhikyaṃ tatjñātvetyabhiprāyaḥ || 18.5:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.6


<kuntavedha>
dattvādau prativāpaṃ lākṣāmatsyādipittabhāvanayā |
tāre vā śulbe vā tārāriṣṭe tathā kṛṣṭau || ṅṛht_18.6 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣeṇa vedhavidhānamāha dattvetyādi || 18.6:1 ||
ādau prathamaṃ lākṣāmatsyādipittabhāvanayā lākṣā pratītā matsyādipittāni matsyamāhiṣamayūrājasūkarasaṃbhavāni pittāni teṣāṃ bhāvanayā kṛtvā prativāpaṃ galite nikṣepaṃ tattāre dattvā athavā śulbe prativāpaṃ kuryātathavā kṛṣṭau hemakaraṇe vāpaṃ dattvā niyuñjyāditi śeṣaḥ || 18.6:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.7


tadanu krāmaṇamṛdite tatkalkenāpi piṇḍitarasena |
atividrute ca tasmin vedho'sau kuntavedhena || ṅṛht_18.7 ||

------------------
*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣāccāha tadanvityādi || 18.7:1 ||
tadanu lākṣāmatsyādipittabhāvanāyā anantaraṃ tasmin lākṣādikalke krāmaṇamṛdite kāntarasakadaradaraktatailendragopādyairmṛdite sati punastatkalkena taccūrṇenāpi piṇḍitarasena vedhaḥ kartavya iti śeṣaḥ || 18.7:2 ||
kasmin tāre vā śulbe vā vidrute jalarūpe kārya ityarthaḥ || 18.7:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.8


<krāmaṇa (?)>
tattailārdrapaṭena sthagayetpalalena bhasmanā vāpi |
vidhivadvedhyaṃ dravyaṃ rasarājakrāmaṇārthaṃ hi || ṅṛht_18.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣamāha tadityādi || 18.8:1 ||
vedhyaṃ vedhocitaṃ dravyaṃ rasarājakrāmaṇārthaṃ yathā raso viśati tadarthaṃ taṃ tailārdrapaṭena sāraṇatailārdravastreṇa sthagayetācchādayetvā palalena kenacinmāṃsena vā bhasmanā ācchādayedityarthaḥ || 18.8:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.9


iti sāritasya kathitaṃ rasasya vedhādi krāmaṇaṃ karma |
viśeṣamāha itītyādi || 18.8:1 ||
sāritasya uktavidhānena sāraṇākṛtasya vedhādi krāmaṇaṃ karma vedhavidhānoditakrāmaṇaṃ karma kathitam || 18.8:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.9


<lepavedhpattrarañjana>
pādādijīrṇabījo yujyate patralepena || ṅṛht_18.9 ||
------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

pādādijīrṇabījaḥ pādādinā pādārdhena samato nyūnena ca jīrṇaṃ bījaṃ yasmin saḥ patralepena yujyate ataḥ patrarañjanaṃ syādityabhiprāyaḥ || 18.9:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.10


<pattrarañjana (2) durchlepa>
amlādyudvartitatārāriṣṭādipatramatiśuddham |
ālipya rasena tataḥ krāmaṇalipte puṭeṣu viśrāntam || ṅṛht_18.10 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atiśuddhaṃ nirmalamamlādyudvartitaṃ tārāriṣṭaśabdātsitaṃ svarṇaṃ grāhyaṃ tataḥ rasenālipya tataḥ kramaṇālipte krāmaṇapiṇḍena lepe kṛte sati puṭeṣu utpalāgnau viśrāntaṃ sthāpitaṃ kuryāt || 18.10:1 ||
patrarañjanavidhirayam || 18.10:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.11


<ḹeiterverarbeitungdespattras; varṇapuṭa>
ardhena miśrayitvā hemnā jyeṣṭhena taddalaṃ puṭitam |
kṣitikhagapaṭuraktamṛdā varṇapuṭo'yaṃ tato deyaḥ || ṅṛht_18.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha svarṇavidhānamāha ardhenetyādi || 18.11:1 ||
ardhena ardhavibhāgena rañjitadalādita iti jñeyam || 18.11:2 ||
jyeṣṭhena hemnā pravarakanakena taddalaṃ rañjitapatraṃ miśrayitvā puṭitaṃ kuryātyathā milati || 18.11:3 ||
punaḥ kṣitikhagapaṭuraktamṛdā kṛtvā kṣitiḥ sphaṭikaḥ khagaḥ pītakāsīsaṃ paṭu saindhavaṃ lavaṇaṃ raktamṛtgairikamekavadbhāvadvandvaḥ tena kṣityādinopari liptaṃ dalaṃ prati ayaṃ puṭo deyaḥ vanopalairiti śeṣaḥ || 18.11:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.12


<vedha:: white gold=> redgold>
bhūpativartakacūrṇaṃ śirīṣapuṣparasabhāvitaṃ bahuśaḥ |
sadyaḥ karoti raktaṃ sitakanakamaśītibhāgena || ṅṛht_18.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

varṇavidhānamāha bhūpatītyādi || 18.12:1 ||
bhūpativartakacūrṇaṃ rājāvartarajaḥ bahuśo bahuvāraṃ śirīṣapuṣparasabhāvitaṃ kuryātityadhyāhāryam || 18.12:2 ||
bhāvitaṃ gharmapuṭitamiti vidhānavititi śeṣaḥ || 18.12:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.13


<vedha:: silver=> gold>
rasadaradavimalatāpyaṃ paṭuśilāmākṣīkanṛpāścaiva |
pravālakaṃkuṣṭhaṭaṅkaṇagairikaprativāpitaṃ sitaṃ kanakam || ṅṛht_18.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha rasetyādi |
rasaḥ sūtaḥ daradaṃ hiṅgulaḥ vimalaṃ tāpyabhedaḥ paṭu saiṃdhavaṃ śilā manohvā mākṣikaṃ pratītaṃ vaṇigdravyaṃ nṛpo rājāvartaḥ pravālaṃ vidrumaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viraṅgaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ gairikaṃ pratītametaiḥ prativāpitaṃ sitadravyaṃ kanakaṃ bhavetityadhyāhāryam || 18.13:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.14

<colouringof gold>
tāpībhavanṛpāvartabījapūrarasārditam |
karoti puṭapākena hema sindūrasannibham || ṅṛht_18.14 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha tāpībhavetyādi || 18.14:1 ||
tāpībhavaṃ mākṣikasattvaṃ nṛpāvartaṃ rājāvartakametaddvayaṃ bījapūrarasārditaṃ mātuluṅgarasamarditaṃ kuryātetadubhayoryogātkanakaṃ puṭapākena vahnividhānena kanakaṃ pūrvoktaṃ yatkanakaṃ vā hīnavarṇakanakaṃ sindūrasannibhaṃ karoti || 18.14:2 ||
sindūravannibhā yasyeti samāsaḥ || 18.14:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.15


<silver:: improvementof colour>
vaṅgābhraṃ sitamākṣīkaṃ śailaṃ vā vāhayetsite |
tattāraṃ ca daśāṃśena tārotkarṣaṃ karoti hi || ṅṛht_18.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha tāravarṇavidhānamāha vaṅgetyādi || 18.15:1 ||
vaṅgābhramiti vaṅgaṃ trapu abhraṃ śvetābhraṃ punaḥ sitamākṣīkaṃ vimalaṃ śailaṃ śvetaśilājatu vā site tāre vāhayet || 18.15:2 ||
punardaśāṃśena etadauṣadhanicayaṃ tārato daśamavibhāgena kṛtvā hi niścitaṃ tārotkarṣaṃ karoti hīnavarṇata uttamaṃ karotītyabhiprāyaḥ || 18.15:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.16


<nirvāhaṇa of diff. substances=> phys. results>
nāgaḥ karoti mṛdutāṃ nirvyūḍhastāṃ ca raktatāṃ ca raviḥ |
tāṃ pītatāṃ ca tīkṣṇaṃ kācastatkālikavināśaṃ ca || ṅṛht_18.16 ||
------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tāre viśeṣamāha nāga ityādi || 18.16:1 ||
nāgaḥ sīsakastāre nirvyūḍho mṛdutāṃ komalatvaṃ karoti || 18.16:2 ||
punaḥ ravistāmraṃ nirvyūḍhaṃ sattāṃ mṛdutāṃ ca punaḥ raktatāṃ lohitanibhāṃ karoti || 18.16:3 ||
ca punastīkṣṇaṃ tāranirvyūḍhaṃ tāṃ raktatāṃ pītatāṃ ca karoti || 18.16:4 ||
punaḥ kācaḥ pratīto loke sa kālikavināśaṃ karotīti tāre nirvyūḍha iti saṃbandhaḥ || 18.16:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.17


<gold:: improvement>
kanakāruṇasamamākṣikakarañjatailāpluto dhmātaḥ |
pāte pāte daśa daśa vindati yāvaddhi koṭimapi || ṅṛht_18.17 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

hemno viśeṣamāha kanakāruṇetyādi || 18.17:1 ||
kanakaṃ hema aruṇaṃ tāmraṃ samaṃ tulyabhāgaṃ mākṣikaṃ tāpyamayaṃ gaṇaḥ karañjatailapluto dhmātaḥ kāryaḥ || 18.17:2 ||
punaḥ pāte pāte vāraṃvāraṃ nikṣepe sati daśa daśa guṇotkarṣaṃ vidanti dhmāta ityadhyāhāraḥ || 18.17:3 ||
hi niścitam || 18.17:4 ||
kiyatkālaṃ yāvatkoṭisaṃkhyāṃ vindati || 18.17:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.18


<gold:: bīja forimprovementof colour>
sa cāyamativilīnaḥ kaṃguṇītailasecito bahuśaḥ |
mākṣīkaravinivāpaṃ vidhyati kanakaṃ śatāṃśena || ṅṛht_18.18 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣamāha sa ityādi || 18.18:1 ||
saḥ karañjatailapluto yogo bahuśo vāraṃvāraṃ kaṅguṇītailena secito yathā syāttathāyamati vilīnaḥ sanmākṣikaravinivāpāṃ punaḥ kārya evaṃvidhaṃ ca kanakaṃ śatāṃśena śatavibhāgena vidhyati sitakanakamiti || 18.18:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.19


<gold:: bīja forimprovement(?)>
śulbahataṃ rasagandhāhatakhagapītaṃ daśāṃśena |
vidhyati kanakaṃ kurute tannirvyūḍhaṃ marditaṃ sudṛḍham || ṅṛht_18.19 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣamāha śulbetyādi || 18.19:1 ||
śulbahataṃ śulbena saha hataṃ rasagandhaṃ sūtagandhaṃ tena āhataṃ pañcatvamāpannaṃ yatkhagapītaṃ pītakāsīsametadauṣadhasamuccayaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ yathā syāttathā marditaṃ kuryātpunastatnirvyūḍhaṃ daśāṃśena vidhyati sitakanakaṃ kurute svarṇamiti viśeṣaḥ || 18.19:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.20


<vedha>
rasakasamaṃ sudhmātaṃ kanakaṃ bhuktvā tato'rkacandralepena |
mākṣikasattvaṃ hemnā karoti jīrṇo rasaḥ śatāṃśena || ṅṛht_18.20 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha rasaketyādi || 18.20:1 ||
tato'nantaramarkacandralepena kanakaṃ rasakasamaṃ dhmātaṃ kuryātpunaretadauṣadhaṃ bhuktvā kanakaṃ syātpunarmākṣikasattvaṃ hemnā saha jīrṇaṃ rasaṃ śatāṃśena vidhyatītyarthaḥ || 18.20:2 ||
____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.21


<vedha; silver=> gold>
aṣṭaguṇaṃ mṛtaśulbaṃ kaladhautena mūkamūṣayā liptam |
tatṣoḍaśāṃśajīrṇaṃ vidhyati tāraṃ śatārdhena || ṅṛht_18.21 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha aṣṭaguṇetyādi || 18.21:1 ||
aṣṭaguṇaṃ mṛtaśulbamarivargeṇa saha hataṃ yatśulbaṃ tāmraṃ tataḥ kaladhautena ṣoḍaśāṃśena jīrṇaṃ śatārdhena pañcāśadvibhāgena tāraṃ vidhyati kanakaṃ karotītyarthaḥ || 18.21:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.22


<vedha:: daśāṃśa~; silver=> gold>
āvṛtya kanakakariṇau śilayā prativāpitau tato bhuktvā |
dolāyantre gandhakajīrṇastāre daśāṃśavedhī syāt || ṅṛht_18.22 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha āvṛtyetyādi || 18.22:1 ||
kanakakariṇau samahemanāgau āvṛtya gālayitvā śilayā manohvayā prativāpitau tato'nantaraṃ dolāyantre kanakakariṇau bhuktvā gandhakajīrṇo yo rasaḥ sa tāre daśāṃśavedhī syātdaśāṃśena vidhyatītyarthaḥ || 18.22:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.23


<vedha:: śatāṃśa; bronśe => gold>
ripunihatalohaṣaṭkaṃ jīrṇo dhānyasthitaścaturmāsam |
sahitaḥ purasurābhyāṃ vidhyati ghoṣaṃ śatāṃśena || ṅṛht_18.23 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha ripunihatetyādi || 18.23:1 ||
ripunihatalohaṣaṭkaṃ ripubhirarivargairnihataṃ māritaṃ yatlohaṣaṭkaṃ svarṇatāratāmranāgavaṅgalohābhidhānaṃ tatcaturmāsaṃ yathā syāttathā dhānyasthito'nnakoṣṭhīdhṛto raso jīrṇaḥ || 18.23:2 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭo dhānyasthitaḥ purasurābhyāṃ guggulumadirābhyāṃ sahito militaḥ śatāṃśena ghoṣaṃ kāṃsyaṃ vidhyati kanakaṃ karotītyarthaḥ || 18.23:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.24


vakṣye samprati samyagyadbījaṃ samarase jīrṇam |
piṣṭistambhādividhiṃ prakāśyamānaṃ budhāḥ śṛṇuta || ṅṛht_18.24 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣamāha vakṣya ityādi || 18.24:1 ||
bho budhāḥ mayā prakāśyamānaṃ satśṛṇuta sāvadhānā ityadhyāhāryam || 18.24:2 ||
tatkiṃ saṃprati yadbījaṃ samarase tulyasūte samyakjīrṇaṃ jāraṇamāpannaṃ tadahaṃ govindanāmā vakṣye kathayāmi || 18.24:3 ||
punaḥ piṣṭistambhādividhiṃ piṣṭistambha ādiryasya vidhestaṃ vidhiṃ pāṭakhoṭajalaukākhyaṃ ca vakṣye || 18.24:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.25-40


<vedha; ghoṣākṛṣṭa => gold>
rājāvartakavimalapītābhragandhatāpyarasakaiśca |
kāṃkṣīkāsīsaśilādaradaiśca samanvitaṃ nāgam || ṅṛht_18.25 ||
ahimārarasaiḥ puṭitaṃ māraya nāgaṃ nirutthakaṃ yāvat |
tadanu ca tasya hi madhye śulbaṃ gandhaṃ ca lavaṇakaṃkuṣṭham || ṅṛht_18.26 ||
tatsarvaṃ śatavārān bhāvaya pakvārkapatrasalilaiśca |
ghoṣākṛṣṭe śulbe cūrṇaṃ nirvāhayecchataśaḥ || ṅṛht_18.27 ||
śulbena tena hi samaṃ rasakapītābhrasatvavimalaṃ ca |
gairikamākṣikasattvaṃ ṭaṅkaṇanāgaṃ ca tīkṣṇayutam || ṅṛht_18.28 ||
etairdvandvaṃ kṛtvā mākṣikavāpena rañjayecchulvam |
vārāṃśca viṃśatirapi galitaṃ secayettadanu || ṅṛht_18.29 ||
nirguṇḍīkākamācīkanyārasamelanaṃ kṛtvā |
vārān sapta ca vidhinā tadapi ca nirvāpayeddhemni || ṅṛht_18.30 ||
yāvaccaturviṃśatiguṇaṃ bījavaraṃ rañjayettacca |
pakvaṃ mākṣikameva hi tena ca vidhinā tadapi caturviṃśatiguṇam || ṅṛht_18.31 ||
tadbījaṃ laghumātraṃ rasarāje saṃskṛte pūrvam |
mūṣāyāṃ khalu dattvā daśaguṇaṃ ca gandhakaṃ dāhyam || ṅṛht_18.32 ||
athavā vālukayantre sudṛḍhe caturdaśāṃgulamūṣāyām |
madhye sūtaṃ muktvā laghutarapuṭayogataḥ pihitā || ṅṛht_18.33 ||
tena samaṃ bījavare piṣṭiḥ pādāṃśataḥ kāryā |
aṃgulinavaparimāṇe mūṣāmadhye ca piṣṭikāṃ dattvā || ṅṛht_18.34 ||
nirguṇḍīkākamācīgojihvādugdhikāraktā |
gṛhakanyāmadhusaindhavapiṇḍairapi samantataśchādyā || ṅṛht_18.35 ||
tāvatkāryaḥ puṭayogo yāvaddṛḍhatāṃ samāyāti |
ṣaḍguṇagandhakatālakakāṃkṣīkāsīsalavaṇakṣāram || ṅṛht_18.36 ||
tāpyaṃ tatsarvasamaṃ deyaṃ bāhye tadauṣadhipiṇḍam |
ṣaḍguṇaṣaḍguṇasahitaṃ piṣṭīṃ yantre'tha kacchape dattvā || ṅṛht_18.37 ||
svedyaṃ puṭayogena tu tridinaṃ ghaṭikātrayaṃ yāvat |
uddhṛtya tato yatnātpiṣṭvā sucūrṇitāṃ kṛtvā || ṅṛht_18.38 ||
samabījena tu sāryo nāgaṃ triguṇaṃ tataḥ samuttārya |
pratisāraṇā ca kāryā jāritasūtena bījayuktena || ṅṛht_18.39 ||
anusāraṇā ca paścāttriguṇaṃ bījaṃ bhavedyatra |
prāguktaṃ tasyopari mṛtanāgaṃ śataguṇaṃ vāhyam |
tena ca ghoṣākṛṣṭe śulbe vedho'tha saptaśataiḥ || ṅṛht_18.40 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha nāgamāha rājāvartaketyādi || 18.25-40:1 ||
rājāvartakaṃ prasiddhaṃ vimalaṃ śvetamākṣikaṃ pītābhraṃ pītavarṇaṃ yadabhraṃ gandho gandhakaḥ tāpyaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ rasakaṃ kharparikametaiḥ punaretaiḥ kāṅkṣī kāhīti loke kāsīsaṃ pītakāsīsaṃ śilā manohvā daradaṃ hiṅgulaṃ taiśca samanvitaṃ militaṃ nāgaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 18.25-40:2 ||
taccāha ahītyādi || 18.25-40:3 ||
pūrvauṣadhasaṃyutaṃ nāgamahimārarasaiḥ karavīradrāvaiḥ puṭitaṃ kuryāt || 18.25-40:4 ||
nāgaṃ sīsakaṃ tāvanmāraya yāvannirutthakaṃ yathā punarutthitaṃ na syāt || 18.25-40:5 ||
tadanu tatpaścāttasya nāgasya madhye śulbaṃ tāmraṃ gandhaṃ pratītaṃ lavaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viraṅgametatsarvaṃ miśritaṃ kuryātityadhyāhāryam || 18.25-40:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.41-46


<kṣepavedha>
krāmaṇametatprāgapi mākṣikadaradagandhakaśilābhiḥ |
rājāvartakavimalapravālakaṅkuṣṭhatutthaviṣaiḥ || ṅṛht_18.41 ||
kāntagairikaṭaṃkaṇabhūmilatārudhiraśakragoparasaiḥ |
mahiṣīṇāṃ karṇamalairmṛtalohaṃ vāyasasya viṣṭhā ca || ṅṛht_18.42 ||
pārāvatasya viṣṭhā strīpayaḥ sarvamekataḥ kṛtvā |
krāmaṇakalkaṃ caitacchatavārān raktapītagaṇaiḥ || ṅṛht_18.43 ||
bhāvyaṃ kaṃguṇitaile krauñcīpittabhāvanāḥ sapta |
kalkenānena pacetsāritapiṣṭiṃ ca haṇḍikāyāṃ hi || ṅṛht_18.44 ||
yāvadraktā bhavati hi gacchati nāgaṃ samuttārya |
tāvatkṣepaṃ ca kṣipetsarvasminsāraṇādau ca || ṅṛht_18.45 ||
evaṃ hi koṭivedhī rasarājaḥ krāmito bhūtvā |
puṃstvāderucchrāyaprado bhūtvā bhogāndatte || ṅṛht_18.46 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

krāmaṇamāha krāmaṇamityādi || 18.41-46:1 ||
prāgapīti pūrvādhyāye'pi proktamiti śeṣaḥ || 18.41-46:2 ||
punaretadvakṣyamāṇaṃ krāmaṇaguṇaṃ kuryādityadhyāhāraḥ || 18.41-46:3 ||
sa kathaṃ tadāha mākṣiketyādi || 18.41-46:4 ||
mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ daradaṃ hiṅgulaṃ gandhakaḥ pratītaḥ śilā manohvā tābhiḥ || 18.41-46:5 ||
punaḥ rājāvartaketyādi || 18.41-46:6 ||
rājāvartakaṃ lājavarada iti bhāṣāyāṃ vimalaṃ sitamākṣikaṃ pravālaṃ vidrumaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viraṅgaṃ tutthakaṃ śikhigrīvaṃ viṣaṃ saktukādikandajametaiśca || 18.41-46:7 ||
kāntetyādi kāntaṃ cumbakaṃ gairikaṃ pratītaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ bhūmilatā bhūnāgaḥ rudhiraṃ śakragopaḥ raso viṣaṃ punaruktādviṣamatra dviguṇaṃ taiḥ || 18.41-46:8 ||
ca punarmahiṣīṇāṃ karṇamalairhayāripatnīnāṃ śravaṇayormalāni taiḥ saha mṛtalohaṃ mṛtaṃ ca tallohaṃ ceti vāyasasya kākasya viṣṭhā śakṛt || 18.41-46:9 ||
pārāvatasya viṣṭhā kapotaśakṛtstrīpayo nārīkṣīrametatsarvaṃ mākṣikādistrīkṣīrāntamekataḥ kṛtvā miśritaṃ vidhāya ca punaretatkrāmaṇakalkaṃ raktapītagaṇaiḥ kiṃśukādiharidrādyaiḥ śatavārān bhāvayedityāgāmiślokasaṃbandhāt || 18.41-46:10 ||
sāraṇakalkavidhānamāha bhāvyamityādi || 18.41-46:11 ||
pūrvoktasāraṇakalkaṃ kaṅguṇītaile jyotiṣmatīsnehe bhāvyaṃ tataḥ krauñcīpittabhāvanāḥ sapta deyāḥ dātavyāḥ || 18.41-46:12 ||
punaḥ sāritapiṣṭiṃ sāritā yā rasendrabījapiṣṭistāmanena kalkena haṇḍikāyāṃ pacetvahninā pākaḥ kartavyaḥ || 18.41-46:13 ||
sāraṇakalkapācanamāha yāvadityādi || 18.41-46:14 ||
pūrvakalkasaṃyutāṃ piṣṭiṃ kiyatkālaṃ pacetyāvadraktā bhavati nāgaṃ ca gacchati nāganāśaḥ syātnāge gacchati sati samuttārya punastāvatsarvasminsāraṇādau ca kṣepakrameṇa kṣepaṃ kṣipet || 18.41-46:15 ||
puṃstvādyānākāśagamanaparyantān bhogān dadātītyabhiprāyaḥ || 18.41-46:16 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.47-48


<silver=> gold>
abhrakamākṣikakanakaṃ nāgayutaṃ militaṃ vidhinā |
sūte piṣṭiḥ kāryā divyauṣadhiyogataḥ puṭitā || ṅṛht_18.47 ||
ṣaḍguṇagandhakadāhaḥ śilayā nāgaṃ samuttārya |
tāre hemākṛṣṭirmilitā syātṣoḍaśāṃśena || ṅṛht_18.48 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

piṣṭividhyantaramāha abhrakamityādi || 18.47-48:1 ||
abhrakaṃ pratītaṃ mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ kanakaṃ hema nāgayutaṃ nāgena sīsakena yutaṃ sahitaṃ vidhinā kartavyopadeśena etatsarvaṃ sūte militaṃ satpiṣṭiḥ kāryā divyauṣadhiyogataḥ puṭitā piṣṭiḥ kāryā iti || 18.47-48:2 ||
tāre hemākṛṣṭimāha ṣaḍityādi || 18.47-48:3 ||
pūrvoktāyāṃ piṣṭyāṃ ṣaḍguṇagandhakadāhaḥ kāryaḥ punaḥ ṣaḍguṇaśilayā kṛtvā nāgaṃ samuttārya sīsakamapahāya sā niṣpannā piṣṭī ṣoḍaśāṃśena tāre rūpye militā satī hemākṛṣṭiḥ syātkanakoddhāraṇaṃ bhavettāmranāgādiṣu dhātuṣu hema sthitameva tata ākṛṣṭiśabdo yuktaḥ || 18.47-48:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.49-50


<silver=> gold>
mākṣikanihataṃ śulbaṃ śilayā nihataṃ ca nāgatulyāṃśam |
puṭitaṃ jambīrarasaiḥ saindhavasahitaṃ pacetsthālyām || ṅṛht_18.49 ||
taccūrṇaṃ ghṛtamadhukaṭaṅkaṇasahitaṃ ca guptamūṣāyām |
tāre triguṇaṃ vyūḍhaṃ hemākṛṣṭirbhaveddivyā || ṅṛht_18.50 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

nāgamāraṇavidhānamāha mākṣiketyādi || 18.49-50:1 ||
mākṣikeṇa nihataṃ māritaṃ yatśulvaṃ śilayā manaḥśilayā nāgaṃ ca nihataṃ māritamubhayaṃ tulyāṃśaṃ samabhāgaṃ kāryaṃ punarjambīrarasaiḥ jambīradrāvaiḥ saindhavasahitamubhayaṃ puṭitaṃ bhāvitaṃ satpacedvahninā pakvaṃ kuryāt || 18.49-50:2 ||
kva sthālyāṃ mṛdbhājane ityarthaḥ || 18.49-50:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.51-52


<copper+ silver=> gold>
śulbaṃ balinā nihataṃ tīkṣṇaṃ daradena nihatasamabhāgam |
ekīkṛtvā puṭayetpacenmātārasenaiva || ṅṛht_18.51 ||
tāre vyūḍhaṃ triguṇaṃ mārjārākṣanibhaṃ bhavettacca |
liptaṃ rasena puṭitaṃ hemārdhena mātrayā tulyam || ṅṛht_18.52 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

nāge tāre hemākṛṣṭimāha śulvamityādi || 18.51-52:1 ||
balinā gandhakena nihataṃ satsamabhāgaṃ tulyāṃśaṃ kuryātiti śeṣaḥ || 18.51-52:2 ||
ubhayamekīkṛtya saṃmiśrya mātārasenaiva nārīkṣīreṇa puṭayetpacediti ślokārthaḥ || 18.51-52:3 ||
hemākṛṣṭervidhānamāha tāre ityādi || 18.51-52:4 ||
pūrvoktaṃ cūrṇaṃ śulbajaṃ tīkṣṇajaṃ vā tāre triguṇaṃ vyūḍhaṃ vāhitaṃ satmārjārākṣasaṃnibha otunetrābhaṃ tāraṃ bhavet || 18.51-52:5 ||
hemārdhena mātrayā tārārdhabhāgena parimāṇena hemnā tulyamanyūnādhikaṃ rasena payasā || 18.51-52:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.53-55


<lead=> gold>
liptaṃ tadanu puṭitaṃ nāgaṃ hi rasena pādayuktena |
kharparakasthaṃ kṛtvā kāryaṃ vidhinā dṛḍhaṃ tāpyam || ṅṛht_18.53 ||
nirguṇḍīrasabhāvitapuṭitaṃ śilayā vartitaṃ ślakṣṇam |
tāvanmṛditapuṭitaṃ nirutthabhāvaṃ vrajedyāvat || ṅṛht_18.54 ||
tāre tannirvyūḍhaṃ yāvatpītaṃ bhavedruciram |
hemasamena ca militaṃ mātrātulyaṃ bhavetkanakam || ṅṛht_18.55 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

punarnāgavidhānamāha liptamityādi || 18.53-55:1 ||
triguṇaṃ yathā syāttathā pādayuktena rasena caturthāṃśasahitasūtena saha nāgaṃ sīsakaṃ kharparakasthaṃ mṛdbhājanakhaṇḍasthitaṃ kṛtvā vidhinā rasajñopadeśena dṛḍhaṃ tāpyaṃ vahniyutaṃ satnihataṃ kuryāditi vākyārthaḥ || 18.53-55:2 ||
taccāha nirguṇḍītyādi || 18.53-55:3 ||
pakvaṃ nāgacūrṇaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ śreṣṭhavidhānaṃ yathā syāttathā śilayā vartitaṃ sannirguṇḍīrasabhāvitapuṭitaṃ pūrṇaṃ bhāvitaṃ gharmapuṭitaṃ paścātpuṭitaṃ vahnipuṭitaṃ kuryāt || 18.53-55:4 ||
punaryāvannirutthabhāvamaśīratvaṃ vrajettāvanmṛditapuṭitaṃ marditapācitaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 18.53-55:5 ||
taccāha tāre ityādi || 18.53-55:6 ||
tannirutthanāgacūrṇaṃ yāvatpītaṃ pītavarṇaṃ tāraṃ bhavettāvadvāraṃ nirvyūḍhaṃ kuryāt || 18.53-55:7 ||
punarhemasamena kanakatulyāṃśena mātrātulyaṃ militaṃ satruciraṃ manoramaṃ kanakaṃ sarvaṃ bhavedityarthaḥ || 18.53-55:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.56-63


<svarṇamākṣika => gold>
tāpyaṃ cāṃgulisaṃjñaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā tadantare dattvā |
śulbasya guptamūṣā kāryā puṭitāpyatha ca dhmātā || ṅṛht_18.56 ||
hemnā militaṃ vidhinā mātrātulyaṃ bhavatyeva |
tāpyavidhānamāha tāpyamityādi || 18.56:1 ||
aṅgulisaṃjñaṃ tāpyaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ cūrṇaṃ kṛtvā tadantare taccūrṇamantare madhye dattvā śulbasya tāmrasya guptamūṣā andhamūṣā kāryā tatra nale ityabhiprāyaḥ || 18.56:2 ||
sā mūṣā puṭitā dhmātā kāryeti vidhānamuktam || 18.56:3 ||
taccāha hemnetyādi || 18.56:4 ||
pakvaṃ yanmākṣikacūrṇaṃ taddhemnā kṛtvā || 18.56:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.57-63


<gold:: rañjana (??)>
pādādijīrṇasūte lihyātpatrāṇi hemakṛṣṭīnām || ṅṛht_18.57 ||
krāmaṇayogena tato vilipya vidhinā nidhāya tulyādhaḥ |
paścāddhemnā sahitaṃ dhmātaṃ mūṣodare samāvartya || ṅṛht_18.58 ||
yantraṃ haṇḍyāṃ pakvaṃ pañcamṛdāvāpya puṭapakvam |
pādādijīrṇasūte pādādinā pādārdhasamānadinā jīrṇo yo'sau sūtaḥ tasmin hemakṛṣṭīnāṃ patrāṇi kaluṣakanakānāṃ patrāṇi lihyātkrāmaṇayogena lepayedityāgāmiślokāt || 18.57-58:1 ||
taccāha krāmaṇetyādi || 18.57-58:2 ||
tato rasalepānantaraṃ krāmaṇayogena kunaṭīmākṣikaviṣamityādinoktena vilipya tulyādhaḥ tulyaṃ yathā syāttathā adhobhāge nidhāya mūṣodare dhmātaṃ kuryāt || 18.57-58:3 ||
punaḥ paścāddhemnā kanakena sahitamāvartya dhmātaṃ kuryāt || 18.57-58:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.59-63


<gold:: production (?)>
vakṣyāmi cālepavidhiṃ kramati ca sūto yathā hi patreṣu |
rañjati yena vidhinā samāsataḥ sūtarājastu || ṅṛht_18.59 ||
kṛtvālaktakavastraṃ liptamanusnehamupari cūrṇena |
avacūrṇitaṃ tu kṛtvā gandhakaśilayā vidhānena || ṅṛht_18.60 ||
tadupari śṛtaṃ ca dattvā gandhakaśilācūrṇaṃ ca sūtavare |
paścādvartiḥ kāryā pātre dhṛtvāyase ca same || ṅṛht_18.61 ||
dīpaṃ pratibodhya tatastailaṃ dattvā tataḥ stokam |
pākaṃ yāmasyārdhaṃ svāṅge śītaṃ tataḥ kāryam || ṅṛht_18.62 ||
gṛhṇīyādatha sūtakṛṣṭīṃ liptā tatastena |
krāmaṇayogairliptvā puṭitā sā hemni nirdhmātā || ṅṛht_18.63 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha yantramityādi || 18.59-63:1 ||
vā yantraṃ pañcamṛdā valmīkamṛtgairikaṃ khaṭikā saindhavamiṣṭikā ceti pañcamṛdaḥ tayā kṛtvā haṇḍyāṃ sthālyāṃ pakvaṃ kāryam || 18.59-63:2 ||
atha puṭapakvaṃ gajapuṭādinā pācyamityarthaḥ || 18.59-63:3 ||
lepanavidhiṃ vakṣyāmi yathā patreṣu lepaḥ kāryaḥ punaryathā patreṣu kramati svaguṇān prakāśayati punaryena vidhinā rañjanaṃ rāgaṃ dadāti samāsataḥ saṃkṣepataḥ vidhinā vidhānataḥ sūtarāja evaṃvidho bhavettamupāyaṃ vakṣyāmīti || 18.59-63:4 ||
atha lepakrāmaṇaṃ rañjanavidhānamāha kṛtvālaktakavastramityādi || 18.59-63:5 ||
prathamamālaktakaṃ vastramalaktena rañjitaṃ yadvastraṃ tadālaktakamanu paścātsnehaṃ kaṅguṇyādīnāṃ tailaṃ liptaṃ kāryaṃ tattailaliptavastropari vakṣyamāṇauṣadhānāṃ cūrṇena avacūrṇanaṃ kuryāttailaliptavastraṃ gandhakaśilayā avacūrṇitaṃ kṛtvā tadupari dātavyaṃ darśayati || 18.59-63:6 ||
punastadupari gandhakaśilācūrṇopari śṛtaṃ dattvā punargandhakaśilācūrṇaṃ sūtavare sūtarājopari dattvā paścāttatkaraṇānantaraṃ vartiḥ kāryā sā vartirāyase lohamaye same samabhūmau pātre dhṛtvā tatropari kāryā || 18.59-63:7 ||
vidhyantaraṃ darśayati dīpamityādi || 18.59-63:8 ||
tato varteḥ pātropari karaṇānantaraṃ dīpaṃ pratibodhya prajvālya tato vāraṃvāraṃ stokamalpaṃ tailaṃ dattvā yāvadyāmasya praharasya ardhaṃ syāttāvatpākaṃ kuryātiti śeṣaḥ || 18.59-63:9 ||
tato'nantaraṃ tatpatitaṃ tailaṃ svāṅgaśītaṃ kāryamaṅge tailadravarūpe śarīre yathāsvaṃ svayameva śītaṃ yathā syāttathā kāryam || 18.59-63:10 ||
taccāha gṛhṇīyādityādi || 18.59-63:11 ||
atha śītakaraṇānantaram || 18.59-63:12 ||
sūtakṛṣṭīṃ ca gṛhṇīyātkarmavititi śeṣaḥ || 18.59-63:13 ||
tato'nantaraṃ sā sūtakṛṣṭī tena patreṇa liptā satī krāmaṇayogairliptvā hemni suvarṇe nirdhmātā kāryeti || 18.59-63:14 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.64-67


<gold:: production>
athavā daradaśilālairgandhakamākṣīkapakvamṛtanāgaiḥ |
kaṃkuṣṭhapravālasahitaiḥ piṣṭaiśca kaṅguṇītaile || ṅṛht_18.64 ||
madhye sūto yukto mṛditaḥ khalve tathāyase vidhinā |
saṃsvedya vaṃśanalikāṃ dolāyantreṇa sveditaṃ tridinam || ṅṛht_18.65 ||
etairliptvā kṛṣṇaiḥ patraṃ pūrvoktavidhānena |
nāgaṃ dattvā prakaṭaṃ stokaṃ stokaṃ krameṇaiva || ṅṛht_18.66 ||
bhavati hi kanakaṃ divyamakṣīṇaṃ devayogyaṃ ca |
evaṃ jāritasūte sakalāḥ khalu haṇḍikāḥ sarvāḥ || ṅṛht_18.67 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha athavetyādi || 18.64-67:1 ||
darado hiṅgulaḥ śilā manohvā ālaṃ haritālaṃ taiḥ gandhakaḥ pratītaḥ mākṣikaṃ yatpakvaṃ sindūrīkṛtaṃ mṛtanāgaśca taiḥ || 18.64-67:2 ||
punaḥ kaṅkuṣṭhapravālasahitaiḥ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viraṅgaṃ pravālaṃ vidrumaṃ tābhyāṃ sahitaiḥ || 18.64-67:3 ||
punaretaiḥ kaṅguṇītaile jyotiṣmatītaile piṣṭaiścūrṇīkṛtaiḥ madhye sūto yuktaḥ kārya ityagrimaślokasaṃbandhāt || 18.64-67:4 ||
taccāha madhye ityādi || 18.64-67:5 ||
pūrvoktairauṣadhaiḥ kṛtvā madhye auṣadhāntaḥ sūto yuktaḥ kāryaḥ || 18.64-67:6 ||
punarmardayitvā vaṃśanalikāṃ riktodarāṃ prati saṃvedya dinatrayaṃ dolāyantreṇa sveditaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 18.64-67:7 ||
taccāha liptvetyādi || 18.64-67:8 ||
etāni sveditauṣadhāni saṃyuktāni taiḥ kṛṣṇaiḥ kṛṣṇalavaṇaiḥ pūrvoktavidhānena sūtakṛṣṭīvidhānena patraṃ liptvā punaḥ prakaṭaṃ yathā syāttathā stokamalpamalpaṃ krameṇa nāgaṃ dattvā kanakaṃ jāyate ityagrimaślokasaṃbandhaḥ || 18.64-67:9 ||
evaṃ kṛte sati akṣīṇamakṣayaṃ divyaṃ pravaraṃ devayogyaṃ devā indrādayastadyogyaṃ kanakaṃ bhavati || 18.64-67:10 ||
yukto'yamarthaḥ || 18.64-67:11 ||
evaṃ jāritasūte jāritakarmakṛte rase khalu niścitaṃ sarvā haṇḍikāḥ sarve dhātvādyāḥ sakalāḥ saprasavāḥ syurityarthaḥ || 18.64-67:12 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.68


<relation between bīja andgold(?)>
nirbījaṃ samajīrṇe pādaikenaiva ṣoḍaśāṃśena |
ardhena pādayogaṃ pādenaikena tulyakanakaṃ ca || ṅṛht_18.68 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣamāha nirbījamityādi || 18.68:1 ||
nirbījaṃ yathā syāttathā samajīrṇaṃ pādena turyāṃśena phalaṃ dadāti tathā ardhena jīrṇena ṣoḍaśāṃśena phalaṃ dadāti ca punastadardhena jīrṇena tatpādayogaṃ tatpādena ekena tatpādayogaṃ phalaṃ dadātīti sarvatra vācyam || 18.68:2 ||
tulyakanakaṃ ca jīrṇaṃ pūrṇaṃ phalaṃ dadātīti || 18.68:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.69


<tārākṛṣṭi>
tārākṛṣṭiṃ vakṣye mṛtavaṅgaṃ tālakena tulyāṃśam |
lambitamatha nirdhmātaṃ tāmraṃ tārachaviṃ vahati || ṅṛht_18.69 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

hemākṛṣṭyanantaraṃ tārākṛṣṭiṃ vakṣye ahaṃ kaviḥ kathayāmi mṛtavaṅgaṃ māritaṃ vaṅgaṃ tālakena haritāleneti || 18.69:1 ||
atha tāmraṃ tulyāṃśaṃ lambitaṃ vistīrṇaṃ yathā syāttathā nirdhmātaṃ sattārachaviṃ vahati rūpyadyutiṃ prāpnotītyarthaḥ || 18.69:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.70


paścānnāgaṃ deyaṃ prakāśamūṣāsu nirmalaṃ yāvat |
tāvaddhmātaṃ vidhinā sunirmalaṃ nistaraṅgaṃ tu || ṅṛht_18.70 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha paścādityādi || 18.70:1 ||
paścādvaṅgatāmrayogānantaraṃ prakāśamūṣāsu yāvatnirmalaṃ malavarjitaṃ syāttāvannāgaṃ deyaṃ punaryāvannirmalamujjvalaṃ nistaraṅgaṃ nāgormivarjitaṃ syāttāvadvidhinā dhmātaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ || 18.70:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.71-72


<??>
tālaśilāsarjikābhiḥ saindhavalavaṇena nayanahitasahitaiḥ |
ekaikaṃ sahitaṃ vā vedhaṃ dattvā punaḥ śulbe || ṅṛht_18.71 ||
chagaṇaṃ māhiṣatakraṃ snuhīkṣīreṇa sarpiṣā kramaśaḥ |
saguḍadugdhamadhuvimiśraiḥ kramaśo vedhe niṣekaśca || ṅṛht_18.72 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣamāha tāletyādi || 18.71-72:1 ||
tālaṃ haritālaṃ śilā manohvā sarjikā pratītā tābhiḥ saindhavaṃ ca tatlavaṇaṃ ca tena nayanahitasahitaiḥ etaiḥ kṛtvā ekaikaṃ pṛthaktvena vā sahitamekatvena punaḥ śulve tāmre vedhaṃ pratidadhyāditi || 18.71-72:2 ||
tāravedhaniṣekānyāha chagaṇamityādi || 18.71-72:3 ||
chagaṇaṃ vanotpannaṃ māhiṣaṃ takraṃ mahiṣyāḥ idaṃ māhiṣaṃ snuhikṣīreṇa sehuṇḍadugdhena saha punaḥ sarpiṣā ghṛtena saha guḍadugdhamadhubhirmiśraiḥ militaṃ kṛtvā kramaśo vedhakarmaṇi niṣekaḥ kāryaḥ || 18.71-72:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.73


<tārakṛṣṭī; copper=> silver>
kāñcī brāhmī kuṭilaṃ tālakaṃ samabhāgayojitaṃ dhmātam |
śulbaṃ viddhamanena tu tārākṛṣṭirbhaveddivyā || ṅṛht_18.73 ||
------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tārākṛṣṭimāha kāñcītyādi || 18.73:1 ||
kāñcī svarṇamākṣikaṃ brāhmī somāhvā kuṭilaṃ sīsaṃ tālakaṃ pratītametatsamayojitaṃ samāṃśamelitaṃ satdhmātaṃ kuryātpunaranena kāñcyādigaṇena viddhaṃ śulbaṃ divyā manoramā tārākṛṣṭirbhavet || 18.73:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.74-75


<silver:: optimiśation>
evaṃ tārākṛṣṭirliptvā viddhā rasena sāritena |
tāraṃ karoti vimalaṃ lepaṃ vā pādajīrṇādi || ṅṛht_18.74 ||
iti miśrīkṛtaviddhaṃ kramitaṃ tvatha mātṛkātulyam |
tāradalaṃ bhavati chedanatāḍananikaṣaiśca nirdoṣam || ṅṛht_18.75 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tārākṛṣṭimāha evamityādi || 18.74-75:1 ||
evamuktavidhānena sāritena sāraṇākarmakṛtena liptvā viddhā satī tārākṛṣṭirbhavet || 18.74-75:2 ||
punariyaṃ tārākṛṣṭiḥ tāraṃ vimalaṃ malavarjitaṃ karoti vā pādajīrṇādi pādena jīrṇaṃ yasminādiśabdādardhasamagrahaṇaṃ kāryaṃ tatlepamiti || 18.74-75:3 ||
viśeṣamāha itītyādi || 18.74-75:4 ||
iti pūrvoktavidhānena miśrīkṛtaṃ militaṃ viddhaṃ kramitaṃ mātṛkātulyaṃ samāṃśaṃ sattāradalaṃ rūpyapatraṃ bhavati tadrūpyadalaṃ chedanatāḍananikaṣaiḥ chedanaṃ khaṇḍanaṃ tāḍanaṃ ghanaghātaḥ nikaṣaṃ śilopari parīkṣaṇaṃ tairiti tāpaiśca nirdoṣaṃ tadbhavati || 18.74-75:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht18.76


evaṃ vedhavidhānaṃ śāstravidhijñena karmakuśalena |
jñātvā gurūpadeśaṃ kartavyaṃ karmanipuṇena || ṅṛht_18.76 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

evamamunā prakāreṇa śāstravidhijñena śāstrasya vidhiṃ jānātīti saḥ tena karmanipuṇena saṃskārapravīṇena kuśalena kartrā gurūpadeśaṃ gururuktalakṣaṇo granthādau tasya upadeśaṃ jñātvā vedhavidhānaṃ kartavyamityarthaḥ || 18.76:1 ||


____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.1


iti rasarājasya vidhau vedhavidhānaṃ prasaṃgataḥ proktam |
adhunā proktānapi vakṣyāmi rasāyane yogān || ṅṛht_19.1 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha bhakṣaṇavidhānamāha itītyādi || 19.1:1 ||
rasarājasya vidhau rasendrakarmavidhāne vedhavidhānaṃ prasaṅgataḥ prastāvataḥ proktaṃ na tu svaprajñāsamam || 19.1:2 ||
adhunā proktānapi api śabdādanubhūtānapi rasāyane jarāvyādhināśanavidhau yogān dravyasamudāyātkān vakṣyāmi kathayāmītyarthaḥ || 19.1:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.2-4


<kṣetrīkaraṇa (śarīraśodhana)>
ādau prātaḥ prātaḥ saindhavayuktaṃ ghṛtaṃ pibettridinam |
tadanu kvāthaṃ tridinaṃ yuñjīyātketakītanujam || ṅṛht_19.2 ||
vidhinā svedyo dehaḥ kartavyo vārtikendreṇa |
kvathitaṃ kaṭurohiṇyāḥ saṃśodhanamanuprayuñjīta || ṅṛht_19.3 ||
tadanu ca śuddhādūrdhvaṃ śleṣmānte recite sakalam |
yāvakapathyaṃ tridinaṃ ghṛtasahitaṃ tatprayuñjīta || ṅṛht_19.4 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

kāyaśodhanamāha ādāvityādi || 19.2-4:1 ||
prathamaṃ prātaḥ prātaḥ pratyūṣe tridinaṃ saindhavamilitaṃ ghṛtamājyaṃ pibet || 19.2-4:2 ||
anyasaṃyogamāha tadanu ghṛtasaindhavānantaraṃ ketakītanujaṃ kvāthaṃ ketakyāḥ tanuḥ śarīraṃ tasmājjātaṃ ketakīmūlasaṃbhavamityarthaḥ aṅge'pyanukte vihitaṃ tu mūlamiti nyāyāttridinaṃ prayuñjīyādityarthaḥ || 19.2-4:3 ||
vidhyantaramāha vidhinetyādi || 19.2-4:4 ||
tato vidhinā svedavidhānena dehaḥ śarīraṃ vārtikendreṇa rasasaṃpradāyavidā svedyaḥ svinnaḥ kartavyaḥ || 19.2-4:5 ||
anyatkiṃ kaṭurohiṇyāḥ tiktāyāḥ kvathitaṃ prasādhitaṃ samyakśuddhikaraṇamanuprayuñjīta svedānantaramityabhiprāyaḥ || 19.2-4:6 ||
vidhyantaramāha tadityādi || 19.2-4:7 ||
tadanu kaṭukarohiṇīsevanānantaraṃ śuddhādūrdhvaṃ yathā syāttathā śleṣmāntarecite sati yathā śleṣmaṇo'ntaḥ syāttathā recite sati tridinaparimāṇaṃ yāvakapathyaṃ ghṛtasahitaṃ prayuñjīteti || 19.2-4:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.5-7


punarapi ca pānayogaṃ vakṣyāmi ca sakalabhuvanahitakṛtaye |
pītvā prathame yāme coṣṇodakasamamidaṃ cūrṇam || ṅṛht_19.5 ||
pathyāsaindhavadhātrīmaricavacāguḍaviḍaṅgarajanīnām |
śuṇṭhīpippalyorapi cūrṇaṃ tridinaṃ prayuñjīta || ṅṛht_19.6 ||
amunā śuddhaśarīraḥ parihatasaṃsargadoṣabalī |
pītvā payasā sahitaṃ yāvakamamunā bhavecchuddhaḥ || ṅṛht_19.7 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha punarapītyādi || 19.5-7:1 ||
punarapīti yāvakapathyayogānantaraṃ ca punaḥ pānayogaṃ vakṣyāmi kimarthaṃ sakalabhuvanahitakṛtaye samastasaṃsārahitakaraṇāya idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ cūrṇaṃ pathyādyamuṣṇodakasamaṃ taptajalena saha prathamayāme prathamapraharāntaḥ pītvā śuddhaśarīro bhavedityāgāmiślokasaṃbandhāt || 19.5-7:2 ||
śodhanāya cūrṇamāha pathyetyādi || 19.5-7:3 ||
pathyā harītakī saindhavaṃ pratītaṃ dhātrī āmalakaṃ marīcamūṣaṇaṃ vacā ugragandhā guḍaḥ pratītaḥ viḍaṅgaṃ kṛmighnaṃ rajanī haridrā śuṇṭhīpippalyorapīti śuṇṭhī nāgaraṃ pippalī māgadhī āsāmauṣadhīnāṃ cūrṇaṃ tridinaṃ prayuñjīta || 19.5-7:4 ||
uṣṇajalasamamiti jñeyam || 19.5-7:5 ||
amunetyādi |
amunā vakṣyamāṇavirecanena yāvakādinā śuddhaśarīraḥ san parihatasaṃsargadoṣabalī bhavati saṃsargeṇa ye doṣāḥ śarīrābhyantarāste saṃsargadoṣāḥ te parihatā jitā yena saḥ parihatasaṃsargadoṣaḥ tena balī balayuktaḥ doṣanivṛttau guṇapravṛttirityavaśyam || 19.5-7:6 ||
kiṃ kṛtvā parihatadoṣaḥ amunā payasā uṣṇodakena yāvakamalaktaṃ pītvā śuddho bhavedityarthaḥ || 19.5-7:7 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.8

akṛtakṣetrīkaraṇe rasāyanaṃ yo naraḥ prayuñjīta |
tasya krāmati na rasaḥ sa rasaḥ sarvāṅgadoṣakṛdbhavati || ṅṛht_19.8 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

saṃśodhanasyākaraṇe doṣamāha akṛtetyādi || 19.8:1 ||
yo naraḥ pumānakṛtakṣetrīkaraṇe dehe iti śeṣaḥ na kṛtamakṛtaṃ kṣetrīkaraṇaṃ yasmin tasminsati rasāyanaṃ jarāvyādhivināśanauṣadhaṃ prayuñjīta tasya puṃso raso na krāmati svaguṇānna prakāśayati tarhi kiṃ sarvāṅgadoṣakṛdbhavati bāhucaraṇādiṣu ṣaṭsvaṅgeṣu vikārakṛtsyāt || 19.8:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.9


iti śuddho jātabalaḥ śālyodanayāvakākhyamudgarasaḥ |
kṣetrīkṛtanijadehaḥ kurvīta rasāyanaṃ vidhivat || ṅṛht_19.9 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rasāyanādhikāratvamāha itītyādi || 19.9:1 ||
pūrvoktavidhānena śuddhaḥ san yo jātabalo bhavati sa kṣetrīkṛtanijadehaḥ akṣetraṃ kṣetraṃ kriyata iti kṣetrīkṛto nijadehaḥ śarīraṃ yena saḥ matimān rasāyanaṃ vidhivatprakurvīta || 19.9:2 ||
kaiḥ jātabalaḥ śālyodanaṃ ca yāvakākhyaṃ ca mudgāśca taiḥ || 19.9:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.10-11


suratarutailaghṛtamadhudhātrīrasapayāṃsi nirmathya |
pītvā viśuddhakoṣṭho bhavati pumānantaritaśuddhaḥ || ṅṛht_19.10 ||
māsena kāntimedhe dvābhyāṃ praśamayati doṣanikaraṃ ca |
māsatritayena punaḥ svādamaravapurmahātejāḥ || ṅṛht_19.11 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidhyantaramāha suratarvityādi || 19.10-11:1 ||
antaritaśuddhaḥ antaritaṃ śuddhaṃ yasya saḥ grahaṇīrogādivarjita ityarthaḥ etāni auṣadhāni nirmathya pītvā viśuddhakoṣṭho bhavati viśuddhaṃ malavarjitaṃ koṣṭhamudaraṃ kasyetyevaṃvidho bhavati || 19.10-11:2 ||
tāni kāni suratarutailetyādīni suratarurdevavṛkṣaḥ tattailapeṣaṇaṃ tailamityarthaḥ ghṛtamājyaṃ madhu kṣaudraṃ dhātrīrasaḥ āmalakīsalilaṃ payo dugdhametāni sarvāṇi nirmathya ekīkṛtyetyarthaḥ || 19.10-11:3 ||
etadauṣadhabhakṣaṇaguṇaṃ māsakrameṇāha māsenetyādi || 19.10-11:4 ||
asya auṣadhasya māsena māsapramāṇena bhakṣaṇātkāntirbhavati medhā ceti dvābhyāṃ dvimāsābhyāṃ doṣanikaraṃ gadasamudāyaṃ praśamayati śāntiṃ nayati punarmāsatritayena trimāsapramāṇena svātsvasāmānyaśarīrātamaravapurdevaśarīro mahātejāḥ dīptimān syādityarthaḥ || 19.10-11:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.12


suradārutailamājyaṃ triphalārasasaṃyutaṃ ca samabhāgam |
pītaṃ tatsaptāhānnayanavikāraṃ śamaṃ nayati || ṅṛht_19.12 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

yogāntaramāha suradārvityādi || 19.12:1 ||
suradārutailaṃ devadārutailamājyaṃ ghṛtaṃ triphalārasasaṃyutaṃ triphalāyā rasena draveṇa saṃyutaṃ sahitaṃ ca punaḥ samabhāgaṃ tulyāṃśaṃ tatpītaṃ satsaptāhātsaptadinapramāṇataḥ nayanavikāraṃ netrasaṃbhavaṃ rogaṃ śamaṃ nayati śāntiṃ prāpayati || 19.12:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.13


suratarutailaṃ saghṛtaṃ pītvā śālyodanaṃ ca sakṣīram |
jīrṇāhāre bhuktvā harati hi sakuṣṭhān pīnasādīṃśca || ṅṛht_19.13 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

yogāntaramāha suratarutailamityādi || 19.13:1 ||
devadārutailaṃ saghṛtaṃ sājyametadubhayaṃ pītvā sakṣīraṃ śālyodanaṃ bhuktvā ṣaṣṭikaudanamityabhiprāyaḥ punarjīrṇāhāre pratidinaṃ dināntavīradvaye veditavyam || 19.13:2 ||
ca punaḥ sakuṣṭhān kuṣṭhaiḥ saha vartante evaṃvidhān pīnasādīn rogān harati dūrīkaroti || 19.13:3 ||
____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.14


ghṛtasahitaḥ pittakṛtāntailayukto vātasaṃbhavān rogān |
guḍasahito madhunā vā kaphajān hantyamaradārurasaḥ || ṅṛht_19.14 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

yogāntaramāha ghṛtasahita ityādi || 19.14:1 ||
amaradāruraso devadārujalaṃ ghṛtasahitamājyamiśritaḥ pittakṛtān rogān harati nāśayati || 19.14:2 ||
punastailayutastailena miśrito devadārurasaḥ vātasaṃbhavān rogān hanti || 19.14:3 ||
punarguḍasahitaḥ vā madhunā kṣaudreṇa sahito devadārurasaḥ vātasaṃbhavān rogān hanti || 19.14:4 ||
punarguḍasahitaḥ vā madhunā kṣaudreṇa sahito devadārurasaḥ kaphajān rogān hantīti vākyārthaḥ || 19.14:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.15


varjitakāṃjikaśākaṃ payasā śālyodanaṃ ca yuñjīta |
dvicatuḥṣaṭpalamānaṃ mātrādhamamadhyamajyeṣṭhāḥ || ṅṛht_19.15 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rasāyane bhojyābhojyamāha varjitetyādi || 19.15:1 ||
varjitakāñjikaśākaṃ varjitaṃ kāñjikaṃ sauvīraṃ śākaṃ vāstukādi ca yasmin tattathā payasā kṣīreṇa saha śālyodanaṃ bhuñjīta || 19.15:2 ||
punaḥ śālyodanaṃ kiyanmānaṃ dvicatuḥṣaṭpalamānam || 19.15:3 ||
atra mātrādhamamadhyamajyeṣṭhā dvicatuḥṣaṭpalapramāṇā adhamamadhyamottamabaleṣu prayojyetyarthaḥ || 19.15:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.16-19

tadanu pātanaśuddhaṃ sūtakamāroṭamaśnīyāt |
svedanamūrcchotthāpanapātanarodhāśca niyamaśca || ṅṛht_19.16 ||
abhrakasahitaḥ pātyo vidhinā yāvatsthiro bhavati |
athavā mākṣikasahitaḥ pātyaḥ sūto vidhānena || ṅṛht_19.17 ||
ityāroṭaḥ sūtaḥ kṣetrīkaraṇe niyujyate prathamam |
atha rasayogamāha tadityādi || 19.16-18 1 ||
tadanu pathyamātropayogānantaraṃ pātanaśuddhaṃ pātanena śuddhaṃ sūtakamāroṭameva sāmānyamevāśnīyātbhakṣayet || 19.16-18 2 ||
ca punaḥ svedanamūrchotthāpanarodhāśca svedanaṃ ca mūrchā ca utthāpanaṃ ca pātanāni ca nirodhaśceti dvandvaḥ ete yadyapi santi niyamaśca yadyapyasti tathāpyāroṭaḥ pātanena syādityarthaḥ || 19.16-18 3 ||
prasaṅgataḥ pātanamāha abhraketyādi || 19.16-18 4 ||
sūtaḥ abhrakasahitaḥ gaganamilitaḥ san pātyaḥ || 19.16-18 5 ||
kena vidhānena ḍamarukayantrādinā || 19.16-18 6 ||
punastāvadyāvatsthiro bhavati || 19.16-18 7 ||
athavā vidhyantare sūto mākṣikasahitaḥ pātyo vidhānena ayamapi yāvatsthiro bhavati || 19.16-18 8 ||
rasāyane sūtasya āroṭādividhānamāha itītyādi || 19.16-18 9 ||
āroṭa iti pūrvoktena pātanakarmaṇā ūrdhvādhastiryagbhavena sādhita āroṭaḥ saḥ prathamaṃ yathā syāttathā rasāyane jarāvyādhināśane niyujyate iti || 19.16-18 10 ||

athavā kalkayogena rasādisambhūtena raso bhasma kṛtvā || 19.17:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.18-19


athavā bhasma ca kṛtvā baddho vā kalkayogena || ṅṛht_19.18 ||
mākṣikaśilājatulohacūrṇapathyākṣaviḍaṅgaghṛtamadhubhiḥ |
saṃyuktaṃ rasamādau kṣetrīkaraṇāya yuñjīta || ṅṛht_19.19 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

dvividhaṃ bhasma ūrdhvagaṃ talabhasma ca varṇabhedena ṣaḍvidhaṃ śvetaṃ bhasma pītaṃ bhasma haritaṃ bhasma raktaṃ bhasma kṛṣṇaṃ bhasma karburaṃ bhasma iti ṣaḍvidhaṃ tatkṛtvā kṣetrīkaraṇe niyujyate prathamam || 19.18 d-19:1 ||
rasayogamāha mākṣiketyādi || 19.18 d-19:2 ||
mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ śilājatu prasiddhaṃ lohacūrṇaṃ māritamuṇḍasya rajaḥ pathyā harītakī akṣo vibhītakaḥ viḍaṅgaṃ kṛmighnaṃ ghṛtamājyaṃ madhu kṣaudrametaiḥ samprayuktaṃ rasaṃ kṣetrīkaraṇāya prayuñjīteti vākyārthaḥ || 19.18 d-19:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.20


iti kalkīkṛtasūtaṃ ghanakāntamadhughṛtādisaṃyuktam |
bhuktvāmaratāṃ gacchetkṣetrīkaraṇaṃ pradhānamidam || ṅṛht_19.20 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

ityuktavidhānena kalkīkṛtaṃ sūtaṃ bhuktvā amaratāṃ devatvaṃ gacchet || 19.20:1 ||
iti kiṃ ghanakāntamadhughṛtādisaṃyuktaṃ ghano'bhrakaḥ kāntaṃ lohajāti madhu kṣaudraṃ ghṛtamājyamādiśabdātsitā grāhyā etaiḥ saṃyuktaṃ satkalkīkṛtamidaṃ ca pradhānaṃ kṣetrīkaraṇaṃ kṣetrī kriyate'neneti || 19.20:2 ||
ityāroṭakriyā || 19.20:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.21


atha kṛṣṇaṃ vā pītaṃ vā saṃyojyaṃ ghanaṃ śikhiprabhaṃ bahuśaḥ |
surabhīkṣīraniṣiktaṃ gatagiridoṣaṃ rasāyane yojyam || ṅṛht_19.21 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha patrābhrakriyā || 19.21:1 ||
kṛṣṇaṃ śyāmavarṇaṃ ghanamabhraṃ pītaṃ pītavarṇaṃ ghanaṃ vā bahuśo naikavāraṃ śikhiprabhamagniprabhaṃ satsurabhīkṣīraniṣiktaṃ secitaṃ kāryaṃ punargatagiridoṣaṃ yathā syāttathā gatā girijā doṣā mahīdharasaṃbhavā doṣā yasmāttadevaṃ saṃśodhayitvā tatghanaṃ rasāyane jarāvyādhivināśakaraṇe yojyam || 19.21:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.22


niścandrikamapi śuddhaṃ viḍaṃgatriphalājyamadhusamāyuktam |
pratidivasaṃ palamekaṃ bhuktvā kṣīrāśano vidhinā || ṅṛht_19.22 ||

------------------
*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

abhrakayogamāha niścandrikamityādi || 19.22:1 ||
ghanaṃ niścandrikamapi śuddhaṃ candrikārahitamapi nirdoṣaṃ viḍaṅgatriphalājyamadhurasamāyuktaṃ kṛmighnaharītakīvibhītakāmalakaghṛtakṣaudramilitaṃ pratidivasaṃ pratidinamekapalapramāṇaṃ sarvaṃ bhuktvā vidhinā śuddhaśarīravidhānena kṣīrāśano bhavetkṣīreṇa saha śālyodanāśanaṃ samācaredityarthaḥ || 19.22:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.23


trikaṭukaviḍaṅgatriphalāmākṣikaśilājatuyutaṃ vyoma |
kṣīraudanamaśnīyājjīvati jantuḥ śataṃ varṣam || ṅṛht_19.23 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha trikaṭuketyādi || 19.23:1 ||
vyoma abhrakaṃ trikaṭukaviḍaṅgatriphalāmākṣikaśilājatuyutaṃ pippalīmaricanāgaraharītakīvibhītakāmalakakṣaudraśilājatumilitamaśnīyādbhakṣayet || 19.23:2 ||
punaḥ kṣīraudanaṃ pathyamaśnīyāt || 19.23:3 ||
eva kṛte sati jantuḥ śataṃ jīvati jantujanyuśarīriṇaḥ ityamaraḥ || 19.23:4 ||
iti patrābhrakaprakriyā || 19.23:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.24


ityevamādayo'nye kāñjikayuktāśca kīrtitā bahuśaḥ |
patrābhrakaprayogā varjyā niryuktikāste hi || ṅṛht_19.24 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

abhrakasyottarottaraṃ darśayannāha itītyādi || 19.24:1 ||
ityevamādayaḥ iti pūrvoktā yogā ādayo yeṣāṃ te anye kāñjikayuktāśca kāñjikenāranālena yuktāḥ yogīkṛtāḥ granthāntare rasāvatārādau bahuśaḥ kīrtitāḥ kathitāḥ te patrābhrakayogāḥ niryuktikāḥ niryukte bhavā ikpratyayāntāste varjyāḥ sevane'yogyā ityarthaḥ || 19.24:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.25


ye patrābhrakayogā rasāyanārthaṃ kīrtitā vidhinā |
ajñātadravyaguṇaistairupadiṣṭo jarāmṛtyuḥ || ṅṛht_19.25 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

avidhinā patrābhrakayogo vikārāyetyata āha ya ityādi || 19.25:1 ||
ye ajñātadravyaguṇaiḥ ajñātā dravyāṇāmabhrādināṃ guṇā yaistairevaṃvidhaiḥ puṃbhiḥ vidhinā sāmānyavidhānena patrābhrakayogāḥ rasāyanārthaṃ kīrtitāstaireva jarā mṛtyuśca upadiṣṭaḥ jarā pālityaṃ mṛtyurvyādhiḥ || 19.25:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.26


aprāptalokabhāvaṃ ghano'sya jaṭharāgnimupaśamaṃ nayati |
agniṃ vināpi naśyati paribhūto vividharogagaṇaiḥ || ṅṛht_19.26 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

jarāmṛtyorupadeśe hetumāha aprāptetyādi aprāptalokabhavaṃ yathā syāttathā asya kartuḥ ghanaḥ || 19.26:1 ||
jaṭharāgniṃ vināpi pumānnaśyati nāśaṃ prāpnoti || 19.26:2 ||
kiṃbhūtaḥ san vividharogagaṇaiḥ nānārogasamūhaiḥ paribhūto vijitaḥ saniti vākyārthaḥ || 19.26:3 ||
kāṣṭhe sthitamapi ghanapaṭalamadhye'pi abhre'bhrasattvaṃ sthitaṃ tadāha abhrasatvaṃ ghanasāraṃ ghanapaṭale sthitamapi nijakāryaṃ svakīyakṛtyaṃ tathā na kurute vahniragniḥ || 19.26:4 ||
kāṣṭhe dāruṇi sthitaḥ sannijakāryaṃ na kurute tu punaḥ yathā ghṛtaṃ payasi dugdhe sthitaṃ satsvīkāryaṃ na kurute cāgrahiriva dhanasthamiva madhye sthitaṃ kāryaṃ kurute'tiniḥsṛtaṃ satpṛthagbhūtaṃ satnijakāryaṃ kuruta iti bhāvaḥ || 19.26:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.27


<abhra:: inedible>
abhrasya rasāyanināṃ bhakṣyamiha kīrtitaṃ paraṃ satvam |
trividhaṃ gaganamabhakṣyaṃ kācaḥ kiṭṭaṃ ca pattrarajaḥ || ṅṛht_19.27 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

bhakṣyābhakṣyamabhramāha abhrasyetyādi || 19.27:1 ||
abhrakasatvamiha kṣetrīkaraṇe rasāyanināṃ jarāvyādhivināśecchūnāṃ paramutkṛṣṭaṃ bhakṣyamaśanayogyaṃ kīrtitam || 19.27:2 ||
punastrividhaṃ triprakāraṃ gaganamabhramabhakṣyamabhojyam || 19.27:3 ||
kiṃ tatkācaḥ kācarūpaṃ kiṭṭaṃ malarūpaṃ pattrarajaḥ sāmānyābhracūrṇaṃ ceti trividhamityarthaḥ || 19.27:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.28-33


ādau ghanaloharajastriphalārasabhāvanaiśca nirghṛṣṭam |
kurvītāñjanasadṛśaṃ sthagitavastreṇa sūryakaraiḥ || ṅṛht_19.28 ||
itthaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ kṛtvā vividhakāntalohacūrṇasamam |
lohaghanaṃ ca tadevaṃ bhṛṅgeṇa ca sādhayedbahuśaḥ || ṅṛht_19.29 ||
triphalāghṛtamadhumiśritamamṛtamidaṃ māsasthitaṃ dhānye |
śastrakaṭorikasampuṭamadhyagataṃ pūjitaṃ mantraiḥ || ṅṛht_19.30 ||
māsena tu taduddhṛtya jñātvā balaṃ tatprayuñjīta |
kāntaṃ vinātha gaganaṃ gaganaṃ vinā tathā ca kāntam || ṅṛht_19.31 ||
sthaulyaṃ paṭalaṃ kācaṃ timirārbudakarṇanādaśūlāni |
hantyarśāṃsi bhagandaramehaplīhādi pālityam || ṅṛht_19.32 ||
etatkurvanmatimān gorasamastupradhānamaśnīyāt |
jāṅgalamudgājyapayaḥ śālyodanaṃ brahmacaryeṇa || ṅṛht_19.33 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

adhunā ghanasattvalohasādhanamāha ādāvityādi || 19.28-33:1 ||
ādau prathamaṃ ghanaloharajaḥ ghanamabhrasatvaṃ loharajaḥ kāntacūrṇaṃ triphalārasabhāvanaiḥ harītakīvibhītakāmalakadravapuṭanairnirghṛṣṭaṃ satañjanasadṛśaṃ sauvīrāñjanatulyaṃ kurvīta kaiḥ kṛtvā sūryakaraiḥ kena sthagitavastreṇa ācchāditapaṭena vastreṇācchādya sūryakarasannidhau dhāryamityarthaḥ || 19.28-33:2 ||
itthamityādi || 19.28-33:3 ||
itthamamunā prakāreṇa ślakṣṇamañjanasannibhaṃ yathā syāttathā ghanasatvakāntaṃ kṛtvā punarlohaghanaṃ lohaṃ muṇḍādi ghanamabhrasatvametadubhayaṃ vividhakāntalohacūrṇasamaṃ vividhā nānājātayaḥ ayaskāntabhedāḥ teṣāṃ cūrṇaṃ tatsamaṃ kṛtvā bhṛṅgeṇa ca bahuśo'nekavāraṃ sādhayedbhāvayedityarthaḥ || 19.28-33:4 ||
taccāha triphaletyādi || 19.28-33:5 ||
tadbhṛṅgarājena bahuśo bhāvitaṃ ghanasatvakāntamidamamṛtaṃ sudhāsamaṃ na mṛtamamṛtaṃ tattriphalāmadhughṛtamiśritaṃ harītakīvibhītakāmalakaghṛtakṣaudramilitaṃ dhānye kasyacidannasyāntaḥ māsasthitaṃ kuryātmāsaikaparimāṇaṃ tatra vidhātavyamiti vyaktiḥ || 19.28-33:6 ||
kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ mantraiśca sampūjitam || 19.28-33:7 ||
tathā mantrastu |
mandāramālākulitālakāyai kapālamālāṅkitaśekharāya |
divyāmbarāyai ca digambarāya namaḥ śivāyai ca namaḥ śivāya |
ityādibhiḥ || 19.28-33:8 ||
taccāha māsenetyādi || 19.28-33:9 ||
dhānyānmāsena māsaikaparimāṇenoddhṛtya bahirnītvā punarapi balaṃ jñātvā prayuñjīta bhoktre dadyātatha viśeṣaṃ darśayati kāntaṃ vinā abhrakasatvameva kṛtvā prayuñjīta ca punargaganaṃ vinā kāntaṃ kevalaṃ pūrvavidhānena sādhayitvā prayuñjītetyarthaḥ || 19.28-33:10 ||
phalamāha sthaulyamityādi || 19.28-33:11 ||
sthaulyamiti medorogaḥ paṭalakācatimirāṇi netrarogāḥ arbudaṃ granthiviśeṣaḥ karṇanādaḥ pratītaḥ śūlamaṣṭavidhametāni hanti arśāṃsi gudajāni bhagandaramehaplīhādi bhagandaraḥ gudavraṇaṃ mehaḥ pramehaḥ bījavikāraḥ plīhā plīharogaḥ ete rogā ādiryasya tathanti pālityaṃ jarāṃ ca nāśayatītyarthaḥ || 19.28-33:12 ||
pathyamāha etadityādi || 19.28-33:13 ||
etanniṣpannauṣadhabhakṣaṇaṃ kurvanmatimān puruṣaḥ gorasamastupradhānaṃ goraso godugdhaṃ mastu dadhimastu evaṃpradhānamannamaśnīyātbhuñjīta || 19.28-33:14 ||
punarjāṅgalamudgājyapayo'śnīyātjāṅgalasyedaṃ jāṅgalaṃ svalpāmbuśākhīdeśaḥ jāṅgalaḥ mudgaḥ pratīto'nnaviśeṣaḥ ājyaṃ ghṛtaṃ payo dugdhaṃ vā salilaṃ yanmudgājyapayaḥ taccāśnīyāt || 19.28-33:15 ||
ca punaḥ śālyodanamaśnīyāt || 19.28-33:16 ||
etatsarvaṃ brahmacaryeṇa kartavyamityarthaḥ || 19.28-33:17 ||
iti satvābhrakriyā || 19.28-33:18 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.34


ghanasatvapādajīrṇaḥ kāntajīrṇo yattīkṣṇasamajīrṇaḥ |
kṣetrīkaraṇaḥ paramaḥ prayujyate'pi punarāroṭaḥ || ṅṛht_19.34 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha jīrṇarasasyādhikyaṃ darśayannāha ghanetyādi || 19.34:1 ||
ghanasatvaṃ pādapramāṇaṃ jīrṇaṃ yasmin saḥ || 19.34:2 ||
kāntajīrṇa iti pādapramāṇakāntajīrṇa ityarthaḥ || 19.34:3 ||
punastīkṣṇaṃ samaṃ jīrṇaṃ yasminnevaṃvidho rasaḥ || 19.34:4 ||
kṣetrīkaraṇaṃ pradhānaṃ sarvatretyarthaḥ |
baddho yaḥ khoṭatāṃ yāto dhmāto dhmātaḥ kṣayaṃ vrajet |
khoṭo bandhaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ śīghraṃ sarvagadāpahaḥ |
iti granthāntare || 19.34:5 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.35


ghanasatvakāntasūtaṃ mṛtahema śatāvarīrasopetam |
ghṛtamadhulīḍhaṃ varṣānnihanti mṛtyuṃ jarāṃ caiva || ṅṛht_19.35 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

ghanasatvamabhrasāraḥ kāntaṃ cumbakotthaṃ sūto rasaḥ ekavadbhāvo dvandvasamāsāttathā mṛtaṃ hema pañcatvamāptaṃ kanakaṃ ca etaccatuṣkaṃ śatāvarīrasopetaṃ śatamūlīdravabhāvitaṃ punarghṛtamadhulīḍhaṃ ghṛtamadhubhyāṃ līḍhamāsvāditaṃ satvarṣādvarṣaparimāṇātmṛtyuvyādhiṃ jarāṃ ca hanti nāśayatītyarthaḥ || 19.35:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.36


eṣāmekaṃ yogaṃ kṣetrīkaraṇārthamāditaḥ kṛtvā |
saṃvatsaramayanaṃ vā niḥśreyasasiddhaye yojyam || ṅṛht_19.36 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

viśeṣamāha eṣāmityādi || 19.36:1 ||
eṣāṃ pūrvoktānāṃ yogānāṃ madhye ādita ārambhataḥ ekaṃ yogaṃ kṛtvā niḥśreyaso mokṣaḥ tatsiddhaye niṣpattaye saṃvatsaraṃ varṣaparimāṇamayanaṃ ṣaṇmāsaparyantaṃ yojyaṃ bhoktṛṣu iti śeṣaḥ || 19.36:2 ||
muktiḥ kaivalyanirvāṇaśreyoniḥśreyasāmṛtamityamaraḥ || 19.36:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.37-38


abhrakasasyakamākṣikarasakadaradavimalavajragirijatubhiḥ |
vaikrāntakāntatīkṣṇairhāṭakatārāratāmraiśca || ṅṛht_19.37 ||
saṃyuktairvyastairvā dvitricaturbhiryathālābham |
jīrṇahato rasendro rasāyane śasyate sadbhiḥ || ṅṛht_19.38 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

bahūnnirdiśan viśeṣamāha abhraketyādi || 19.37-38:1 ||
abhrakaḥ prasiddhaḥ sasyakaścapalaḥ mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ rasakaṃ kharparaṃ daradaṃ hiṅgulaṃ vimalaṃ sitamākṣikaṃ vajrakaṃ hīrakaṃ girijatu śilājatu etaiḥ || 19.37-38:2 ||
punarvaikrāntetyādi vaikrāntaṃ vajrabhūmijaṃ rajaḥ kāntaṃ lohajāti tīkṣṇaṃ sāraḥ etaiśca || 19.37-38:3 ||
hāṭakatārāratāmraiśca hāṭakaṃ hema tāraṃ rūpyamāraṃ rājarītiḥ tāmraṃ śulbametaiśca etairuddiṣṭaiḥ abhrakāditāmrāntaiḥ samastairekatrīkṛtairvyastairvā pṛthakkṛtairvā yathālābhaṃ lābhamanatikramya bhavatīti yathālābhaṃ dvitricaturbhirvā abhrādyairjīrṇahato rasendro jīrṇābhrādīnāṃ hatiryasmin sa tathoktaḥ rasāyane jarāvyādhivināśane rasaśāstramarmajñaiḥ śasyate abhrādayaḥ praśastā uktā ityarthaḥ || 19.37-38:4 ||
yugmam || 19.37-38:5 ||
vajrādibhirhataḥ sūto hatasūtasamo'paraḥ |
śṛṅkhalābaddhanāmā syāddehalohavidhāyakaḥ |
asya prabhāvādvegena vyāptirbhavati niścitam |
iti granthāntare || 19.37-38:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.39


viṣanāgavaṅgabaddho bhukto hi rasaḥ karoti kuṣṭhādīn |
uparasabaddhe tu rase sphuṭanti bhukte tathāṅgāni || ṅṛht_19.39 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

kutsitavidhānaṃ darśayannāha viṣetyādi || 19.39:1 ||
viṣanāgavaṅgabaddho rasaḥ viṣaṃ saktukādikaṃ nāgaḥ sīsakaḥ vaṅgaṃ trapu etairbaddho bandhanamāptaḥ sa bhuktaḥ san hi niścitaṃ kuṣṭhādīn kuṣṭhajvarakṣayādīn || 19.39:2 ||
uparasabaddhe rase uparasairgandhādibhiḥ aṣṭabhiḥ baddho bandhanamāpanno yo'sau rasaḥ tasmin bhukte sati bhokturaṅgāni hastapādādīni sphuṭanti || 19.39:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.40-41

ghanasattvakāntakāñcībhāskaratīkṣṇaiśca cīrṇajīrṇasya |
sūtasya guñjāmātrā māṣakamekaṃ parā mātrā || ṅṛht_19.40 ||
śatavedhino dviguñjā tathā sahasraikavedhino guñjā |
ardhā ca lakṣavedhinaḥ siddhārthaḥ koṭivedhinaḥ sūtāt || ṅṛht_19.41 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

jīrṇarasasya mātrāmāha ghanetyādi || 19.40-41:1 ||
etaiścīrṇajīrṇaṃ pūrvaṃ cīrṇaṃ kavalitaṃ paścājjīrṇaṃ jāraṇamāpannaṃ tasya sūtasya guñjā mātrā bhakṣaṇāya guñjā yathā |
ṣaṭsarṣapairyavastveko guñjaikā tu yavaistribhiḥ |
iti || 19.40-41:2 ||
parā anyā mātrā anyajīrṇasūtasya mātrā māṣamekaṃ māṣako yathā |
guñjābhirdaśabhiḥ prokto māṣako brahmaṇā purā |
iti || 19.40-41:3 ||
etaiḥ kaiḥ ghanasattvakāntakāñcībhāskaratīkṣṇaiḥ abhrakasattvacumbakatāpyatāmrasārairiti || 19.40-41:4 ||
atha vedhaviśeṣeṇa parimāṇamāha śatetyādi || 19.40-41:5 ||
śatavedhinaḥ sūtasya guñjāpramāṇā mātrā jñeyā tathā tena prakāreṇa sahasraikavedhinaḥ sūtasyāpi guñjāmānameva lakṣavedhinaḥ sūtātardhā raktikā punaḥ koṭivedhinaḥ sūtātsiddhārthaḥ sarṣapamānā || 19.40-41:6 ||
śatāṃśena vedho vidyate yasmin sa śatavedhī tasya śatavedhinaḥ || 19.40-41:7 ||
evaṃ sarvatra jñeyam || 19.40-41:8 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.42


hemaniyojitasūtaṃ kāntamaṇiṃ vividhaguṭikāśca |
japahomadevatārcananirataḥ pumāniti dhārayet || ṅṛht_19.42 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

japaḥ aghorādijapaḥ homastaddaśāṃśena havanaṃ devatārcanaṃ devatānāṃ gaṇeśaviṣṇuraviśivacaṇḍīnāmarcanameteṣu nirataḥ saktaḥ evaṃvidhiḥ pumān || 19.42:1 ||
iti kiṃ hemaniyojitasūtaṃ dhārayethemnā saha niyojito miśrito yaḥ sūtaḥ taṃ kāntamaṇiḥ kāntaścāsau maṇiśca vā kāntamaṇiḥ kāntasaṃjñako maṇiḥ ca punaḥ vividhaguṭikāḥ vividhāśca tā guṭikāśceti || 19.42:2 ||

____________________________________________________
ṅṛht19.43-44


śālestu piṣṭakodbhavabhojanamājyaṃ ca mudgamāṃsarasaiḥ |
yavagodhūmānnāni ca gokṣīraṃ mastu ca viśeṣāt || ṅṛht_19.43 ||
pāne jalamakṣāraṃ madhurāṇi yāni kāni śastāni |
peyaṃ cāturjātakarpūrāmodamuditamukham || ṅṛht_19.44 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

atha pathyānāha śālerityādi || 19.43-44:1 ||
śāleḥ ṣaṣṭikādeḥ piṣṭodbhavabhojanaṃ piṣṭakotpannaṃ ca tadbhojanaṃ ceti || 19.43-44:2 ||
ājyaṃ ghṛtam || 19.43-44:3 ||
kaiḥ saha mudgamāṃsarasaiḥ saha mudgāḥ pratītāḥ atra viśeṣātmāṃsāni bhojyāni gokṣīraṃ ca bhojyaṃ punarmastu gorasasaṃbhavaṃ viśeṣātbhojyam || 19.43-44:4 ||
pāne akṣāraṃ jalaṃ miṣṭajalaṃ prayojyaṃ punaḥ yāni kāni miṣṭāni dravyāṇi atrānuktatamāni praśastāni śreṣṭhāni punaḥ caturjātakakarpūrāmodamuditamukhaṃ yathā syāttathā dravyaṃ peyaṃ pātavyaṃ caturjātakaṃ tvakpatrailānāgakesaraṃ karpūraṃ ghanasārameṣāmāmodena parimalena muditaṃ yanmukhaṃ vāsitamukhamityarthaḥ || 19.43-44:5 ||
yuvatyā jalpanaṃ kāryaṃ yuvatyā cāṅgamardanam |
tasyāḥ sparśanamātreṇa dehe kramati sūtakaḥ |
iti viśeṣo granthāntarāt || 19.43-44:6 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.45


madyāranālapānaṃ tailaṃ dadhi vā rase neṣṭam |
kaṭutailenābhyaṅgaṃ vapuṣi na kuryādrasāyane matimān || ṅṛht_19.45 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

apathyānyāha madyetyādi || 19.45:1 ||
madyāranāletyādi madyaṃ surā āranālaṃ kāñjikaṃ tayoḥ pānaṃ neṣṭaṃ na praśastaṃ vā tailaṃ dadhi na neṣṭaṃ tailaṃ tilodbhavaṃ dadhi dugdhavikāraḥ etayorapi pānaṃ na praśastaṃ kaṭutailena sarṣapatailena vapuṣi abhyaṅgaṃ mardanaṃ na kuryāt || 19.45:2 ||
matimān rasāyane adhikaraṇe ityarthaḥ || 19.45:3 ||

____________________________________________________
ṅṛht19.46


dagdhamapakvamamadhuramuṣṇaṃ kṣīraṃ na naṣṭamāṃsaṃ tu |
paryuṣitaṃ phalamūlaṃ bhakṣyaṃ naivātra nirdiṣṭam || ṅṛht_19.46 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

dagdhaṃ dravyaṃ rasāyane neṣṭamapakvaṃ ca neṣṭamamadhuraṃ kaṭutiktakaṣāyāmlalavaṇaṃ ca neṣṭamuṣṇaṃ vahnitaptamiti tu punaḥ naṣṭamāṃsaṃ ninditamāṃsaṃ neṣṭaṃ punaḥ paryuṣitaṃ saṃdhānīkṛtamevaṃvidhaṃ phalamūlaṃ phalaṃ mūlaṃ ca atra rasāyane bhakṣyaṃ na nirdiṣṭaṃ kathitam |
kūṣmāṇḍaṃ karkaṭī caiva kaliṅgaṃ kāravellakam |
kusumbhikā ca karkoṭī kadalī kākamācikā |
kakārāṣṭakametaddhi varjayedrasabhakṣakaḥ |
iti granthāntare || 19.46:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.47


atha laṅghanaṃ na kāryaṃ yāmādho bhojanaṃ naiva |
ityapanīya niṣiddhaṃ rasarāje dhīmatā kāryam || ṅṛht_19.47 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

asmin rasāyane laṅghanaṃ na kāryaṃ punaryāmādhaḥ praharamadhye bhojanaṃ na kāryamityarthaḥ || 19.47:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.48


varjitacintākopaḥ kuryācca sukhāmbunā snānam |
noccāṭayedgrahajvararākṣasabhūtāni mātṛdevīṃśca || ṅṛht_19.48 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha varjitetyādi || 19.48:1 ||
varjitacintākopa iti cintā ca kopaśceti cintākopau tau varjitau yena saḥ evaṃvidhaḥ san sukhāmbunā sukhoṣṇāmbunā snānaṃ kuryāt || 19.48:2 ||
graharākṣasabhūtāni noccāṭayetgrahaṇādgrahāḥ piśācādayaḥ jvaro rogarājaḥ rākṣasāḥ kravyādāḥ bhūtāni devayonayaḥ etāni noccāṭayetsvasthānānna cālayet || 19.48:3 ||
punarmātṛdevīṃśca mātaraḥ saptamātaraḥ devyo dakṣiṇyādayaḥ tā api noccāṭayet || 19.48:4 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.49


parame brahmaṇi līnaḥ praśāntacittaḥ samatvamāpannaḥ |
āśvāsayan trivargaṃ vijitya rasānandaparitṛptaḥ || ṅṛht_19.49 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

cintākopanirodhe hetumāha parame ityādi || 19.49:1 ||
rasāyanakartā parame brahmaṇi citsvarūpe līnaḥ tanmayatāṃ prāpto bhavetpraśāntacittaśca viṣayebhyo nivṛttamanā bhavetsamatvamāpannaḥ svasute śatrau ca nirvairo yathā syāttathā trivargaṃ dharmārthakāmarūpaṃ vijitya rasānandaparitṛpto bhavetharṣaparipūrita ityarthaḥ || 19.49:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.50


yastyaktvā śāstravidhiṃ pravartate svecchayā rase mūḍhaḥ |
tasya viruddhācārādajīrṇamutpadyate nitarām || ṅṛht_19.50 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

vidherniyatatvaṃ darśayannāha ya ityādi || 19.50:1 ||
yaḥ pumān śāstravidhiṃ tyaktvā svecchayā ucchṛṅkhalamanasā rase sūte pravartate sa mūḍhaḥ jñānaśūnyaḥ tasya puṃsaḥ viruddhācārātnitarāmatiśayena ajīrṇamutpadyate tadrasājīrṇamiti || 19.50:2 ||
____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.51


sambhavatīhājīrṇe nidrālasyaṃ jvarastamo dāhaḥ |
nābhitalaśūlamalpaṃ jaḍatāruciraṅgabhaṅgaśca || ṅṛht_19.51 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rasājīrṇalakṣaṇamāha sambhavatītyādi || 19.51:1 ||
iha rasāyane ajīrṇaṃ yadā sambhavati utpadyate tadā etāni lakṣaṇāni syuḥ || 19.51:2 ||
nidrā atiśayena nidrā ālasyamaṅgāṅgaśaithilyaṃ jvaraḥ prasiddhaḥ tamo mūrchā dāha ūṣmā punarnābhitale bastau alpamalpaṃ śūlaṃ jaḍatāsyasya aruciḥ nirabhilāpitā bhaṅgo'ṅgasya aṅgamardanaṃ bhokturetāni lakṣaṇāni rasājīrṇe syurityarthaḥ || 19.51:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.52


jñātvetyevamajīrṇamasya pracchādanāya yogo'yam |
kāryo divasatritayaṃ saṃtyajya rasāyanaṃ sudhiyā || ṅṛht_19.52 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

ajīrṇe'pyupāyamāha jñātvetyādi || 19.52:1 ||
ityevamuktaprakāreṇa nidrādilakṣaṇenājīrṇaṃ jñātvā dhīmatā puṃsā asyājīrṇasya pracchādanāya vināśāya rasāyanaṃ saṃtyajya divasatritayaṃ yogaḥ kāryaḥ || 19.52:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.53


karkoṭīmūlarasaṃ kaṣāyamatha sindhunā pibettridinam |
sauvarcalasahitaṃ vā gojalasahitaṃ rasājīrṇe || ṅṛht_19.53 ||
------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha karkoṭītyādi || 19.53:1 ||
karkoṭīmūlarasaṃ karkoṭī yā vallī tasyāḥ mūlarasaṃ tridinaṃ pibetvātha kaṣāyaṃ kathaṃ sindhunā saindhavena sahitaṃ pibetvā tatkvāthaṃ gojalasahitaṃ gomūtramilitaṃ rasājīrṇe pibetsauvarcalasahitamiti sauvarcalasya prativāpaṃ karkoṭīrase nikṣipya pibettridinaṃ sarvatretyarthaḥ || 19.53:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.54


piṣṭvātha mātuluṅgīṃ pibati rasaṃ śuṇṭhisaindhavaṃ prātaḥ |
kvathitaṃ gosalilena tu rakṣati samyakrasājīrṇam || ṅṛht_19.54 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

anyaccāha piṣṭvetyādi || 19.54:1 ||
mātuluṅgasyeyaṃ jaṭā mātuluṅgī tāṃ piṣṭvā tasyā rasaṃ śuṇṭhī saindhavaṃ ca yaḥ pumān prātaḥ pibati tu punaḥ kvathitaṃ tasyāḥ kaṣāyaṃ gosalilena yaḥ pibati rasājīrṇe taṃ puruṣaṃ rakṣati na vināśayatītyarthaḥ || 19.54:2 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.55


kathamapi yaccājñānātnāgādikalaṅkito raso bhuktaḥ |
tannodanāya ca pibetgojalakaṭukāravalliśiphāḥ || ṅṛht_19.55 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

nāgādiyuktarasabhuktopāyamāha kathamapītyādi || 19.55:1 ||
ca punaḥ yatyasmātnāgādikalaṅkito rasaḥ nāgavaṅgasahito raso'jñānātkathamapi bhuktaḥ tannodanāya tasya nāgavaṅgāṅkitarasasya nodanāya gojalakaṭukāravalliśiphāḥ gojalaṃ gomūtraṃ kaṭutiktā kāravallīśiphā kāravallīlatāyāḥ śiphā jaṭā etadauṣadhaṃ pibettena nāgavaṅgādidoṣo vinaśyati || 19.55:2 ||

____________________________________________________
ṅṛht19.56


śarapuṃkhāsuradālīpaṭolabimbīśca kākamācī ca |
ekatamā ceduditā śṛtāmajīrṇe hi seveta || ṅṛht_19.56 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha śarapuṅkhetyādi || 19.56:1 ||
āsāmauṣadhīnāṃ madhye ekatamā yā uditā kathitā śṛtā kvathitā tāṃ hi niścitamajīrṇe seveta tena ajīrṇaṃ naśyatīti bhāvaḥ || 19.56:2 ||
tāḥ kā auṣadhyaḥ śarapuṅkhā prasiddhā suradālī devadālī paṭolaṃ pratītaṃ nāma bimbī golā kākamācī prasiddhā etā ityarthaḥ || 19.56:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.57


atyamlalavaṇakaṭukai rasasaṃsrāvo jaro bhavati |
atimadhuraiśca vinaśyati jaṭharavahniḥ satatabhuktaiśca || ṅṛht_19.57 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

taccāha atyamletyādi || 19.57:1 ||
atyamlaṃ cukrādi atilavaṇaṃ kṣārādi atikaṭukaṃ nimbakaṭukītyādi etai rasasaṃsrāvo jaro jāraṇakaro bhavati jaratīti jaraḥ || 19.57:2 ||
punarapi madhuraiḥ ikṣurasādibhiḥ satatabhuktaiḥ jaṭharavahniḥ koṣṭhāgniḥ vinaśyati abhyāśrayo vinaśyatītyabhiprāyaḥ || 19.57:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.58


yaḥ punarevaṃ satataṃ karoti mūḍhaḥ samāhāram |
tasya vinaśyatyagnirna khalu krāmati raso bhavedvyādhiḥ || ṅṛht_19.58 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

tadviśeṣamāha ya ityādi || 19.58:1 ||
yaḥ punarmūḍho mūrkho'jīrṇānantaramatyamlalavaṇakaṭukāhāraṃ satataṃ nirantaraṃ karoti tasyāgniḥ koṣṭhāgnirvinaśyati rasaśca na krāmati svaguṇānna prakāśayati || 19.58:2 ||
tarhi kiṃ bhavedityāha vyādhirbhavedityarthaḥ || 19.58:3 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.59


yastu mahāgnisahatvādrasācchatasahasralakṣavedhīśaḥ |
anayā kriyayā sidhyati sa yatnādrasakriyāyogāt || ṅṛht_19.59 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

rasācchatasahasralakṣavedhī bhavetsa yatnātanayā pūrvoktakriyayā sidhyati || 19.59:1 ||

____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.60


śatasahasralakṣavedhī koṭirathārbudanirbudaṃ vāpi |
piṣṭaṃ bhuñjīta rasaṃ balisahitaṃ siddhido bhavati || ṅṛht_19.60 ||

------------------

*Comm. ṃugdhāvabodhinī:

athottaravidhānena lakṣaṇamāha śatetyādi || 19.60:1 ||
śatasahasralakṣavedhī rasaḥ || 19.60:2 ||
balisahitaṃ piṣṭaṃ rasaṃ bhuñjīta balinā gandhakena sahitaṃ śatādārabhya sahasralakṣakoṭyarbudānāṃ krameṇa daśaguṇottaraṃ saṃkhyā jñātavyā || 19.60:3 ||
evaṃ kṛtaḥ san rasaḥ siddhido yathoktaguṇakṛdbhavatītyarthaḥ || 19.60:4 ||
____________________________________________________

ṅṛht19.61-64


krāmati tato hi sūto janayati putrāṃśca devagarbhābhān |
strīṣu ca niścalakāmo bhavati valīpalitanirmuktaḥ || ṅṛht_19.61 ||
buddhirbalaṃ prabhāvaḥ saha cāyuṣā vardhate rasāyaninaḥ |
prāptasya divyabuddhiṃ divyāśca guṇāḥ pravardhante || ṅṛht_19.62 ||
evaṃ rasasaṃsiddho duḥkhajarāmaraṇavarjito guṇavān |
khegamanena ca nityaṃ saṃcarate sakalabhuvaneṣu || ṅṛht_19.63 ||
